	maṟaimuṭi taṉṉil niṟaitiru vuruviṉai!
	kaṇṇum uṇarvuṅ katuvāp peṟṟiyai!
	aimperum pūtamum āki niṉṟaṉai!
	ulakuniṉ ṉuruvē, oḷikaṇiṉ vaḻiyē,
225	alakilāc camayat tavvavar tamakkup
	palavē ṟuruviṉ nilavunai nīyē!
	āṟuniṉ mukamē, aintuniṉ vaṭivē,
	nāṉkuniṉ moḻiyē, mūṉṟuniṉ kaṇṇē,
	iraṇṭuniṉ ṟuṇaiyē,oṉṟu niṉ vēlē,
230	maruṇṭa uṇarvoṭu māṭcimai eytā
	muruṭa ṉēṟkun tiruvaruḷ tarunaiyeṉ
	ṟiyāṉaṟi yaḷavaiyiṉ ēttat tāṉuvantu
	kuṟipparu miṉpattup piṟakkiṉaṉ eṉaiyē, atumutal
	iṉpam ārntaṅ kiruttal allatu
235	tuṉpa meṉpa taṟiyēṉ, aṉpoṭu
	nīyum avaṉvayiṟ celiṉē pāyaruḷ
	oruvantan tarukuvaṉ maṉṟa; maruvantu
	talaippeyaṉ māriyiṉ malaippaṭu nīttam
	painniṟap pīliyuñ cenniṟat tukirum
240	vēḻa maruppuṅ kāḻakiṟ ṟuṇiyum
	oruṅku varaṉṟi maruṅku viraiik
	kuṇṭukaṭ paṭukariṉ maṇṭi niṟaintāṅku,
	avalak kavalaiyum uvalaic cuvaiyum
	muraṇuṟu aṟivun tirukiya cerukkum
245	mutalaṟap peyarttuc citaiyā tōṭi
	aṉpiṉ niṟainta iṉpa veḷḷattup
	paḻiyāk kaṭumpoṭum viḻumitiṟ ṟuvaṉṟi
	ēḻula kaḻiyiṉum aḻiyātu
	ūḻi yūḻiyum vāḻmati ciṟantē!	(28)
29. kāppuk kaimmikkuk kāmam perukiyuraittal
ciṟanta poruḷoṟṟic cēvvēḷ ivarai
maṟantiṅ kuyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - paṟantōṭit
tiṇṭōḷ taḻuvuvam iṉṟēṟ ceḻuvaraiyaik
kaṇṭēṟik kīḻviḻuvaṅ kāṇ.	(29)
30. aṭiyuṟaiyiṭutal
kāṇap peṟātator kāṭciyai yēṉuṅ kacinta aṉpāṟ
pēṇap peṟumuk karuviyiṟ kāṇpar piṟaṅku moṟṟi
vāṇap peruntakai yeṉpatu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkumaṉpāṟ
pūṇap peṟuminta mummaṇik kōvaiyuṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē.	(30)
tiruvoṟṟimurukar
mummaṇikkōvai
muṟṟum

ōm civam
tiruvoṟṟi murukar 
mummaṇikkōvai urai


kāppuc ceyyuḷ
nīr vaḷam naṉṟu ōṅkum tiruvoṟṟi nīḷ nakaril - nīrvaḷam peritum ōṅkukiṉṟa tiruvoṟṟiyū  reṉṉum pukaḻālnīṇṭa paṭṭiṉattil tirukkōyil koṇṭa, cūrvaḷam tiṉ vēlōṟku - cūrmāliṉatu ceḻumaiyait tiṉṟa vēṟpaṭaiyai yuṭaiya murukakkaṭavuḷukku, tūyanūl - `muṉṉamaṇik kōvai yeṉṉum ikkuṟṟamaṟṟanūlai, cīr vaḷara - ciṟappōṅkumpaṭi, emmāl iyaṟṟuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - emmaikkoṇṭu ceyvatu oṉṟu uḷḷatu, atu kaimmā mukattaṉ kaḻal ākum - aṅṅaṉañ ceyyuṅ karuvi yāṉaimukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaṭi yākumeṉka.
`naṉṟu perumaipporuḷatu; naṉṟu peritākum eṉṉun tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇka.
`cūrvaḷantiṉ vēlōṉ eṉpatu, cūraṉuṭaiya valimai celvam mutaliyavaikaḷai aḻitta vēlō ṉeṉṉum poruṭṭu; uṭaiyōṉ ṟoḻil uṭaimaimē lēṟṟappaṭṭatu.
`tūyanūl tūyaṉākiya murukakkaṭavuḷaip pāṭutalpaṟṟi innūl tūya eṉa aṭaikoṭukkappaṭṭatu.
innūlaippāṭum ākkiyōṟkum itaṉai ōtuvārkkuñ ciṟappuṇṭāka eṉṉumporuṭṭuc `cīr vaḷara veṉṟār.
`emmāl āl karuvipporuḷil vantatu.
āciriyar innūlait tam muṉaippiṉṟi iṟaivaṉ vaḻiyāy niṉṟu avaṉiyaṟṟa iyaṟṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, iyaṟṟuvatu eṉattaṉviṉaiyāṟ kūṟiṉār; āl, acai.
`kaimmā kaiyaiyuṭaiya vilaṅku; aḵtu yāṉai.
`atu kaḻal ākum eṉṟu viṉai muṭikka.
eṭuttukkoṇṭa nūl iṉitu muṭiyumāṟu mutaliṟ kāppuc ceyyuḷ kūṟutal nūlāciriyarkku marapātaliṉ, aṭikaḷum icceyyuḷāṟ kāppukkūṟiṉar. piṟkāla vaḻakku yāṉaimukak kaṭavuḷ mēl niṟṟaliṉ icceyyuḷum avaraiyē paraviṟṟu.
ippāṭṭu nēricai veṇpā. itu `nīr eṉpatu mutaṟ `kaḻal eṉpatu iṟuvāyāka muppatu coṟkaḷāṟ kūṟappaṭu talāl, innūlum muppatu pāṭṭukkaḷā lākkap paṭṭateṉpatu kuṟippu. itaṉkaṇ il ku āl eṉṉum vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷum ēṉaic coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭṭaṉa. eṉṉai? avvurupukaḷ tāmuñ coṟkaḷōṭoppap poruḷuṇarttuta luṭaimaiyi ṉeṉpatu. aṟṟāyiṉ, aṉ a mutaliya peyar viṉai vikutikaḷum poruḷuṇarttuta luṭaimaiyiṉ, avai tāmum avvurupukaḷaip pōlac coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭal vēṇṭumā leṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu, oru coṟ ṟoṭariṉiṭaiyē il ku āl mutaliya vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ coṟkaḷiṉuḷ niṉṟum poruḷuṇarttutaṟ kaṭ pirinticaittal pōl, aṉ a mutaliya peyar viṉai vikutikaḷ aṅṅaṉam pirinticaiyāmaiyiṉ, av vikutikaḷ coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭutaṟku uriyaṉavallaveṉ ṟuṇarntukoḷka.
iṉi, ikkāppucceyyuḷ `nīr eṉṉuñ collai mutalākavum, `kaḻal eṉṉuñ collai iṟutiyākavuṅ koṇṭu viḷaṅkalāl, nūlum avvāṟē `nīr eṉpatai mutaliluṅ `kaḻal eṉpatai iṟutiyiluṅ koṇṭu viḷaṅku meṉpatūuṅ kuṟippā ṉuṇarappaṭum.
iṉṉum, ippāṭṭiṉ mutalaṭiyil tiruvoṟṟinakarc ciṟappum, iraṇṭāmaṭiyiṟ cūraṉaḻivum, mūṉṟāmaṭiyil tāmum piṟarum payaṉ peṟumāṟum, nāṉkāmaṭiyil avaraip payaṉ peṟuvikkum iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭinilaiyuṅ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, avaitām muṟaiyē poruḷaṟiviṉ virivaiyum, aṟiyāmai yiṉ tēyvaiyum, avviraṇṭaiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkutaṟkuriya uyiriṉ takutiyaiyum, attakutik kēṟpa atu peṟum pēriṉpap pēṟṟaiyuṅ kuṟittu, avvāṟṟāl ittiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇikkōvaiyeṉṉum nūlun tiruvoṟṟi nakaraic ciṟappikkumukattāl uyiriṉaṟivaip puṟapporuḷkaḷiṟ celutti akaṉṟuviriyumāṟu ceytu, piṉṉar ammukattāl aṟiyāmaitēyttu ataṉ uṇarviṉai nuṭpamākki, iṟaivaṉatu pēriṉpattiṉai nukarntu kiṭakkac ceyyumeṉṉum muṟai teḷivittavāṟu.
mēlum, icceyyuḷ `ākum eṉṉum viṉaiyaiyē muṭiviṟ peṟutaṟkurittāyiruppavum, āciriyar ataṉaic `cīrvaḷar eṉpataṟkuṅ `kaḻal eṉpataṟkum iṭaiyē yiṭṭu muṭittiruttalāl, innūlun taṉṉaip payilvārkku immaippayaṉkaḷai muṟṟun tantu iṟutiyil iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭippēṟṟaip payakkumeṉap payaṉ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟellāṅ kāppuc ceyyuḷeṉpatu nūliṉ kaṭkāṇappaṭum nūlamaitikaḷaiyum poruṭkūṟupāṭukaḷaiyum payaṉ mutaliya piṟavaṟṟaiyu mellāṅ kuṟippāṟ ṟaṉkaṭkoṇṭu viḷaṅkutal atutaṉakku ilakkaṇamāmeṉka. āciriyar civañāṉamuṉivarun tamatu civañāṉapōtamāpāṭiyattu maṅkalavāḻttuc ceyyuḷiṉ uraiyiṟutiyilum iṅṅaṉam nūṉutal poruḷellāṅ kuṟippāṟ ṟaṉṉakat taṭakkiniṟṟal maṅkalavāḻttukku ilakkaṇameṉṟuṇarka eṉṟu urai kūṟiṉar. kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkameṉpatum maṅkalavāḻt teṉpatum orupuṭaiyoppumaiyuṭaiya.
1. ceñcoṭu kiḷattal
neñcoṭu kiḷattal eṉpatu taṉ talaivaṉ perumaiyum avaṉṟaṉ aruḷuruvai niṉaitalāl taṉakku viḷaiyum naṉmaiyun talaivi taṉ neñciṟku eṭuttukkūṟal; immuṟaiyiṉai `neñcaṟivuṟūu veṉavum vaḻaṅkupa.
itu mutal nāṟpattōraṭikāṟum maruta nilattiṟkum neytaṉilattiṟkun tiṇaimayakkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār.
(1 mutal 4 aṭi) nīr vaḷam kaviṉṟa cīr vaḷar paḻaṉattu - nīr vaḷattāl aḻakupeṟṟa ciṟappōṅkiya kaḻaṉiyil, nel kural kavviya nal kural paim kiḷi - neṟpayiriṉ katiraik kauviya aḻakiya kuraloliyiṉai yuṭaiya paccaikkiḷi, kāṉal kāvil tāṉ viraintu eyti - kaṭaṟkaraiccōlaiyil viraivāyp paṟantuceṉṟu, poṉvīñāḻal polintu iṉitu iruppavum - poṉniṟamāṉa malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya nāka marattiṉmēl maṉaeḻucciyōṭu aḻakāy uṭkārntirukkavum.
`vaḷaṅ kaviṉṟa mūṉṟām vēṟṟumait tokai, cīr - viḷaiviṉperumai. kural - neṟkatir; itu neṟkatirum... kuraleṉamoḻipa eṉṉum piṅkalantaiyā laṟiyappaṭum.
itu, maruta nilattukkiḷi neytal nākamarattiṟ ceṉṟiruttal kūṟiṟṟu.
(5-8) karum kaḻi vāriya peru mīṉ kuvaii - kariyaniṟat tiṉaiyuṭaiya kaḻiyiliruntu vāriyeṭutta periya mīṉkaḷaik kuvittu, val tiṟal paratavar muṉṟiltoṟum uṇakkum - valiya uṭaluram vāynta cempaṭavar tammuṭaiya vīṭṭu muṟṟaṅka ṭōṟum ularttukiṉṟa, tuṭikkaṇ tuṇiyal koṭikkulam kavarntu - uṭukkaiyiṉ kaṇkaḷaippōl vaṭṭattuṇṭu kaḷāyirukkuṅ karuvāṭukaḷaik kākkaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ tiruṭiyeṭuttukkoṇṭu, marutaṅ kāvil peritu vaikavum - maruta nilattu iḷamaraccōlaiyil ēkip perumitattuṭaṉ amarntirukkavum,
tiṟal - uṭampiṉ uram.
ularttutaṟkāka vakuntu veyilil virittuvaikkap paṭṭukkāyum mīṉ kāṇpārkku uṭukkaiyiṉ kaṇkaḷaippōl iraṇṭu vaṭṭattuṇṭukaḷāyt tōṉṟutalāl, atu `tuṭikkaṭṭuṇiya leṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtivvāṟu tōṉṟutal koḻumīṉ kuṟaiiya tuṭikkaṭṭuṇiyal eṉṉum maturaik kāñciyālum (320) āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tuṭiyiṉkaṇ pōluruṇṭa tuṇikaḷ eṉṟu ataṟkuk kūṟiya uraiyālun teḷiyappaṭum.
itu, neytalnilattuk kākkaiyum mīṉum marutanilattuc ceṉṟamai kūṟiṟṟu.
(9-11) kuḷaṉ uṟai vāḻkkai vaḷa nīrkkōḻi - maruta nilattuk kuḷattiṉkaṇ vāḻum vāḻkkaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya uṭaṟceḻumai vāynta nīrkkōḻi, maṉaiyuṟai nīṅkit tuṇai kūp peyarntu - avviṭattu uṟaitalait tavirntu taṉatu peṭaippaṟavaiyaik kūviyaḻaittuk koṇṭu, kālvaḻi ōṭik kaḻiyiṟ cēravum - vāykkāl vaḻiyāka nīnticceṉṟuneytaṉilattuk kaḻinīriṟ cēravum.
`vaḷam eṉpatai nīrkkuk kūṟutalumām. `uṟai iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu viṉaittokai, piṉṉatu mutaṉilaittoḻiṟpeyar. `tuṇai iccol irupāṟkum urittēyāyiṉum, perum pālum peṇpāl mēṟṟākavē vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
`kūppeyartal orucol; aḻaittaleṉṉum poruṭṭām.
itu, marutanilattu nīrkkōḻi neytaṉilattuk kaḻiyiṟ cērntamai kūṟiṟṟu.
(12-17) pulavu maṇam muṉaiiya nilavuuṟaḻ kuruku - neytaṉilattuk kaḻiyil mikairuntamaiyāṟ pulāl nāṟṟattai veṟuttuviṭṭa nilavaiyotta veḷva kaṇ akal vicumpil naṇṇiya mukilmēl ivarntuḷiya nāraikaḷ eḻutiṅkaḷ - iṭamakaṉṟa vāṉiṟ poruntiya kariya mukiliṉ mēl ēṟit tōṉṟun tiṅkaḷaip pōla, parantu keḻu tuṇinīrt taṭattiṉ maṇam malar ārum - iṭamakaṉṟu uḷḷa teḷinta nīriṉai yuṭaiya marutanilattuk kuḷattiluḷḷa maṇaṅkamaḻum pūkkaḷai mēyntukoṇṭirukkum, irumputirittu aṉṉa aṟal maruppu erumai karumpuṟam maruṅkil - irumpai muṟukkiṉāṟpōl aṟuppu amainta maruppai yuṭaiya erumaikaḷiṉ kariya mutukākiya iṭattil, oruṅki niṟpavum - kūmpi niṟkavum,
`muṉaiiya veṟutta; iḵtipporuṭṭātal cēṟṟu nilai muṉaiiya ceṅkaṭ kārāṉ eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟiṟ (4) kāṇka. `nilavu niṟattiṟ kuvamai. `naṇṇiya poruntiya. `ivarnteḻu orucol nīra.
`tuṇinīr eṉpataṟkut `teḷintanīreṉṟē āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum tuṇinīr mellaval (maturaikkāñci, 283) eṉpuḻi urai kūṟiṉār.
`irumputirittaṉṉa veṉpatu, maruppiṉ valimai yuṇarttiṟṟu. ivvuvamaiyai irumputirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppu eṉṟu kuṟuṅkuṭimarutaṉārum akanāṉūṟṟil (46) eṭuttāṇṭār.
aṟal, toḻilaṭiyākap piṟantapeyar.
`puṟamaruṅku puṟamākiya iṭattil; iṅku maruṅku eṉpatu iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟatu; `maruṅkiṉkaṇ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu urupu keṭaccāriyaiyuṭaṉ niṉṟatu; melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi eṉṉuñcūttirattil meypeṟa eṉṟamaiyāl iṉṉōraṉṉa muṭikkappaṭumeṉpar uraikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (tolkāppiyam, eḻuttu, 157).
vicumpu nīrkkuḷattiṟkum, mukil erumaikkum, tiṅkaḷ kurukukkum uvamai; erumaikkuk karumpuṟaṅ kūṟiṉamaiyāl, ataṉ uvamaiyākiya mukilukkuṅ karumaiyuraikkappaṭṭatu.
itu neytal nilattu nārai marutanilattu vayalerumai mēṟceṉṟu niṉṟamai kūṟiṟṟu.
(18-19) poṟikeḻuvāḷai - marutanilattu nīr nilai kaḷiluḷḷa viḷakkam amainta vāḷaimīṉ, veṟipaṭap pāyntu muṭaikkaḻiccuṟavoṭu kaṟuvoṭu maṟiyavum - matarppiṉāṟ carēleṉap pāyntu muṭai nāṟṟattaiyuṭaiya kaḻikkaṇuḷḷa cuṟāmīṉoṭu ciṉattuṭaṉ puraḷavum,
`poṟi keḻu vāḷai `oḷi poruntiya vāḷai yeṉap         poruṭarutal poṟi vari varāl eṉpuḻi (cīvakacintāmaṇi, 44) nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruraitta uraiyālaṟiyappaṭum.
veṟi - matarppu.                                                                                                                               
`kāletirn tōṭuṅ kaḻikkaya leṉṟu piṉvarutaliṉ, ivvāḷai mīṉuṅ kālvaḻicceṉṟu pāyumeṉak koḷka. `kālvaḻiyōṭik kaḻiyiṟcēravu meṉṟu mēlē vantamaiyum niṉaivu kūraṟpāṟṟu.
`oṭu urupukaḷ iraṇṭaṉuḷḷum muṉṉatu kalappuṟu poruḷilum, piṉṉatu aṭaimoḻip poruḷilum vantaṉa.
`muṭaikkaḻi pulālnāṟṟa muṭaiya kaḻi; ēṉaiyellā nīr nilaikaḷiṉum uppaḷattukkaḻiyē muṭaimikutiyum iyalpiluṭaimaiyāl, ilakkiyaṅkaḷellām am muṭaināṟṟattaik kaḻikkē ēṟṟikkūṟutal marapu. pulavunāṟiruṅkaḻi yeṉṟu akappāṭṭilum (180) vantatu. pulavukkaḻi yeṉṟu aṭikaḷē mēlum (22 - 109) uraippar.
itu marutanilattu vāḷai neytalnilattuc cuṟavoṭu pōr purital kūṟiṟṟu.
(20-21) kāl etirntu ōṭum kaḻikkayal - nīrkkālkaḷil etirmukamāy ōṭum neytaṟkaḻiyiṉ kayal mīṉkaḷ, marutaccēloṭu kūṭiccērntu tuḷḷavum - marutanilattuc cēlmīṉkaḷoṭu kalantu oṉṟāyk kutikkavum,
marutanilattu vāykkālkaḷ kaṭaṟpakkam nōkkic cellumātaliṉ, ak kaṭaṟpakkattiliruntu marutanilattai nōkki ēku kayal mīṉkaḷ akkālvāykaḷai etirntu ōṭiṉaveṉṟār. etirntu, etirttu; ivvaṉmai meṉmaikaḷiṟ poruḷvēṟupāṭu uṇṭu; muṉṉaiyatu etirmukamāyc cellutalaiyum, piṉṉaiyatu pōrkku muṉaitalaiyum uṇarttum. kayaloṭu cēluntuḷḷiṟṟeṉpār, `cērntu tuḷḷavum eṉṟār.
itu neytaṟkaḻiyiṉ kayal mīṉkaḷ marutanilattuc cēṟkeṇṭaikaḷoṭu cērntu tuḷḷutal kūṟiṟṟu.
(22-25) muḻuneṟi aviḻkkuṅ koḻuṅkāl ōṭi - maruta nilattu arumpukaḷai itaḻaviḻkkum avviṭattuk koḻuviya kāṟṟu ōṭicceṉṟu, poṉ am tāmaraip polam tukaḷ vāri - poṉṉiṟamāṉa aḻakiya centāmaraippūkkaḷiṉ poṉṉiṟamāṉa poṭiyai aḷḷi, kōḻimuṭṭai pōḻpaṭuttāṅku - kōḻimuṭṭaiyai uṭaittāṟpōla, veḷ ayirk kāṉal koḷḷaiyiṉ vīcavum - veḷḷiya nuṇmaṇalaiyuṭaiya kaṭaṟkaraiccōlaiyil mikutiyāyt tūvavum,
`muḻuneṟi muṟukkuviṭāmuḻukkaṭṭuṭaiya pūmoṭṭu.
nīrvaḷa nilavaḷamuṭaiya marutak kāṟṟākaliṉ `koḻuṅkāl eṉappaṭṭatu.
tukaḷ, iṅku makarantappoṭi; atu poṉṉiṟamā yiruttaliṉ, `polantukaḷeṉa aṭai koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
kōḻimuṭṭaiyai uṭaittakkāl, mañcaṭkaru viraviya veṇkuḻampē nilattiṉkaṭparavutaliṉ, atu tāmaraippūviṉ mañcaṭ poṭiyiṉai veṇmaṇaliṟ citaṟutaṟku uvamai yāyiṟṟu.
`āṅku uvamaccol leṉṟār parimēlaḻakar (tirukkuṟaḷurai, 2: 5).
`ayir nuṇmaṇalukkup peyarātalai iṭikkalap paṉṉa īr ayir (cilap, 6 : 146) eṉṟu āciriyar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ōruvamaiyiṉ vaittu uraittār.
itu marutanilattuk kāṟṟu annilattut tāmaraimalariṉ pūntātukaḷai aḷḷikkoṇṭu neytal nilattuk kaṭaṟkaraic cōlaiyiṟceṉṟu tūvutal kūṟiṟṟu.
(26-30) veḷḷi veṇ tōṭṭut tāḻaiyiṉ aḷaii veḷḷittakaṭṭaiyotta veḷḷiya maṭalkaḷai yuṭaiya tāḻampū vaittuḻavi, kaiyarikkoṇṭa kaṭal kāl ōṭi - ataṉ makarantap poṭiyai vārikkoṇṭa kaṭaṟkāṟṟu maruta nilattukku ōṭi, nīṟu meypūtta nimalaṉpōla - tirunīṟu uṭampeṅkum polivupeṟat tōṉṟiya malattiṉ nīṅkiṉa civaperumāṉaippōla, cemtāmaraikaḷ ciṟantu iṉitu viḷaṅka - civanta tāmaraip pūkkaḷ niṟañciṟantu aḻakāyt tōṉṟumāṟu, veṇpoṭi vīcip paṇpoṭum ulāvavum - av veṇpoṭiyaic citaṟi iṉitāy vīcavum,
`tāḻaiyiṉ iṉ cāriyai, kaṇ urupu tokkatu.
`kaiyarikkoḷḷal kainirampa arittukkoḷḷal; iṅku vāḷāvārik koḷḷal eṉṉum poruḷil vantatu; ivai oru coṉṉīrmaiya. vaṭamoḻi `upacarkkaṅ kaḷaippōlat tamiḻ moḻiyiluṅ `kai yeṉpatu, uriya peyar viṉaic coṟkaḷōṭu iyaintuvarum iṭaiccollāy niṟkum. kaḷittaṟiyē ṉeṉpatu kaiviṭuka veṉpatiṟpōla, (tirukkuṟaḷ 93, 8).
`pūttal polivupeṟṟuttōṉṟal: `mīṉpūttaṉṉa tōṉṟalar (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai, 169).
kaṭaṟkāṟṟu kuḷircciyu miṉimaiyu muṭaimaiyāl, avviṉimai yataṉ `paṇpoṭu meṉac cuṭṭappaṭṭatu.
`nimalaṉ malamillātavaṉ; avaṉ civaperumāṉ; iccolliṉ mutalil iṉmaipporuḷai yuṇarttum `nir eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyiṭaiccol (upacarkkam) īṟu keṭṭup puṇarntatu.
aṭikaḷ civapirāṉ māṭṭu iṭaiyaṟā aṉpuceluttuñ caivap periyārākaliṉ, tamatu aṉpiṟkuriya poruḷaik kaitaiyantātu paṭinta centāmarai malarukku uvamai kūṟiṉār; ītu aṉpumikkārkku iyalpēyām. toṇṭar cīr paravuvārāṉa āciriyar cēkkiḻār kaitaiyantātu paṭinta kamalavaṇṭiṟku nīṟupuṉainta toṇṭarai uvamai kāṭṭik kamala vaṇṭalar kaitait, tuṉṟu nīṟupuṉai mēṉiyavākit tūyanīṟupuṉai toṇṭarkaḷeṉṉac, ceṉṟu ceṉṟu muralkiṉṟaṉa veṉ ṟaruḷicceyyum aruṇmoḻiyilum ivviyalpiṉaik kāṇka.
(31-40) tēm moḻic cevvāyt tirumuka uḻattiyar - iṉiya moḻikaḷ nirampiya, civanta vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya mukam vāynta uḻavar makaḷir, kāmaru koḻum cuvai vāḻaiyiṉ paḻaṉum - viruppam mikuttaṟkuriya mikka cuvaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vāḻaiyiṉ paḻaṅkaḷum, puṉcey payanta pal niṟap payaṟum - kollaiyil viḷainta palaniṟaṅkaḷ vāynta palavakaip payaṟukaḷum naṉcey payanta poṉniṟa maṇiyum - vayalil viḷainta poṉṉiṟam vāynta neṉmaṇikaḷum, vaṭṭikaip peytu - kūṭaiyiṟ koṭṭi kaṭṭiya cumaṭṭiṉ eṭuttucceṉṟu - vaḷaittuk kaṭṭiya cummāṭṭiṉmēl eṭuttuk koṇṭupōy, aṅku aṭuttu uyar pākkattu - avviṭattaiyaṭuttu maṇaṉmēṭāy uyarntuḷḷa cempaṭavar cēriyil, kumiḻ iṭai ukaḷum amaiviḻip parattiyar - kumiḻam pūvai yotta mūkkiṉ aṭiyiṟ puraḷum amainta viḻikaḷaiyuṭaiya neytal makaḷir, pakuttu uṇarvu iṉṟi mikuttuk koṭutta - ivvaḷavukku ivvaḷavu eṉṟu pirittu uṇarum uṇarcciyillāmal mikutiyāyk koṭuttuviṭṭa, taraḷamum mīṉum niralpaṭa aḷantu koṇṭu - muttukkaḷaiyum mīṉkaḷaiyum tām viṟṟa paṇṭaṅ kaḷukku oḻuṅkāka aḷanteṭuttuk koṇṭu, uvappoṭu peyarntu tam irukkai cēravum - tām mikutiyāyppeṟṟa makiḻcciyoṭu tirumpit tam iruppiṭam aṭaiyavum,
moḻi cevvāy mukam eṉpavaṟṟiṟku ummai virittalu mām. `tēm moḻi eṉpataṟkut `tēṉaṉaiya moḻi yeṉṟum poruḷuraippar.
`kāmaru eṉpataik kāmam maru eṉap pirittu viruppam poruntiya allatu viruppam mikuttaṟkuriya eṉap poruḷuraikka; iṉik kāmaru kuvaḷai yeṉpataṟkuk `kāmam varumeṉpatu vikārattāṟ kāmaruveṉaniṉṟu viruppamvaru meṉpatāyiṟṟu eṉṟu āciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār uraittār (cilappatikāram, anti. 40).
koḻumai, cuvaimikutimēṟṟu. paḻaṉ, īṟṟup pōli,
cey, viḷainilam; itu potuppeyar; puṉmai ciṟumaiyātaliṉ, viḷaiccaliṟ ciṟumaiyuṭaiya pulam `puṉce yāyiṟṟu; viḷaiccaliṟ perumaiyuṭaiyatu `naṉcey, itaṉkaṇ naṉmaiperumaip poruḷatu. `pal leṉṉum aṭai            moḻiyaip payaṟṟukkuṅkūṭṭuka. paccaippayaṟu. karumaiyuḻuntu, paḻuppukkaṭalai, civappukkoḷḷu, veḷuppumoccai mutaliya palaniṟappayaṟukaḷ uḷavātal kāṇka.
`vaṭṭikai kūṭaikkup peyarātalai, vaṭṭikai kūṭai yeṉṉum piṅkalantaiyālaṟika.
vayalmēl viṉaiceyvār vaikkōṟ ṟāḷaic curuṭṭi vaḷaituppaḷainārāṟ kaṭṭic cummāṭeṭupparātaliṉ, kaṭṭiya cumaṭṭi ṉeṉṟār. itaṉkaṇ iṉ eṉṉum cāriyai niṟka ēḻaṉ urupu tokkatu.
pākkameṉpatu neytal nilattūr; pākkattup parattiyareṉka kumiḻ, uvamaiyākupeyar. iṭai, ēḻaṉurupu.
koṭutta paṇṭaṅkaṭku māṟṟāka mikuttuk koṭuttamaiyiṟ pakuttuṇarviṉṟi eṉṟār.
(41-44) marutamum neytalum mayaṅkiya marapiṉ - ivvāṟu marutanilap poruḷkaḷum neytaṉilapporuḷkaḷum oruṅku kalakkumiyalpiṉāl, ulakam ellām oruṅku vantu uṟiṉum - ulakattārellārum oṉṟāy vantu cērntālum, tolaiyā vaḷattoṭu - avarkaḷ nukarvaṉavellām vaḻaṅkiyum aḻiyāta vaḷattoṭu, nilavuṟum vaippiṉ oṟṟi mānakarkku - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa ūriṉaiyuṭaiya tiruvoṟṟiyū reṉṉum periya paṭṭiṉattiṟku,
marutamum neytalum ākupeyarāy avaṟṟiṉ karup poruḷkaḷai uṇartti niṉṟaṉa. karupporuḷ ivaiyeṉpatu,
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puḷ paṟai
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokaii
avvakai piṟavum karueṉa moḻipa (tol. akat. 18)
eṉṉum cūttirattāṟ ṟeḷiyappaṭum. iccūttirattāṉ nuvalap paṭāvāyiṉum, iṅku aṭika ḷeṭuttāṇṭa nīrvāḻvaṉavum vaḷi nilaiyum pūvum nilamakkaḷum, `avvakaipiṟavu meṉpataṉāl aṭakkik koḷḷappaṭum. eṉṉai? piṟavu meṉṟataṉāl nilamakkaḷum nīr nilaiyum vārkoṭiyum pūvuṅ koḷḷappaṭu meṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikārappatika uraiyiṟ kūṟiṉārātaliṉ.
`mayaṅkiya mara peṉṟatu, tiṇaimayakkam. ulaku, makkaḷulaku; aṉṟi ākupeyareṉṟalum poruntum.
`tolaital muṉpu uṇṭāyp piṉpu illai yātalām eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (pālaikkali, 1). vaippu - ūr. villuṭai vaippiṉ eṉṉum perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭaiyiṟ kāṇka (82).
iruppavum vaikavum cēravum niṟpavum maṟiyavum tuḷḷavum vīcavum ulāvavum cēravum mayaṅkiya marapiṉ nilavuṟu vaippiṉ nakareṉṟu muṭikka; iruppavum mutalāyiṉa ummaiyēṟṟa viṉaiyeccaṅkaḷ.
itukāṟum marutattukkum neytalukkun tiṇaimayakkam kūṟumukattāl, tiruvoṟṟiyūriṉ ciṟappuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.
eṉavē, tiruvoṟṟiyū reṉpatu vayal vaḷam vāyntu kaṭaṟkarai yōrattil amaintirukku muṇmai itaṉāṟ peṟappaṭum.
(44 - 46) uṟṟa kaṇ eṉavum - poruntiya kaṇṇeṉavum, akkaṇiṉ uḷ vaḷar aru maṇi eṉavum - akkaṇṇiṉ uḷḷē oḷivaḷarum ariya karumaṇiyeṉavum, ammaṇi uḷ uṟai pāvaiyai eṉavum - akkaruviḻiyiṉuḷḷē uṟaiyum pāvai yeṉavum,
pāvaiyai, ai cāriyai; iṟavaraiyumpar eṉṉun tiruk kōvaiyāriṟpōnta `kuṟavarai yārkkum eṉpataṟkup pērāciriyar kūṟiya vuraiyaikkāṇka.
iṟaivaṉai mutaṉmaiyum arumaiyum nōkki iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṇṇeṉavum maṇiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār. iṅṅaṉamē kaṇṇē kaṇṇiṟ karumaṇiyē maṇiyāṭu pāvāy (tiruvāvaṭutuṟai) eṉṟu appar perumāṉum aruḷicceytaṉar. `arumaṇi yeṉṟatu karumaṇiyēyā meṉpatum itaṉkaṭ peṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
oṟṟiyamānakarkkuk kaṇṇeṉavumeṉṟu toṭarka.
(47-50) pāvaiyiṉ akattup paṇpu palakāṭṭi mēvi - appāvaiyiluḷḷē iyalpukaḷ pala viḷaṅkak kāṭṭiyuṟaintu, āṅku atu puṭai peyarntu iyaṅka - ak karuviḻiyiṉuḷ atu pakkam peyarntu acaiya, takkavāṟu mikkatu puriyum - nērattiṟkut takuntapaṭi ciṟantatu ceyyum, ōr iyalpu illā ār uyir eṉavum - ōriyalpiṉṟip palvēṟu iyalpukaḷ uṭaiya ariya uyireṉavum, eṉṟuraittu mēl `viḷaṅkum eṉpataṉoṭu kūṭṭuka.
paṇpu, uyirppaṇpaik kāṭṭum nuṇṇiya ceyalkaḷ, mēvi. uyiriṉviṉai; atu, pāvai, `pāvaiyiṉakattu mēvi atu iyaṅka mikkatu puriyum āruyi reṉka.
`takkavāṟu mikkatupuriyum eṉṟatu nērattiṟku icainta vāṟu ceyyum uyiriṉtiṟattai; eṉavē `paṇpu uyiriṉ palvēṟiyaṟ  kaiyiṉai yuṇarttiṟṟu. `mikkatu aṟiviṉ ciṟappaik kāṭṭiṟṟu. `mikkatu oru tokutippaṭṭa ciṟanta ceyalkaḷai yuṇarttutaliṉ orumaip paṉmai mayakkam.
uyir, viḻaivu aṟivu ceyalkaḷ (iccā ñāṉakkiriyai) pala uṭaiyavātalpaṟṟi ōriyalpillā āruyir eṉṟār.
(51-53) iruvēṟu ulakiṉum - uyirpporuḷ ulakam uyiril poruḷ ulakam eṉṉum iraṇṭu vēṟupaṭṭa ulakaṅkaḷilum, iraṇṭu aṟa viravi - avai vēṟu tāṉ vēṟu eṉa iraṇṭāyp pirintu niṟṟal illaiyāmpaṭi avaṟṟōṭu oruṅku kalantu, aṟivum toḻilum neṟi uṟaviḷakkum mātu oru kūṟaṟku - uyirkaḷiṉ aṟivaiyum uyirillaṉavaṟṟiṉ ceyalaiyum muṟaiporunta viḷaṅka vaikkum umaiyammaiyai yorupākamā vuṭaiya civapirāṉukku, ōtiya mutalvaṉ - ōmeṉum orumoḻi maṟaipporuḷai ōtiyaruḷiya mutaṟkaṭavuḷ,
iruvēṟulaku; aṟivuḷḷa uyirttokaiyum aḵtillā maṇ nīr tī mutalāṉa uyiril poruṭṭokaiyum,
`iraṇṭaṟaviravi yeṉṟār, kalapput taṉmaiyāl oṉṟāyum poruṭṭaṉmaiyāl iraṇṭāyum viravi oṉṟeṉṟum iraṇṭeṉṟuṅ kūṟa muṭiyāmaik kalantu niṟṟaliṉ; ivviravutalaiyē `iraṇṭaṉmai (attuvitam) eṉṟu caivacittānta nūl kūṟum.
iṟaivaṉ ammaiyiṉ vāyilākap pirippiṉṟi niṉṟu ivaṟṟai viḷakkutaliṉ, avaṉai `mātoru kūṟaṉeṉṟu vitantār. iṅku ammaiyoṉṟatu aruḷ.
viḷakkuṅ kūṟa ṉeṉṟu koḷka.
ivvaṭikaḷ,
  avaiyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉaiyīṟ
  pōkku varavu puriya āṇaiyiṉ
  nīkkam iṉṟi niṟku maṉṟē
eṉṉuñ civañāṉapōta nūṟpāvaip peritottut teḷinta tamiḻil viḷaṅkiniṟṟal peritum viyantu makiḻaṟpālatu.
`pāvaiyiṉakattu... āruyireṉavum eṉṟu mēlvanta āruyircceyalai iraṭṭuṟamoḻintu `iruvēṟulakiṉum... mātoru kūṟa ṉeṉṉum iv iṟaivaṉ ceyalukku uvamit taluñ ceyaṟpālatu. uṭampaiyiyakkum uyirai ulakuyirkaḷai yiyakkum iṟaivaṉukku uvamaikāṭṭum muṟai.
kaṭṭu muṟuppuṅ karaṇamuṅ koṇṭuḷḷa
  miṭṭatoru pēraḻaikka veṉṉeṉṟāṅ koṭṭi
  yavaṉuḷamā killā ṉuḷamavaṉā māṭṭā
  tavaṉuḷamā yallaṉumā maṅku
eṉṟu āciriyar meykaṇṭa tēva nāyaṉāraruḷicceyta civañāṉapōta veṇpāviṟ kāṇappaṭum.
iṉik `kaṇṇeṉavum `maṇiyeṉavum `pāvaiyai yeṉavum eṉpavaṟṟaiyum iṅṅaṉamē uvamitturaikkalāmō veṉiṉ, tiruvoṟṟimānakar mātoru kūṟaṉait toṭarntu, `mutalvaṉ eṉṟa murukavēḷait toṭarātoḻiyumākaliṉ, avvāṟuraittalā kātu.
iṉi `viḷakku meṉpatai mutalvaṉukkuk kūṭṭi muṭittu mālōveṉiṉ, ulakuyirkaḷukkellām aṟivun toḻilum viḷakkum mātoru kūṟaṉukkē ōmeṉu morumoḻi ōtiṉāṉ mutalvaṉeṉṉum peruñciṟappu āṇṭup peṟappaṭāmaiyiṉ, atuvum uraiyaṉṟeṉka. mēlum, `mutalvaṉ eṉpataṉ coṟporuḷāṟṟal ataṉkaṭ ciṟavāmaiyum, ataṉāl ataṉaip peytu niṟuttiya aṭikaḷiṉ karuttu āṇṭu nirampāmaiyuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.
(54-58) cellal uṟṟu aḻuṅkiya mallal tēvar kuḻām peritu uyya - tuṉpamaṭaintu iraṅki muṟaiyiṭṭa viṇvaḷam poruntiya tēvar kūṭṭam nīṇṭu uyirvāḻa, vaḻā vēl ōccippāṭu il cūrukku vīṭu nalki - kuṟi tavaṟāta vēṟpaṭaiyai yeṟintu aḻitalillāta cūrapatumaṉukku vīṭupēṟaḷittu, vēntaṉ payanta kūntaṟkumariyai maṇantu ulaku aḷitta - tēvarkku araiyaṉ āṉa intiraṉ peṟṟeṭutta teyva yāṉaiyait tirumaṇam porunti ulakavarkku aruḷ purinta, niṇam tikaḻ vēlaṉ-  koḻuppuk kaṟai viḷaṅkum vēlai yuṭaiyavaṉ,.
cellal, tuṉpam; aḻuṅkal, iraṅkal; ivaṟṟaic, `cellaluṟ ṟiraṅki yeṉṉum maṇimēkalaiyiṟ kāṇka (2, 9).
`mallaṟ ṟēva reṉṟatu, `celvappēṟṟil vaikuntēva reṉṉum poruṭṭu; tām tēṭāmalē ellā nukarporuḷum peṟṟa tēvareṉka; mallal - vaḷam, celvum; mallalvaḷaṉē eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār (uri - 7).
`peritu kālap perumai yuṇarttiṟṟu; `peritu vaikavum eṉpatiṟpōla (ivvakaliṉ 8ām aṭi)
`pāṭil cūr eṉṟār, cūraṉ taṉatu tava valimaiyāl aḻiyāta nilaiyaip peṟṟiruntāṉātaliṉ; ivaṉṟaṉ ṟavavalimaiyai aṭikaḷ mēṟ pattoṉpatām pāṭṭiṟ patiṉeṭṭām aṭimutal aimpattōrām aṭivaraiyil mikavum viritturaippar; āṅku avaṉ tavac ciṟappiṉ vakaimaikaḷai viḷaṅka uraippām. pāṭu, mutal nīṇṭa mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar. cūr, īṟukeṭṭatu.
`vīṭunalki yeṉṟamaiyiṉ, cūraṉatu ūṉuṭampai vēlōccik koṉṟaṉa reṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. `ōcci yeṉṉum viṉaiyeccattukkuk `koṉ ṟeṉpatu avāy nilai.
cūraṉ pakaivaṉāy etirttup porutāṉāyiṉum, avaṉuyir viṉaivi ṉīṅki iṟaivaṉ ṟiruvaṭinilaiyai yuṟutaṟkēṟṟa muṟṟiya tavappēṟum nalviṉaiyum uṭaimaiyiṉ, iṟaivaṉ avaṉait taṉṟiruvaṭikkaṭ cērttamai kuṟittu `vīṭunalki yeṉṟār.
`vēntaṉ maruta nilak kiḻavaṉ, aḵtu īṇṭu vāṉōr talaivaṉai yuṇarttiṟṟu. payattal īṉṟeṭuttal.
`kūntaṟ kumari kūntalaiyuṭaiya kumari yākiya teyvayāṉai.
`ulakaḷitta veṉpataic cūraṉaik koṉṟamaiyāl tēvaraiyum, ammuṟaiyē makkaḷaiyum pātukātta veṉak koḷka.
iṟaivaṉ aruḷoṭu puṇarntu ulakattai yaḷikkum uṇmai paṟṟik, `kūntaṟ kumariyai maṇantu ulakaḷitta vēla ṉeṉṟu ōtiṉār; iṉi atu varalāṟṟuṇmaiyumām.
(59-65) aiyaṉ - evvuyirkkun tantai, ceyyaṉ - cenniṟa muṭaiyavaṉ, paṉṉiru kaiyaṉ - paṉṉiraṇṭu kaikaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉ, vaṟiyēm iṭumpai  poṟipaṭuttu etirum periyōṉ - ēḻaiyēmākiya em tuṉpattait tukaḷākki etirtōṉṟum pērāṟṟaluṭaiyōṉ, periyar aṟiviṉukku ariyaṉ - tavattāṟ periyarāyiṉā raṟivukkum eṭṭātavaṉ (ākiya murukapperumāṉatu), tiru uru orumaiyiṉ eḻuvittu - tiruvuruvattai neñcorumaiyuṭaṉ eḻuntaruḷacceytu, arumaiyoṭu vaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟi - ciṟappuṭaṉ vaṇakkavaḻipāṭu ceytu, viḻi nīr uṟaippa aṉpu uruvāki iṉpu uṟal aṟiyātu -kaṇṇīrtuḷippa aṉpu vaṭivāki iṉpam aṭaital  aṟiyāmal, puṉporuḷa kavarum eṉ miṉpurai maṉaṉē - ciṟumaiyuṭaiya poruḷkaḷaip piṟaraṟiyāmaṟ koḷḷum vēṭkai vāynta eṉ miṉṉalai yokka neñcē.
civapirāṉ piḷḷaiyār murukappirāṉ eṉpārellām uṇmaiyāṉ nōkkuṅkāl orē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāy muṭitaliṟ civapirāṉaic cemmēṉi yemmāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla murukakkaṭavuḷaiyum īṇṭuc `ceyyaṉ eṉṟār.
`paṉṉirukaiyaṉ eṉṟatu, avaṉatu āṟṟaluṇarttiyapaṭi yeṉka.
`vaṟiyēmeṉpatu iṅku iṉpattiṉ ēḻaimaiyaik kuṟittatu; tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ mutaliyōr aṭiyār perumakkaḷai ēḻaiyaṭiyārkaḷ (tiruvaikāvūrt tēvāram, 1) eṉṟu aruḷicceytatum atu. vaṟiyēmeṉṉun taṉmaip       paṉmai, taṉṉiṉamāṉa ēṉaiyōraiyun taḻuvi niṉṟatu. itu pōlvaṉavaṟṟai uḷappāṭṭuttaṉmai (tolkāppiyam, viṉaiyiyal 5) yeṉpar cēṉāvaraiyar; taṉakku orumaiyalla tiṉmaiyiṟṟaṉmaip paṉmaiyāvatu taṉṉoṭu piṟarai uḷappaṭuttatēyām eṉṟu avarataṟku ētu uraittalum āṇṭukkāṇka.
etirtal, etirvaral; āvatu etirtōṉṟal.
`tavattāṉ muṟṟiya periyarkkum ariyaṉāya murukaṉ vaṟiyē miṭumpaiyaip poṟipaṭuttetirum periyaṉāyiruttaliṉ, avaṉai vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟi iṉpuṟalaṟiyātu puṉporuḷ kavarkiṉṟaṉaiyē neñcē yeṉṟu iraṅkik kūṟiṉāreṉṟu koḷka.
`uṟaippa tuḷippa; `uṟaippaveṉṟataṉait tuḷippa veṉṟu āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭai (379) yuraiyil uraippar.
`puṉporuḷ ciṟumaiyaiyuṭaiya ciṟṟiṉpap poruḷkaḷ. avai `maṇṭirivākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavu meṉpatu mutalāka mēlē kūṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷeṉka.
`kavaru meṉṟār, piṟaraṟiyāmaṟ koḷḷutaliṉ; iṉi `virumpu meṉṟuraittalumām.
ennēramum etaṉaiyēṉum neñcam caṭutiyiṟ caṭutiyil māṟi māṟi niṉaittukkoṇṭiruttaliṉ, maṉaṉai `miṉpuraimaṉaṉ eṉṟār. ītu toḻiluvamam. maṉaṉ, īṟṟuppōli; ē, viḷiyurupu.
`oṟṟimānakar mutalvaṉ tiruvuru eḻuvittu vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟi yiṉpuṟa laṟiyātu puṉporuḷ kavaru maṉaṉē yeṉka.
(66-76) varampu aṟum āṟṟal nirampiya murukaṉ - aḷavu illāta aṟivāṟṟal nirampap poruntiya murukap perumāṉ, tāṉ eḻuntaruḷum vāṉkuṭil āka niṉṉait tantaṉaṉ āka - tāṉ vīṟṟiruntaruḷun tūya ciṟu vīṭāka niṉṉai eṉakkuk koṭuttāṉāka, piṉṉai - ataṉpiṉpu, maṇtirivu ākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavum - maṇṇiṉ tiripukaḷākak kāṇappaṭum uṭampu mutalākiya cila poruḷkaḷum, puṉaltirivākap pukuvaṉa cilavum - nīriṉ tiripukaḷāka māṟit tōṉṟum pāl mutalākiya cila uṇavukaḷum, aṉaltirivāka aṭaivaṉa cilavum - tīyiṉ tiripukaḷākap poruntum maṇiyiṉ oḷi mutalākiya cilavum, vaḷi iṭaṉākat teḷivaṉa cilavum - kāṟṟē tōṟṟuvāyākat teḷiyappaṭum maṇam mutalākiya cilavum, vicumpu iṭaṉāka ucumpuṉa cilavum - vāṉam piṟappiṭamāka iyaṅkum icai mutalākiya cilavum, ōvātu iruppa mēvunai - oḻivillāmal vantirukka virumpukiṉṟaṉai, ataṉāl tuṉpamum kavalaiyum niṉpuṟaṉ āka- akkāraṇattiṉālē tuṉpamuṅ kavalaiyum niṉpakkattaṉavāka, iṉpu uṟal aṟiyātu iṭarppaṭukunaiyē - iṉpam nukartalaṟiyāmal nī iṭarppaṭukiṉṟaṉaiyē, atu aṉṟu - atuvallāmal,
`kuṭil ciṟuvīṭu; atāvatu kuṭicai; iṟaivaṉatu ellaiyaṟṟa perumaikku uḷḷam oru kuṭilāvatēyāyiṉum avaṉ eḻuntaruḷutaṟkēṟṟa aṉpurimai ataṟkuṇmaiyiṉ, atu `vāṉkuṭilāyiṟṟu.
neñcam mey mutalāṉa aimpoṟikaḷiṉ vāyilāy maṇ mutalāṉa aimporuḷkaḷilum porunti ūṟu mutalāṉa aimpulaṉkaḷaiyum nukarumeṉṟu iṅkuk kūṟiyavāṟām; ivvuṇmaiyai,
  ōcainaṟ parica rūpa viratakan taṅka ḷeṉṟu
  pēcumāt tiraika ḷaintum piṟakkumpū tātikatti
  ṉēcavin tiriyaṅ kaṭku nikaḻaṟi vitaṉāṟ kāṇṭu
  mācaicēr maṉāti taṉmāt tiraipuri yaṭṭakantāṉ
(civañāṉacitti, 2-āñ cūttiram, 64) eṉṉum aruṇanti civaṉār tirumoḻiyiluṅ kāṇka.
`ucumputal iyaṅkutal; ōcaiyaṇukkaḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu unti iyaṅkic cevippulaṉāmeṉṟu iyaṟkaipporuṇūlār kūṟuvar.
`kaṇṭaṉa mutaliya aintum viṉaippeyarkaḷāyc `cilavum eṉpataṉoṭu paṇput tokaiyāy niṉṟaṉa.
`mēvunai yeṉpatil aikāram muṉṉilai viṉaimuṟṟu vikutiyeṉavum, nakaramey aḵtūrntu varutaṟkēṟṟa eḻuttup pēṟēṉavuṅ koḷka. `iṭarppaṭukunai yeṉpatil ivaṟṟoṭu `ku cāriyaiyeṉṟaṟika. ēkāram, irakkapporuḷatu.
`nī yeṉṉuñ col mēl `iṉi nī yiṅṅaṉa moḻiyātu eṉṟa viṭattuḷḷatu. puṟam, pakkam.
`atāaṉṟu, atu aṉṟu; varumoḻi mutal nīṇṭu aḷapeṭuttup puṇarntatu;
(77-81) uṟutuṇai ākac ceṟiyum eṉṉaiyum - uṉakku uṟṟa tuṇaiyākap poruntum eṉṉaiyum, pul cāyttu ōṭum puṉalpōla - tāṉ tuṇaiyātaṟkuriya pullait taṉvaḻic cāyt tōṭum veḷḷam pōla,niṉ vaḻippaṭīi  - niṉ vaḻippaṭutti, eṉ uyirkku uyirām aṟumukattu aiyaṉai maruvaloṭṭātu - eṉ uyirkku uyirākum āṟu tirumukaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉai aṇaiyavoṭṭāmal, vaṉmai ceyta niṉ puṉmaiyō peritu - koṭumai ceyta niṉ ciṟumaiyō mikapperitu,
puṉmai, iṅku vañcittoḻukuñ ciṟumai yuṇarttiṟṟu. ēkāramiraṇṭum acai.
`maṉaṉē! murukaṉ niṉṉaip paṭaitta aruṭkuṟippiṉai yaṟiyāmal iṭarppaṭukunaiyē; atāaṉṟu, eṉṉaiyum niṉvaḻip paṭīi aiyaṉai maruvaloṭṭātu vaṉmai ceyta niṉ puṉmaiyō peritu eṉṟu iṇaittukkoḷka.
(82-87) iṉi nī iṅṅaṉam oḻiyātu - iṉi nī ivvāṟu aruṭkuṟippiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāmal, paṉimalarkkaṭampu cūṭiya taṭam tōḷ murukaṉ - kuḷirnta malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṭampamālaiyaic cūṭiya periya tōḷkaḷaiyuṭaiya murukapperumāṉatu, tiru urup potiyum oru kalaṉāka viḻumitiṉ nīṭu vāḻmati - tiruvuruviṉaip pātukāttu vaikkum iṇaiyaṟṟa kalaṉākac cīruṭaṉ nīṭu vāḻvāyāka; koḻuviya nalam keḻu naṟum pāl peyta polam kalam poliyum peṟṟi eṉa - iṉiya cuvaiporuntiya nallamaṇamuṭaiya pālai iṭṭu nirappiya poṉṉālāṉa kalamviḷaṅkun taṉmaiyai yoppaveṉka.
`kaṭampu mālaiyai yuṇarttutaliṉ ākupeyar.
`taṭam perumaip poruṭṭātalait taṭavuṅkayavum naḷiyum perumai eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāl (uri 22) aṟika.
viḻumañ cīrmaiyuñ ciṟappu meṉṟu tolkāppiyaṉār (uri. 55) kūṟutaliṉ, `viḻumiti ṉeṉpataṟkuc cīruṭaṉeṉṟu uraiyuraikkappaṭṭatu.
`vāḻmati, mati, muṉṉilaiyacai.
polam, poṉ; `poṉ ṉeṉṉuñcol īṟukeṭṭu lakaramum makaramum muṟaiyē poruntip `polam eṉattirintu muṭintateṉṟu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, 61) yeṉṉum nūṟpāviṟ kaṭṭaḷai yiṭuvar.
`eṉa uvamaccol; itaṉaip piṉṉuḷḷōr uvamavurupeṉṟē koṇṭaṉar. eṉṉa vikala viḻaiya vetira veṉpatu taṇṭiyalaṅkāram (poruḷaṇi 33.)
`peṟṟiyeṉa orukalaṉāka vāḻmati yeṉṟu muṭikka.
eṉavē `oṟṟimānakar mutalvaṉ tiruvuru eḻuvittu vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟi yiṉpuṟa laṟiyātu puṉporuḷ kavaru maṉaṉē yeṉṟu neñcai viḷittu, `murukaṉ tāṉeḻuntaruḷum vāṉkuṭilāka niṉṉait tantaṉaṉākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavum pukuvaṉa cilavum aṭaivaṉa cilavum teḷivaṉa cilavum ucumpuṉa cilavum mēvunaiyataṉāl tuṉpamuṅ kavalaiyum niṉpuṟaṉāka iṉpuṟal aṟiyātu irppaṭukunaiyē, atāaṉṟu eṉṉaiyum niṉvaḻippaṭīi aiyaṉai maruvaloṭṭātu vaṉmaiceyta niṉ puṉmaiyō peritu eṉṟu ataṉ piḻaipāṭṭucceyalai yeṭuttukkūṟi, `iṉi nī iṅṅaṉam oḻiyātu naṟumpāl peyta polaṅkalam eṉa murukaṉ tiruvuruppotiyum oru kalaṉāka nīṭuvāḻmati yeṉṟu aṟivurai kiḷantārātaliṉ, itu neñcoṭu kiḷatta lāṉamai kāṇka.
2. aruḷiyaluraittal
`peṟṟi ariya perumāṉ eṉpatai ariyapeṟṟip perumāṉ eṉa māṟi, uyarnta iyalpukaḷaiyuṭaiya eṉ talaivaṉ eṉṟuraikūṟuka; periya tiruvoṟṟi nakar vanta oḷvēlāṉ - periya tiruvoṟṟi nakarattil eḻuntaruḷiya oḷimikka vēṟpaṭaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉ; nal tavarum kāṇā ariya kaḻalāṉ - mikka tavamuṭaiyāruṅ kāṇamāṭṭāta ariya tiruvaṭiyai yuṭaiyavaṉ ākiya murukaṉ, eṉatu uḷattu nāṇātu amarntavā naṉṟu - ēḻaiyēṉuḷḷattiṟ kūcātu eḻuntaruḷiya vāṟu mika naṉṟu! `ariya peṟṟipperumā ṉeṉka. ariyapeṟṟi yāvaṉa, `taṉṉurimaiyaṉātal mutaliya eṭṭiyalpukaḷumām. taṉṉurimai yaṉātal, tūya vuṭampiṉaṉātal, iyaṟkai yuṇarviṉaṉātal, muṟṟu muṇartal, iyalpākavē viṉaikaḷi ṉīṅkutal, pēraru ḷuṭaimai, muṭivilāṟṟaluṭaimai, varampiliṉpa muṭaimai eṉavivai.
`ariya iyalpukaḷ, vāynta tiruvoṟṟiyaṅkaṭavuḷ tavamuṭaiyārāṉuṅ kāṇṭaṟkariya kaḻaluṭaiyaṉāyiruntum, ēḻaiyēṉuḷḷattum eḻuntaruḷiyavāṟu ā, mikanaṉṟu eṉṟu viyanturaittapaṭiyām; eṉavē avaṉ aruḷiyalpuraittā rāyiṟṟu.
`kāṇā, `amarntavā, eṉpavaṟṟil īṟu kuṟaintaṉa.
`eṉatuḷattu meṉṟu iḻivuciṟappummai viritturaittalu moṉṟu;  `naṟṟavarum eṉpataṉ ummai uyarvu ciṟappu.
`nāṇātu, `naṉṟu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ uyiriṉ tāḻvaiyum iṟaivaṉ uyarvaiyum viḷakkāniṉṟaṉa.
`perumāṉ vēlāṉ kaḻalāṉ eṉṟavum, `mutalvaṉ vēlaṉ ceyyaṉ kaiyaṉ periyōṉ ariyaṉ eṉṟu muṉ vantaṉavum oru poruḷ kuṟitta vēṟupeyark kiḷavi (tolkāppiyam kiḷaviyākkam, 42)
3. kāmamikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷavi
akal īrm kaḻikāḷ - akaṉṟa kuḷirnta kaḻikaḷē, miṉ taṅku noy ciṟaivaṇṭu iṉaṅkāḷ - miṉoḷi poruntiya melliya ciṟakukaḷaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷē, naṉṟu aṉṟu uṉakkut tiruvoṟṟi vēlarai nāṭi iṭar iṉṟu iṅku nī koḷḷal eṉṟu uraiyīr  -  `tiruvoṟṟi nakariluḷḷa vēluṭai murukarai virumpi iṅkippoḻutu nī tuṉpaṅkoḷḷutal uṉakku nallataṉṟu’ eṉṟu colkiṉṟilīr!, iṉimēl emakku niṉṟu iṅku nīr ceyyum naṉmai eṉṉō - iṉimēl eṉakku nīṅkaḷ arukiruntu ceyyum naṉmai eṉṉavō, oṉṟum nērvatu iṉṟu - ētum uṅkaḷāl āvatu illai.`naṉṟu, peyar. `nī emakku, orumaippaṉmai mayakkam. noymai, meṉmai.
ō oḻiyacai; ē īṟṟacai.
`kaḻikāḷ vaṇṭiṉaṅkāḷ uraiyīreṉṟatu ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ aṟivē nāṇē meṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum (tolkāppiyam, ceyyuḷ 202). kāmam mikka uṟutuyarc collātaliṟ, kaḻikaḷaiyum vaṇṭukaḷaiyum viḷittu ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār.
4. tōḻi viravik kūṟal
`tōḻi viravikkūṟal eṉṟatu, talaivaṉatu kuṟaiyirappukku matiyuṭampaṭṭa tōḻi, taṉ talaiviyaiyum ataṟku icaivittal karuti, meṉmaiyum vaṉmaiyum viravikkūṟa leṉpatu, mutalil meṉmaiyāṟ kūṟiyatōḻi, talaimakaḷ taṉatu nāṇattāṟ kuṟai nērāmai kaṇṭu, iṅṅaṉam viravik kūṟukiṉṟāḷeṉka. tirucciṟṟampalakkōvaiyāruḷ (84) `viravikkūṟa leṉṟu varuntuṟaikku āciriyar pērāciriyar meṉmoḻiyoṭu ciṟitu vaṉmoḻipaṭak kūṟāniṟṟal eṉṟu iṅṅaṉamē viḷakkamuraittār.
(1 mutal 5 aṭi) nēr iṟai muṉkai - otta varikaḷaiyuṭaiya muṉkaikaḷaiyum, vār olik kūntal - nīṇṭa taḻaitta kūntalaiyum, arikaṭai oḻukiya perumatar maḻaikkaṇ - cevvarikaḷ muṉaivaraiyil ōṭiya periya kaḷippupporuntiya kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷaiyum, ilavu uṟaḻceyvāy - ilaviṉ itaḻaiyotta civanta vāyitaḻkaḷaiyum, nilavu uṟaḻ tirunutal - eṭṭāmpiṟait tiṅkaḷaippōṉṟa aḻakiya neṟṟiyaiyum, koṭipuraimaruṅkul - vañcikkoṭiyai nikarkkum iṭaiyiṉai yum, kaṭitikaḻmēṉi - oḷiviḷaṅkum niṟamamainta uṭampiṉaiyum uṭaiya, karumpiṉum iṉiya - karuppañcāṟṟiṉum iṉiya iyalpiṉaiyāṉa, eṉ arumpeṟal pāvāy - eṉ peṟalariya pāvaiyaiyaṉaiyāy! eṉṟatu, tōḻi talaiviyaiyeṉka.
`nēriṟai muṉkai eṉṟatu ottavarikaḷai (irēkaikaḷai) yuṭaiya muṉkaiyai; iṟai eṉpatu variyeṉap poruṭarutal piṟaṅkuvari poṟivarai iṟaiyākum eṉṉum piṅkalantaiyiṟ kāṇka (āṭavarvakai). `olikūntaleṉṟatu, kūntaliṉ aṭartti kūṟiyavāṟu. olimeṉ kūntal eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcippāṭṭut (2) toṭarkku āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruraikkum uraiyil ivvuṇmai kāṇka. olittal, taḻaittaleṉṉum poruṭṭu. viḷaṅkumnutal ōr araivaṭṭampōṟ ṟōṉṟalāl, `nilaveṉṟaṟku īṇṭu eṭṭāmpiṟait tiṅkaḷeṉṟu uraikkappaṭṭatu.
mākkaṭal naṭuvaṇ eṇṇāṭ pakkattup
  pacuveṇ ṭiṅka ṭōṉṟi yāṅkuk
  katuppayal viḷaṅkuñ ciṟunutal.
eṉṟu kuṟuntokaip pāṭṭilum (129) ivviyalpu āciriyar kōpperuñcōḻarāl viḷakkamāy eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭṭiruttal aṟiyaṟpālatu.
mātariṭaikku vañcikkoṭiyuvamaiyātal, vañcim    maruṅkul, vañciyañcum iṭai eṉṉun tirukkōvaiyāriṟ (22, 32) kāṇka.
mēṉiyai niṟameṉavum uṭampeṉavum iraṭṭuṟamoḻintu koḷka. eṉṉai? niṟamamaiyātu uṭampiṉkaṇ viḷakkan tikaḻātākaliṉeṉpatu.
`kaiyuṅ kūntaluṅ kaṇṇum vāyum nutalum maruṅkulum mēṉiyum uṭaiya pāvāyeṉa eṇṇummai virittut toṭariyaittuk koḷka.
pāvāy, uruvakam; avaṟṟuḷ i īyākum aiāy ākum (tolkāppiyam, col, 4) eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāṉ itaṉkaṇ aiyīṟu āyeṉattirintu viḷiyēṟṟatu.
(6-7) vaḻivaḻic ciṟakkum em kaḻiperum kulattiṉ - talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyākap perumaiyiṟ ciṟantuvarum ematu mikauyarnta kulattiṟpiṟanta, pālkaṭal tōṉṟiya cīrttiru aṉaiyāy - tiruppāṟkaṭaliṉiṉṟum eḻunta aḻakiya tirumakaḷaiyottavaḷē;
vaḻi, kālvaḻi; aṭukkup paṉmaipporuḷatu.
em, taṉmaippaṉmai; uḷappāṭṭut taṉmaip paṉmaiyaṉṟu.
`aṉaiyai yeṉṉum muṉṉilaimuṟṟuccol īṟutirintu viḷiyēṟṟatu.
(8-9) valampuri aṉṉa - valampuric caṅkiṉaiyotta, emkulampuri kōmakaḷ - eṅkaḷ kulattōr virumpukiṉṟa kōpperuntāy, aritiṉ īṉṟa perumuttu aṉaiyāy - arumaiyāyp peṟṟeṭutta muttaiyoppavaḷē;
valampuri, valamākac cuḻintamainta caṅku; itaṉaiyē caṅkiṉaṅkaḷil mikavum uyarntateṉpa.
ilaṅkunīrp parappiṉ vaḷaimīkkūṟum
  valampuri yaṉṉa vacainīṅku ciṟappiṉ.
eṉṉum perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai yaṭikaḷilum, (34-35) viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nīraiyuṭaiya kaṭaliṭattup piṟanta caṅkil, mēlāka ulakaṅkūṟum valampuriccaṅkaiyotta kuṟṟanīṅkun talaimaiyiṉaiyum eṉṟu avvaṭikaṭku āciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyaruraikkum uraiyilum iḵtu aṟiyaṟpālatu, iṅkum `valampuriyaṉṉa kōmaka ḷeṉpataṟku ittalaimaipporuḷē poruḷām. eṉavē itu paṇpuvamaiyeṉka.
kulattukkēṟṟa oḻukkamuṭaimaiyiṟ,kōmakaḷaik `kulampuri kōmaka ḷeṉṟār. kulam, ākupeyar.
kōmakaḷeṉṟatu naṟṟāyai. naṟṟāyai valampuri yeṉṟamaiyiṉ, avaḷ aritiṉ īṉṟamakaḷ perumut teṉappaṭṭāḷ. ivvāṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟutal ilakkiyamarapu; itu,
tīmpāl cumantu mulaivīṅkit tirumuttīṉṟa valampuripōṟ
  kāmpēr tōḷār kaḷiṟīṉṟār
eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷāṉum (muttiyilampakam, 104) aṟiyappaṭum.
muttukkaḷuḷḷum parumuttaṅkaḷē ciṟantaṉavātal parūukkāḻ āram (cilappatikāram, 4, 41) eṉṉum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tirumoḻiyilum, kimpurippakuvāyk kiḷarmuttu (cilap. 5, 150) eṉṉumiṭattuk `kiḷarmut teṉpataṟkup `parumuttu eṉa uraikkum aṭiyārkku nallār uraiyilum aṟiyappaṭum.
(10 - 13) miṭi aṟiyā em kuṭi eṉum muṟṟattu - vaṟumaiyeṉpataṟiyāta ematu kuṭumpameṉṉum muṉṟilil, aṉpunīr pāycci - aṉpu eṉṉum nīrai nāṭōṟum pāycci, iṉpuuṟakkaiceytu - nīyum niṉṉaik kaṇṭārum makiḻcci poruntumpaṭi oppaṉaiceytu, āyamum yāṉum cīritiṉ vaḷarkkum - tōḻiyar kūṭṭamum yāṉuñ celvamāy vaḷarttuvarum, nalpayaṉkaṉinta poṉ cuṭarkkoṭiyē - nallavākiya payaṉkaḷ mutirnta poṉṉiṟamāṉa oḷimikka pūṅkoṭiyaṉaiyāy;
muṟṟam, vīṭṭukku muṉṉiṭam; itaṉai `muṉṟileṉavum uraippa.
nekiḻcci, iṭaṅkaṇṭavaḻi viraintōṭal, aṭaittum aṭaipaṭāmai, uyirkaḷai vaḷarttal, īramuṭaimai mutaliya otta viyalpukaḷāṉ, aṉpai nīreṉavē ellāt tamiḻilakkiyaṅ kaḷum ottu uruvakappaṭutturaippavāyiṉa.  uraiyāciriyar parimēlaḻakar mūṉṟām paripāṭaluraiyil (65) aṉpiṉai meṉmaiyeṉṟuraittamaiyum iṅku niṉaivukūraṟpāṟṟu.
koṭikaḷukkuk kaiceytalāvaṉa: koḻukoḻumpukaḷai arukaṇaittu avaṟṟiṉ mēl avaikaḷai eṭuttuviṭal, muṭkaḷāl nerukkuṇṭu maṭintukiṭakkum ilaikaḷaiyum malarkaḷaiyum koḻuntu varuṅ koṭikaḷaiyum nimirttiviṭal mutalāyiṉaveṉka.
`āyamum yāṉum eṉṟu taṉṉai āyattiṉiṉṟum pirittuk kūṟiṉāḷ, mutaṉmai karuti.
maṅkaipparuvattiṟ peṇṭirkku nāṇam maṭam accam payirppu mutalāṉa iṉiya peṇmaiyiyalpukaḷ viḷaitaliṉ, avaitāmē īṇṭut talaivikku `naṟpayaṉ eṉappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu.
muṟṟam, nīr, payaṉ, koṭimutalāṉavellām uruvakaccoṟkaḷ.
(14-15) nakkum - cirittum, pukkum - em āyattiṭaip pukuntum, mikka makiḻceyyum - emakku mikka makiḻcciyai viḷaikkiṉṟa, ceyir aṟa eḻutiya uyir ōviyamē - kuṟṟam illaiyāka eḻutiya uyiruṭaiya ōviyamaṉaiyāy.
kāṭcikku iṉitākiya ōr ōviyam nakutalum acaitalu māṉa uyircceyalum uṭaiyatāyiṉ, atu kaṇṭārkku mikka makiḻcciyai viḷaittiṭumākaliṉ, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu. makiḻ, mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar.
(16-18) arumaiyiṉ mikunta emperumutal tantai - arumaiyiṉāṟciṟanta eṅkaḷ perumaimikka mutalvaṉāṉa kōmakaṉ, āṟṟā viruppoṭum ēṟṟippārkkum - tāḻāta viruppattōṭum ēṟṟip pārkkiṉṟa, viḷivuaṟa viḷaṅkiya oḷimaṇiviḷakkē - avitaliṉṟi eṉṟum oru peṟṟittāy viḷaṅkuñ cemmaṇiyiṉ oḷivāynta viḷakkaṉaiyāḷē;
kalvi, kēḷvi, celvam, celvākku, nalloḻukkam, īkai, talaimai, mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ piṟarellāriṉum ariyaṉāyc ciṟantavaṉeṉṟaṟku `arumaiyiṉ mikunta veṉappaṭṭatu.
viḷakkileriyun tīppiḻampu cemmaṇiyiṉ vaṭivattai ottiruttaliṉ, maṇiviḷak keṉṟu uvamikkappaṭṭatu. aṉṟi aḻakiya viḷakkeṉalum ām.
viḷakkēṟṟip pārttalait talaivikku uraikkumpōtu, `tantai avaḷai īṉṟeṭuttukkāṇṭaleṉṟu karuttuk koḷka. ovvoru kuṭiyilum naṉmakkaḷaip peṟṟeṭutta leṉpatu akkuṭikku viḷakkēṟṟi vaittal pōlām eṉak koḷvatu tamiḻnāṭṭār vaḻakku. ivvuṇmai,
 varunāḷeṉ ṟumpiḻaiyāt teyvap poṉṉi
	vaḷamperukka vaḷavarkulam perukkun taṅka
  ṭirunāṭu pōṟceḻiyar teṉṉāṭu viḷakkuñ
	cīrviḷakkiṉ ceyyacī ṟaṭikaḷ pōṟṟi
eṉṟu cēkkiḻār perumāṉ maṅkaiyarkkaraciyāraic `cīrviḷakku eṉakkūṟi makiḻum aruḷuraiyiṉum naṉku peṟappaṭum.
(19-21) niṉ aruḷ nāṭi - niṉatu aruḷicceykaiyai etirvirumpi, iṅkut tuṉṉi niṉṟu -  niṉṉai aṇuki niṉṟu, aṭiyēṉ kūṟum oṭiyāyc ciṟumoḻi - aṭiyēṉ colkiṉṟa kēṭillāc ciṟumoḻiyai, vaṟitueṉa oḻiyātu aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭum - ciṟiyateṉṟu nīkkāmal tiruvuḷampaṟṟal vēṇṭum;
iṅku `aruḷ eṉṟatu, kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭavaḻip poṟukkum aruḷai. `tuṉṉi niṉṟiṅ keṉpatu, uḷḷattiṉ aṇukkattai uṇarttutaṟkeḻuntatu; uṭalaṇukkamum ataṉvaḻit tākaliṉ.
`oṭiyāyciṟumoḻi payaṉ viḷaittaliṟ keṭāta ciṟucol; eṉṟatu poruḷuraiyākumeṉṟaṟku; oṭiyā ipporuṭṭātal oṭiyāvuḷḷam eṉpataṉuraiyiṟ kāṇka (paripāṭal, 2, 36). vaṟituciṟitākum (tol. uriyiyal, 40). paṇivukāṭṭal karutic ciṟumoḻiyeṉṟāḷ. `aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭu meṉṟatu, ārāytal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku; ciṟu moḻiyai ēṟṟoḻukal vēṇṭumeṉpatu kuṟippu.
aṭikaṇ muṉṉar yāṉaṭi vīḻntēṉ
  vaṭiyāk kiḷavi maṉakkoḷal vēṇṭum
eṉṉuñ cilappatikāravaṭikaḷ (13) ivvaṭikaḷōṭu ottunōkki yuṇaraṟpālaṉa.
`pāvāy, tiruvaṉaiyāy, muttaṉaiyāy, cuṭarkkoṭiyē, uyirōviyamē, maṇiviḷakkē, aṭiyēṉ kūṟum oṭiyāyc ciṟumoḻi aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭu meṉat toṭarpupaṭuttuka.
iṉi, mēl `vayaluḻavar mutal `viḷaiyuḷ īṟākap patiṉāṉkaṭikāṟum oṟṟimānakaraic ciṟappikku mukattāl, marutanilavaḷaṉ kūṟukiṉṟār.
(22- 26) vayaluḻavar kuvaḷaikaṭṭu - kaḻaṉiyiluḷḷa uḻavarkaḷ kuvaḷaippūṭu kaḷaintu, viyalvarampil - akaṉṟa varampiṉmēl, taṭintiṭṭa kayaliṉ iṭaippaṭa eṟiya - koṉṟupōṭṭa kayalmīṉkaḷiṉ naṭuvil viḻumpaṭi eṟiya, marutamakaḷ karuviḻipōl poruvu iṉṟit tiruviḷaṅka - akkuvaḷaiyiṉ malarkaḷ marutanila makaḷiṉ karuviḻiyaippōl oppiṉṟi aḻakuviḷaṅkavum,
 `veḷḷai veḷē leṉṟu miṉṉikkiṭakkuṅ kayal mīṉkaḷ marutamakaḷiṉ veḷviḻiyāka, avaṟṟiṭaiyiṟ poruntiya kuvaḷai malarkaḷ avaṭkuk karuviḻiyāy viḷaṅkiṉaveṉka.
vayaluḻavar, - vayaṟkaṇ uḻavar.
kaṭṭal, kaḷaital; eṉṟatu `kaḷaikaṭṭa leṉka; kaḷ eṉṉum mutaṉilai iṭainilaiyum viṉaiyecca vikutiyumāṉa takaravukarañ cērntu, ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum (tol. eḻuttu, 150) eṉṉum puṇarcci vitippaṭi ḷakaratakaraṅkaḷ ṭakaramāy iṭaitirintu `kaṭṭu eṉa muṭintamai kāṇka.
viyal, uriccol; viyaleṉ kiḷavi yakalap poruṭṭē eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār (uri. 68).
marutanilam, vayalum vayalcārnta iṭamumām. innilam iṅku `makaḷ eṉṟu uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
(27-29) nennel vittiya poṉ uṟaḻ vāṉmuḷai - ciṉṉāṭkumuṉ vittiya cennel vitaiyiṉ poṉṉokkun tūya muḷaikaḷ, puri aviḻat talai virintu - muṟukkuṭaiyat tāḷ virintu, paittueṉṉap parantu iruppa - paccaip pacēleṉap parantu tikaḻavum; 
`vittiya cenneṉmuḷaiyeṉa māṟuka.
`cennel, neṟkaḷuḷ uyarntatu; itaṉāl itaṟku `naṉṉel eṉavum oru peyaruṇṭu; itu, cenne ṉaṉṉel ceñcālip peyar eṉṉum piṅkalantai (marappeyar) yiṟ kāṇka.
`poṉ pacumpoṉ; iṅku niṟattiṟku uvamai.
`paittu, pacumaiyeṉṉum paṇpuppeyariṉ ṟiripu; īṟukeṭṭu muṉṉiṉṟa meytirintu taṉṉoṟṟiraṭṭi muṭintateṉak koḷka; itu pacumainiṟattai yuṇarttutal tivākarattiṟ (8) kāṇka.
(30-34) teṉoḷip paḷiṅkiṉuḷ - teḷinta oḷiyaiyuṭaiya oru paḷiṅkuppēḻaiyuḷ, ayirpeytu - nuṇmaṇalaiyiṭṭu, pācilaiyum cētāmpalum kaḷ kuvaḷaiyum muḷ tāmaraiyum - paciya ilaikaḷaiyuñ civanta āmpaṉ malarkaḷaiyun tēṉ poruntiya kuvaḷaippūkkaḷaiyum muḷḷuḷḷa tāmarai malarkaḷaiyum, oruṅkupaṭa eḻuti maruñku vaittāṅku - oṉṟāy eḻutip pakkalil vaittāṟpōla, nalpūmkayaṅkaḷ poṟ poṭum imaippa - aḻakiya pūkkaḷ niṟainta nīrkkuḷaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅku aḻakāy viḷaṅkavum.
paḷiṅku kuḷattiṉ teḷinīrukku uvamai. `ayir nuṇmaṇaṟ poruṭṭātal `veḷḷayirkkāṉal (innūl, 1, 25) eṉpuḻik kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
pacumai ilai `pācilaiyeṉavuñ, cemmai āmpal `cētāmpa leṉavun tirintu puṇarntaṉa. itaṉai `īṟupōtal (naṉṉūl, pata. 9) eṉpataṉāl muṭikka.
kuḷattiṉ nīraip paḷiṅkeṉakkoṇṭu, ataṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa maṇaṟṟarai `ayirpeytu eṉṉuntoṭarāṟ kaṇṇukkuk kāṇappaṭuvatāka uraikkumukattāṟ kuḷattiṉ aṭimutal mēlvaraiyil eṭuttukkāṭṭutalē īṇṭu āciriyar karuttu. āmpal, kuvaḷai, tāmarai, mutalākupeyar.
`āṅku uvamavurupu; kaṭaṟkaṇṭāṅku (tirumurukāṟṟup     paṭai, 2) eṉpuḻippōla.
āciriyar īṇṭuc cētāmpaluṅ kaṭkuvaḷaiyum mutalāyiṉa veṭuttukkāṭṭip poykaiyiṉiyalpai viḷakkutalpōlavē,
 muṭṭāḷa cuṭarttāmarai
  kaṭkamaḻu naṟuneytal
  vaḷḷita ḻaviḻnīla
  mellilai yariyāmpaloṭu
  vaṇṭiṟai koṇṭa kamaḻpūm poykai.
eṉṟu āciriyar māṅkuṭimarutaṉārum maturaikkāñciyil (249- 253) uraittal kaṇṭukoḷka.
(35) taṇṇaṭaimaruviya taṇiyāviḷaiyuḷ - marutanilaṅkaḷ poruntiya kuṟaiyāta viḷaiviṉaiyuṭaiya, tiruvoṟṟiyū reṉṟu mēlaṭiyōṭu oṭṭuka.
taṇṇaṭai, marutanilam; iḵtipporuṭṭātal, talaiyaḷittāṉ taṇṇaṭaiyun tantu eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālaiyiṟ (2, 12) kāṇka.
`viḷaiyuḷ uḷ vikutipeṟṟa toḻilaṭiyākap piṟanta peyar; peyalum viḷaiyuḷum eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (545).
viḷaṅkavum (26), parantiruppavum (29), imaippavum (34) taṇṇaṭai maruviya viḷaiyuḷ `oṟṟimānakar eṉṟu viṉaiyeccaṅkaṭku ummaivirittuk kūṭṭikkoḷka.
(36-44) oṟṟimānakariṉ - tiruvoṟṟimāk kōyiliṉkaṇ, muṟpaṭa amarnta - civapirāṉeḻuntaruḷiya karuvaṟaikkunērē muṉṉiṭattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭeḻuntaruḷiya, uṭku uṭai murukaṉ - teyvattaṉmaiyāṟ kaṇṭār añcutaṟku uriya murukap pirāṉai, kaṇkaṇṭāṅku - kaṇṇetirē kaṇṭāleṉṉa, pārttu āṉāp payilvaṭiviṉ - kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭu amaiyāta akakkaṇṇetirē palakālun tōṉṟum vaṭiviṉaiyum, ōrttu āṉā uraṉ uṇarviṉ - ārāyn tamaiyāta ciṟanta aṟiviṉaiyum uṇarcciyiṉaiyum, col āṉāp palpukaḻiṉ - colliyamaiyāta mikka pukaḻiṉaiyum uṭaiya, tāḷtoṭa vīḻnta kaiyaṉ - muḻantāḷiṟ poruntumpaṭi vaḷarnta kaiyiṉaṉum; tōḷtoṭu poṉñāṇ piṇitta villaṉ-  tōḷiṟporuntum poṟkayiṟukaṭṭiya villiṉaṉum; kaṇaiyaṉ - avvilliṟ ṟoṭukkum ampukaḷaiyuṭaiyavaṉum; miṉñāṇ piṇitta kuñciyaṉ - miṉṉukiṉṟa poṉñāṇāl vaḷaittukkaṭṭiya mayirmuṭiyiṉaṉum; iḷaiñaṉ - kaṭṭiḷam paruvattiṉaṉum; ariyaṉ - yāvarālum aṇaitaṟkariyavaṉum; periyaṉ - ataṟkēṟpap peruntakaimaiyuṭaiyavaṉum; tiruvaḷar celvaṉ - celvaṅkaḷ ōṅkukiṉṟa niṟaivuṭaiyavaṉum āṉa avaṉ,
vaṭiviṉ uṇarviṟ pukaḻiṟkaiyaṉ; villaṉ; kaṇaiyaṉ; kuñciyaṉ; ariyaṉ; periyaṉ celvaṉ eṉa iyaikka.
innūlkoṇṭa murukapperumāṉ tiruvoṟṟiyūrc civapirāṉ tirukkōyiṉ mukappil eḻuntaruḷiyiruppava ṉeṉpār, muṟpaṭavamarnta veṉṟu kiḷantār.
āṉā, īṟukeṭṭa etirmaṟaip peyareccam; amaiyāta veṉpatu poruḷ; amaitippaṭāta veṉṟukoḷka; kolaiyāṉāk kūṟṟam eṉpatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai (kāñci, 13).
payilal, palakāluntōṉṟal; payiṟṟi eṉpataṟkup palakāluṅkūṟi eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟuraikārar (34) poruḷurait tamaiyāl accoṟ palakāl nikaḻum nikaḻcciyiṉai yuṇarttutal aṟiyappaṭum.
vaṭivu, uṇarvu, pukaḻ eṉpavaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāvatu virikka. iṉ, cāriyai.
`tāṭoṭa vīḻntakai eṉpatu tāṭōy taṭakkai (puṟanāṉūṟu, 90) eṉṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ payiṉṟuvaralaṟika.
kaiyaṉ, villaṉ mutalāyiṉa kuṟippuviṉaip peyarkaḷ. `celvam niṟaivu; celvameṉpatu cintaiyiniṟaivē yeṉṉum maṇimēkalaiyaṭikaḷ iṅku niṉaivukūraṟpālatu.
(45) iṉṉuṅ, kuṭiyiṉāluṅ kulattiṉāluṅ kuṟṟamoṉṟu millātavaṉ.
kuṭiyeṉpatu kuṭumpam, atāvatu oruvaṉaic cūḻnta akaccuṟṟam; kulameṉpatu puṟaccuṟṟam. kuṭiyiṉ kaṇṇuṅ kulattiṉ kaṇṇumeṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittalumām.
(46-47) peṟuvatu oṉṟu uṭaiyaṉpōla - eṉ māṭṭup peṟuvatoru poruḷuṭai yāṉpōla, maṟuvantu - cuḻaṉṟu, palnāḷ eṉiṉum eṉcol nilaitavaṟāṉ - ataṉaip peṟutaṟkup palanāṭkaḷ kaḻintaṉavēṉum yāṉ eṉatu collāl niṟuttiya nilaiyiṉiṉṟun tavaṟāṉ.
maṟuvantu, maṟuvā pakuti; cuḻaṉṟu, iḵtipporuṭṭātal maṟuvantumayaṅki eṉṉum peruṅkataiyilum (33, 127), maṟuvaraṟpoḻutil eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟiṉuṅ (22)  kāṇka.
(48) kuṟittatu - tāṉ colluvataṟku uḷḷattiṟ kuṟittatai, kiḷavātu ceṟittalum ceṟippaṉ - veḷippaṭac collāmal taṉṉuḷḷattē aṭaṅkutaluñ ceyvaṉ.
kuṟittatu, ceyappāṭṭu viṉaipporuḷiṉ vanta viṉaippeyar kiḷattal -vāyviṭṭuc collal; ceṟittal, uḷḷaṭakkutal; ipporuṭṭātal ceṟivaṟintu cīrmai payakkum eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ (123) kāṇka. ceṟittaluñ ceṟippaṉ eṉṟatiṟ piṉṉiṉṟatu ceyvaṉ eṉak kāriyavācaka māy niṟṟal nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruraiyiṟ (tolkāppiyam, 112) kāṇka.
(49-51) urumum uḷiyum aravum piriyā - iṭiyōcaiyum karaṭikaḷum pāmpukaḷum viṭṭunīṅkāta, āratar nīntic cīritiṉ pōntu - koṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyaik kaṭantu nalamāy vantu, tiritarum nāḷkaḷum pala - nām irukkum puṉaṅkaḷiṟṟirikiṉṟa nāḷkaḷum palavākum; uḷiyam - karaṭi; kōḷval uḷiyam eṉpatu cilappatikāram (13, 5). atar - vaḻi, āṉiṉaṅ kalitta atar eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu (138); ārkoṭumai mēṟṟu; aṉṟik kaṭattaṟkariya vaḻiyeṉalumām, nīnti, kaṭanteṉṟaṟku; āvatu kaṭantuvarutaliṉ arumaiyaik kuṟittatu, ummai muṟṟu.
(51-56) orunāḷ - orunāḷaṉṟu, nam malai akaṉcāral - namatu akaṉṟa malaippakkattil, talaimaiyoṭu poliyum ārāmattiṉ - mutaṉmaiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkuñ cōlaikkaṇ, nī vārāyāka orutaṉic ceṉṟēṉ - nīvaravillaiyākat taṉiyē ceṉṟa yāṉ, parivoṭu pukuntu - varuttattōṭu uḷnuḻaintu, pālnīrvāviyiṉ mēviniṟpa - pālaippōlun teḷinīrai yuṭaiya kuḷattiṉkaraiyiṟceṉṟu niṟka, avaṉum āṇṭu eyti eṉṉoṭu niṉṟaṉaṉ - attalaimakaṉum avviṭattil vantu eṉṉoṭu niṉṟāṉ;
`ārāmam, malaiccōlai; ipporuṭṭātal piṅkalantaiyiṟ kāṇka. `vārāyāka veṉpataṉ āka, ceyaveṉ viṉaiyeccavīṟu
ceṉṟēṉ, viṉaippeyar.
parivu - varuttam; paripāṭal, 8.
maṇvaḷamuma iṉimaiyum mikka nīr pālpōl veṇṇiṟa muṭaiyatāyk kāṇappaṭumākaliṉ, `pāṉīr eṉappaṭṭatu.
ummai, eccam.
(56-64) noytiṉ tiraiporu karaiyil purai iṉṟu ōṅki - ilēcāka alaikaḷ mōtukiṉṟa akkuḷakkaraiyiṟ kuṟṟamillāmal vaḷarntu, pacumkuḻai kalittu - paciyataḷirkaḷ taḻaittu, nacai naṉai arumpi - viruppam uṇṭākkum pūmoṭṭukaḷ arumpeṭuttu, teṉṟaloṭu pukunta vaṇṭu icaippāṇaṉ iṉimaiyiṉ miḻaṟṟum taṉi icaikku uvantu - teṉṟaṟkāṟṟoṭu nuḻainta vaṇṭeṉṉum icaimikka pāṭṭuvallāṉ iṉitāka icaikkum oppaṟṟa icaikku makiḻntu, poti aviḻ malariṉ naṟavu uṇakkoṭuttu - ataṟkupparicilāka itaḻkkaṭṭu aviḻnta taṉ malarkaḷiṉ tēṉai avai uṇṇumpaṭi īntu, niṉaitoṟum cuvaikkum nīlniṟak kaṉikaḷ ilaitoṟum kuḻuma nalam mikappayakkum kōḻaraināval - niṉaikkuntōṟum nāvūṟukiṉṟa nīlaniṟap paḻaṅkaḷ ilaikaḷtōṟun tokutiyāyp paḻuttiruttalāl taṉṉai aṇukiṉārkku naṉmaiyai mikavum viḷaikkiṉṟa vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nāvaṉmaram, kōṭṭiṉ ukuppa - icaikēṭṭa makiḻcciyiṉāṟ kiḷaikaḷ acaintu appaḻaṅkaḷaic coriya;
noymai, ilēcu; kalittal, taḻaittal; itaṟku ipporuḷuṇmai tūvaṟkalitta tēmpāy puṉṉai eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟ (24)ṟuraiyil kāṇka.
`vaṇṭicaip pāṇaṉ iṭaiyē paṇpoṭṭu virittuk koḷka. nīl, kaṭaikuṟaintatu; nīṉiṟavicumpu (paṭṭiṉappālai, 67) eṉpatiṟpōla, nāvaṟpaḻaṅkaḷ tokuti tokutiyāyk kāyttup  paḻuttiruttalāṟ `kuḻuma veṉṟār. kuḻumal tokutip poruṭṭātal tivākarattiṟ (8) kāṇka.
marattiṉ araikaḷ kōḻarai, pokuṭṭarai, muḷḷarai, poriyaraiyeṉa nāṉku vakaippaṭum; nāval kōḻaraiyiṉatu.
`ōṅki kalittu arumpi uvantu koṭutta nāval ukuppa veṉka.
(65-73) pāl iṭai viḻūum nīlamaṇi kaṭuppa - pāliṉuḷ viḻukiṉṟa nīlaniṟamāṉa maṇiyai oppa, ām iṭai viḻūum arum cuvaikkaṉikaḷ - kuḷattu nīriṉuḷ viḻukiṉṟa mikka cuvaiyiṉai yuṭaiya appaḻaṅkaḷai, aḷaiiṭait tuñcum arumpeṭaikku aḷippa - vaḷaiyiṉuḷ tuyilkoṇṭirukkum arumaiyuṭaiya taṉ peṭṭai naṇṭukkuk koṭukkum poruṭṭu, vēppiṉ koḻunaṉai ēykkum neṭum kaṇ kavaikkāl alavaṉ tāḷ iṭai iṭukki - vēmpiṉ ceḻumaiyāṉa arumpaiyotta nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷaiyum irupiḷavāṉa kālkaḷaiyum uṭaiya āṇñeṇṭu taṉ kālkaḷiṉiṭaiyil iṭukkiyapaṭiyāy, aṉpuṭaṉ ceṉṟu āṅku aruntutal nōkki - aṉporu pōy avvaḷaiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa taṉpeṭai naṇṭukku ūṭṭutalaip pārttu, eṉṉaiyum nōkkiṉaṉ aṉṉatum nōkkiṉaṉ-  ataṉ kātalai viyantu eṉṉaiyum pārttāṉ ataṉaiyum pārttāṉ, uykuvatu aṟiyāṉpōlac ceykuvatu oṉṟum kāṇātu aṉṟu niṉṟaṉaṉē - uyirpiḻaippataṟku vaḻi yoṉṟum aṟiyātavaṉ pōlac ceyvatoṉṟum aṟiya māṭṭātavaṉāy aṉṟu alamantu niṉṟāṉ.
pāṉīrvāviyiṉ nīṉiṟakkaṉikaḷviḻutaliṟ, `pāliṭai viḻūum nīlamaṇikaṭuppa veṉṟu uvamai kūṟiṉār.
`ām, nīr; iḵtipporuṭṭātal aṟunīrppaiñcuṉai ām aṟappulartaliṉ eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟaṭiyiṟ (1, 12) kāṇka.
arumai, īṇṭu mikutimēṟṟu. kaṉikaḷ ceyappaṭu poruḷurupu virittukkoḷka.
tuñcumeṉṟu iṉṟuyil kuṟikkappaṭṭatu, iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭuṅ kāmaviṉpattuk kēṟpa; `vēppu, iṭaivalittatu. vēmpiṉ naṉai alavaṉ kaṇkaḷukku uvamaiyātal paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ peruvaraviṟṟu; vēppunaṉai yaṉṉa neṭuṅkaṭ kaḷvaṉ eṉpatu aiṅkuṟunūṟu (kaḷvaṉ pattu, 10 ).
ñeṇṭukkup piḷavupaṭṭa kālkaḷuṇmaiyiṟ, `kavaikkālalavaṉ eṉṟār; ivviyaṟkai yuṇmai.
mitiyulaik kollaṉ muṟikoṭiṟ ṟaṉṉa
  kavaittā ḷalava ṉaḷaṟṟaḷai citaiya 	(perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, 208)
eṉa āciriyar uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār ōrariya uvamaiyuṭaṉ eṭuttuk kūṟi viḷakkutaliṉvait taṟintukoḷḷappaṭum. ñeṇṭuviṉ vaḷaikku `aḷai yeṉṉum peyar vaḻakkātalum, ivvaṭikaḷil `aḷaicitaiya veṉa varumāṟṟāṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
aruttal, aruntumpaṭi ceytal; piṟaviṉai.
`āṇñeṇṭu taṉ peṭaikkuk kātal aṉpuṭaṉ arutti makiḻtal kaṇṭu, ammukattāl murukaṉun taṉ talaiviyiṉ niṉaiveḻappeṟṟuk kātalaṉpil uruki āṟṟāṉāyt taṉateṇṇattai muṭittuvaikkumāṟu eṉṉai nōkkiṉaṉ; mīṇṭum vēṭkaimikutiyāl aññeṇṭaiyum nōkkiṉaṉ; aṅṅaṉam nōkkiṉamai eṉak kataṉaik kuṟippittamai pōlāyiṟṟu eṉpatu. orukāṟ kaṇṭu tarikkun taṉmait taṉṟām vēṭkai eṉṉum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai (30-ām nūṟpāurai;) iṅku niṉaivukūraṟpālatu.
`uykuvataṟiyā ṉeṉṟatu, vēṭkaimikuti karuti; avvēṭkaiyiṉ mayakkattāṟ ceykuvatoṉṟuṅ kāṇātu niṉṟaṉaṉ. ceykuvatu, kucāriyai; niṉṟaṉaṉē, ē irakkap poruḷatu.
(74-79) koṉ uṟu pēykaṇṭa aṉaiya peṟṟiyaṉ āki - añcutal poruntiya pēyiṉaip pārttāṟpōlvatoru taṉmaiyaṉāy, niṟaiyum aṟivum muṟaimuṟai cāya - uḷattai oruvaḻi niṟuttutalum aṟivum oṉṟaṉ piṉoṉṟāyk keṭa, tuṉṟu nilai nīṅkā marampōl - poruntum nilaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāta marattaip pōl, kaṉṟiya nōkkamoṭu niṉṟavaṉ nilai - nainta nōkkattoṭu niṉṟavaṉatu nilaiyai, nī kaṇṭaṉaiyēl nī - pārttaṉaiyāṉāl, neñcam nekkuuruki - uḷḷam uṭaintu uruki, iṉ uyir vāḻalai - niṉatu iṉiya uyiroṭu vāḻtal ceyyāy. avaṉ eṅṅaṉam vāḻtal ceyvāṉ eṉa oḻintaporuḷai yuṇarttaliṉ, maṉ, oḻiyicaip poruḷatu; ēkāraṅkaḷiraṇṭum acaikaḷ. `vāḻalai etirmaṟaik kaṇ vanta muṉṉilai muṟṟu.
koṉ accapporuṭṭāyp pēyiṉai aṭuttuvantatu. iḵtipporuṭṭātal tivākarattiṟ kāṇka.
pēykaṇ ṭaṉṉatuṭaittu eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷukkup (565) parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟiyavuraiyaiyuṅ kāṇka.
niṟai, toḻilaṭiyākap piṟanta peyar; karutiya vaḻiyellām uḷḷattaic cellaviṭāmal niṟuttuta leṉpatu; kaṇṭuḻiyellām uḷḷattaiccelīi uḷḷattiṉ vaḻi ōṭumākalāṉ niṟaiyilaṉām eṉavaruṅ kaḷaviyalurai yilum (35) ivvuṇmai kāṇka. `tuṉṟu nilai, poruntiya nilai; āvatu niṉṟanilai yeṉka.
`niṉṟavaṉ nilai nī kaṇṭaṉaiyēl uyirvāḻalai maṉṉēyeṉka. 65 ām aṭimutal iv īṟṟaṭikāṟum uraikkappaṭṭa ipporuḷ,
  nīkaṇ ṭaṉaiyeṉiṉ vāḻalai nēriḻai yampalattāṉ
  cēykaṇ ṭaṉaiyaṉceṉ ṟāṅkōralavaṉṟaṉ cīrppeṭaiyiṉ
  vāyvaṇ ṭaṉaiyatōr nāvaṟ kaṉinaṉi nalkakkaṇṭu
  pēykaṇ ṭaṉaiyatoṉ ṟākiniṉ ṟāṉap peruntakaiyē
eṉṟu tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaiyāruḷ (84) `tōḻi viravik kūṟal eṉṉum ittuṟaimēlatākavē collum poruḷum oppa uṇarttappaṭṭiruttal aṟiyaṟpālatākum.
icceyyuḷai, `arumpeṟaṟpāvāy! (5) tiruvaṉaiyāy (7) muttaṉaiyāy (9) cuṭarkkoṭiyē (13) uyirōviyamē (15) maṇiviḷakkē (18) aṭiyēṉ kūṟuñciṟumoḻi aṟivukoḷal vēṇṭum (18-21), kuvaḷai karuviḻipōl (25) tiruviḷaṅka (26), vāṉmuḷai (27) paitteṉṉap parantiruppa (29), naṟpūṅkayaṅkaḷ poṟpoṭum imaippat (34) taṇṇaṭai maruviya taṇiyāviḷaiyuḷ (35) oṟṟimānakariṉ (36) murukaṉaik kaṇṭāṅku (37); kaiyaṉ (41), villaṉ, kaṇaiyaṉ (42), kuñciyaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ, (43) ariyaṉ periyaṉ, tiruvaḷarcelvaṉ (44), vaṭuvoṉṟillāṉ; peṟuvatoṉṟuṭaiyaṉ pōla maṟuvantu (46) eṉcoṉṉilai tavaṟāṉ (47); kuṟittatu kiḷavātu ceṟittaluñ ceṟippaṉ (48); cīritiṟ pōntu (50) tiritarunāḷkaḷum pala; orunāḷ nam (51) malaiyakaṉcāral (52) ārāmattil nī vārāyākat (53)taṉicceṉṟēṉ pukuntu (54) vāviyiṉ mēviniṟpa, avaṉum āṇṭu (55) eyti eṉṉoṭu niṉṟaṉaṉ (56); aṅkuk karaiyiṉ ōṅki (57) nalamikap payakkum (63) nāval ukuppa (64); āmiṭaiviḻūum aruñcuvaik kaṉikaḷaip (66) peṭaikkaḷippa (67) alavaṉ iṭukkic (69) ceṉṟu aruttal nōkki (70), eṉṉaiyum nōkkiṉaṉ aṉṉatum nōkkiṉaṉ (71), ceykuvatoṉṟuṅ kāṇātu niṉṟaṉaṉ (73); niṉṟavaṉnilai (79) nī kaṇṭaṉaiyēl (74), iṉṉuyirvāḻalai (75); yeṉṟu viṉaimuṭivu ceytukoḷka.
5. tōḻi muṉṉuṟavuṇartal
eṉṟatu, tōḻi, talaivikku muṉiyaṟkaippuṇarcci yuṇmaiyaik kuṟippāluṇartal; eṉṉai?
muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal kuṟaiyuṟa vuṇartal
  iruvaru muḷvaḻi yavaṉvara vuṇartaleṉ
  ṟammūṉ ṟeṉpa tōḻik kuṇarcci
eṉpatu kaḷaviya(7)lātaliṉ, `muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal eṉpataṉai `muṉ `uṟavu `uṇartal eṉa mūṉṟu collākap pirittu, `muṉṉē talaivikkun talaivaṟkum uṇṭāṉa uṟavākiya iyaṟkaip puṇarcciyiṉai yuṇartal eṉṟu poruḷuraittuk koḷka; muṉṉuṟṟataṉai yuṇartalāṉē itaṟku muṉṉuṟa vuṇartaleṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu eṉṟār pērāciyarum, (tirukkōvaiyār, 62).
nilaiyun tirintu niṟaiyuṅ kaṭantu
  talaiyuṅ kaviḻnta takaiyaṇ - malainivanta
  teṉṉap poliyu meyiloṟṟic cevvēṇmēṉ
  maṉṉuṅ karuttuṭaiya mātu.
(i-ḷ) malainivantatu eṉṉap poliyum - malai uyarnta taṉmaittu eṉṉumpaṭi viḷaṅkukiṉṟa, eyil oṟṟic cevvēḷmēl - matilkaḷaiyuṭaiya tiruvoṟṟiyūril tirukkōyil koṇṭaruḷiya murukapperumāṉmēl, maṉṉum karuttuuṭaiyayai mātu - nilaipeṟṟa uḷḷam uṭaiya talaivi, nilaiyum tirintu - peṇmai nilaiyum māṟi, niṟaiyum kaṭantu - maṉattuḷḷa maṟaiporuḷaiyun taṉvayamiṉṟiyē veḷiviṭṭuc colli, talaiyum kaviḻnta takaiyaḷ - piṉ atu colliṉamai niṉaintu nāṇattāl talaiyuṅ kuṉinta taṉmaiyaḷāvaḷ.
cevvēṇmēṉ maṉṉuṅ karuttuṭaiyamātu talaiyuṅ kaviḻnta takaiyaḷeṉka.
nilaitiritalāvatu, peṇmaiyeṉappaṭum nāṇam maṭa maccam payirppukaḷ taṉvaya miṉṟiyē māṟutal.
niṟai, uḷḷattuḷḷa maṟaiviṉaip pulappaṭātu niṟuttutaleṉpatu.
talaivi, maṟaiviloḻukiya kaḷavoḻukkattāl nilaiyum niṟaiyuṅkaṭantu nāṇmikkut talaiyuṅ kaviḻntatakaiya ḷāyiṉāḷeṉka.
ummaikaḷ, eccam.
malai, uvamai; nivanta, uyarnta; ōṅkiya vorukuṭai yurukeḻu matiyiṉivantu eṉpar āciriyar kōvūrkiḻārum (puṟanāṉūṟu, 31).
mēl: ēḻāmvēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷatu.
`maṉṉum nilaipēṟuṇarttum maṉṉeṉṉum iṭaic  collaṭiyākap piṟanta ceyyu meṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccaviṉai (5).
6. pirivāṟṟāta talaivikkut tōḻi kūṟal
mātē peritum varuntuva teṉṉai maṉantirita
lētē yaṟiyiṟ koṭumaiyaṉ ṟōvaṉṉai yīrmpotumpiṟ
cūtēy tuṇaimulai yākan taḻuvit tuyarmikutta
pōtēr poḻiloṟṟic cevvēḷ varaiyap pukuntaṉaṉē
(i-ḷ) mātē - eṉ arumait talaimakaḷē, peritum varuntuvatu eṉṉai - nī mikavun tuṉpuṟuvatu eṉ, maṉam tirital ētu aṉṉai aṟiyiṉ koṭumaiyaṉṟō - niṉatu neñcan tiritaṟkāṉa ētuvaittāy aṟiyumāṉāl tīṅku nērumaṉṟō, īrm potumpil - kuḷirnta poḻilil, cūtu ēy tuṇaimulai ākam taḻuvit tuyarmikutta - cūtukkāykaḷai māṟupaṭukkuṅ koṅkaikaḷ neruṅkiya niṉmārpai aṇaintu ittuṉpattai viḷaitta, pōtu ērpoḻil oṟṟiccevvēḷ - malarkaḷiṉ aḻaku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa cōlaikaḷ cūḻnta tiruvoṟṟip perumāṉ, varaiyap pukuntaṉaṉ - varaitaṟku vantuviṭṭaṉaṉ; eṉavē nī varuntaṟka veṉpatu.
maṉan tiritalētu, ētup poruḷiṉ varum nāṉkaṉurupum payaṉum virittukkoḷka.
ōkāram, viṉā; ītu aṟiporuḷ viṉāviṉ kaṇaṭaṅkum; iḷam pūraṇar iṉṉōraṉṉavaṟṟai `aṟivoppukkāṇṭal viṉā eṉpar. ivviyalpukaḷellām ceppum viṉāvum vaḻāa lōmpal eṉṉun tolkāppiyac collatikāra nūṟpā (13) vukkuppōnta iḷampūraṇar cēṉāvaraiyarurai kaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷḷappaṭum.
aṉṉai, īṇṭu naṟṟāy; aṉṟic cevilittāy eṉiṉum avariruvareṉiṉum amaiyum.
potumpeṉappaṭṭatu, maracceṟivu mikka cōlaiyātaliṉ; īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum ākantaḻuviya ceyti kuṟiyiṭattu nikaḻcciyai yuṇarttukiṉṟamaiyāṉ, annikaḻccik kēṟpap potumpu kūṟappaṭṭatu. ip potumpiṉiyalpai nallicaippulamai vāynta āciriyar nakkīraṉār.
cantaṉamuñ caṇpakamuntēmāvun tīmpalavum āciṉiyum acōkuṅ kōṅkum vēṅkaiyuṅ kuravamum virintu, nākamun tilakamum naṟavum nantiyum mātaviyum mallikaiyum mauvaloṭu maṇaṅkamaḻntu, pātiriyum pāvai ñāḻalum paiṅ koṉṟaiyoṭu piṇiyaviḻnta porippuṉkum puṉṉākamum murukkoṭu mukaiciṟantu, vaṇṭaṟaintu, tēṉārntu, varikkuyilka ḷicaipāṭat taṇṭeṉṟal iṭaivirāyt taṉiyavarai muṉivuceyyum poḻil eṉṟum,
vaṇṭun tumpiyum varikkaṭaip piracamuṅ koṇṭupuṇar narampeṉṟu muraṉṟu, aintu vāyilāṉuṅ koḷḷappaṭun tuppuraviṉait taṉṉakattuṭaittāyp palvakaippaṭṭa maraṅkaḷāṉum polivuṭaittāyp, puṟattār akattāraik kāṇpataritāy akattārpuṟattāraik kāṇpateḷitāy viḻaivu viṭutta viḻumiyōraiyum viḻaivu tōṟṟuvikkum paṇpiṟṟu (iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ, 2, 18). eṉṟum mika aḻakāka uraittu viḷakkukiṉṟamai iṅkup peritum niṉaivukūrntu makiḻaṟpālatākum.
ēy, uvamavurupu; kañcukamēykkuṅ kaṉaṅkuḻaiyē (tirukkōvaiyār, 15) eṉpatiṟpōla.
`pōtu kālavākupeyarāy malarai yuṇarttiṟṟu.
`pukuntaṉa ṉeṉṟatu, iṉippukuvāṉai; iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉāḷ, pukutaliṉ viraivu uṇarttit talaimakaḷait tēṟṟutaṟporuṭṭu; viraiviṉkuṟipput tōṉṟu iṟanta kālattiṉ vaittu ivvāṟu kiḷattalellām vārākkālattu (tolkāppiyam, viṉaiyiyal, 46) meṉpataṉāṉ muṭikkappaṭum.
ēkāraṅkaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ muṉṉatu viḷiyeṉavum ēṉaiya acaikaḷeṉavuṅ koḷka



7. iṉanalaṉuraittal
aṭikaḷ, tamakkuc camayanūluṇmaikaḷ aṟivuṟutta camayāciriyaraiyē `iṉanalaṉ eṉakkoṇṭu, avaratu cārpāl tamakkut tiruvaruḷuṇeṟicārum vāyil vāyttamai itaṉkaṇ urait taruḷukiṉṟār.                                            
(1-2) pukal aru nūḻaiyil - pukutal arumaiyāṉa ciṟiya nuḻaivāyilil, pōkku arum poruḷpōl - nuḻaittal arumai yāṉa periya poruḷaippōl, ikal aṟu caivat tiṟam celā neñciṉar - māṟupāṭaṟṟa caivakkōṭpāṭu kaḷ ēṟāta neñciṉaiyuṭaiyavarum;
`pukalum, pōkkum toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ; ikal, toḻilāku peyar; nuḻaiyum vāyil `nūḻai yeṉappaṭṭatu; ipporuṭṭātal piṅkalantai (4) yiṟkāṇka.
caivacamayam ēṉaic camayaṅkaḷoṭu māṟupaṭāmal, avaiyaṉaittaiyun taṉakku uṟuppukkaḷākakkoṇṭu avaṟṟoṭu poruntiyamaitaliṉ, `ikalaṟucaivam eṉap paṭṭatu; ivvariya uṇmai,
 ōtucama yaṅkaḷporu ḷuṇaru nūlkaḷ
	oṉṟōṭoṉ ṟovvāma luḷapalavu mivaṟṟuḷ
  yātucama yamporuṇūl yātiṅ keṉṉi
	ṉituvāku matuvalla teṉumpiṇakka tiṉṟi
  nītiyiṉā ṉivaiyellā mōriṭattē kāṇa
	niṉṟatiyā torucamaya matucamayam poruṇū
  lātaliṉā livaiyellā marumaṟaiyā kamattē
	yaṭaṅkiyiṭu mavaiyiraṇṭu maraṉaṭikkī ḻaṭaṅkum
eṉac civañāṉacittiyā (8,13) reṉṉuñ caivacamaya nūlil teḷivāy eṭuttōtappaṭutal kāṇka. ītuṇaramāṭṭāp piṟacamayattār caivacamayan tattañ camayaṅkaṭkumāṟāvateṉṟu piḻaipaṭakkaruti, ataṉai ēlātoḻivarākaliṉ, avar karutumāṟupōṟ caivacamayam attaṉmaiya taṉṟeṉṟu iṅkut teḷivāy uṇarttiyavāṟu.
`tiṟa meṉṉuñ col īṇṭuc caivacamayattiṉ poruṭ kūṟupāṭu kuṟittu niṉṟatu; tiṟam - kūṟupāṭu; niṟṟiṟañciṟakka eṉṟār puṟattiṉum (6).
`celā neñciṉa reṉṟatu, nuṇporuḷ cellun takutiyaṟṟa parupporuṇiṉaiviṉai yuṭaiyā reṉṟaṟku.
(3-4) neṟippaṭu makkaṭku - vaḻiccellum makkaṭku, aṟivu pētuṟukkum - avaraṟivai mayaṅkac ceykiṉṟa, kavalaiyiṉ - kavartta vaḻiyaippōl, piṟarai mayakkunar - piṟarai mayaṅkac ceykiṉṟavarum; 
`neṟippaṭumakkaḷ eṉṉuṅ kuṟippuc caivaneṟiyiloḻukā niṟkuñ caiva naṉ makkaḷaik kuṟittatu; piṟarai eṉṟatum avaraiyē; ītu atikārattāṟ peṟappaṭum. `kavalai yeṉṟatu īṇṭuc camaṇ camayattiṟkuvamai. mēlvaruvaṉavum aṉṉa.
`pētuṟuttal aṟivai mayaṅkacceytal.
`mayakkunar eṉpatil nakaravoṟṟup peyariṭai nilai.
(4-5) uval iṭu patukkaiyiṉ - taḻaikaḷ peytu mūṭappaṭṭa kaṟkuviyalpōl, puṉporuḷ akattu iṭu karaviṉar - iḻintakaruttukkaḷai neñciṉuḷ iṭṭuvaitta kaḷḷa muḷḷavarumāṉa,
`uval taḻaiyeṉṉup poruṭṭātal puṟanāṉūṟiṟ kāṇka (262).
`patukkai, maṟaippiṭam; patukkivaittaliṉ vanta toḻilāku peyar; vaḻippaṟikārar tammāṟ kollap paṭṭārmēṟ kaṟkaḷaik kuvittu akkuviyalmēl taḻai peytu mūṭivaikkum uṇmai,
centoṭai piḻaiyā vaṉkaṇ āṭavar
  ampupaṭa vīḻntōr vampap patukkai
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟ (3) ṟiṉālum peṟappaṭum; patukkai, akattukku uvamai.
poruḷeṉṟatu, karuttu; niṟpeṟu poruḷil eṉṟu mēl (39) varutaluṅkāṇka.
(6-13) puṉaimāṇkōtai poruntac cūṭṭiyum - puṉaital māṭcimaippaṭṭa pūmālaiyaik kūntalukkup poruntumāṟu cūṭṭiyum, viṉaimāṇ viḷaṅku iḻai vīṟupaṭat tiruttiyum - toḻil nalañ ciṟanta oḷiviḷaṅkum maṇikaḷiḻaitta aṇikaḷait taṉicciṟappuṇṭākat tiruttamāy aṇintum, koḻumkayal maḻaikkaṇ piṟaḻtoṟum maṉamtirintu - koḻuviya kayal mīṉkaḷaiyotta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ puraḷuntōṟum aṟivu mayaṅki, ceḻumtuvarccevvāy muttam koṇṭu - ceḻuviya pavaḷampōṉṟa civanta vāyiṉ muttam peṟṟu, nacaipiṟakkiṭātu - vēṭkai piṟpaṭāmal, mulaitaḻīic cellum neñcu amarkātalar piṉ celum maṭavār - mulaiyaṇaittup pirintu piṟaku muṉṉē cellum neñcuvirumpiya tam kātalariṉ piṉṉē uṭaṉ cellum makaḷiruṭaiya, pañciṉmēl aṭi - pañcaip pōṉṟa melliya aṭikaḷ, paital uṟṟu uḻappa - tuṉpam aṭaintu varuntumpaṭi, uṟuttuk kiḻikkum paral keḻu murampiṉ - kuttik kīṟum parukkaik kaṟkaḷ nirampiya mēṭṭu nilattaippōl;
puṉai, mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar.
nacai, iṅku vēṭkai; nannacai vāyppa eṉṉum akanāṉūṟ (22) ṟuraiyil iḵtip poruṭṭātal kāṇka.
`neñcamar kātalar eṉpatu uḷḷamvirumpiya kātalarai; mukattāṉ amarntiṉitunōkki eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ (93) pōla.
`pañciṉ, murampiṉ eṉṉum aintaṉurupukaḷ oppup poruḷaṉa.
paital, tuṉpam; patimaruṇṭu paitaluḻakkum eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ (12-29).
talaivaṉ taṉ talaiviyaip piriyuṅkāl, avaṉ avaṭkuk kōtaicūṭṭi, iḻaitirutti, mulaitaḻīic cellum ivviyalpukaḷellām. valimuṉpiṉ valleṉṟa veṉṉum pālaikkaliyiṉ naṉkeṭutturaikkap paṭutal kāṇka.
paralkeḻu murampu maṭavāraṭikaḷai varuttu miyalpiṉatātal,
oḷicceñ cīṟaṭi yurukkarak kēyppa
  uḷittalai vempara lūṉṟupu naliya
veṉavarum peruṅkatai (53: 163-4) yaṭikaḷāl naṉkuṇarappaṭum.
(14-23) puli ataṉ uṭukkai taii-puliyiṉ tōlākiya uṭaiyiṉai uṭuttu, valikeḻu mūilai vaṭivēl oruvayiṉ viḷaṅka - valimaiporuntiya mūṉṟu ilaikaḷaip pōṉṟa vaṭivamainta kūrvēl orupakkattē tōṉṟa, koṉṟai naṉai piṇaitta maṉṟal amtoṭalai - koṉṟai mukaikaḷait toṭuttukkaṭṭiya maṇam poruntiya aḻakiyamālai, pavaḷamalai uṟaḻum mārpil tuvaḷa - pavaḷaṅ kaḷālāṉa malaiyai okkum civantamārpiṟ puraṇṭacaiya, malaitaru maṭantai kuṟaṅkiṉ micai iruntu - malaiyaracaṉ īṉṟeṭutta umaippirāṭṭiyār tamatu toṭaiyiṉmēl amarntu, valampaṭukaiyāl perumpuṟam kavaii -valimai poruntiya valakkaiyāl akaṉṟa mutukaittaḻuvi, ulampaṭu tiraḷtōḷ orumulai oṟṟa - uruṇṭa kallukku nikarāṉa tiraṇṭatōḷai orupakkattuk koṅkaiyāl ñemuṅka, narai ēṟu ūrnta puraicāltōṟṟam niṉaitoṟum - veṇṇiṟamāṉa viṭaiyiṉ mēl ēṟiya uyarvumikka civapirāṉatu tōṟṟattai eṇṇu toṟum, kuḻaiyum uravōrkku neñcurukum aṟiñarāṉa caivarkku, vaikalum kēṭupuri neñciṉ pīṭuilā amaṇar - nāḷun tīmaiceyyum neñcaiyuṭaiya perumaiyillāta camaṇarkaḷ;
ataḷ, tōl; uṭukkai toḻilākupeyar.
itai, aḷapeṭai icainiṟaittaṟporuṭṭāy vantatu; īkaikkaṇṇiyilaṅkat taii eṉpuḻip (puṟanāṉūṟu, 353) pōla.
`valampaṭukaiyāṟ perumpuṟaṅ kavaii yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippāl umaiyammaiyār civapirāṉatu iṭappakkattut toṭaimicai amarntiruttal uṇarappaṭum.
`ulampaṭutiraṭōḷ eṉṉum vaḻakku ulantarutōḷiṉāy  eṉac cīvakacintāmaṇiyilum (nāmaka, 175) varutal kāṇka.
`purai īṇṭu uyarviṉmēlatu; aracuvīṟṟiruntu puraiyōrp pēṇi (25: 81) eṉpatu maṇimēkalai.
`neñciṉar, mayakkunar, karaviṉar, camaṇar eṉat toṭarntu koḷka.
`paralkeḻu murampiṉ (13) kēṭupurineñciṟ pīṭilāvamaṇar eṉa īṇṭuk kūṭṭiyuraittukkoḷka.
(24-29) pīṭu uyar centamiḻk kūṭalil tōṉṟi - perumaimikka centamiḻmoḻippayiṟci viḷaṅkiya maturaimānakariṟ putitu vantu, poypaṭu puṉporuḷ kāṉṟu iruḷparappi - poymai nirampiya tam pulliya camaṇ kōṭpāṭukaḷaik kakki eṅkum iruḷaipparappi, meytaru caiva nalneṟi piḻaippa - meymaiporuntiya caivam eṉappaṭuma nalvaḻitavaṟi, māṇāvaḻippaṭūum kūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ mutal - ciṟavāta tīyavaḻiyil akappaṭuṅkūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ mutalāṉa, āṟucel mākkaḷaic cūṟaikoṇṭu eṟintu - vaḻiccelluma māntarai vaḻipaṟittu ūṟuceytu, perumtuyar uṟukkuṅkālai - mikka tuṉpattai viḷaikkumpoḻutu;
kūṭalil tamiḻccaṅkaṅkaḷ niṟiip pulavarperumakkaḷ tamiḻārāyntu vantaṉarākaliṟ `pīṭuyar centamiḻkkūṭal eṉṟu maturai ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu. maturaikku uriya ēṉaic ciṟappukaḷiṉun tamiḻārāyntaciṟappē ataṟkup peruñ ciṟappākum. kūṭaṉmānakaril tamiḻārāynta perumaiyai māṇikkavācakapperumāṉum uyarmatiṟ kūṭaliṉāynta oṇṭīntamiḻ (tirukkōvaiyār, 20) eṉṟaruḷic ceytal kāṇka.
camaṇkōṭpāṭukaḷ `poypaṭu puṇporu ḷeṉappaṭṭaṉa, avai kaṭavuṭkōṭpāṭṭiṉ vaḻippaṭāmai yiṉ; aṅṅaṉamē caiva camayamum, `meytarucaiva meṉap paṭṭatu; eṉṉai, atu kaṭavuṭ kōṭpāṭṭaiyē taṉakku uyireṉakkoṇṭu niṟṟaliṉeṉpatu.
camaṇarai muṉ `murampu eṉṟuraittamaiyiṟ, caivacamayaṅkaḷ `āṟuceṉmākka ḷeṉavum, avarkaḷ caivamoḻukutalait taṭaiceytu neṟiyallā neṟipukutti ūṟuceytal `iruḷparappi neṟipiḻaippac cūṟainoṇ ṭeṟita leṉavum ēṟṟapeṟṟi iṅku uruvakañceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi `murampiṉ (13) tuyaruṟukkuṅkālai (29) yeṉa viṉaimuṭivuceytal okkumeṉiṉ, aḵtamaiyātu; ēṉeṉṟāl, `piṉcelu maṭavār (11) kuḻaiyumuravōrkku (22) uvamai yātaliṉeṉpatu.
(29-36) aruntavam niṉpeṟuporuḷiṉ palpakal āṟṟi - niṉṉaittamakku makavākappeṟṟeṭukkum nōkkattāl ariyatavattaic `civapātavirutayar palanāḷum iyaṟṟi, vēṇṭiya periyōrkku vēṇṭiyāṅku aḷippa - virumpiya cāṉṟōrkku avar virumpiyavāṟē vēṇṭuvaṉa īya, karukkuḻikkiṭakkum taṉmaiyai aṉmaiyiṉ - karuppai eṉṉuṅ kuḻiyiṟkiṭantu varuntum iyalpuṭaiyai allaiyākaliṉ, uruppeṟa varukunai pōla - uruvuṇṭāka immaṇṇulakil eḻuntaruḷuvōypōla avar akattu oru perumāyam perukuṟacceytu - avar māṭṭuk karuvilamarntāṟpōl oruperiya māyacceykaiyai mikavañ ceytukāṭṭi, puṟam peyarntu eyti - avar maṭiyakattiṉiṉṟum veḷippōntu vantu, aṟam taru perumaic caṇpaiyil tōṉṟiṉai - aṟattaip piṟarkku aruḷum perumaiyiṉai yuṭaiya caṇpaiyeṉṉun tiruppatiyil tōṉṟiyaruḷiṉai;
pakal, nāḷ; āṟṟalōṭu iyaṟṟutaliṉ; `āṟṟi yeṉappaṭṭatu. `ava reṉṟatu, iṅkuc `civapātavirutayarum avartaṅ kaṟpiṟ ciṟanta kātaṉ maṉaiviyār pakavatiyārumeṉka.
viṉaiyiṉīṅkiya muḻumutaliṟaivaṉāṉa murukapperumāṉ piṟaviyuṭpaṭum iyalpiṉaṉ allaṉākaliṟ, pakavatiyār maṇivayiṟṟiṟ karuttaṅki uruppeṟṟatu īṇṭu orumāyamākak kiḷanturaikkappaṭṭatu. ellām valla murukapperumāṉē tiruñāṉacampantarāyp piṟantaruḷiṉāreṉpatu purāṇavaḻakku.
 pūtala mataṉiṟ kāḻimā nakariṟ
	puṇṭarī kaṉṟaṭat torucār
  mātavam purivōṉ muṉṉiḷaṅ katirpōṉ
	matalaiyāy vantaṉaṉ kantaṉ
eṉṟu cīkāḻit talapurāṇam `tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷaiyār tiruvavatāra vattiyāyattil (6) iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟutal kāṇka.
`puṟampeyarn teṉpatu, īṇṭuppiṟattal.
`caṇpai yeṉṟatu `cīkāḻiyai, cīkāḻikkuriya paṉṉiraṇṭu tiruppeyarkaḷiṟ `caṇpai yeṉpatum oṉṟātal caṇpainakar vaḷarkāḻi (tiruñāṉacampantar purāṇam, 14) tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattālaṟiyappaṭum.
`mātu, ō acaikaḷ.
(36 - 40) piṉpu - aṅṅaṉantōṉṟiya piṉṉar mūṉṟāmāṇṭi lorunāḷ, kiṇkiṇi olippat tantaipiṉ ceṉṟu - cataṅkai oli ceyyat tam tantaiyiṉpiṉṉē pōy, taṇ kaḻunīrum cen tāmaraiyum oruṅku talaimayaṅkiya perum taṭamaruṅkiṉ - kuḷirnta kaḻunīr malarum civanta tāmaraimalarum oṉṟāykkalantu viḷaṅkiya akaṉṟa tirukkuḷattiṉ karaippakkattil, arumpeṟal tantaiyait talaikkūṭiṉai - peṟalariya tantaiyiṉai etirppaṭṭuk kūṭiyaruḷiṉai.
`kiṇkiṇi īraṭukkolikkuṟippiṉāl vanta kāraṇap peyar.
`oruṅku talaimayaṅkuta lāvatu, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu pirivu tōṉṟāmaṟ kalattal.
taṭamaruṅku, karai; maruṅkiṉkaṇ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu maruṅki ṉeṉaccāriyainiṟka urupukeṭṭatu.
tantaiyeṉṟatu, īṇṭu iṟaivaṉai; tiruñāṉacampantarai murukappirāṉeṉ ṟuraittaliṉ, avarkkuc civapirāṉ tantai yeṉappaṭṭār; mēlum iṅṅaṉamē koḷka.
`talaikkūṭutal, eṉpatu ōriṭattu oruṅkukūṭutal.
(40-48) ataṟpuṟam ataṉpiṟaku, muruntuuṟaḻ veḷnakaip perumpeyar aṉṉai- mayil iṟakiṉaṭimuḷḷaippōlum veḷḷiya paṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya periya pukaḻmikka tiruvaruṭṭāyāṉa umaip pirāṭṭiyār, karumpumkaṉiyum perumcuvaippālum tiruntiyatēṉum oruṅkuṟakkūṭṭi - karuppañcāṟum paḻaccāṟum mikkacuvaiporuntiya āviṉpālun teḷintatēṉum oṉṟāy viravaccērttu, kuḻaittu eṭuttu aṉṉa - iḷakupatamāyk kāycciyeṭuttāṟpōla, viḻaivuaṟā marapiṉ mulaipoḻi amiḻtam vaḷḷattu ūṭṭa - vēṭkaitīrāta cuvait taṉmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tamatu tirumulaiyiṟ curantoḻukiya pālaip pirāṭṭiyār poṟkiṇṇattiṉāl ūṭṭa, pavaḷavāy  maṭuttut tivaḷ oḷi ciṟantu - tamatu pavaḷampōṉṟa cevvāyiṉāl uṭkoṇṭu acaiyum oḷiyāl mikuntu, paḻakuṟu tantaikku maḻaviṭai amarnta tantaiyait tāyoṭumkāṭṭi - paḻakutal poruntiya tantaiyārāṉa civapātavirutayarukku iḷaiya ēṟṟiṉmēl eḻuntaruḷivanta uriyatantaiyārāṉa civaperumāṉai ammaiyoṭuñ cuṭṭik kāṭṭiyaruḷi;
muruntu, paṟkaḷiṉ veṇmaikkuṅ kūrmaikkuñ ciṟumaikkum uvamai; iḵtivvāṟu uvamikkappaṭṭuvarutal, muruntēriḷanakai (tuyileḻup, 88) eṉṉum maṇimēkalai yāṉum, muruntēykku muṭpōleyiṟṟiṉāy eṉṉum ēlāti (8) yāṉum aṟiyappaṭum.
`peyar, iṅkup pukaḻ; perumpeyart talaittāṇ maṉperuñciṟappiṉ mānitikkiḻavaṉ eṉṉuñ cilappati kārattiṟ (16: 84) pōlaveṉpatu.
kuḻaitteṭuttalāvatu, kuḻaiyakkāycci yeṭuttaleṉka.
`vāymaṭuttal, uṇṇutal; matiyuṇaramakaḷeṉa vāmpal vāymaṭuppa veṉṉum paripāṭa laṭiyiṉum (78) ipporuṇmai teḷiyappaṭum.
`tivaḷoḷi, acaiyum oḷi; nilantivaḷa eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇi (3022) yāṟpeṟappaṭum.
civapātavirutayar paḻakuṟu tantaiyeṉappaṭṭār, avar ciṟitu kālam maṭṭumē tantaiyeṉa uṟavu payiṟaliṉ; aṭikaḷ civaperumāṉai vāḷāt tantaiyeṉak kuṟittal koṇṭu, avarē tiruñāṉa campantarkku urimait tantaiyārāvareṉpatu iṉituṇarappaṭum.
(48-51) piṉṟai - piṉpu, kāṇtaku - tiruviḷaiyāṭal - kāṇṭaṟkuttakka ceyaṟ karuñceyalkaḷai, māṇtaka - māṭcimaiyuṇṭāka, ceytiṟam aṟiyār uytiṟamnōkki - tavañceytaṟkuriya vakaikaḷait teriyātavarkaḷ uyyum vakaikaruti, palpakal iyaṟṟiyapiṉṉar - paṟpalanāṭkaḷ ceytaruḷiyapiṉ;
kāṇ, mutaṉilaittoḻiṟpeyar; tiruñāṉacampantar ceytaruḷiya tiruviḷaiyāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum aṟivāṉ aḷantukāṇṭaṟkuriya arumaiperumai vāyntaṉavā meṉṟaṟkuk `kāṇṭaku veṉa aṭaikoṭuttōtappaṭṭatu. kāṇṭaku ciṟappiṟ kaṇṇaki yeṉṉuñ cilappatika rattukku (2, 90) uraikārar aṭiyārkku nallār `kāṭcitakuñ ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya kaṇṇaki yeṉṟuraittatūum iṅku uṟṟunōkkaṟ pālatākum.
piḷḷaiyar ceyta tiruvaruṭceyalkaḷ piṟarkkellāñ ceyaṟkaruñ ceyalkaḷēyāyiṉum, avarkkavai eḷiyavātal paṟṟi, aṭikaḷ avaṟṟait `tiruviḷaiyāṭa leṉac cuṭṭi makiḻntār. avaiyāvaṉa; `poṟṟāḷam peṟṟamai, muttuccivikaiyum muttukkuṭaiyum muttucciṉṉaṅkaḷum peṟṟamai, kollimaḻavaṉ putalvikku muyalakanōy tīrttamai, kuḷirkāyccal nīkkiyaruḷiṉamai, muttuppantar niḻaṟṟap peṟṟamai, āyirampoṉ aṭaintamai, vaṇikaṉukkup pāmpiṉnañcu tīrttamai, vācitīrak kācu peṟṟamai, tirumaṟaikkatavu aṭaittaruḷiṉamai mutaliyaṉa. ivaṟṟai yellān tiruñāṉacampantarvaralāṟu kūṟun tiruntoṇṭar purāṇattuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.
`uytiṟam eṉṟaviṭattut tiṟam vakai eṉṉumporuṭṭu.
(51-53) muṉpaṭarntu - muṉviraintuceṉṟu, aruntamiḻ āynta perumtamiḻkkūṭal - ariya centamiḻc caṅka miruntu ārāynta periya tamiḻvaḷam vāynta maturaiyiṉkaṇ ṇirunta, kulacciṟai muṉivaṉ etirkoḷacceṉṟu - kulacciṟaiyeṉṉun tavamuṭaiyōṉ tammai etirkoṇṭaḻaikka aṅku eḻuntaruḷi;
muṟpaṭarntu, muṟceṉṟu; eṉṟatu, nāḷuṅkōḷum nalla vallaveṉa apparperumāṉ taṭuttun, tiruñāṉacampantar, tiruvaruṭṭuṇai iruppiṉ aṭiyārkku ellām nallaṉavē yāmeṉak kūṟip pāṇṭināṭu ceṉṟamai kuṟittateṉka.
kūṭaṉ mānakarkkuc centamiḻaṭaikoṭuttuc ciṟappikkumuṇmai `pīṭuyar centamiḻkkūṭa leṉpuḻi (24) yuraikkappaṭṭatu.
`tamiḻkkūṭa leṉṟu mīṇṭun tamiḻaṭaikoṭuttatu, tamiḻvaḷarccikkuk kūṭaṉmānakarē urittāyiruntamai valiyuṟuttaṟku. aṉṟiyun, tamiḻnāṭṭunakarkaḷuḷ ellāc ciṟappukaḷālun talaimai peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉamaiyāṉum cāṉṟōrkaḷāṉiyaṉṟa centamiḻp pāṭṭukaḷ nirampa uṭaimaiyāṉun, tiruñāṉacampantar tēvāram mutaliya tamiḻvētaṅkaḷ taṉmāṭṭē arunikaḻccikaḷ vāyilāy meyppikkappaṭṭamaiyāṉum `peruntamiḻkkūṭal eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu.
kulacciṟai muṉivaṉ, pāṇṭimaṉṉaṟku amaiccar.
(54-62) pālkaṭalpiṟanta cīrttiru ākaliṉ - tiruppāṟ kaṭaliltōṉṟiya pukaḻporuntiya tirumakaḷātaliṉ, irumulaikkuṭattiṉ oruvaḻi aṭakki - appāṟkaṭalirunta pāl muḻumaiyum taṉ iraṇṭu koṅkaikaḷeṉṉuṅ kuṭaṅkaḷil orumikka aṭakkivaittu, malaimakaḷ ātaliṉ mulaitaral curuṅka vaḷḷattu ēntiya cilamutu arunti āṉāvēṭkaiyiṉ varuvōṉ ivaṉeṉa mulaimukamtiṟantavaḻi - cīkāḻiyiṉkaṇ tirumulaippālūṭṭiya umaiyammaiyār malaiyaracaṉ putalviyākaliṉ avartam tirumulaikaḷiṟpāṟ curappukkuṟaiya avvāṟṟāl avaṟṟiṉkaṇ iruntavaraiyil oru poṟ kiṇṇattiṟ kaṟanteṭutta ciṟitāṉapālaip paruki ataṉāl aṭaṅkāta peruvēṭkaiyuṭaṉ varukiṉṟavaṉ ivaṉeṉṟu taṉmulaikaḷiṉ kāmpunuṉikaḷ vāytiṟanta viṭattu, vaḻippōkkiya pālkaṭal ūṭṭiya pavaḷac cevvāyt teṉṉavaṉ tēvikkum - attiṟantavaḻiyē yoḻukac ceytu tāṉ aṭakkivaittirunta pāṟkaṭalmuḻumaiyum ūṭṭiya pavaḷattai yotta civanta vāyitaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya pāṇṭiyaṉ maṉaikkiḻattiyārāṉa maṅkaiyarkkaraciyārukkum, poṉpōl putalvaṉ āyiṉai eṉpatu aṟiyatteruṭṭi - poṉṉaip pōṉṟa ariya putalvaṉāyiṉai yeṉpatai ellārum aṟiyumpaṭi teḷivittaruḷi.
tirumāliṉ kūṟāṉa aracaṉ ṟēviyākaliṉ, maṅkaiyark karaciyār `tiru veṉa uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭār.
pāṟkaṭaliṟ piṟantamaiyiṉ araciyārkkup pāl mikuntiruntateṉavum, malaiyakattup piṟantamaiyāṉ umaiammaiyārkku atu kuṟaintirunta teṉavum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟaintiruntamaiyāl ammaiyār tammakattirunta acciṟitaḷavu pāliṉaiyē vaḷḷattēnti yūṭṭiṉāreṉavum, avvāṟṟāl tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷaiyār pāluṇṇum vēṭkai taṇiyārāy avāmikkup pōntāreṉavum, atukaṇṭu tiru mulaippālniṟainta pāṇṭimātēviyār mulaimukantiṟantu tāṉē perukkeṭuttu vaḻintapālai avarkkuvēṇṭumaḷavum ūṭṭi vēṭkai taṇivittā reṉavum, ataṉāṟpiḷḷaiyār avvaraciyārkkum poṉpōlum putalvarāyiṉāreṉavum āciriyar tiṟampaṭak kaṟpittuk kūṟiṉamai nuṇmāṇuḻaipula mikka pulavaraṉaivarāṉum peritumpārāṭṭi makiḻaṟpālatākum. maṅkaiyarkkaraciyārkkut tirumulaicuranta uṇmai,
curanta tirumulaikkē tuyyaciva ñāṉañ
  curantuṇṭār; piḷḷaiyeṉac collac - curanta
  taṉamuṭaiyāḷ teṉpāṇṭi mātēvi tāḻnta
  maṉamuṭaiyāḷ aṉpirunta vāṟu
eṉṉun tirukkaḷiṟṟup paṭiyārālum (54) iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum.
`cillamutu ciṟitaḷavāṉa amuteṉaka; tēṉeṉap pāleṉac cillamir tūṟṟeṉa eṉṉuñ cīvakacintāmaṇi yiṉum (nāmaka, 163) iḵtipporuṭṭāy varutal aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.
`vēṭkaiyiṉ eṉṟaviṭattu, iṉ oṭupporuḷatu;
`poṉpōṟ putalvaṉ; eṉṟatu, poṉṉaippōṟ pōṟṟappaṭum putalvaṉeṉṟaṟku.
`ceṉṟu, teruṭṭi yeṉattoṭuttukkoḷka.
(63-65) vaḻutikoṇṭa muḻunōy tīra - pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭumāṟaṉ aṭainta nōy muḻumaiyun tīrumporuṭṭu, aṉṉai ūṭṭiya amiḻtai - umaiyammaiyār cīkāḻiyil uṇpitta tiruvaruṭpālai, iṉ uraic centamiḻ ākkit tantu - iṉiya coṟkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa centamiḻppāṭṭukaḷāka iyaṟṟikkoṭuttu, atukeṭuttu - annōy muḻumaiyum nīkkiyaruḷi,
`nōy eṉṟatu īṇṭu `veppunōy mutaliyavaṟṟaiyeṉka. camaṇkurumār tīrttaṟkeṉṟu pakuttuviṭappaṭṭa uṭampiṉ iṭappākattu nōyaiyum avar tīrkkamāṭṭāmaiyiṟ piḷḷaiyār tāmē tīrttaruḷiṉamaiyoṭu pāṇṭiyaṟkup piṟavitoṭṭē iruntakūṉaiyum nimirntiṭṭamaitōṉṟa, `muḻunōy tīra veṉṟār.
aruṭ karuttukaḷait tamiḻālicaittāreṉṟaṟku, `amiḻtait tamiḻākkittantu eṉṟār. atu, nōy.
(66-69) pāl paṭu pūtamum mēlpaṭuporuḷum  aintu kūṟṟiṟpaṭṭa mutaṟporuḷkaḷum avaṟṟiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa nuṇporuḷkaḷum, taṉ vaḻippaṭūum taṉmaiya ākaliṉ - taṉniṉaiviṉvaḻiyē naṭaipeṟum iyalpuṭaiyaṉa vākaiyāl, tāṉ piṟitu ākal vēṇṭiṉ - orukāl oruporuḷ piṟitoru poruḷāka māṟutalait tāṉ tiruvuḷat teṇṇiṉāl, talaimayaṅki - avaimuṉṉilaimai māṟi, tamtoḻil tirintu nantum eṉpatum - tamakku iyalpāka uḷḷa toḻilkaḷum māṟupaṭṭuk keṭum eṉpatum;
`pāṟpaṭu pūtameṉṟatu maṇ puṉal aṉal kāl vāṉ eṉṉum mutaṟporuḷkaḷ aintum aintu vakaiyākappirintu niṟṟalaiyuṇarttiṟṟu.
`mēṟpaṭu poru ḷeṉṟu vēṟu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, īṇṭup pūtameṉap paṭṭavai tūvā māyaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟi maṇmutalāka eṇṇappaṭṭa ipparupporuḷkaḷ aintumēyām eṉpatum, iṉimēṟpaṭuporuḷka ḷeṉappaṭuvaṉa ivvaimperum poruḷkaḷukkum vēṟākat tūyamāyaikkaṭṭōṉṟum vintuvum nātamum mutalāṉa nuṇporuḷkaḷāmeṉpatun tuṇiyappaṭum. tāṉ, iṟaivaṉ.
(70) iḻittuk koṭukkappaṭum poruḷkaḷuñ ceḻittut tōṉṟumeṉpatum;
iḻikkumeṉpatu ceyappāṭṭu viṉai.
(71) muṟaiyē nīrilum neruppilum vaittu orupāl         viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉai;
nīr, pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ vaikaiyāṟṟu nīr.
neruppu, tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷaiyār kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi      pāṇṭiyaṉ aracavaikkumuṉ oru tīkkuḻiyil vaḷarkkappaṭṭa tīyākum.
nikaḻnta muṟaiyē ivaṟṟai neruppiṉu nīriṉumeṉa māṟṟip poruḷuraippiṉumām.
tiṟam, īṇṭup pāl: pakkam.
nīriṉum neruppiṉu meṉpuḻic cāriyai niṟka urupu tokkatu. mēl varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum.
(72) kāyāta paṉaimarattilum elumpilum vaittu maṟṟorupāl viḷakkikkāṭṭiṉai;
`paṉai yeṉpatu tiruvōttūriṟ civaṉaṭiyāroruvar vaḷartta paṉaikaḷ; avai  kāyāta āṇpaṉaikaḷāy niṉṟaṉa.
`eṉpiṉu meṉappaṭuvatu pūmpāvaiyiṉelumpu.
nīriṉuneruppiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉai yorutiṟameṉpatu, `tantoḻiṟirintu nantutalai yeṉavum, paṉaiyiṉumeṉpiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉai maṟutiṟameṉṟatu `iḻikkumporuḷ ceḻittalai yeṉavum niraṉiṟai yākavaittup poruḷuraittukkoḷka.
pāṇṭimānāṭṭu maturaimānakarkkuc ceṉṟa piḷḷai yār, tamakku māṟāyniṉṟa camaṇkurumār virumpiyapaṭi coṟpōrilaṉṟi nikaḻccippōrilum velvāṉkaruti oru tēvārappāṭṭeḻutiya tiruvēṭṭai eriyiliṭa, atu ventaḻiyāmaṟ pacceṉṟiruntatu. aṅṅaṉamē piṉṉum avar, mikavumviraivāy ōṭukiṉṟa vaikaiyāṟṟu nīril maṟṟoru tēvārac centamiḻeḻutiya ēṭuiṭa, atu nīrōṭum vaḻiyē cellāmal etirēṟi nīraikkiḻittukkoṇṭu ceṉṟatu. `neruppiṉu nīriṉuṅ kāṭṭiya orutiṟam ivaiyām; ivaṟṟāl nīruntīyumeṉṉum mutaṟporuḷkaḷ talaimayaṅkit tantoḻil tirintu nantiṉamai kāṇka.
iṉip, pāṇṭināṭṭai viṭṭu nīṅkip piṟa tiruppati kaḷaiyum vaṇaṅkikkoṇṭuvanta tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷai yār, toṇṭai nāṭu pōntu tiruvōttūriṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷi yirunta ñāṉṟu, āṇṭirunta civaṉaṭiyāroruvar civa perumāṉukkāka vaḷarttuvanta paṉaimaraṅkaḷellām āṇpaṉaikaḷāy vaḷarntu kāyāvāy niṟpa, atukaṇṭu avvaṭiyāroṭu māṟupaṭṭa avvūrc camaṇmāntaraṉaivarum avarai atuvē ētuvākak koṇṭu iḻitturaittār; piḷḷaiyār aḵtaṟintu uṭaṉē oru tīntamiḻttiruppatikam ōti avvāṇpaṉaikaḷellāṅ kurumpaik kulaikaḷuṭaṉ peṇpaṉaikaḷāka māṟit tōṉṟumāṟu ceytār. piṉpu tirumayilāppūriṉ kaṇṇuñ civanēcareṉṉum meyyaṉpa roruvar pāmpukaṭittamaiyāl uyir nīṅkiya taṉ arumaip putalviyiṉ uṭampelumpukaḷai eṭuttuvaittuk koṇṭu tiruñāṉa campantariṉ nalvaravu nōkkiyiruppa, avarum aṅkeḻun taruḷiyakkāl annikaḻcciyaṟintu avvelumpu kaḷait tirukkōyiliṉ muṉṉark koṇarvittu, ariyatoru tiruppatikam pāṭip `pūmpāvāy eṉa aḻaittu attirumakaḷai eḻuppiyaruḷiṉār. `paṉaiyiṉum eṉpiṉuṅ kāṭṭiya maṟutiṟam ivaiyām. ivaṟṟāl, iḻikkappaṭṭu niṉṟa paṉaimaraṅkaḷum elumpukaḷuma piḷḷaiyāratu tiruvaruṇōkkattāṟ peṇ paṉaikaḷākavum pūmpāvaiyākavuñ ceḻittamai teḷiyappaṭum.
(72-76) iṉiyē - ataṉmēl, aṟivu oḷi koḷīi - aṟivu viḷakkattai ellāruṅ koḷḷavaittu, puraineṟip poyc camaṇmuṟiya nūṟi - kuṟṟamporuntiya muṟaimaiyiṉai yuṭaiya poyyāṉa camaṇmatattai iṭaiyaṟṟu viḻat tukaḷākki, meyttiṟam kiḷakkum meypporuḷ caivam meyvakaiviḷakki - meypporuḷ vakaikaḷaik kiḷantukūṟum meymmaiyaiyē poruḷākkoṇṭa caiva camayattiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷai meymmuṟaikaḷāl viḷakkikkāṭṭi, paraṅkuṉṟu amarntaṉai - tirupparaṅkuṉṟeṉṉun tiruppatiyiṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷi yiruntaṉai;
koḷīi, koḷḷacceytu; āvatu viḷaṅkacceytal;
`purai yeṉpatu, parupporuḷaṟiviṉārkku uṇmai pōl mēltōṉṟic, ciṟitu nuṇṇaṟivuṭaiyarāy uḷnōkku vārkkum aṅṅaṉam ētōruṇmaiyum uṭaiya taṉṟāyp purai paṭutalākum. eṉavē, atu `poyccamaṇ eṉappaṭṭateṉka.
`nūṟutal poṭipaṭuttal, iṭittal; tivākarattilum eḻuteḻiṉ māṭat tiṭaṉelā nūṟi yeṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai (uḻiñai, 26) yilum itaṟku ipporuḷkaḷ uṇmaikāṇka.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟu; tirumurukaṉatu āṟu paṭaivīṭukaḷu ḷoṉṟu; ēṉaiya tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭi (paḻaṉi), tiruvērakam (cuvāmi malai), tiruccīralaivāy (tiruccentūr), kuṉṟu tōṟāṭal, paḻamutircōlai yeṉpaṉavām.
innāṉku aruntiṟalnikaḻccikaḷun tiruvaruṭkōṭ pāṭṭāl iyaṟkaikku vēṟākavum nikaḻntamaiyāl, attiruvaruṭkōṭpāṭu vāyāta camaṇmatam ivvāṟṟāl tāṉē aḻintoḻiyā niṉṟateṉ ṟaṟintukoḷḷappaṭum.
uyiruṭaipporuḷkaḷ allāta nīriṉuneruppiṉuṅ kāṭṭiya tiṟattai `orutiṟa meṉṟu muṉṉōti uyiruṭaip poruḷkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiya ēṉaiyiraṇṭaṉaiyum maṟutiṟameṉap piṉṉōtiṉār āciriyar.
(76-81) atu peyarntu - piṉṉart tirupparaṅkuṉṟiṉiṉṟu nīṅki, iraṅkuvāl aruvi nirampattōṉṟum āviṉaṉ kuṭiyiṉ acaiyiṉai - olikkiṉṟa veḷḷiya aruvikaḷiṉ vīḻcci kuṉṟeṅkuṅkāṇappaṭun tiruvāviṉaṉ kuṭiyeṉṉun tiruppatikkaṇ iruntaṉai; ataṟpuṟam ērakattu eḻuntaṉai - ataṉpiṟaku tiruvērakameṉṉun tiruppatiyiṉkaṇ eḻun taruḷiṉai; nēritiṉ - nuṭpamāka, orumoḻivaitta uṭporuḷ virippat tiruvaḷar taṇikaiyiṉ amarntaṉai - ōmeṉṉum orumoḻi taṉkaṭpotintuvaitta uṭporuḷkaḷai virittu viḷakkumporuṭṭuc ciṟappu ōṅkukiṉṟa tiruttaṇikaimalaiyi lamarntaṉai.
`iraṅkutal, olittal, `iraṅkuvālaruvi yeṉa itaṉkaṇ varumāṟupōlavē, ilaṅkuveḷḷaruvi (maturaikkāñci, 299) yeṉavum, vālveḷḷaruvi (akanāṉūṟu, 308) yeṉavum paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil varutal kāṇka.
