(174-185) āṅku - avviṭattil, poḻinīṟu iṭṭu - aruḷ poḻikiṉṟa tirunīṟṟai iṭṭu, viḻimaṇi tāṅki - civapirāṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷ eṉappaṭuñ civamaṇikaḷ aṇintu, kāntaḷpōlac cēntoḷir aṅkaiyil civañāṉapōtam navaiyaṟac civaṇi - ceṅkāntaḷ malarkaḷpōlac civantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aḻakiya kaiyiṟ `civañāṉapōtam eṉṉum meykaṇṭanūlaik kaṟpāratu kuṟṟam nīṅkumpaṭi ēnti, paḻutuaṟu māṇavar kuḻuvoṭu cūḻa - kuṟṟamaṟṟa māṇākkarkaḷ ēṉait takutiyuḷḷa aṉpar kūṭṭattuṭaṉ cūḻntirukka, vāṉkatir maṇṭilam īṅku vantatupōl tiruvoṭu viḷaṅkum uruku poṉ tavicil - vāṉattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa oḷimaṇṭilamē iṅku iṟaṅkivantāṟ pōla mēṉmaiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa urukāniṉṟa poṉṉiṉ oḷivāynta irukkaiyil, kalmaṉam urukkum kāṇtaku kōlamoṭu - kallotta vaṉṉeñcaiyum urukkukiṉṟa kāṇṭaṟkuttakka tirukkōlattuṭaṉ, eṉmaṉam urukki eḻuntiṉitaruḷiya - ēḻaiyēṉatu uḷḷattaiyum urukki iṉiteḻun taruḷiya, cōmacuntara kuravaṉaik kāṇūu - cōmacuntara tēcikaṉaik kaṇṭu, ceykuvatu aṟiyēṉ meytaṭu māṟi nilampaṭavīḻntu kalaṅkiniṟpa - ceyvatu teriyēṉāy uṭampu vitirttu nilattiṟ porunta viḻuntu vaṇaṅki añci niṟpa;
`poḻinīṟu aruḷpoḻiyun tirunīṟu; viḻimaṇi, viḻiyākiya maṇi, āvatu `akkamaṇi yeṉpatu. `cērntu, civantu; itaṟkuc cemmai, mutaṉilai.
`navaiyaṟa veṉṟatu, `kuṟṟamnīṅka eṉṉum poruṭṭu; kuṟṟamāvatu malam māyai viṉai yeṉṉum mūṉṟaṉ cērkkai yāluyir kaṇ māṭṭu uṇṭāvatu; `navai kuṟṟameṉṉum poruṭ ṭātal, arunavai āṟṟutaliṉṟu eṉṉum nālaṭi yāriṟ (295) kāṇka.
`civañāṉapōtam eṉpatu `cuttāttuvita caiva cittāntam niṟuvum meykaṇṭanūl.
`civaṇal kaiyiṟ porunta vaittal; āvatu `ēntal eṉṉum poruṭṭām.
vāṉkatir maṇṭilam - cūriyamaṇṭilam, aḵtinnilattu iḻintāṟ pōl eḻuntaruḷiya cōmacuntara kuravaṉeṉakkūṭṭuka.
urukāniṉṟa poṉ oḷi mikutiyumuṭaimaiyiṉ poṉ miṉukkup pūciya tavicukku aḵtu aṭaimoḻiyāyiṟṟu.
`kaṉmaṉam `eṉmaṉam eṉpavaṟṟil iḻivu ciṟappummai tokkatu. kāṇūu, ceyyūveṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉai yeccam; aṟiyēṉ, muṟṟeccam.
(186-192) yāraiyō eṅkaḷ aṟumukattu aiyaṉ tanta tōr aruḷiṉ vantaṉaipōlum - nī yār eṅkaḷ āṟumukak kaṭavuḷ aḷittatō raruḷiṉāl iṅku vantaṉaipōlum, nallai nallai - nī naṉṉilaiyuṭaiyai naṉṉilaiyuṭaiyai, ellaiyil kālam aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ nilaiyiṉai - aḷavupaṭāta kālam aḻiyāppēṟṟuṭaṉ nilaiyāka vāḻvai; viḻumiyai - ciṟap puṭaiyai; cevviyai periyai auviyam ilai - cemmaiyuṭaiyai perumaiyuṭaiyai aḻukkāṟu mutaliya tīya iyalpukaḷilai, eṉṟu iṉiyaṉa palavum paṉivarak kiḷantu - eṉa iṉiyavāṉa coṟkaḷpalavum uḷḷaṅ kuḷirumpaṭi iṉimai yāyc colli, taṉ malarkkai eṉ puṉtalaic cērtti - tamatu malaraṉaiya tirukkaiyai eṉatu eḷiya talaimēl vaittu;
malarkkaiyait talaiyiṟ cērttal, taṉkaṇ niṟainta aruḷāṟṟalai māṇākkaṉukku uyttu avaṉṟaṉ uṭal uyirkaḷait tūymaiceytaṟ poruṭṭu āciriyaṉceyyum aruḷvayamāṉa aṟituyilmuṟai; itaṉaic camayanūlkaḷuḷ `tīkkai yeṉpa.
(192-207) pulappaṭa - viḷaṅkumpaṭiyāka, nīyē uṭampum poṟiyu kaṭampaṭukaruviyum aṟivum vaḷiyum allai - uyirākiya nī uṭampum aimpoṟiyum av vaimpoṟikaḷākiya kāṭṭu vaḻic cellum akakkaruvikaḷ nāṉkum aṟivum uyirppum allai; aṟivāyc ceṟiyum peṟṟiyai-  aṟiviyaṟkai yuṭaiyaiyāy yātoṉṟu paṟṟiṉ ataṉ vaṇṇamāy niṟkun taṉmaiyai; maṟṟuavai aṟiyāp paṇpiṉa - coṉṉa avaiyellām aṟiyāta taṉmaiyaṉa; aṉaiyaiyiṉum muṉpē paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇṇiṉ miṭaipaṭu mācupotinta nīrmaiyai - nī aṅṅaṉam aṟiviyaṟkai uṭaiyaiyāyiṉun toṉṟutoṭṭē paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇkaḷaippōṟ ceṟikiṉṟa aṟiyāmaiyākiya kaṟai mūṭiya iyalpiṉai, vīciya oḷip piḻampāki veḷippaṭu murukaṉ āṉātu curanta aruḷiṉaṉākaliṉ - āṉāl iyaṟkaiyē viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟivoḷip piḻampāy veḷippaṭu murukapperumāṉ oḻiyātu ūṟṟeṭukkum aruḷuṭaiyaṉātalāl, niṉṉaimaṟaitta tuṉṉiruḷ turantu taṉ aruṭpolivāl niṉ aṟivu koḷīi - niṉṉai iyaṟkaiyē maṟaitta ceṟinta malaviruḷai ōṭṭit taṉ aruḷviḷakkattāl niṉ aṟivai viḷakki, arukā iṉpam tarutaṟporuṭṭu - ammukattāl niṉakkuk kuṟaiyāta pēriṉpattait tarum poruṭṭu, meynilai irutti mācupōkkit taṉ nilai niṉakkut tantaṉaṉ - cemporuḷ kāṇum meyyuṇarviṉ nilaiyil niṉṉai irutti niṉatu malavaḻukkaik kaḻittut taṉatu tiruvaṭip pēriṉpanilaiyai niṉakkuk koṭuttaruḷiṉaṉ; kōṭi  - nī ataṉaip peṟṟukkoḷvāyāka; eṉṟu uṟuti kūṟi viṭuppat tēṟip paṉṉirukaiyaṉ muṉṉar eyti - eṉṟu uyirkkuṟuti payappaṉa aṟivuṟuttu viṭuppa avvāṟṟāl uḷḷanteḷivaṭaintu paṉṉiru tirukkaikaḷaiyuṭaiya murukappemmāṉṟirumuṉ aṭaintu;
pulappaṭak (192) kūṟi (206) eṉa iyaittuk koḷka.
kaṭampaṭal - neṟiccellal, kaṭam - kāṭṭuneṟi; kaṭampala kiṭanta kāṭuṭaṉkaḻintu (kāṭukāṇ, 90) eṉpatu cilappatikāram; aimpoṟikaḷiṉūṭu taṉvayamiṉṟic cellum maṉavaṟivu palavāṟṟālum alaikkappaṭutaliṉ aimpoṟikaḷ kāṭākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. vaḷi - uyirppu, mūccu.
`maṟṟavai yeṉṟaṉa; uṭampu aimpoṟi akakkaruvi vaḷi mutaliyaṉa. `mācu āṇavamalameṉṉum aḻukku; `vīciya oḷi vīciya; aḵtāvatu oḷiviḷaṅkiya veṉṟaṟku. `viṉaiyiṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya veṉpatu karuttu.
`tuṉiruḷ ceṟinta āṇava valliruḷ; āṇavamalam aṟiyāmai payattaliṉ, iruḷeṉappaṭuvata marapu; tuṉṉiruḷē eṉṉun tiruvācakattil (civapurāṇam) pōla. `turantu ōṭṭi; eṉṉuṭaiyiruḷai ēṟat turantum (kīrttit tiruvakaval) eṉṟu maṇivācakapperumāṉ ipporuḷilēyē aruḷic ceytal kāṇka; punāṉūṟṟilum (14) muṉpu turantu caman tāṅkavum eṉappōntatu.
`emnilai yeṉṟatu, ematu cērkkaiyil eṉṟaṟku. kōṭi, koḷ pakuti; tēṟi, kalaṅkiya uḷḷam teḷinteṉka.
ippakutiyiṟ caivacittānta nuṭpaṅkaḷ mika aḻakākat teḷittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(208-231) imaiyampūtta paṉikeḻu cuṉaiyil amaiyātu āṭiya umaitaru putalvōy - imaiya malaiyiṉkaṇ viḷaṅkiya kuḷircciporuntiya nīrūṟṟil tōṉṟi avāaṭaṅkāmal viḷaiyāṭiya umaippirāṭṭiyār tanta kuḻantāy, eriviḻittu imaikkum tirunutal tantaikku orumoḻiviritta teḷuṟum uṇarvōy - tīkkaṇ tiṟantu oḷiviṭum aḻakiya nutalaiyuṭaiya tantaiyākiya civaperumāṉukku ōmeṉum orumoḻip poruḷai virittaṟivuṟutta teḷivu mikka pēraṟivuṭaiyōy, vāṉōr uṟukaṇ tīrppa mēl ōr aivaṭivēlaik kaiyiṉil eṭuttōy - tēvarkaḷuṭaiya tuṉpaṅkaḷai nīkkumporuṭṭu muṉṉorukāl oppaṟṟa takaṭṭuvaṭiviṉai yuṭaiya kūriya vēlaik kaiyiṉil ēntiyōy, vēntaṉ tanta ēntu - eḻil celvikku varaipurai mārpam varaiyātu aḷittōy - tēvarkōṉ īnta mikka aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya putalviyākiya teyvayāṉaikku niṉatu malaipōṉṟa mārpāṟ ṟaḻuvum iṉpattai aḷavu paṭāmal utaviyōy, kuṉṟavar vaḷartta poṉtikaḻ vaḷḷikkuk koḻukompu ākiya viḻumiya tōḷōy - kuṉṟattuṟaivārāṉa kuṟavarkaḷ eṭuttu vaḷartta aḻaku viḷaṅkiya vaḷḷiyammaiyākiya pūṅkoṭi pōl vāḷukkuk koḻukompāyniṉṟa ciṟanta tōḷkaḷai yuṭaiyōy, arumtamiḻk kīraṉaip poruntik kāttōy - ariya tamiḻnalamvāynta nakkīratēvarai aṇukik kātta pemmāṉē, emmaṉōrkku eḷivanta cemmaiyāḷa - ēḻaiyēṉ pōṉṟārkku eḷitil eḻuntaruḷivanta cemmaiyuṭaiyōṉē, oruva - oru muḻu mutalōṉē, ciṟuva - iḷaiyōṉē, karumayil muruka - nīlamayilai ūrtiyāka uṭaiya murukapperumāṉē, maṟaimuṭitaṉṉil niṟai tiru uruviṉai- tirunāṉ maṟaikaḷiṉ muṭipil niṟaikiṉṟa ciṟanta uruvuṭaiyai, kaṇṇum uṇarvum katuvāp peṟṟiyai - kaṇṇuṅ karuttum aṇukip paṟṟaviyalāta peruntaṉmaiyuṭaiyai, aimperum pūtamum ākiniṉṟaṉai - maṇ mutalāṉa aimperum poruḷkaḷum puṇarntu niṉṟaṉai, ulakuniṉ uruvē - avvaim mutaṟporuḷkaḷiṉ kalavaiyāṉa ivvulakam niṉakku uruvāyuḷḷatu, oḷikaḷ niṉ viḻiyē - ñāyiṟun tiṅkaḷun tīyumāṉa oḷikaṉ niṉakkuk kaṇkaḷāyuḷḷaṉa; alakilāc camayattu avvavar tamakku palavēṟu uruvil nilavukai nīyē - eṇṇilāc camayaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya avvaccamayat tārkkum palavēṟu vakaiyāṉa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷil viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉai nīyē, āṟu niṉ mukam - āṟu niṉ tirumukamām, aintu niṉ vaṭivu - maṇmutalāka aimmutaṟ poruḷkaḷum niṉ uruvākum, nāṉku niṉmoḻi - tirunāṉmaṟaikaḷākiya nāṉkum niṉ tirumoḻikaḷākum; mūṉṟu niṉ kaṇ - mūṉṟu niṉ kaṇkaḷākum, iraṇṭu niṉ tuṇai - vaḷḷi teyvayāṉaiyeṉa iruvar niṉ vāḻkkaittuṇaivarāvar, oṉṟu niṉ vēl - oṉṟu niṉ vēlākum, maruṇṭa uṇarvoṭu māṭcimai eytā muruṭaṉēṟkum tiruvaruḷ tarukai - mayaṅkiya aṟivoṭu aruṭṭaṉmai aṭaiyamāṭṭāta muraṭṭuttaṉa muṭaiyēṟkun tiruvaruḷ tarukiṉṟaṉai;
`pūtta, iṅku `viḷaṅkiya veṉṉumporuṭṭu; mīṉāram pūtta viyaṉkaṅkai nantiya eṉṉum paripāṭaliṟ (16, 36) pōla. ciṟumakār nīril avāvaṭaṅkāmal viḷaiyāṭutal iyaṟkai yākaliṉ `amaiyātu āṭiya veṉṟār. `eri ākupeyarāl neṟṟikkaṇṇai uṇarttum; imaittal - oḷiviṭal, tivākaram. `orumoḻi, `ōm eṉpatu, aḵtu ākupeyarāy ataṉ poruḷai uṇarttiṟṟu; iḵtu eṇṇi yurukum kurunātaṉ eṉpāl uraitta ōrmoḻiyē eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum aruḷicceytaṉar. uṟukaṇ - tuṉpam; āvatu tēvarkaṭkuc cūraṉāl viḷainta tuṉpam eṉka.
aimmai - takaṭṭuvaṭivu, tivākaram.
vaṭivēl - vaṭittuk kūrmaiyākkiyavēl; murukappirāṉ kaiyatu vaṭikkappaṭṭa taṉṟāyiṉum, vēl eṉṉum peyarop pumai paṟṟi avvāṟaṭai koṭuttār; piḷḷaiyāratu kaivēl aruḷ vaṭiviṉatu; upāṅkamellān tāṉaruḷ eṉṟār civañāṉa cittiyārilum (1, 47). tēvarkaṭku vēntaṉ intiraṉ.
`vaḷḷi yeṉpatu `orukoṭi yeṉavum poruḷpaṭu mākaliṉ, atukaṇṭu aṭikaḷ `koḻukompu eṉat tanturaitta aḻaku iṅku niṉaivukūrntu makiḻaṟpālatākum. eṉavē `vaḷḷi yeṉṉuñ collukku `vaḷḷiyammai yeṉṉum pūṅkoṭi pōl vāḷuk keṉa uraikūṟi makiḻkaveṉpatu. teyvayāṉai ammaikku `mārpam aḷittōy eṉavum, vaḷḷiyammaikkuk koḻukompākiya tōḷōy eṉavum aruḷicceyta kuṟippu, teyvayāṉaiyaip pōlallāmal vaḷḷiyammaiyaik kaḷaviṉāl vēṭṭa vēṟupāṭṭuṇmai viḷaṅkavaittaṟ poruṭṭeṉka.
karumai, iṅku nīlaniṟam uṇarttum. maṟaimuṭi, vētattiṉ muṭinta karuttu. alaku - aḷavu. `uruviṉai eṉpatil `ai muṉṉilai vikuti; `nilavunai, nakaram peyariṭainilai; `tarukai eṉpuḻiyum atu. `mukamē mutalāṉavaṟṟil varum ēkāraṅkaḷ acai. maruṇṭa uṇarvuṭaiyār eytā māṭcimaiyāvatu iṟaivaṉatu aruṭ ṭaṉmaiyēyāku mākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam urai kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(231 - 235) eṉṟu yāṉ aṟi aḷavaiyiṉ ētta - eṉṟiṅṅaṉamellām yāṉ terintavaraiyil vaḻutta, tāṉ uvantu kuṟippu arum iṉpattup piṟakkiṉaṉ eṉai - apperumāṉ maṉamakiḻntu cuṭṭiyuṇartaṟkariya pēriṉpattil uyarttu vaittaṉaṉ eṉṉai, atumutal iṉpam ārntu aṅku iruttal allatu tuṉpam eṉpatu aṟiyēṉ - atumutalāka iṉpam nukarntu avaṉ ṟiruvaṭikkaṇ vāḻntirutta lallāmal tuṉpameṉpatu ciṟitum aṟiyēṉ;
tāṉ, perumāṉ; iṟaivaṉṟarum pēriṉpanilai iṉṉa teṉṟu kuṟittukkūṟal iyalātatākaliṉ, `kuṟipparum iṉpam eṉappaṭṭatu. eṉṟatu, avaṉ cuṭṭiyuṇarappaṭā ṉeṉpatu karutteṉka.
`piṟakkiṉaṉ, piṟaviṉai; `uyarttu vaittaṉaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ; `piṟaṅkal, uyartal; perumai piṟaṅkiṟ ṟulaku eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ (23) pōla; ārtal - nukartal.
(235-269) aṉpoṭu nīyum avaṉvayiṉ celiṉē pāy aruḷ oruvantam tarukuvaṉmaṉṟa - uḷḷaṉpoṭu nīyum ap perumāṉpāṟ ceṉṟāl avaṉṟaṉ perukiya tiruvaruḷai uṟutiyāy niṉakkut tantaruḷuvāṉ mikavum niṉṉai aṇuki, talaippeyal māriyiṉ maruvantu malaippaṭu nīttam - kārkālat tuvakkattiṟ peyyum peru maḻaiyiṉāṟ porunti malai mukaṭṭi luṇṭākum aruviveḷḷam, paim niṟap pīliyum cemniṟat tukirum vēḻa maruppum kāḻ akil tuṇiyum oruṅku varaṉṟi maruṅku viraiik kuṇṭu kaṇ paṭukaril maṇṭi niṟaintāṅku - pacumaiyāṉa niṟam vāynta mayiṟṟōkaiyum civanta niṟam vāynta pavaḷamum yāṉaikaḷiṉ veṇṇiṟamāṉa kompum akakkāḻ uḷḷa atiṟkaṭṭaikaḷiṉ tuṇṭum oṉṟāka vārikkoṇṭu ammalaiyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil viraintōṭi vīḻntu āḻamuṉa iṭattaiyuṭaiya perum paḷḷaṅkaḷil mikkuc ceṉṟu niṟaintāṟpōl, avalak kavalaiyum uvalaic cuvaiyum muraṇuṟu aṟivum tirukiya cerukkum mutalaṟap peyarttuc citaiyātu ōṭi aṉpiṉ niṟainta iṉpa veḷḷattu - vaṟumaiyāluṇṭāṉa kavalaiyum ulakap poy nukarcciyum māṟupaṭukiṉṟa tīya aṟivum muṟukiyacerukkum aṭiyōṭu illaiyāmpaṭi nīkki iṭaiyil aḻiyāmaṟ ceṉṟu aṉpiṉāl niṟainta iṉpameṉṉum veḷḷattiṉkaṇ, paḻiyāk kaṭumpoṭum viḻumitiṉ tuvaṉṟi ēḻulaku aḻiyiṉum aḻiyātu ūḻi ūḻiyum vāḻmaticiṟantē - paḻikkappaṭāta cuṟṟattāruṭaṉ ciṟappāy niṟaintiruntu ēḻulakam aḻintālum aḻiyāmal ūḻiūḻikkālamum ellā nalaṅkaḷum mikuntu vāḻvāyāka veṉpatu.
`nīyum eṉṟatu, etirppaṭṭa pulavarai; pāyaruḷ - perukiya tiruvaruḷ; oruvantam - uṟuti; iccol ipporuṭṭātal,
  veruvanta ceytoḻukum veṅkōla ṉāyiṉ
  oruvantam ollaik keṭum
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷāl (563) teḷivāyp peṟappaṭum. `maṉṟa teḷivupporuḷatu; maṉṟaveṉkiḷavi tēṟṟañ ceyyum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (col. 267). maruvaral - maruval; āvatu aṇukaleṉka. aṇaittal, kiṭṭal, poruntutal mutaliya vaṟṟiṟkellām itaṉaip potuc collāka uraippa; talaippeyal māriyiṉ maruvantu malaippaṭunīttam, eṉak kūṭṭuka.
pīli - mayiṟṟōkai; `pīlipey cākāṭum acciṟum eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ, 475. kuṇṭu - āḻam; paṭukar - paḷḷam, tivākaram.
tuṇi - tuṇṭu. varaṉṟal - vārutal. maṇṭi - mikkucceṉṟu. kaṭaṟpaṭai kuḷippamaṇṭi (puṟanāṉūṟu, 6) eṉpataṉuraiyaik kāṇka.
avalam - miṭi, vaṟumai; keṭuniṉṉavalam perum pāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, 38.
`uvalaiccuvai yeṉṟatu, iṅku ulakap poy nukarcciyai; uvalai - poy; uvalaiccamayaṅkaḷ ovvāta cāttiramām (tiruvācakam, tirutteḷḷēṇam, 17) eṉpuḻip pōla. poynukarcci yāvatu `poruḷallavaṟṟaip poru ḷeṉṟuṇarum (tirukkuṟaḷ, meyyuṇartal, 1). uṇarcci yāṉākum nukarcciyeṉka. iṅṅaṉam oṉṟu kiṭakka oṉṟāṉa nilaiyaṟṟa meyyallāc ciṟu nukarcci iṅku uvalaic cuvaiyeṉṉun toṭarāṟ cuṭṭiyuraikkappaṭṭamai peritum iṉpam payappatākum.
tiruvaruḷ nōkkattiṟku māṟupaṭṭa aṟivu  eṉṟaṟku `muraṇuṟu aṟivu eṉṟu aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭatu. tirukiya cerukku - mikutiyāṉa allatu muṟukiya cerukku, kaṭuṅkatir tirukaliṉ cilappatikāram, 12, 1.
kaṭumpu - cuṟṟattār, (puṟanāṉūṟu, 153) tuvaṉṟi - niṟain tiruntu. iḷaiyarum mutiyaruṅ kiḷaiyuṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi (perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, 268) eṉpataṉ uraiyaik kāṇka. (28)
29. kāppuk kaimmikkuk kāmam peruki yuraittal
  ciṟanta poruḷoṟṟic cevvē ḷivarai
  maṟaṅtiṅ kuyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - paṟantōṭit
  tiṇṭō ṭaḻuvuva miṉṟēṟ ceḻuvaraiyaik
  kaṇṭēṟik kīḻviḻuvaṅ kāṇ.
(i-ḷ) ciṟantaporuḷ - ēḻaiyēṉ uyirkkuc ciṟanta poruḷ, oṟṟic cevvēḷ - tiruvoṟṟinakaril eḻuntaruḷiyirā niṉṟa murukap perumāṉē yāvar, ivarai - ipperumāṉai, maṟantu iṅku uyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - maṟantu ivvulakil uyir vāḻamāṭṭōm, paṟantu ōṭittiṇtōḷ taḻuvuvam - viraintu ceṉṟu avaratu valiya tōḷait taḻuvuvōm, iṉṟēl - aṅṅaṉam atu kūṭātāyiṉ, ceḻuvaraiyaik kaṇṭu ēṟik kīḻviḻuvam - oru periya malaiyaik kaṇṭu ataṉ ucciyil ēṟik kīḻē uruṇṭu viḻuntu uyir tuṟappōm;
`ciṟantaporuḷ oṟṟic cevvēḷ ivarai maṟantu iṅku uyirvāḻa māṭṭōm eṉpataṉ karuttu,
  eṉṉi lāru meṉakkiṉi yārillai
  eṉṉi lummiṉi yāṉoru vaṉṉuḷaṉ
  eṉṉu ḷēyuyirp pāyppuṟam pōntupuk
  keṉṉu ḷēniṟku miṉṉampa rīcaṉē
eṉṉum apparaṭikaḷ tēvāra (tiruviṉṉampar, tirukkuṟun tokai, 1) aruṇmoḻik karuttōṭu ottuniṟṟal niṉaivu kūraṟ pāṟṟu.
`cevvēḷ civanta niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya vēḷ eṉa murukap pirāṉ mēl niṉṟatu; vēḷ - kāmaṉ; māṭṭōm, taḻuvuvam, viḻuvam eṉap paṉmaiyāka vantatu, talaivi taṉṉaiyotta ēṉaik kaṟpuṭai makaḷiraiyañ cērttuk kūṟiyavāṟām; allatu avaḷ taṉṉait taṉmaip paṉmaiyil vaittuk kūṟiṉa ḷeṉṟaluḷ oṉṟu. `paṟantu eṉṟatu, viraivuk kuṟippu; ceḻuvarai, vaḷamāṉa malai āvatu uyarntamalai yeṉṟaṟku; kāṇ, muṉṉilaiyacai.
itu, cevilittāy mutaliyōratu kāvaliṟ ciṟaip paṭṭuḷḷa talaivi kāmamperukit taṉatu kaṟpuṭaimai uraitta vāṟām. taṉṉilaimaiyaip piṟaraṟiyavoṭṭātapaṭi evvaḷavu aṭakkiṉālum taṉṉuḷḷa nikaḻcciyāl aḵtu aṭaṅkātu veḷippaṭutalē iṉaiya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ `kaimmikal eṉappaṭum. kaimmikal nalital cūḻcci vāḻttal (poruḷ, 260) eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāṉum ataṉ uraiyāṉum itu teḷiyappaṭum. (29)
30. aṭiyuṟaiyiṭutal
`aṭiyuṟai yiṭutalāvatu, tiruvaṭikkāṇikkai celuttu taleṉka.
kāṇap peṟātator kāṭciyai yēṉuṅ kacittavaṉpāṟ
  pēṇap peṟumuk karuviyiṟ kāṇpar piṟaṅkumoṟṟi
  vāṇap peruntakai yeṉpatu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkumaṉpāṟ
  pūṇap peṟuminta mummaṇik kōvaiyuṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē.
(i-ḷ) kāṇap peṟātatōr kāṭciyaiyēṉum - ēṉai evvāṟṟāṉuṅ kāṇṭaṟkiyalātatōr ariya tōṟṟa muṭaiyaiyē yāyiṉum, kacinta aṉpāl - nekiḻnta uḷḷaṉpiṉāl, pēṇappeṟum mukkaruviyiṉ kāṇpar - pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa niṉaivu coṟ ceyal eṉṉum mūṉṟu karuvikaḷālum niṉṉaik kāṇum peruntavamuṭaiyōr niṉṉaikkāṇpar, piṟaṅkum oṟṟi vāṇap peruntakai eṉpatu koṇṭu - viḷaṅkum tiruvoṟṟimānakaril tirukkōyil koṇṭuvāḻum pēraruṭṭaṉmaiyuṭaiya perumāṉē! eṉṉum ak karuttai uṭkoṇṭu, vaḻaṅkum aṉpāl - ēḻaiyēṉ tēvarīrukku aṭiyuṟaiyāka vaḻaṅkum uḷḷaṉpiṉāl, pūṇappeṟum inta mummaṇikkōvai uṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē - tēvarīr aṇiyappeṟum inta mummaṇikkōvai yeṉṉum nūl num aḻakiya tiruvaṭikaṭku eṉpatu.
pēṇal iṅkuppātukāttup pōṟṟutal eṉka; eṉṟatu, aṉpu neṟiyiṉiṉṟu piṟaḻātapaṭi kāttup pōṟṟutaleṉpatu, vāṇar, vāḻnar eṉpataṉ tirivu. `peruntakai yeṉṟatu, vaḻaṅkuvār evarāyiṉum avaratu aṉpiṉaiyuṉṉi ataṉai ēṟkum peruntaṉmaiyuṭaiyā reṉṟaṟku. `mummaṇikkōvai innūliṉ peyar.
cālavum ariyaiyāyiṉum aṉpiṉāl eḷiyaiyātal pōl im `mummaṇikkōvai yeṉṉum nūlai nī pūṇṭaruḷutal icaiyātēṉum, aṉpiṉāl vaḻaṅkutaliṉ nī ataṉai ēṟṟu aṇintaruḷutal kūṭum eṉṟu uraittaruḷiya vāṟām. aṉpiṟkoṉṟumē ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉ eḷiyaṉām uṇmai, yāvarāyiṉum aṉpa raṉṟi aṟiyoṇā malarccōtiyāṉ (tiruvācakam, ceṉṉip pattu, 1) eṉṉuñ centamiḻ aruḷuraiyāl iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum. (30)

tiruvoṟṟimurukar mummaṇikkōvai urai 
- muṟṟum -


poruḷaṭakkam
		pakkam
	iraṇṭām patippum ataṉ uraiyum mukavurai	3
1.	tiruvoṟṟi murukaṉ mummaṇikkōvai	9
2.	tiruvoṟṟi murukaṉ mummaṇikkōvai uraikāppuc ceyyuḷ	60	
3.	coṟporuṭ kuṟippukaḷ	290

iraṇṭām patippum ataṉ uraiyum
mukavurai

cālivākaṉa, 1820 ām āṇṭākiya viḷampi āṉittiṅkaḷ, 15-ām nāḷ (1898 , jūṉ, 27) emakkuṇṭāṉa koṭiya nōytīrtta murukappirāṉ ṟiruvaruḷai viyantu iyaṟṟiya ematu mummaṇikkōvai avaṉ ṟiruvaṭikkuc cāttappaṭṭa varalāṟu, innūliṉ mutaṟ patippu mukavuraiyil varaiyappaṭṭuḷatu.
mēṟcoṉṉa nōyāl tuṉpuṟṟanāḷil emakku āṇṭu, 21, mika iḷaiñaṉāyirunta kālam; appōtutāṉ yām ceṉṉaikkiṟittuvak kallūriyil tamiḻāciriyarāy amarntu māṇākkarkkut tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaṟpittu varalāṉatum. annāḷil yām paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya nūṟpayiṟciyil maṭṭuppaṭā viḻaivu koṇṭu karuttūṉṟi yiruntēm. ematu patiṉaintām āṇṭil muṟaippaṭit tuvaṅkiya tamiḻppayiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟ perumpālum nirampiya teṉṉalām, irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam. tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaiyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaiyum muḻutum neṭṭurucceytu muṭittēm; kalittokai, pattuppāṭṭu, cilappatikāram, nālaṭiyār mutaliya nūlkaḷiṟ perumpakutikaḷ aṅṅaṉamē neṭṭurucceytu muṭikkappaṭṭaṉa; civañāṉa pōtam, civañāṉacittiyār eṉṉum nūlkaḷiraṇṭum muḻumaiyum neṭṭurucceytu muṭikkappaṭṭaṉa; ivaiyēyaṉṟi, naṉṉūl virutti, tolkāppiyac cūttiravirutti, yāpparuṅkalakkārikai, iṟaiyaṉārakapporuḷurai, taṇṭiyalaṅkāram mutalāṉa nūlkaḷum muṉṉamē muḻumaiyum neṭṭurucceytu muṭikkap paṭṭaṉavākum. kallāṭam, cīvakacintāmaṇi, periyapurāṇam eṉṉum nūlkaḷiṉ coṟcuvai poruṭcuvaikaḷiṟ peritumūḻkiyiruntum avaṟṟilirunteṭuttup pāṭañceyta ceyyuṭkaḷ mikutiyāyillai; eṉṟālum, avaṟṟiṉ coṟ poruṇayaṅkaḷ ematuḷattil vērūṉṟi niṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭuniṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē, ceyyuḷum uraiyun taṉiccentamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutun tiṟam ematiḷamaip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppatāyiṟṟu; eḻutum tiṟam vāyppavē. avaikkaḷaṅkaḷiṟ pēcuntiṟamum ataṉaiyoṭṭi vāyppatāyiṟṟu. paḻantamiḻp payiṟciyāl emakkuṇṭāṉa coṟporuṇalaṅkaḷ attaṉaiyum immummaṇikkōvaiyiṉ kaṇ viḷaṅkikkiṭattal eḷitil aṟiyappaṭum. iññāṉṟait tamiḻmāṇavarum piṟarum innūlaik karuttūṉṟik kaṟparāyiṉ, avar paḻantamiḻnūṟ coṟporuṇayaṅkaḷai eḷitil uṇarn tiṉpuṟutaṟku iḵtoru vaḻikāṭṭiyāmeṉṉum nampikkaiyuṭaiyēm.
iṉi, emakkuvanta koṭunōy tīrtta murukappirāṉ ṟiruvaṭikku oru pāmālai toṭukka viḻainta ematu karuttu, annōy tīrnta oṉṟaraiyāṇṭukaṭkup piṟakutāṉ niṟai vēṟalāyiṟṟu. kallūriyil uyarvakuppu māṇavarkkut tamiḻ nūlkaṟpikkun toṭarnta muyaṟcikkiṭaiyiṭaiyē, avaikaḷiṟ coṟpoḻivukaḷ nikaḻttum muyaṟciyum, ematillattē pōntu tamiḻkaṟka viḻaivu mītūrnta māṇavarkku atu kaṟpikkum muyaṟciyum, aññāṉṟu naṭaipeṟṟa tamiḻveḷiyīṭu āṅkila veḷiyīṭukaṭkut tamiḻ āṅkilakkaṭṭuraikaḷ eḻutiviṭukkum muyaṟciyum piṟavum naṭaipeṟṟuvantaṉa. avaṟṟiṟ kiṭaiyē  em maṉaivi irumal nōyāl tuṉpuṟṟaṉar. yām appō tīṉṟa peṇmakavuñ cilantikkaṭṭikaḷāl neṭunāḷ tuṉpuṟṟatu; emakkappōtu kallūriyiṟkiṭaitta campaḷamō ciṟitātalāl ematu vāḻkakaiyuñ ceḻumaiyāka naṭaipeṟṟilatu. ākavē, ittaṉai muyaṟcikaṭkun tollaikaṭkum naṭuvē, innūṟpākkaḷ maṉavamaiti peṟṟa oḻivunēraṅkaḷil maṭṭumē toṭukkappaṭṭuvantaṉa. emmuṭaiya paḻaiyanāṭ kuṟippukaḷait tēṭippārttatil, immummaṇikkōvai muṭikkappaṭṭanāḷ cārvariyāṇṭu puraṭṭāci, 13 (1900, cepṭampar, 28) eṉpatu pulaṉāyiṟṟu; itaṉ 14āvatu akavaṟpā. 1900, pippiravari, 4il muṭikkappaṭṭateṉṉuṅ kuṟippu akappaṭṭamaiyāl, innūl tuvakkañceyyappaṭṭakālam. 1899 iṉ iṭaiyil, atāvatu ematu irupattumūṉṟām āṇṭiṉ tuvakkattiṉ kaṇṇatākal vēṇṭumeṉpatūum uyttuṇarappaṭukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, itaṟkuraiyeḻuta muyaṉṟa māṇavar palariṉ muyaṟciyiṟ pālacuntarampiḷḷai yeṉṉun tiru. iḷavaḻaka ṉāriṉ muyaṟciyē niṟaivēṟalāyiṟṟu. ivar paṉṉiraṇ ṭāṇṭukaṭku muṉ emmiṭan tamiḻnūl kaṟṟuvantakālat tilēyē, itaṟkuraiyeḻutak karutiya tamatu kuṟikkōḷait terivittu, itaṉai muṟaiyāka emmiṭam pāṭaṅkēṭṭu vantaṉar. yāñ collivanta uraikaḷaiyuṅ kuṟippukaḷaiyum avvappōtu kuṟittukkoṇṭu vantu piṉṉar avaikaḷai yellām ōroḻuṅkupaṭutteḻuti, eḻutiya kaiyeḻuttup paṭiyiṉai 1932. mārccu, 28-ām nāḷ emmiṭañ cērttu, ataṉai muḻutum pārttuttirutti acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭumpaṭi emmai vēṇṭik koṇṭaṉar. aṅṅaṉamē ataṉai muḻutuntirutti acciṭṭu muṭippataṟku eṭṭāṇṭukaṭku mēṟ ceṉṟaṉa. uraiyiṉkaṭ ceyyap paṭṭa tiruttaṅkaḷ pala: ākkiyōṉ karuttukku māṟāyk kāṇappaṭṭavaikaḷai ammāṟukōḷ nīkki iyaipu paṭuttiyatum; uraiyeḻutāmal viṭappaṭṭa cila pakutikaṭku uraivakuttatum; coṟporuḷ kaṭkup piṟkālattu nūlkaḷi liruntu eṭuttukkāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷkaḷai nīkki, muṟkālattuc centamiḻp paḻa nūlkaḷiliruntē mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṭṭiyatum; mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭappaṭātavaṟṟukku mēṟ kōḷkaḷ kāṭṭiyatum; ilakkaṇak kuṟippukaḷ pala māṟṟiyatum, pala putiyavāyc cērttatum; urainayaṅkaḷ pala māṟṟiyatum putiyavāyc cērttatum; coṟkaḷaiyuñ coṟṟoṭar kaḷaiyuñ centamiḻccuvai tuḷumpa māṟṟiyatum; iṉṉum ivaipōṉṟa nuṇṇiya tiruttaṅkaḷ pala uraineṭukac ceytatumākum.
oru nūlukku eḻutappaṭum urai annūlāciriyaṉ kālattē ceyyappaṭṭu avaṉālēyē muḻutuntiruttap paṭṭu amaiyu māṉāl, attakaiya uraiyē nūlāciriyaṉṟaṉ karuttaṟinta uraiyākum. apperunalam immummaṇik kōvaikku vāykkumāṟu murukappirāṉ ṟiruvaruḷ iyaittamaikaṇṭu itu taṉṉaip payilvarar mika makiḻaṟ pālār. ematiḷamaikkālattē iyaṟṟappaṭṭa icceyyuḷ nūl, nāṟpattōrāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ ippōturaiyuṭaṉ veḷippōntu tamiḻulakukkup payaṉpaṭu māṟaruḷ purinta murukappirāṉ ṟiruvaṭippōtukaṭku ematu pulliya vaṇakkam urittākuka!
eṭṭāṇṭukaṭku mēlāka innūliṉ uraiyiṉait tiruttuvatilum, itaṉai acciṭuvatilum emakkuṇṭāṉa uḻaippuc ciṟitaṉṟu, poruṭcelavō aiyāyira rūpāykku mēlāyiṟṟu. ipperuñcelavil orupakutikkup payaṉpaṭumāṟu, yāḻppāṇan tellippaḻaiyil tamiḻkkalaivalla nuṇṇaṟi viṉarāyttikaḻnta vaḻakkaṟiñarum em naṇparumāṉa tiruvāḷar tampaiyā piḷḷaiyavarkaḷiṉ iḷaiyaputalviyārāṉa tirumakaḷ civanāyakiyammaiyārum avartaṅkaṇavarum empāl meypporuḷ kēṭṭañāṉṟu emakku ainnūṟurūpā kāṇikkai celuttiṉar. avarkaḷ ellānalaṅkaḷuṭaṉum nīṭiṉitu vāḻkaveṉṟu ampalavāṇarammai tiruvaṭikaḷai vēṇṭutum.
pallāvaram, potu nilaikkaḻaka nilaiyam,
tiruvaḷḷuvar āṇṭu, 1972, 
mārkaḻi, 18 (1942, jaṉavari, 1)	maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ


ōm
tirucciṟṟampalam
tiruvoṟṟimurukar
mummaṇik kōvai

kāppu
nīrvaḷanaṉ ṟōṅkun tiruvoṟṟi nīṇakariṟ
cūrvaḷantiṉ vēlōṟkut tūyanūl - cīrvaḷara
emmā liyaṟṟuvatoṉ ṟuṇṭā latuvākuṅ
kaimmā mukattaṉ kaḻal.
nūl
1. neñcoṭukiḷattal
	nīrvaḷaṅ kaviṉṟa cīrvaḷar paḻaṉattu
	neṟkural kauviya naṟkuraṟ paiṅkiḷi
	kāṉaṟ kāviṟ ṟāṉvirain teytip
	poṉvī ñāḻaṟ polintiṉi tiruppavuṅ,
5	karuṅkaḻi vāriya perumīṉ kuvaii
	vaṉṟiṟaṟ paratavar muṉṟiṟoṟu muṇakkun
	tuṭikkaṭ ṭuṇiyal koṭikkulaṅ kavarntu
	marutaṅ kāviṟ peritu vaikavuṅ,
	kuḷaṉuṟai vāḻkkai vaḷanīrk kōḻi
10	maṉaiyuṟai nīṅkit tuṇaikūp peyarntu
	kālvaḻi yōṭik kaḻiyiṟ cēravum,
	pulavumaṇa muṉaiiya nilavuṟaḻ kuruku
	kaṇṇakaṉ vicumpil naṇṇiya mukiṉmēl
	ivarnteḻu tiṅkaḷ pōlap parantukeḻu
15	tuṇinīrt taṭattiṉ maṇamalar ārum
	irumputirit taṉṉa aṟaṉmarup perumai
	karumpuṟa maruṅki ṉoruṅki niṟpavum,
	poṟikeḻu vāḷai veṟipaṭap pāyntu
	muṭaikkaḻic cuṟavoṭu kaṟuvoṭu maṟiyavuṅ,
20	kāletirn tōṭuṅ kaḻikkayaṉ marutac
	cēloṭuṅ kūṭic cērntu tuḷḷavum,
	muḻuneṟi yaviḻkkuṅ koḻuṅkāl ōṭip
	poṉṉan tāmaraip polantukaḷ vārik
	kōḻi muṭṭai pōḻpaṭut tāṅku
25	veḷḷayirk kāṉaṟ koḷḷaiyiṉ vīcavum,
	veḷḷiveṇ ṭōṭṭut tāḻaiyi ṉaḷaiik
	kaiyarik koṇṭa kaṭaṟkā lōṭi
	nīṟumey pūtta nēriyōṉ pōlac
	centā maraikaḷ ciṟantiṉitu viḷaṅka
30.	veṇpoṭi vīcip paṇpoṭu mulāvavun,
	tēmoḻic cevvāyt tirumuka vuḻattiyar
	kāmaru koḻuñcuvai vāḻaiyiṉ paḻaṉum
	puṉcey payanta paṉṉiṟap payaṟum
	naṉcey payanta poṉṉiṟa maṇiyum
35	vaṭṭikaip peytu kaṭṭiya cumaṭṭiṉ
	eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṅ kaṭuttuyar pākkattuk
	kumiḻiṭai yukaḷum amaiviḻip parattiyar
	pakuttuṇar viṉṟi mikuttuk koṭutta
	taraḷamum mīṉum niralpaṭa aḷantukoṇ
40	ṭuvappoṭu peyarntutam mirukkai cēravum,
	marutamum neytalum mayaṅkiya marapiṉ
	ulaka mellām oruṅkuvan tuṟiṉun
	tolaiyā vaḷattoṭu nilavuṟu vaippiṉ
	oṟṟi mānakark kuṟṟakaṇ ṇeṉavum,
45	akkaṇi ṉuḷvaḷa rarumaṇi yeṉavum,
	ammaṇi yuḷḷuṟai pāvaiyai yeṉavum
	pāvaiyi ṉakattup paṇpupala kāṭṭi
	mēvi yāṅkatu puṭaipeyarn tiyaṅkat
	takka vāṟu mikkatu puriyum
50	ōriyal pillā āruyi reṉavum,
	iruvē ṟulakiṉum iraṇṭaṟa viravi
	aṟivun toḻilum neṟiyuṟa viḷakkum
	mātoru kūṟaṟ kōtiya mutalvaṉ,
	cellaluṟ ṟaḻuṅkiya mallaṟ ṟēvar
55	kuḻāmperi tuyya vaḻāvē lōccip
	pāṭil cūrukku vīṭu nalki
	vēntaṉ payanta kūntaṟ kumariyai
	maṇantula kaḷitta niṇantikaḻ vēlaṉ,
	aiyaṉ ceyyaṉ paṉṉiru kaiyaṉ,
60	vaṟiyē miṭumpai poṟipaṭut tetirum
	periyōṉ periya raṟiviṉuk kariyaṉ
	ṟiruvuru vorumaiyi ṉeḻuvit tarumaiyoṭu
	vaḻipā ṭiyaṟṟi viḻinī ruṟaippa
	aṉpuru vāki yiṉpuṟa laṟiyātu
65	puṉporuḷ kavarumeṉ miṉpurai maṉaṉē!
	varampaṟu māṟṟal nirampiya murukaṉ
	ṟāṉeḻun taruḷum vāṉkuṭi lāka
	niṉṉait tantaṉa ṉākap, piṉṉai
	maṇṭiri vākak kaṇṭaṉa cilavum
70	puṉaṟiri vākap pukuvaṉa cilavum
	aṉaṟiri vāka aṭaivaṉa cilavum
	vaḷiyiṭa ṉākat teḷivaṉa cilavum
	vicumpiṭaṉāka ucumpuṉa cilavum
	ōvā tiruppa mēvunai, yataṉāṟ
75	ṟuṉpamuṅ kavalaiyum niṉpuṟa ṉāka
	iṉpuṟa laṟiyā tiṭarppaṭu kuṉaiyē, yatāaṉ
	ṟuṟutuṇai yākac ceṟiyu meṉṉaiyum
	puṟcāyt tōṭum puṉalē pōla
	niṉvaḻip paṭīi yeṉṉuyirk kuyirām
80	aṟumukat taiyaṉai maruvaloṭ ṭātu
	vaṉmai ceytaniṉ puṉmaiyō peritē
	yiṉinī yiṅṅaṉa moḻiyātu, paṉimalark
	kaṭampu cūṭiya taṭantōṇ murukaṉ
	ṟiruvurup potiyu morukala ṉāka
85	viḻumitiṉ nīṭu vāḻmati koḻuviya
	nalaṅkeḻu naṟumpāl peyta
	polaṅkalam poliyum peṟṟiyā leṉavē.	(1)
2. aruḷiyalpuraittal
	peṟṟi yariya perumāṉ periyatiru
	voṟṟi nakarvanta voḷvēlāṉ - naṟṟavaruṅ
	kāṇā ariya kaḻalā ṉeṉatuḷattu
	nāṇā tamarntavā naṉṟu.	(2)
3. kāmamikka kaḻipaṭarkiḷavi
naṉṟaṉ ṟuṉakkut tiruvoṟṟi vēlarai nāṭiyiṭar
iṉṟiṅku nīkoḷḷa leṉṟurai yīraka līrṅkaḻikāṇ!
miṉṟaṅku noyciṟai vaṇṭiṉaṅ kāḷiṉi mēlemakku
niṉṟiṅku nīrceyyu naṉmaiyeṉ ṉōvoṉṟu nērvatiṉṟē.
4. tōḻi viravikkūṟal
	nēriṟai muṉkai vārolik kūnta
	larikaṭai yoḻukiya perumatar maḻaikkaṇ
	ilavuṟaḻ cevvāy nilavuṟaḻ tirunutaṟ
	koṭipurai maruṅkuṟ kaṭitikaḻ mēṉik
5	karumpiṉu miṉiyaveṉ ṉarumpeṟaṟ pāvāy!
	vaḻivaḻic ciṟakkumeṅ kaḻiperuṅ kulattiṟ
	pāṟkaṭaṟ ṟōṉṟiya cīrttiru vaṉaiyāy!
	valampuri yaṉṉaveṅ kulampuri kōmakaḷ
	ariti ṉīṉṟa perumut taṉaiyāy!
10	miṭiyaṟi yāveṅ kuṭiyeṉu muṟṟat
	taṉpunīr pāytti yiṉpuṟak kaicey
	tāyamu miyāṉuñ cīritiṉ vaḷarkkum
	naṟpayaṉ kaṉinta poṟcuṭark koṭiyē!
	nakkum pukkum mikkamakiḻ ceyyuñ
15	ceyiraṟa veḻutiya vuyirō viyamē!
	arumaiyiṉ mikuntavem perumutaṟ ṟantai
	yāṟṟā viruppoṭu mēṟṟip pārkkum
	viḷivaṟa viḷaṅkiya voḷimaṇi viḷakkē!
	niṉṉaru ṇāṭit tuṉṉi niṉṟiṅ
20	kaṭiyēṉ kū ṟu moṭiyāc ciṟumoḻi
	vaṟiteṉa voḻiyā taṟivukoḷal vēṇṭum,
	vayaluḻavar kuvaḷaikaṭṭu
	viyalvarampiṟ ṟaṭintiṭṭa
	kayaliṉiṭaip paṭaveṟiya
25	marutamakaḷ karuviḻipōṟ
	poruviṉṟit tiruviḷaṅkac,
	cennel vittiya poṉṉuṟaḻ vāṉmuḷai
	puriyaviḻat talaivirintu
	paiyatteṉṉap parantiruppat,
30	teḷḷoḷip paḷiṅki ṉuḷḷayir peytu
	pācilaiyuñ cētāmpaluṅ
	kaṭkuvaḷaiyum muṭṭāmaraiyum
	oruṅkupaṭa veḻuti maruṅkuvait tāṅku
	naṟpūṅ kayaṅkaḷ poṟpoṭu mimaippat
35	taṇṇaṭai maruviya taṇiyā viḷaiyuḷ
	oṟṟimā nakariṉ muṟpaṭa vamarnta
	uṭkuṭai murukaṉaik kaṭkaṇ ṭāṅkup,
	pārttāṉāp payilvaṭiviṉ
	ōrttāṉā vuraṉuṇarviṟ
40	collāṉāp palpukaḻiṟ
	ṟāṭoṭa vīḻnta kaiyaṉ, ṟōṭoṭu
	poṉñāṇ piṇitta villaṉ kaṇaiyaṉ,
	miṉñāṇ piṇitta kuñciya ṉiḷaiñaṉ,
	ariyaṉ periyaṉ ṟiruvaḷar celvaṉ,
45	kuṭiyiṉuṅ kulattiṉum vaṭuvoṉ ṟillāṉ,
	peṟuvatoṉ ṟuṭaiyaṉ pōla maṟuvantu
	paṉṉā ḷeṉiṉumeṉ coṉṉilai tavaṟāṉ,
	kuṟittatu kiḷavātu ceṟittaluñ ceṟippaṉ,
	urumum uḷiyamum aravum piriyā
50	āratar nīntic cīritiṟ pōntu
	tiritaru nāḷkaḷum palavē, yorunāḷnam
	malaiyakaṉ cāraṟ ṟalaimaiyoṭu poliyum
	ārā mattiṉī vārā yāka,
	orutaṉic ceṉṟēṉ parivoṭu pukuntu
55	pāṉīr vāviyiṉ mēvi niṟpa, avaṉumāṇ
	ṭeytiyeṉ ṉoṭuniṉ ṟaṉaṉē, noytiṟ
	ṟiraiporu karaiyiṟ puraiyiṉ ṟōṅkip
	pacuṅkuḻai kalittu nacainaṉai yarumpit
	teṉṟaloṭu pukunta vaṇṭicaip pāṇaṉ
60	iṉimaiyiṉ miḻaṟṟun taṉiyicaik kuvantu
	potiyaviḻ malariṉ naṟavuṇak koṭuttu,
	niṉaitoṟuñ cuvaikkum nīṉiṟak kaṉikaḷ
	ilaitoṟuṅ kuḻuma nalamikap payakkuṅ
	kōḻarai nāval kōṭṭi ṉukuppap,
65	pāliṭai viḻūum nīlamaṇi kaṭuppa
	āmiṭai viḻūu maruñcuvaik kaṉikaḷ
	aḷaiyiṭait tuñcu marumpeṭaik kaḷippa
	vēppiṉ koḻunaṉai yēykkum neṭuṅkaṭ
	kavaikkā lalavaṉ ṟāḷiṭai yiṭukki
70	yaṉpuṭaṉ ceṉṟāṅ karutta ṉōkki,
	eṉṉaiyu nōkkiṉaṉ aṉṉatum nōkkiṉaṉ,
	uykuva taṟiyāṉ pōlac ceykuva
	toṉṟuṅ kāṇā taṉṟuniṉ ṟaṉaṉē,
	nīkaṇ ṭaṉaiyēl neñcanek kuruki
75	yiṉṉuyir vāḻalai maṉṉē, koṉṉuṟu
	pēykaṇ ṭaṉaiya peṟṟiya ṉāki
	niṟaiyum aṟivum muṟaimuṟai cāyat
	tuṉṟunilai nīṅkā marampōṟ
	kaṉṟiya nōkkamoṭu niṉṟava ṉilaiyē.	(4)
5. tōḻimuṉṉuṟavuṇartal
nilaiyun tirintu niṟaiyuṅ kaṭantu
talaiyuṅ kaviḻnta takaiyaṇ- malainivanta
teṉṉap poliyum eyiloṟṟic cevvēṇmēṉ
maṉṉuṅ karuttuṭaiya mātu.	(5)
6. pirivāṟṟāta talaivikkut tōḻikūṟal
mātē peritum varuntuva teṉṉai maṉantirital
ētē yaṟiyiṟ koṭumaiyaṉ ṟōvaṉṉai yīrmpotumpiṟ
cūtēy tuṇaimulai yākan taḻuvit tuyarmikutta
pōtēr poḻiloṟṟic cevvēḷ varaiyap pukuntaṉaṉē
7. iṉanalaṉuraittal
	pukalaru nūḻaiyiṟ pōkkarum poruḷpōl
	ikalaṟu caivat tiṟañcelā neñciṉar;
	neṟippaṭu makkaṭ kaṟivu pētuṟukkuṅ
	kavalaiyiṟ piṟarai mayakkunar uvaliṭu
5	patukkaiyiṟ puṉporuḷ akattiṭu karaviṉar;
	puṉaimāṇ kōtai poruntac cūṭṭiyum
	viṉaimāṇ viḷaṅkiḻai vīṟupaṭat tiruttiyuṅ
	koḻuṅkayaṉ maḻaikkaṇ piṟaḻtoṟu maṉantirintu
	ceḻuntuvarc cevvāy muttaṅ koṇṭum
10	nacaipiṟak kiṭātu mulaitaḻīic cellum
	neñcamar kātalar piṉcelu maṭavār
	pañciṉ mellaṭi paitaluṟ ṟuḻappa
	uṟuttuk kiḻikkum paralkeḻi murampiṟ,
	puliyata ḷuṭukkai taii valikeḻu
15	mūvilai vaṭivē loruvayiṉ viḷaṅkak
	koṉṟainaṉai piṇaitta maṉṟalan toṭalai
	pavaḷamalai yuṟaḻum mārpiṟ ṟuvaḷa
	malaitaru maṭantai kuṟaṅkiṉmicai yiruntu
	valampaṭu kaiyāṟ perumpuṟaṅ kavaii
20	ulampaṭu tiraṭō ḷorumulai yoṟṟa
	naraiyē ṟūrnta puraicā ṟōṟṟam
	niṉaitoṟuṅ kuḻaiyu muravōrkku vaikaluṅ
	kēṭupuri neñciṟ pīṭilā vamaṇar,
	pīṭuyar centamiḻk kūṭaliṟ ṟōṉṟip
25	poypaṭu puṉporuḷ kāṉṟiruḷ parappi
	meytaru caiva naṉṉeṟi piḻaippa
	māṇā vaḻippaṭūuṅ kūṉpāṇ ṭiyaṉmutal
	āṟuceṉ mākkaḷaic cūṟaikoṇ ṭeṟintu
	peruntuya ruṟukkuṅ kālai, yaruntavam
30	niṟpeṟu poruḷiṟ paṟpaka lāṟṟi
	vēṇṭiya periyōrkku vēṇṭiyāṅ kaḷippak
	karukkuḻik kiṭakkun taṉmaiyai yaṉmaiyiṉ
	uruppeṟa varukunai pōla vavarakat
	toruperu māyam perukuṟac ceytu
35	puṟampeyarn teyti yaṟantaru perumaic
	caṇpaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉai mātō, piṉpu
	kiṇkiṇi yolippat tantaipiṟ ceṉṟu
	taṇkaḻu nīruñ centā maraiyum
	oruṅkutalai mayaṅkiya peruntaṭa maruṅkiṉ
40	arumpeṟaṟ ṟantaiyait talaikkūṭiṉaiyē, ataṟpuṟam
	muruntuṟaḻ veṇṇakaip perumpeya raṉṉai
	karumpuṅ kaṉiyum peruñcuvaip pālun
	tiruntiya tēṉum oruṅkuṟak kūṭṭik
	kuḻaitteṭut taṉṉa viḻaivaṟā marapiṉ
45	mulaipoḻi yamiḻtam vaḷḷat tūṭṭap
	pavaḷavāy maṭuttut tivaḷoḷi ciṟantu
	paḻakuṟu tantaikku maḻaviṭai yamarnta
	tantaiyait tāyoṭuṅ kāṭṭip, piṉṟaik
	kāṇṭaku tiruviḷai yāṭaṉ māṇṭakac
50	ceytiṟam aṟiyār uytiṟam nōkkip
	paṟpaka liyaṟṟiya piṉṉar muṟpaṭarn
	taruntami ḻāynta peruntamiḻk kūṭaṟ
	kulacciṟai muṉiva ṉetirkoḷac ceṉṟu
	pāṟkaṭaṟ piṟanta cīrttiru vākaliṉ
55	irumulaik kuṭattiṉ oruvaḻi yaṭakki,
	malaimaka ḷātaliṉ mulaitaral curuṅka
	vaḷḷat tēntiya cillamu tarunti
	āṉā vēṭkaiyiṉ varuvō ṉivaṉeṉa
	mulaimukan tiṟanta vaḻivaḻip pōkkip
60	pāṟkaṭa lūṭṭiya pavaḷac cevvāyt
	teṉṉavaṉ ṟēvikkum poṉpōṟ putalvaṉ
	āyiṉai yeṉpa taṟiyat teruṭṭi,
	vaḻuti koṇṭa muḻunōy tīra
	aṉṉai yūṭṭiya vamiḻtai yiṉṉuraic
65	centami ḻākkit tantatu keṭuttup,
	pāṟpaṭu pūtamum mēṟpaṭu poruḷun
	taṉvaḻipa paṭūun taṉmaiya vākaliṟ
	ṟāṉpiṟi tākal vēṇṭiṟ ṟalaimayaṅkit
	tantoḻil tirintu nantu meṉpatum,
70	iḻikkum poruḷuñ ceḻikku meṉpatum
	nīriṉum neruppiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉai yorutiṟam
	paṉaiyiṉum eṉpiṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉai maṟutiṟam, iṉiyē
	aṟivoḷi koḷīip puraineṟip poyccamaṇ
	muṟiya nūṟi meyttiṟaṅ kiḷakkum
75	meypporuṭ caivam meyvakai viḷakkip
	paraṅkuṉ ṟamarntaṉai mātē, atupeyarn
	tiraṅkuvā laruvi nirampat tōṉṟum
	āviṉaṉ kuṭiyiṉ  acaiyiṉai mātō, ataṟpuṟam
	ērakat teḻuntaṉai mātō, nēritiṉ
80	orumoḻi vaitta vuṭporuḷ virippat
	tiruvaḷar taṇikaiyi ṉamarntaṉai, ataṟpuṟam
	niṉkaḻaṟ kiṭanta arukā aṉpiṟ
	caivam vaḷarkkum meyvakai marapiṉ
	uṭalporu ḷāvi yuṉakkeṉa niṟuvi
85	niṟkuṟit teḻūum aṟkum iṉpattu
	niṉpeya rallatu piṟitoṉṟu navilā
	maṉperum pulavōṉ, ṟantaiyaik kāṭṭun
	tāyeṉap pōntu nāyiṉuṅ kaṭaiya
	eṉṉaiyu moruporuṭ paṭuttu niṉṉiyal
90	porutuṟaip pukutti yuraikku muravōṉ
	cōma cuntara tēcikap peyariṉ
	ēmuṟa vantōṟ karuḷak kāmuṟṟup
	poruḷuṭa lāvi poruḷpeṟa vaḻaṅki
	vēṇṭuḻi yellām virumpiniṉ ṟētta
95	avaṟkeḷi vanta tavapperun taṉmaiyai
	yoṟṟiyūrp pukuntu vaikiṉai, muṟṟavum
	niṉperun taṉmaiyai yaṟintu niṉṉaṭi
	yiravoṭu pakalum oruvātu vaṇaṅki
	nāttaḻu taḻuppa ētturai kiḷantu
100	neñcanek kuṭaintu meyvitir vitirppap
	peṟuvataṟ keḷiyēṉ ariye ṉāyiṉum
	avaṉoṭu keḻūuñ cārpiṉ ivaṇatu
	civaṇavum peṟukuve ṉaṉṟē, kuvaṭukeḻu
	peruniṟap poṉmalai yaṭainta
105	karuniṟak kākkaik kuṟuvatā leṉavē.	(7)
8. vaḷḷaṉmai kiḷattal
uṟuporuḷ kāṇā vuṇarvilār māṭṭup
peṟuporuḷ vēṇṭal piḻaiyāl - naṟuneñcē
yēntumulai vaḷḷi koḻunaṉ eḻiloṟṟip
pōntu kuṭiyirukkum pōtu.	(8)
9. talaimakaḷ  tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal
pōtāyntu veṇṇilavokkum poṭimaṇaṟ pūñciṟṟiloṉ
ṟōtāyañ cūḻa iḻaittāṭuṅ kālai yoruvaṭivēṟ
cūtāṉa kāḷai tiruvoṟṟi yīcar tuṇaipputalvaṉ
kātāruṅ kaṇṇi citaittunak kōṭik karantaṉaṉē	(9)
10. oppumaikūṟi aruṇilai vēṇṭal
	karavaṟu māntarkku viravuṟu paḻiyiṉ
	maṟuvoṭu viḷaṅku niṟaikatir matiyam
	veṇkatir virikkun taṇṇeṉ kālaip
	pulavumaṇaṅ kamaḻum pākkattu nalaṅkeḻu
5	teriviṉ mākkaḷ puritimil pukuntu
	nirainirai vakutta vakaiyamai viḷakkam
	vāṉa mīṉiṉ vayiṉvayi ṉimaippavuñ,
	ciṟutuṭi maruṅkiṟ karuṅkaṇ nuḷaicciyar
	eḻuvarum eṇmarum uḻiyuḻik kaipiṇain
10	toruvā marapiṟ kuravai yayaravum,
	avartaru ciṟāar ciṟumīṉ mukantu
	maṇalakaḻ kēṇiyiṉ muṟaimuṟai viṭuttum
	porikeḻu puṉṉaip pūntuṇar vīḻppap
	purivaḷai yeṟiyak kōṭu tākkip
15	palavē ṟuṭainta nalaṅkiḷar nittilaṅ
	koḻuviya naṉaiyeṉak kuñcip peytu
	vaḻīi vīḻak kaṇkaḷuḻpu peyaravuṅ,
	koḻumaṭa laviḻnta kuḻūukkoḷ kaitai
	vāṉcitar aḷāya tēṅkamaḻ āmpal
20	nīṟuṟu taḻalē pōlavum pālkeḻu
	kuṟunaṭaip putalvar tuvaritaḻ pōlavum,
	aṟiviṉ mākkaṭkum aṟivu pētuṟukkum
	puṉaiyāp poṟpiṉ muṟaimuṟai ciṟappavum,
	neytal cāṉṟa kaitaiyaṅ kāṉal
25	oṟṟiyū ramarnta veṟṟivēṟ kuricil!
	koṉṉoṉṟu kiḷakkuveṉ kēṇmati peruma!
	nīyē, āruyirt tokutikkup pēṟutaral vēṇṭi
	aintoḻil iyaṟṟuvai mātō, yāṉē
	oruneṟi yiṉṟip poṟivaḻi yōṭi
30	aimpulaṉ nukarntāṅ kamaikuve ṉaṉṟē,
	nīyē, makkaṇmēṟ koṇṭa poccami laṉpiṟ
	ṟāyē yaṉaiya vaḷiyiṉai, yāṉē
	maṉaiviyum makkaḷum poruḷeṉak koṇṭu
	niṉaivatoṉ ṟillāp pēraṉ piṉaṉē,
35	nīyē, yāṉaiveṇ maruppun tēṉiṉ iṟālum
	mayiṟṟaḻaip pīliyum viraikamaḻ cāntamum
	oruṅkutalai mayaṅkiya irumperuṅ kuṉṟat
	tāṉā tuṟaiyum iyalpiṉai, yāṉē
	pulamil kalviyum nalamil pukaḻum
40	vaḻuviya voḻukkamuṅ kaḻuvāk kuṟṟamum
	oruvaḻi yamaintu perukiya cerukkeṉuṅ
	kōṭukeḻu kuṉṟiṟ pīṭuṟu kuveṉē,
	nīyē, aṟalneṟit taṉṉa ceṟikuḻaṟ kaṟṟaiyum
	piṟaiceṟit taṉṉa naṟaikamaḻ nutaluṅ
45	kayaliṇait taṉṉa akaliru viḻiyuṅ
	kumikaimukiḻt taṉṉa amaivaru mūkkum
	paḷiṅkiṉ aṉṉa tuḷaṅkoḷik katuppum
	vaḷḷai yaṉṉa teḷḷoḷic ceviyuṅ
	kulikan tōytta ilavuṟaḻ itaḻum
50	muṟuval pūppa miṉṉeṉa miḷiruṅ
	kuṟumut taṉṉa ciṟumuḷ ḷeyiṟuṅ
	koḻunīrp paṇilattu viḻumiya kaḻuttum
	emakkaruṇ marapiṟ ṟamakkamai kaiyuñ
	civiṟivirit taṉṉa ciṟutiru vaṭiyum
55	emmuḷam nīṅkāc cemmaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟa
	vīṟukeḻu pakutiyiṉ vēṟiru maṭavār
	kūṟukoṇ ṭirupuṭai viḷaṅkac cīṟiya
	mayiṉmicai varukuvai maṉṉō, yāṉē
	karuneṟi maṟamum arukiya vaṟivum
60	virivuṟu mayakkamun tirukiya ciṉamun
	toṇṭarp paṇiyā nāṇamum eṇṭōṇ
	mukkaṭ perumāṉ perumpukaḻ nakkuc
	camaiyak kaṇakkar amaivila kūṟum
	pullurai koḷḷum pollāk kiḻamaiyum
65	vekuḷiyoṭu paṭūun takaiyiṉ māṟṟamum
	aṟivōr nakaippa maṭavōr tikaippap
	poruḷoṭu poruntāp pukuntucoṉ moḻiyum
	uṟuvaraip pēṇā teḻumiṟu māppum
	eḷivan tōrum iravaṉ mākkaḷum
70	aḻuviḷik kampalai koḷḷak kuḻaiviṉṟip
	paḻutuṟac ceṉṟāṅ keṟintunaku muruṭum
	aṟivoṭu paṭāac ciṟutoḻiṉ muṟaimaiyum
	nīṅkā marapiṉ ōṅkip poliya
	yāṉeṉa teṉṉuṅ kūṉaṟ kiḻattiyar
75	perumpuṟaṅ kavavi vayiṉvayiṉ amaittu
	maṟantēr vāḻkkai mayiṉmicaik koṇṭu
	ciṟantatōr tiṟattiṟ civaṇuve ṉaṉṟē,
	nīyē, kāntaḷaṅ kaṇṇi yēntiya mārpattuc
	ceñcāntu nīviya ciṟanta kōlamoṭu
80	kuṟavar makaḷir viḻavoṭu kuḻīi
	veṟiyayar kaḷattil viḷaṅkuvai, yāṉē
	mullaiyan toṭalai murukeḻa aṇintu
	veṇcāntu pūciya kēḻkiḷar mārpamoṭum
	iḻiciṉa makaḷir iyainta iṉpa
85	veṟiyāṭu kaḷattu veḷippaṭu kuveṉē, eṉavāṅku
	niṉṉoṭu meṉṉiṭaip paṭṭaviv viyaipāl
	eṉakkunī aruṭaral iyaiyun, taṉakku
	nikarā kunaroṭu nakaitaru kēṇmai	
	ceyalā kuvateṉa moḻikuvar, ataṉāl
90	nuntaiyum muṉikuvaṉ allaṉ aṉṉaiyum
	nuntai vaḻippaṭūun, takaiyaṇ, maintukeḻu
	poruppaip pōḻntu viruppoṭum etirnta
	arimā mukattaṉaip poripaṭa nūṟik
	kāḻuṟu marakkarai nūḻi lāṭṭip
95	poriyarai māvāy virikaṭaṉ mūṭiya
	cūrmutaṉ mutalaṟa murukkip pēraruḷ
	aḻivupaṭum uḷḷattup paḻiyoṭu piṟaṅkum
	ulaiyā vāḻkkait tēvarkkut
	talaimaiyoṭu nalkun tamiḻkiḻa vōyē.	(10)
11. pirivāṟṟāta talaivi matiyoṭu varuntal
kiḻamai niṉaiyāta kēḷmatiyē! oṟṟik
kuḻakaṉ pirinta kuṟippāṟ - poḻutaṟintu
cellumuyirp pētaikkut tīṅkiḻaippāy niṉṉaiyaṭum
vallaravaik kollā mayil.	(11)
12 . tōḻi paruvaṅkāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttal
mayiṉmī tivarntu tiruvoṟṟi yūrcceṉṟa maṉṉavartām
ayiṉmēl amarttakaṇ ṇāypirin tārallar aṉpumikkup
payiṉōṭu cērttiya kaṟpōṟ ṟuṇaiyaip payirntakuraṟ
kuyiṉmā voṭuṅkum poḻutumvan taṉṟiṉik kūṭuvarē.	(12)
13. aruḷnilai viyattal
	kūṭiya iruḷiṉ vāṭuva toṉṟō
	pīṭuyar niṉpukaḻ nāṭā toṉṟō
	peṟuva taṟiyāc ciṟumai yoṉṟō
	maruvarak kiṭanta terivil kālattup
5	poruvil iṉpan taruvatu kuṟittup
	puṉmuta lākiya meyyiṟ pukutti
	ōraṟi vuṟuttiṉai cilanāṭ, cilanāḷ
	nantumuta lākiya vuṭampiṟ pukuttut
	tantaṉai īruṇar vaṉṟē, cilanāṭ
10	citaṉmuta lākiya kurampaiyiṟ celīi
	mūvuṇar vuṇarttiṉai maṉṉō, cilanāḷ
	naṇṭumuta lākiya poṉṟuṭaṟ kaṭavi
	nāṉkaṟi vōṅkuṟac ceytaṉai, īṅku
	māvum mākkaḷum mēviya piṉṟai
15	aiyuṇar veṅkum meypeṟak koḷuvi
	oḻuṅkuṟa uṇarttiṉai yorucila pakalē,
	āṅṅaṉam oḻinta aḷavil kālattu
	niṟaivuṟu kuṟṟam muṟaimuṟai tēyap
	pakuttuṇar kāṭci mikuttut tōṉṟa
20	makkaḷ yākkaiyiṟ ṟakkavā niṟuvip
	palapiṟap puṟīiya nāḷkaḷum palavē,
	avvā ṟoḻintaṉa pōkac, cevviya
	innāḷ eṉakku naṉṉeṟi kāṭṭac
	cevvantip pōnta poyyā nāviṟ
25	centamiḻ varampu muntukaṇ ṭuṇarntu
	meypporu ḷaṟivuñ coṟporuḷ vaṉmaiyum
	poṟaiyuñ ceṟivum niṟaiyak koṇṭu
	tamiḻmā ṇākka ramiḻteṉa nukara
	viritturai kiḷakkun teripparum perumai
30	nārā yaṇaṉeṉun tōlāp pulavōṉ
	nērpaṭu muḷattuc cīrpaṭa vamarntu
	karuvinū luṇarttiṉai palavē, yataṟpiṉ
	oruvā yāṇar maruvuṟu ceṉṉait
	tavampeṟat tōṉṟi navaiyaṟu tamiḻum
35	vaṭanūṟ parappum nilaikaṇ ṭuṇarntāṅ
	kuḷḷuṟai karuvāyt teḷḷoḷi virikkuñ
	caiva cittānta muṭiporu ḷeṭuttīṅ
	kaṟaṅkarai nāviṟ ṟiṟampaṭa viḷakkip
	puṟampaṭu camayap poypporuḷ nūṟi
40	nalaṅkiḷar taṉmaiyum pulaṅkoḷāk kāṭciyum
	iyaintoruṅ kīṇṭiya amaiyāp perumaic
	cōma cuntarat tōmaṟu kuruvaṉ
	uṭalporu ḷāvi yiṭameṉak koṇṭu
	poruḷār caivan teruḷuṟak koḷīi
45	yataṟkamai poruḷum nīyeṉak kāṭṭit
	tiṟappaṭa vuṇarttiṉai mikavē, yataṟpiṉ
	toḻumpupūṇ ṭoḻukum aḻuṅkā vuḷḷattut
	toṇṭarum narakarum aṇṭarum piṟarum
	vaḻivaḻi yaṭimai perukuṟa veḻutiya
50	ōlai koṇṭa vorumara pāṉum
	ulakiṟ ciṟantutā ṉoṉṟē yākiyum
	alaki lāṟṟal talaivaru meṉpatu
	valloṟ ṟākkiya kuṟippi ṉāṉum
	oṟṟai yiyakkum uyirē pōla
55	neṟṟiyaṅ kaṇṇaṉ nilavuta lāṉum
	iṟaivaṉ aintoḻil niṟaiyuyir māṭṭup
	poruntac ceyyum aruntiṟa lāṉum
	aintoḻiṟ paṭṭa āruyirt tokaikaṭ
	kantaḻip poruttum mainti ṉāṉum
60	oṟṟiyū reṉumpeyar muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	perunaṉ mānakar tiruviṟ polintu,
	kuvaḷai pūtta pavaḷat tāmarai
	ceviramum paṇilamum puṭaipaṭa iṭaiyiṟ
	ṟōṉṟi yaṉṉa kāṇṭaku tirumukaṅ
65	karuneṟik kūntalum perukiya kaḻuttum
	mēluṅ kīḻun tōṉṟa vāliya
	naṟumut taṉṉa muṟuval ceṟitoṟum
	miṉṉeṉa miḷirum poṉmā mēṉi
	makaḷiroṭu viḷaṅkun takaicāl tōṟṟattu
70	vaṭivē lēntik kaṭimayi lūrnteṉ
	uḷḷat tāmarai viḷḷaṟvīṟ ṟiruntaṉai,
	pakaloḷi viḷakkum palkatir ñāyiṟu
	malainivan taṉṉa nilaiyuyar māṭattuṅ
	kuṭikaḷ taṅkāp paḻiyoṭum iṭintu
75	veruppēy cēra erukku muḷaittup
	pāḻpaṭu pollā villiṉum
	pōḻkatir virittup polivatā leṉavē.	(13)
14. talaimakaḷ pirivāṟṟātu pulampal
poliyum matiyum pulipōlat teṉṟal
naliyum namaṉāṉāṉ nalvēḷ - olitamiḻ
ōvā ticaikkun tiruvoṟṟi yoḷvēlāṉ
mēvāṉ ceyalaṟiyēṉ mēl	(14)
15. arumaiceytu ayarppat talaivi kūṟal
mēlāṉa muttē tiruvoṟṟi mēviya vēlavaṉē
kōlā kalaṅceytu kūṭāma lēkal kuṟaivukaṇṭāy
nūlā vuṭaikaṭṭi yēlāta kuṉṟiṉum nōṉmaimikuṅ
kōlā vuyarkuṇak kuṉṟiṉuñ ceṉṟa kuṟippaṟintē.	(15)
16. aruṇilaiyuraittal
	aṟivum avāvum oruvaḻic ceṉṟāṅ
	korutiṟa ṉiṉṟip palatiṟap paṭumē;
	kuykamaḻ kaṟiyum neykamaḻ tuvaiyum
	poṉṉiṟap puḻukkaluṅ kaṉṉalpey pālun
5	taṉṉikar kuḻampum paṇṇiya vakaiyum
	mikkeḻu cuvaiyiṉ mukkaṉi vakaiyuṅ
	kōḻarai vāḻaik kuruttakam virittup
	pālkeḻu taṉmaiyiṟ palavē ṟamaittu,
	muravai pōkiya muḻuveḷ ḷarici
10	kaivaṉ maṭaiyaṉ aṭutaliṉ neykaṉintu
	viraleṉa nimirnta avaiyaṉ meṉpatam
	mullai mukaiyeṉa mellitiṟ kuvaiip,
	puḻukkaluma piṟavum aḷāay viḻuttaka
	neyyuṭaṉ peytu meymmaṟan tuṇṭāṅ
15	kāṉā tuṟaivōr aḷavila raṉṟē;
	mukiṟoṭu kuṭumiyum muḻaikeḻu vāyum
	aruvi yeṉṉum aṟuvaiyum uṭīi,
	yantik kālattu ñāyiṟoṭu porutu
	cevvā ṉeṉṉum meykāl kurutiyuṅ
20	kaṇaitoṭu puṇṇiṉ mukuḷit taṉṉa
	pavaḷamuñ citarnta tivaḷoḷi keḻūum
	arakkaṉ aṉṉa poruppiṉ kāṭciyum,
	maṭamāṉ piṇaikaḷ kaṉṟoṭu kuḻīik
	kāṉyāṟ ṟoṇpuṉal nirainirai yaruntak
25	kāṉaṅ kōḻi tuṇaikūp peyarntu
	ñāyiṟu tōṉṟuṅ kālai yaṟivippa
	niṟaṅkeḻu tārāk kuṟuṅkayam maruṅkiṟ
	kuṟukuṟu naṭantu nīnta veṟipaṭu
	taḷavam arumpi eyiṟeṉa viḷaṅkak
30	koṉṟai poṉvī yukuppa maṉṟal
	kāvataṅ kamaḻuṅ kāṭukeḻu kāṭciyuṅ,
	koḻuvaḷai yīṉṟa ceḻunīr muttañ
	centā maraiyiṉ miḷirvaṉa maṅkaiyar
	tirumukaṅ kuṟuviyar poṭittaṉ māṉak
35	kuṭampurai ceruttaṟ ṟaṭaṅkaṇ mēti
	illuṟai kuḻakkaṉ ṟuḷḷutō ṟoḻukun
	tīmpāṉ māntip paruvarāl ukaḷa
	malipūm poykai marutak kāṭciyun;
	tēṟṟāp pulavar nūṟṟiṟaṅ kaṭuppa
40	eḻuvāy ōṅki muṟaimuṟai tēyntu
	karaicārn taviyun tiraiyē, karaitoṭuttu
	maṟukarai kāṇā takaṉṟunaṉi yāḻntu
	nallicaip pulavar ceyyuḷ pōlap
	palporuḷ ceṟitta kaṭalē, pollā
45	oḻukkan talaivanta paḻippuṭai yāḷarum
	viḻukkuṇa mēvuva reṉpatu nalattaka
	muḷḷuṭaik kuḻaiyiṭaik koḷḷaimaṇam aviḻttu
	vālitiṉ virinta kaitaiyaṅ kaḻiyē,
	miṉcitarn taṉṉa poṉcitar parappi
50	erikiḷarn taṉṉa viripū ñāḻaṟ
	poḻilē, yeṉumivai yeḻilpeṟat tōṉṟum
	neytaṟ kāṭciyuṅ kaṇṭu meyyeṉa
	aṟivu māḻāntu vāḻvōrum palarē;
	kāṇṭaku ciṟappiṟ pāṇṭiṉ mēlāl
55	aivakai yamaḷiyuṅ kūṟupaṭa vamaittuk
	kālkaḷain tāynta vāṉmukai mullai
	kaṭimaṇaṅ kamaḻat tūuy maṭipaṭu
	nuraimukan taṉṉa tūveḷ ḷaṟuvai
	ceṟivuṟa virittu naṟuṅkuṟa ṭuraitta
60	kēḻkiḷar tēyvaiyiṟ kōḻakil neyyum
	puḻukum māṉmatamuñ ceḻuṅkarup puramuṅ
	koḻumpaṉi nīrum vaḻuvaṟak kūṭṭi
	vēriyiṉ amaitta civiṟiyiṟ ṟōyttu
	nirainirai maṅkaiyar muṟaimuṟai yiraṭṭa
65	uṟaṅkum vāḻkkaiyiṟ piṟaṅkuvōr palarē;
	tannalam pakarum poṉvilai mātar
	tivaviyāḻ eḻīi navaināṉ kakaṟṟi
	miṭaṟṟeḻu mōcaiyum narampimi roliyum
	oruneṟic ceṉṟāṅ korunilai nikaḻap
70	puḷḷiṉan tuṇaimaṟan totuṅka vaḷḷiya
	kuḻaimukan tōṉṟi vaṟumaram uyirppa
	viḻumitiṉ eḻūum miṭaṟṟiṉ pāṭaluṅ
	koṉṟaiyaṅ kuḻaluṅ kuṉṟamar vēyun
	turuvi yūtum poruvi lōcaiyuñ
75	cevivāy māntic ciṟantōrum palarē;
	pittikaik koḻunaṉai muttār mullai
	naṟuvirait tāmarai tuvarkkāl vakuḷañ
	caṇpakaṅ kuvaḷai poṉmaṭaṟ ṟāḻai
	koḻuntum maruvuñ ceḻuntaṇ vēriyun
80	toṭalai yākkiyum viṭupūc citaṟiyuñ
	ceṇṭum pantuṅ koṇṭu viḷaiyāṭiyum
	naṟumporuḷ virāay meyyiṟ ṟimirntum
	vaṟitiṉ vāḻum makkaḷum palarē;
	aimpoṟi vaḻicceṉ ṟimpariṉ nukarum
85	aivakaic cuvaiyum orukāṟ kāṭṭum
	maiviḻi maṭavār poypaṭu miṉpattu
	meypaṭat tuḷaṅkum viḻalarum palarē,
	tāmun tuvvātu piṟarkkum īyātu
	veruvaru pēyeṉa uravōr ikaḻa
90	āṉā tarumporuḷ ivaṟik kūṭṭi
	māṇā vāḻkkai makkaḷuṅ kaḷvaruṅ
	kavarntu cella nimirntuvāṉ nōkkic
	ceyva taṟiyāk kaiyarum palarē;
	ivvā ṟoḻintaṉar pōkac cevviya
95	meypporuḷ uṇartumeṉ ṟoppuṭaṉ pukuntu
	kaṭavuṭ ṭaṉmaiyum uyiriṉ ṟaṉmaiyum
	puṭaipaṭa voṟṟi varampaḷan tuṇarātu
	mayaṅkak koṇṭu makkaḷait talaivareṉ
	ṟokkak kūṟi yuḻitaru vōrum,
100	aṟivāy aruḷāyc ceṟiyum oruporuḷ
	ciṟumaiyum maṟumaiyum eyti veṟuvitiṉ
	uyirum ulakamum ākum eṉpōruṅ,
	kaṭpulaṉ ākuva karuttiṉ ṟōṟṟamāy
	uṭpukun tuṇarvōrkkup poypporu ḷākaliṟ
105	kōyi leṉṉē! mēvi yāṅkuṟaiyun
	tūya pēroḷip piḻampeṉa leṉṉē!
	vañca māntarpiṟar neñcam piṇikkak
	kallaiyum maṇṇaiyum vallitiṟ poruttic
	cūḻcciyiṉ amaitta kīḻttiṟa maṉṟō
110	eṉṟurai kūṟuvōrum ākap
	poṉṟiya mākkaṇ maṇaliṉum palarē;
	āṅṅaṉam oḻinta aḷaviṉ māntaruḷ
	yāṉum oruva ṉāka nōṉātu
	vēṟu piritteṭuttuk kūṟupaṭu matiyiṉ
115	maṟaipukaḻ caivam niṟaivuṟa virittut
	teḷivār aḷavaiyiṉ viḷakki malaivutarum
	māṟuporu tōṭṭi vīṟuṟat tikaḻuñ
	cōma cuntara kuruvaṉoṭu kūṭṭi,
	iṉpam eṉpa taimpoṟi yāṉun
120	tuṉpamoṭu muraṇit tuyppatō vaṉṟē,
	mukiḻnakai mātar kuḻuvoṭu keḻīi
	itaḻcuvait tirukkun tiṟattatō vaṉṟē,
	oṉṟiṉum paṟṟātu taṉṟiṟat teḻūuṅ
	kuṉṟalil poruḷō vaṉṟē, toṇṭareṉum
125	vaṇṭiṉaṅ kuḻumik koṇṭi koḷḷumniṉ
	tiruvaṭit tāmarai yatuveṉa moḻivit
	teṉṉaiyum uyyak koṇṭu, piṉṉum
	maṟainavil antaṇar vaikaṟai yuṇarntu
	vāvi kuṭaintārkkun tīviya vōtaiyun,
130	tāmaraip paḷḷit tuyiluṇar puḷḷiṉam
	pārppoṭu keḻīic cilampum ōcaiyuñ,
	ciṟunutaṟ karuṅkaṭ kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir
	kuṅkumac cāntuṅ koṅkulāṅ kūntalum
	pālkeḻu taṉmaiyiṟ palavē ṟaḻīi
135	nīṉiṟa mukiliṉ maṟaiyum miṉpōl
	nīrmūḻ kāṭum pērvilārp poliyun,
	tiruviṉ celvi yarumaiyiṟ peṟṟa
	kuṟunaṭaic ciṟuvar ciṟutē ruruṭṭit
	tiṟalkeḻu maṟavar maṟpayil kaḻakattuk 
140	kaipuṭait tārkkum icaiyiṉuñ ciṟantu
	viṭṭuviṭ ṭicaikkum maṭṭil kampalaiyum
	ōvātu kaṟaṅkum oṟṟi mānakar
	tāvā iḷamai talaittalai ciṟappap
	paṉṉiru viḻiyeṉuṅ kaṇṇakaṉ cuṉaiyil
145	aruḷeṉum aruvi virivuṟap peruki
	muḻavut tōḷeṉuṅ koḻuviya kuṉṟiṟ
	paṉmukam parantu taṇṇeṉac cella,
	nittilam peyta viḻuttuvarc ceppiṉ
	mūytiṟan taṉṉa vāynakai tōṉṟa,
150	varaivilaṅki yōṭum miṉṉē pōla
	aramporu vaivēl uraṅkiṭan toḷirat,
	tirumāṉ mukattiṉ iruviḻi yeṉṉa
	maññaiyiṟ kiṭanta virutāḷ malara,
	aññaiyar iruvarum irumaruṅ kiruppa,
155	amaitta kaiyum aruṭaru kaiyum
	imaippa tillāt tēvarum piṟaruṅ
	kaṇṭukaṇ ṭuvappak kaviṉ ṟunaṉi viḷaṅkuṅ
	kāṇṭaku kōlan taḻīi māṇṭaka, eṉ
	kaṇṇetir polintaniṉ iṉṉaruḷ niṉaippiṉ
160	vayāvuṟu nōyum pārā tīṉṟa
	putalvōṟ kaṇṭu kaḷikkuñ
	citaivil tāyiṉ aruḷiṉum peritē.	(16)
17. neñcaṟivuṟuttal
tēvā tiruvoṟṟic cēvār putalvaeṉa
nāvāra vāḻtti naṭampurivāy! - āvā
viyantu piḻaiceyyum naṉṉeñcē vēlōṉ
nayantuṉ etirtōṉṟu nāḷ.	(17)
18. taṉṉuṭ kaiyā ṟeytiṭu kiḷavi
nāḷāṉ malarntu maṉakkiṉi yāṉceṉṟa nāḷaṟintu
vāḷār maṭaliṉ aḷiyēm uyirpōḻ vakaiyumaṉṟik
kōḷāṉa collip puṇarntār kariyuṅ kuṟippaṟiyāy
kēḷāṉa meyyum muṭaṅkumoṟ ṟikkaḻik kētakaiyē 
19. aruṇilai peṟuta lariteṉa moḻital
	kētakai paḻitta mātoru kūṟaṟku
	nutalkiḻit timaikkuṅ kataṅkeḻu viḻiyiṟ
	poṟiyeṉat tōṉṟi veṟikamaḻ cuṉaiyil
	aṟuvar ūṭṭiya naṟumpāṉ mānti
5	aṟuvē ṟuruviṉ viḷaiyāṭ ṭayarntāṅ
	kammaiyum appaṉum aṇaiyac cemmaiyiṉ
	ulaku purantaruḷum umaitiru maṭiyil
	aṟuvēr uruvum oruvaṭi vāki
	viḷaiyāṭ ṭamarnta iḷaiyōy! uḻaippirintu
10	naṟṟavar ōṅkum oṟṟi mānakar
	veṟṟi vaivēl orutiṟam poliyap
	paḷikkaṟaip pukunta oḷipparu matiyiṉ
	muṟaimuṟai miḷirnatu niṟaikaviṉ poḻiyūn
	tirumukac celviyar irupuṟam viḷaṅka
15	ulakelām viḻuṅkum alakilā vaṉṟiṟaṟ
	kaḷikkaru maññaiyiṉ nalappaṭa amarnteṉ
	viḻiyetir tōṉṟiya aḻiyāp periyōy!
	tīyiṭai niṉṟum, nīriṭai mūḻkiyum,
	maramē livarntu talaikīḻt toṅkiyum,
20	aṟuvakai yirukkaiyil neṟipaṭa iruntum
	puṟampaṭut tiḻuttāṅ kakampaṭa niṟaittut
	teḷinilai mukiḻkkum vaḷinilai yuḻaṉṟun, 
	taṉṟoḻil taputtut taṉimutaṟ poruḷiṉ
	aruḷeṉum veḷḷam varampiṟa mikuntu
25	nilaiyum iṭaṉeṉa ulakelām nōkkiyum,
	aimperum pūtamum aimpuṟam niṟīi
	aivakait tēvum āṅkāṅ kamaittup
	poymmuṟai yiṉṟi meymmuṟai paraviyuṅ,
	karuviyum pulaṉum puriviṉṟi nikaḻa
30	orumutaṟ poruḷoṭum aṟiviṉaik kaṭaviyum,
	iruvakai nilaiyum orumuṟai yiṟantu
	mēṉilai niṉṟu vāṉporu ḷuṇarntum
	ūḻi yūḻiyum ōviṉṟi nōṟpap,
	pōḻmatic caṭaiyōṉ pōtā ṉākak,
35	koḻuṅkuṟai taṉmeyiṟ palamuṟai taṭintum
	uṟuppiṉai yīrntu neṟippaṭa vīciyum,
	pacciḷaṅ kurutiyai nacci yukuttum,
	uyirppali vēḷviyiṟ kaṭaṉpala kaḻippi
	yaritiṉ muyaṉṟa piṉṟaiyum puricaṭaip
40	periyōṉ pōntila ṉāka, erikiḷar
	vēḷviyāṟṟum āḻkuḻiyiṉ
	vainnuṉaiya eḻuniṟīi
	muḻuvuṭampum paḻutupaṭap
	pōḻntu paṭuttup poṉṟat, tampiyar
45	pāyvataṟ kūkkiya kālai, vēypurai
	paṇaittōṭ pāvaiyun tāṉumāṅ keḻuntu
	vāṉavar talaivaṉ cīritiṉ aruḷiya
	varampeṟu perumaiyiṉ uramperi teytiniṉ
	cīṟaṭit tāmarai vēṟiṉṟi vaikum
50	aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ vaḻivaḻic ciṟanta
	cūrmutal pōlap pēraṉ pillēṉ;
	ālavāy amarnta aḻaṉiṟak kaṭavuḷ
	centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuniṉ ṟicaippak
	kaṭāviṭai nikaḻttun taṭāpperum pēṟum,
55	iṟaivaṉ kaṇṭa poruḷvaram paṟintu
	coṉṉeṟi māṭciyum poruṇeṟi māṭciyum
	aḷavaiyiṉ viḷaivun teḷivuṟa virittuc
	cuvaipeṟa vuraitta navaiyil pulamaiyum,
	maṟaipporuṭ kuṟippu neṟippaṭa āyntu
60	civaṉaiyē mutaleṉac civaṇiya kāṭciyuñ,
	cīritiṉ iyainta kīraṉ pōlac,
	coṟṟoṟuñ curakkum, aṟṟamil iṉpamum
	paṭṭāṅku kiḷakkum muṭṭaṟu ciṟappuṅ
	kaṉṉeñcu nekiḻttum aṉpuṟu moḻiyum
65	oruvaḻic ciṟappa varumuṟai koḷīi
	yāṟṟup paṭaicolum āṟṟalu millēṉ;
	āṅkataṉ karuvāy uḷḷuṟai nuṇporuḷ
	viḻumitiṉ eṭuttu vaḻuvaṟa amaittut
	toṇṭa rārat taṇṭātu koṭuttu, niṉ
70	ṟiruvaṭik kiṭanta perukiya aṉpiṉun
	tamiḻvaram puṇarnta kamaḻuṟum aṟiviṉum
	uraittiṟam nilaiyiṭum varaippaṭā viṟaliṉun
	tāṉē taṉakku nikareṉa viḷaṅkiya
	nacciṉārk kiṉiyaṉum allēṉ; niccalum
75	varukkaic cuḷaiyum porukkarai māvuṅ
	koḻuṅkaṉi vāḻaiyuñ ceḻuñcuvaik kaṉṉalum
	oruṅkutalai mayaṅkiya arumperuṅ kalavaiyiṉ
	aruñcol vaḻakkamun tiruntiya naṭaiyum
	vaṇṇa vēṟṟumaiyun taṇṇeṉum oḻukkamum
80	oṉṟu nirampiya kuṉṟāt tiruppukaḻ
	vēṟuvē ṟiyampiya vīṟuṟu tavattiṉ
	aruṇa kiriyeṉum periyaṉum allēṉ;
	nūliṭai vaitta vāliya poruḷiṉum
	uraiyiṭai virinta puraiyamai teḷiviṉuṅ
85	kēṭpōr uṇarap pāṟpaṭa vakuttu
	niṉpukaḻ aṉaittun taṉperu matiyiṉ
	uḷaṅkoḷa viḷakkum vaḷaṅkeḻu pulamai
	nārāyaṇaṉ eṉuṅ kuruvaṉu mallēṉ;
	kaḷaviyal taṉakkup palapaṭa viyampip
90	paḷakaṟu naṉporuḷ teḷiyā tuḻaṉṟa
	pulavōr kaḷippa nalamuṟat tōṉṟi
	meypporuḷ kāṭṭit tamiḻvaḻip paṭuttum,
	pullamaṇ mikuntu nalluṇar vaḻintu
	perumpeyar vaḻutiyum aruntamiḻk kūṭalum
95	uyvaḻi kāṇā tuḻitaru kālai
	oyyeṉap pōntu meynneṟi kāṭṭiyun,
	taṇaruḷ purinta niṉṉē pōlac,
	centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuṟa virittu
	muṭinilaic caivam viṭiñāyiṟeṉap
100	paiṅkatir virittuk kaṅkuliṟ kūmpiya
	viraiyaviḻ tāmarai puraiyura vōruḷam
	puriñekiḻn talarat tarumiyal ciṟappa
	uraiyiṉuṅ karuttiṉum varaiyamai nōkkiṉum
	poruntak kāṭṭi varuntiṟaṉ mikuttu
105	malaivupaṭu muḷḷat taṟivoṭu kūṭāc
	ciṟupuṉ māntar kuṟumoḻi kaḷaintu
	niṉpukaḻ virikkum aṉpiṟ ciṟanta
	cōma cuntara kuruvaṉu mallēṉ;
	poruḷperi tīṭṭap perukuṟum avāviṉum
110	īṭṭiya oṇporuḷ ivaṟik kūṭṭi
	makkaḷum maṉaiviyun tuykka nalki
	niṉkaḻal vaṇaṅkā iṉṉā maṭiyiṉum
	ariya nāṭkaḷai vaṟitē pōkki
	aṟantiṟam puḷiyiṟ ciṟantaṉeṉ maṉṉē;
115	iṉaiya tīyeṉniṉ niṉaivarun tiruvaṭi
	viḻinīr uṟaippa muḻumeyum paṉippa
	nirainirai vārā turainaṉi kuḻaṟap
	poṟivaḻi aṟivuñ ceṟiyum uṭkaruviyum
	palavaḻik kavarā toruvaḻi nikaḻa
120	aṉpuru vākuniṉ toṇṭar pōla
	nīṅkā aṉpiṉ nilainiṉ
	ṟiyāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō pāṅkupaṭa moḻimō!	(19)
20. tōḻi kiḷḷaiyait tūtuvēṇṭal
moḻiyuṅ kuḻaṟa muḻuvuṭampum paittu
viḻiyun tuyilkūṭā veyyōḷ - paḻiyait
tiruvoṟṟic cēyōṟ kuraiyāy! kiḷiyē
yuruvuṉṉai yoppa tuṇarntu.	(20)
21. aruṇilaiyuṇarttal
uṇarntār aṟiviṉun tōṉṟā toḷikkum orumurukaṉ
iṇarntār curikuḻaṉ maṅkaiya rōṭeṉ iruviḻiyiṟ
puṇarntā reyiloṟṟi mēviya vāṟu puṉiṟucellā
vaṇantāḻ kuḻaviyait tāyceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmaiyaṉṟē
22. eḻiṉalaṅ kūṟi neñcaṟivuṟuttal
	vaṇpuṉal nilaiyil taṇṇaṟal pōlavun
	tāmarai moytta vaṇṭiṉam pōlavum
	paḷiṅkiṟ ṟōynta nīṉiṟam pōlavun
	tirumuka maruṅkiṉ varumuṟai tuṇarntāṅ
5	kiṭaiyiṭai curintu nīṇṭu kaṭaikuḻaṉṟu
	kaṇṇoḷi katuvuṅ kuñciyai yaṉṉai
	irupāṟ kūṟiṭ ṭorupāṟ cūḻiyum, maṟ
	ṟorupāṟ paṉiccaiyun tiruvatiṉ amaippa
	naṟuṅkatup poḷirum peruñciṟap pāṉuñ,
10	cemmaṇip palakaiyiṉ ammaṇi kuyiṟṟi
	varampuṟa viṭutta vaṇṇam pōlap
	puruvakkoṭi paṭarnta virinuta lāṉum,
	annutal kiḷarnta poṉpōṟ cevviḻi
	utti eṉṉun tuttip paiyara
15	muṭimēṉ maṇiyeṉak kuṭikoḷa lāṉum,
	veṇpaṉit tiraḷaik kaṇṇuṟap pōḻntu
	tuvarniṟam ūṭṭic civaṇuṟa amaittāṅ
	kirupuṟaṅ katuppu maruvuta lāṉuṅ,
	kuḻaiyoṭu keḻumun taḻaicevi yiraṇṭum
20	ōveṉum vaṭivoṭu mēvuta lāṉum
	naṭuviṟ kuḻintu vaṭṭit tamainta
	paṭupoṟ kiṇṇam mukaṅkaviḻt taṉṉa
	mōvāy nivanta muṟaimai yāṉuṅ,
	kiḷaiyari nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku maṇaṟkīṉṟa
25	muḷaiyum muruntum viḷainīr muttamuṅ
	kūrntu niraintu valleṉa oḷivirin
	teḻuntoḷi reyiṟṟoṭu poruntuta lāṉuṅ,
	kovvaik kaṉiyuṅ koḻuviya pavaḻamuñ
	cevviya alleṉa ilavitaḻ puraiyuñ
30	cennīr itaḻoṭu maṉṉuta lāṉum,
	muttak kōvai muṟaimuṟai vaitta
	nattuvarc ceppu naṉkaṉan tiṟanteṉa
	arakkuru vūṭṭiya āmpal naṟumukai
	taḷainekiḻn talarnta taṉmait teṉṉa
35	muḷḷeyi ṟilaṅka nakutoṟum nakutoṟuñ
	cevvāy tōṉṟum avvāṟ ṟāṉum,
	eḻumiṭaṅ kuḻintu viḻumitiṉ oḻukit
	taḷavarum peṉṉa vaḷaṅkaṉin tilaṅki
	muṉṉeḻu mūkkiṉ ṟaṉmai yāṉum,
40	ōraruṭ kaṭaliṅ kīruru vākik
	karaipuṇarntu kiṭanta muṟaimai pōlap
	puruvakkoṭi kaṭavāp peruviḻi yāṉum,
	viraiyaviḻ tāmarai naṟaiyukut teṉṉak
	kēṭṭoṟum iṉikkum pāṭṭoṭu taḻīic
45	ceḻuvāy malarnta moḻiyi ṉāṉum,
	valampuri yaṉṉa vāṉaṟuṅ kaḻuttoṭum
	ēṟṟiṉat teruttiṉ murippeṭut taṉṉa
	kuvavip puṭaitta piṭari ṉāṉum,
	muḻaveṉac carintuval ulameṉat tiraṇṭu
50	varaiyeṉa nimirntupiṉ eḻuveṉat tiṇintu
	malaiyoṭu poruta mālkaḷi ṟeṉṟu
	pulavōr kūṟum poruṇmoḻi kāṭṭa
	yāṉai yeṉṉum pāṉaku tirumoḻi
	koṅkai maruppiṉ nuṅkak kutti
55	orupāṟ perumpōr viḷaippa, orupāl
	vaḷḷi yeṉṉuṅ kaḷḷaviḻ kuḻalāḷ
	taṭamulai vētiṟ paṭupuṇ oṟṟa
	uḷaṅkoṇ ṭeḻīi yuṟumiṭaṉ paṭāa
	torupuṟam oṟṟa varumuṟai nōkkik
60	koḻunaṉai piṇaitta marānaṟun toṭalai
	muṟuvalit teṉṉa muṟukkuṭain tacaiya
	vāḷvali nilaiyun tōḷi ṉāṉuṅ
	kaival vēkaṭi aituṟak kaṭaintu
	vaḻuvaḻup pākkiya koḻuntukirp palakai
65	puṇarntoruṅ kiraṇṭu kiṭantatu pōlak
	kaṇṇakaṉ ṟōṅkiya kaviṉkeḻu mārpat
	tiyāṉaimarup puḻuta cuvaṭu pōlavum
	varaiyoṭu paṭarnta valli pōlavum
	varimūṉ ṟoḻukiya tiruvi ṉāṉum,
70	maḻaiyum maraṉuṅ kuḻaintoruṅ kōṭa
	viḻumiya poruḷpala vēṇṭinark kutavun
	tāṭoṭu taṭakkaiyiṟ ṟāyuvan tītta
	vālitiṉ viḷaṅkum vēli ṉāṉum,
	pāṉurai mukanta paricoṭu porūun
75	tūveḷ ḷaṟuvai yacaii mūvakai
	maṭiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅki vaṭivuṭaṉ carintu
	naṭattoṟuṅ kuluṅkum vayiṟṟi ṉāṉum,
	eḻumiṭan tacaintupiṉ ṉoḻukutoṟum melintu
	maḻaikkaṭak kaḷiṟṟiṉ puḻaikkai yottuṅ
80	koḻuṅkaṉi vāḻaiyiṉ kōḻarai yēyppa
	ūṟum oḷiyum vīṟuṟat tikaḻum
	niṟaṅkeḻu marapiṟ kuṟaṅki ṉāṉum,
	eppāṟ poruḷun tuppuṟa vaikum
	nilaikkaḷa meṉpatu peyariṟ kāṭṭi
85	muḷaiyiḷa ñāyiṟṟiṟ ṟaḷaiñekiḻntu virinta
	eriyakai tāmarai pōla viripukaḻt
	toṇṭarka ḷetiriṟ kaṇṭiṭa malarntu
	nikaril iṉpa naṟaimuṟai yoḻukkip
	perumaiyoṭu vaikun tiruvaṭi yāṉum,
90	perumaṇal ekkarc ciṟukuṭi vāḻkkaik
	kuṟumoḻi mākkaḷ uṟumīṉ paṭuttum
	āḻtirai mūḻki vīḻmaṇi yeṭuttuṅ
	karuṅkaṭa lūrnta veḷvaḷai vāriyum
	nirainirai karaiyiṟ kuvaiyiṉar cella
95	maṟikaya lukaḷun tirumuka nuḷaicciyar,
	koṭicci tanta kaṭuppuṭai naṟavuṅ
	kuṭatti koṇarnta palappaṭu mutiraiyum
	uḻatti yeṭutta viḻuttaku paḻaṉum
	peṟuvilai yākac ciṟumī ṉoṭuttum,
100	iṭaiyiṭai muttam puṭaipuṭai yaḷantuṅ,
	kōṭu koṇṭu pīṭuṟa māṟiyum,
	orucār pēroli nikaḻtta, orucār
	paṉimalai nivanta paṇpu pōlac
	cēṇṭoṭa vōṅkiya vuppuk kuṉṟan
105	talaiyaḻit tumaṇar potiyuṟac cērttiya
	oḻukai yuruḷai muḻumaṇaṟ putaitaliṟ
	piṭarkoṭut teḻuppik kaṭumpaka ṭurappun
	tāḷāṇ mākkaḷ talaippaṭum uñaṟṟum,
	pulavukkaḻi muḷaitta tuvaritaḻt tāmarai
110	puṉṉai nuṇṭātu poṟippat tuṉṉiya
	uruvoṭu viḷaṅkun tōṟṟam eriyiṭai
	yurukiya pacumpoṉ kaviḻppa ōṭiya
	tiruvoṭum poliyuñ cevviyum maruviya
	neytalaṅ kāṉal aituṟak kiṭanta
115	oṟṟimā nakariṟ kaṟṟait tōkai
	mayiṉmē liruntu payiluta lāṉuṅ,
	kāṇṭoṟum iṉikkuṅ kāṭciyu muṭaiyaṉ
	uraippat tīrāc ciṟappu muṭaiyaṉ
	tammuyirk kiṉiya cemmoḻip putalvarai
120	nōkka nōkka mīkkiḷar kātal
	tantaiyark keḻutal pōlak kaṇṭiṭum
	viḻiyaḷa vamaiyāk kaḻiperuṅ kātal
	eytiṉark kuṟukkuñ ceytiyu muṭaiyaṉ,
	urukeḻu tōṟṟam maruvuva ṉeṉṟu
125	veruvara loḻitiyā liṉṟē, murukaṉ
	kuḻavik kōlattum viḻumiti ṉamarvaṉ
	maṭavaraṉ makaḷir taṭamulai kuḻaikkum
	iḷamaik kōlattum vaḷamaiyoṭu polivaṉ, ataṉāṉ
	miṉṉeṉat tōṉṟik koṉṉuṟa maṟaintum
130	vātuvaṉ naṭavāk kalimā pōlat
	tītuṟu neṟiyiṟ ṟiṟampaṭac ceṉṟum
	pākaṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉ paṇpu pōla
	uṟuti koḷḷāc ciṟumaiyiṉ mikuntum
	oruvaḻi nilaiyā tōṭip
135	palavaḻik kavarkkumeṉ ṉilaimaiyiṉ maṉaṉē.	(22)
23. aruṭara vēṇṭal
maṉṉu moruyāṉai yūṭa maṟuyāṉai
taṉṉutavi vaḷḷi taṭamulaikaḷ - tuṉṉuvippa
oṟṟi nakarvaikum vēlāy! oruperitō
paṟṟi yeṉaiyāḷ paricu.	(23)
24. tōḻi talaivi kuṟippaṟital
	paricu piṟitoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parumuttap paimpaṇaicūḻ
	viripuka ḻoṟṟi varuvēlar pōlum viṭaikkaḷiṟoṉ
	ṟaritiṟ peyarntiṅku vantatuṇ ṭōveṉpa raṅkucelvar
	teriviṟ periyar ceyalō ciṟiyareñ cēyiḻaiyē.	(24)
25. kaḻaṟṟetirmaṟai
	iḻainekiḻ paruvaral eytiya makaḷiriṉ
	maḻaittaṭan tōḷka ḷiḷaittut tōṉṟap,
	pāṟkaṭal pōlat tūttakap paranta
	nūṟpaṭu kēḷviyuñ cīrppaṭā toḻiyac,
5	ciṟuneṟic cellum aṟiyā māntarum
	nalvaḻic cellat teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭum
	iḻukkā naṭaiyum vaḻukkip pōka,
	innilai nīyun tiriyiṟ piṉṉoru
	pauvanīr vetumpiṉ vaḷāvunī ruṇṭō?
10	kuṉṟunilai tavaṟip pantupō luruḷiṟ
	ceṉṟuvaḻi yaṭaikkuṅ kuṉṟiyu muṇṭō?
	kaṭuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal toṭumaṭut tuṇṇiṉ
	muruṅkā tōmpum peruṅkala muṇṭō?
	eṉṟupala kūṟi niṉṟeṉai neruṅki
15	naṉmai nāṭiya eṉṉuyir naṇpā!
	maṟiyiḷaṅ kaṉṟu muṟiyuṇun tāyoṭu
	kuḻaipputal tōṟuṅ kuḻīi yukaḷa,
	varaiyāṭu varuṭai yiṭaivāy tāṇṭi
	yuḷḷañ cerukkit tuḷḷit tiritara,
20	nilantoṭṭu vīḻnta kuluṅkumayirk kavari
	meṉmela acaii mētakac cella,
	muḷavumā tolaicciya vaṉkaṭ kāṉavar
	ñelikōṟ pottiya neruppiṟ kāycca,
	ōri paranta tēṉiṟāl eṭumār
25	kaḻaikkaṇ kuṟaittu nalattaka iyaṟṟiya
	mālpuvait tiṭantoṟuṅ kuṟava rēṟat,
	tiṉaikkuṟu makaḷir cuvaippaṭa miḻaṟṟuṅ
	kiḷḷai yuṟaṅkum vaḷḷai yolippa,
	maruppiṭaic cuṟṟiya poruppuyar vēḻattup
30	puḻaikkai yēyppa viḻuttaka mutirnta
	tāḷoṭu vaḷainta kuraṟṟiṉaip puṉattup
	paṭukiḷi yōppum viṭukavaṇ cuḻaṟṟik
	kōlvaḷaik koṭicci mēlitaṇ amara,
	iruṅkal viyalaṟai varippat tāaya
35	nalamuṟu vēṅkai polamvī yukuppat,
	tēkkilai puytta puṉṟalaic ciṟāar
	mīkkiḷar kōṭṭiṟ paritiyiṟ ṟoṭutta
	tēttaṭai piṟaikkō ṭuḻuteṉak kīṇṭu
	māyōṉ mārpiṟ ceñcēṟu kaṭuppap
40	pāay iḻiyum paṭunaṟavu mukantu
	pacuntiṉaip piṇṭiyo ṭacumpuṟa aḷāy
	nirainirai vaittu viraivoṭum ārak,
	kaṇkaḷi koḷḷum paṉmalai yaṭukkattu
	maravamuṅ kuravamum porikeḻu puṉkum
45	erikiḷar ceyalaiyum viriyiṇar māvum
	parāraic cāntum pāvai ñāḻalum
	viritalai vāḻaiyum viyaṉciṉaip palavuṅ
	koṉṟaiyum puṉṉai yun tuṉṟiya naṟavamuṅ
	kuḻaimukan tōṟṟik koḻunaṉai yarumpip
50	puriyaviḻ malariṟ ciṟuvaṭuk kiḷarntu
	tīvaḷi yākit tīṅkaṉi tūṅkit
	taṇṇiḻal payantu kaṇkavar vaṉappiṉ
	mukilkaṇ ṇuṟaṅkat talaimaiyoṭu polimē; orupāṟ
	cuḷainirai yamainta koḻumpaḻan tūkkik
55	kuṟumala raviḻnta naṟuvirai teḷittu
	vīcukā lacaiyum pācilai narantamum,
	nalaṉuṟu makaḷi rilavitaḻ katuviya
	muṟuva laṉṉa ciṟuvitai peyta
	polaṉkuṭa meṭutta ulavai mātuḷaiyuṅ,
60	karumaṇi puraiyum orukaṉi nāvalum,
	uvarkkaḻi maṇakkun tuvarkkāl vakuḷamum,
	poṟṟiraḷ kaṭuppat teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum
	eḻiṟkaṉi paḻutta elumiccam putaluṅ,
	kāvataṅ kamaḻa mēvuva vaṉṟē; orupāṟ
65	kulikam ūṭṭiya talaimaiyiṟ ṟikaḻntu
	navvi nōkkiyar cevvāy pōlak
	koḻuṅkaṉi yuṭaiya kovvaik koṭiyuṅ
	kaṟpuṭai makaḷir muṟpaṭac cūṭum
	varicaiyiṉ mikunta paricuṭai mullaiyun,
70	tiṉpatu kallāp puṉṟalai manti
	kaṟittut tuḷḷuṅ kaṟivaḷar koṭiyum,
	aṇiṟpuṟaṅ kaṭukkum variyuṭaik koṭuṅkāy
	koḷḷaiyiṟ kāytta veḷḷarik koṭiyuñ,
	colluyar marapiṉ mellilaik koṭiyuñ,
75	ciṟupaḷik kuruṇṭai ceṟivuṟut taṉaiya
	tīṅkaṉik kulaikaḷ tūṅka vayiṉvayiṉ
	nantātu vaḷarum muntirik koṭiyum
	iṭaiyiṭai tāay miṭaiyumāl; orupāl
	naṭuviṟ, pāyoḷi matiyam pariti vemmaiyiṉ
80	iḷakip puṉalā yiḻintatu kaṭuppat
	teḷinīr vāviyoṉ ṟuḷatāl; maṟṟatu
	ciṟukāl tōṟum uṟunī roḻukki
	vālukam paranta kōliya pāttiyiṟ
	pacciḷam pullai niccalum vaḷarkkum, atā aṉ
85	ṟaṉṉac cēva laṇimayirp peṭaiyoṭum
	poṉṉan tāmaraip polintuvīṟ ṟiruppavum,
	nittilam payanta nattu vāṉmaṭuviṉ,
	mīṉiṉam vaḷaiiya nāṇmati pōlap
	pālkeḻu marapiṟ parantu cellavuṅ,
90	koḻuṅkayaṉ mātar viḻiyeṉa miḷiravuṅ,
	karuverin uṭaiya paruvarā lukaḷavum,
	muṟṟā mañcaṭ ciṟupuṟaṅ kaṭukkum
	iṟavu karaimaruṅkiṟ curintu tuḷḷavuñ,
	ciṟuvaḷi eṭuppac ciṟutirai yeḻīi
95	muṟaimuṟai yurāayk karaiyiṉaic cāravuñ,
	ceḻunīrk kuvaḷaiyuṅ kaḻunīrp pōtum
	muḻuneṟi āmpaluṅ kaḻiyavuṅ kuḻumik
	kāṇpōrp piṇikkum māṇpiṉa taṉṟē; āṅkup
	potiyac cāntan tataiya acaii
100	arumpaṟa virinta naṟumpū aḷaii
	viraiyun tātum niṟaiya mukantu
	talaittalai vīci nalattaka ulāan
	teṉṟalum eṉṟum ōvātu, ata aṉṟu
	kuṉṟik kaṇṇa kuyilkaḷ eṅkun
105	tuṉṟiya makiḻviṉ naṉṟukū vummē,
	āṭuvāṟ ciralkaḷ nāṭoṟuṅ kūṭi
	yōṭumī ṉarunti vīṭuṟā vaṉṟē,
	tukirkōt taṉṉa ukiruṭaic ciṟukāṟ
	puṟamarap potumpiṉ iṟaikū rummē,
110	tūṅkaṇaṅ kurīip pāṅkupaṭat teṟṟiya
	aruntoḻiṟ kuṭampaiyiṟ poruntu maṉṟē,
	paiñciṟaik kiḷḷai orukāl tūkkip
	puṉciṟu kiḷaiyiṟ kaṇṭuyil koḷumē,
	aṟuvaimaṭit taṉṉa paṟaikeḻu nārai
115	ciṟukāl tōṟum varumīṉ nōkkit
	tokuti yāka mikutiyoṭu vatimē,
	vaṇṭun tēṉum varik kaṭaip piracamum
	immeṉa muraṉṟunaṉmalar tōṟum,
	aḷinaṟa māntik kaḷikū rummē,
120	pūvāk kaṇṇa tōkai virittu
	muḷaiyiḷa ñāyiṟ ṟiḷaveyi leṟippap
	pīṭuṟu maññai yāṭuṟu maṉṟē,
	iṉṉuṅ kūṟal vēṇṭiṟ piṉṉumeṉ
	collaḷa vamaiyā mallalam potumparp
125	palperuñ ciṟappuṅ kaṇṭilai maṉṉō! aṉṟiyāṅ
	kaṉiccamēṟ paṭiṉun taṉittuyar uḻakkun
	taḻaimalarc cīṟaṭi kuḻaipaṭa naṭantu
	poḻiliṭam pukuntu pūntaṭa maruṅkiṉ
	viḻaitaka niṉṟa paḻutil pāvaiyai,
130	eṉṉuḷam eṉṉun taṉṉamar kiḻiyil
	vaḻuviṉ ṟeḻutiya eḻilō viyattai,
	yamiḻtupoti tuvarvāyk kiḷavicila miḻaṟṟum
	māniṟaṅ koṇṭa tūniṟak kiḷiyaik,
	kāṭuṟai vāḻkkait taṉṉiṉam pirintu
135	nāṭuṟai vāḻkkai naṉkaṉam maruviya
	kuṭamāṇ koṅkai maṭamāṉ piṇaiyaik,
	kiḷicciṟai yeṉṉum paḻippaṟu pacumpoṉ
	karaittīṇ ṭeḻutiya taraikkiḷar valliyaik,
	kāmak kaṭumpaci nāmuṟak kaḷaiyum
140	peṟutaṟ kariya uṟutuṇai maruntaik,
	kāṇṭal cellā aruvap poruḷeṉap
	pūṇṭōr pukaḻurai puraipaṭṭu oḻiya
	miṉṉeṉa miḷirnteṉ kaṇṇetir polinta
	karumpiṉum iṉikkumeṉ ṉarumpeṟa luyirai,
145	eṉṉuḷam maṉṉiya tuṉṉiruḷ nīṅka
	muṟpaṭak koḷuviya poṟcuṭark koḻuntaip,
	pulaṅkoḷak kāṇum poṟiyum ilaiyāl!
	āvaṇa oliyum aruṅkaṭa loliyum
	oṉṟutalai mayaṅki eṉṟum ōvā
150	oṟṟimā nakariṟ ṟaṟṟakap polinta
	paciyatōr maññai micaiyamar cevvēḷ,
	tēvarum mayaṅku mūvā māyam
	palamuṟai iyaṟṟiya talaimaitīr cūriṉ
	pēruram vacinta kūrvaṭi vēlaṉ
155	nōṉā vuḷḷattu nāṉmukaṉ ṟeḷiyat
	talaiyiṟ kuṭṭiya nalaṉuṟu kumaraṉ,
	viṇṇavark kariyaṉ, emmaṉōrk keḷiyaṉ,
	piṟaimuṭi putalvaṉ, maṟaimuṭi mutalvaṉ,
	umaitaru ciṟuvaṉ, emaiyaḷi yuṟuvaṉ,
160	paṉṉiru kaiyaṉ, eṉṉuyirk kaiyaṉ,
	tiruvaṭi niṉaiyā aṟivilar pōla
	eṉṉetir tōṉṟip paṉmuṟai kaḻaṟiṉai!
	īṅkitu viṭuttumaṟ ṟāṅkuc ceṉṟu
	tuṭiyiṭai orukai ūṉṟi vaṭiviḻi
165	ticaimukam parappi nacaipaṭap pārkkum
	iruṅkaṇ māṉpiṇai kāṇiṉ
	neruṅki niṉṟiṅ kuraiyalai yiṉiyē.	(25)
26. pirivāṟṟāta talaivi tōḻiyoṭu kūṟal
iṉiyā reṉakkiṅ kiṉiyā riyaintār
muṉiyār muṉintu moḻiyār - paṉimoḻiyāy
puḷḷoṉ ṟivarntu pukaḻoṟṟit tannakarkku
naḷḷiruṭkaṭ ceṉṟār namar.	(26)
27. neñcaṟi vuṟuttal
namarā yiruntupiṉ ētila rāvar nayamilarun
tamarāva reṉṉiṟ ṟamarpiṟa reṉṟu takavucolli
amarāṭi niṟṟal vaṟitukaṇ ṭāyalai yāḻiyoṟṟik
kumarāveṉ ṟōtik kuṭantaṅkoṇ ṭēttak kuṟikkoṇeñcē
28. pulavarāṟṟuppaṭai
	neñcunek kuṭaintu pañcukaṇ aṭaiyā
	uṭumpurit taṉṉa kaṭumpaci maruṅkuṟ
	paḻuppuṭai tōṉṟic ceḻippiṉṟi vaika
	vaṟumai yuḻanta uṟumaṉaik kiḻattiyoṭu
5	nāḷum nāḷum vāḷātu kalāyttu
	nōṉāp paciyiṉ āṉā taḻūum
	puṉṟalaik kuḻavi taṉṟiṟam nōkkik
	kaṉavukāṇ poḻutum naṉaveṉat tōṉṟum
	aruntuya reṉṟum peruntuya ruṟuppa
10	miṭikeḻu vāḻkkaiyiṟ kuṭiyā yiruntu
	maḻuṅkiya vuḷḷat toṭuṅkiya pulavōy!
	iyaṟṟamiḻ varampu tiṟappaṭa vāyntu
	pulaṅkoḷa nirampiya nalaṅkiḷar aṟivōy!
	vaḻuvoṉ ṟillā viḻumiya oḻukkam
15	uyiriṉum ōmpuñ ceyiraṟu periyōy!
	koḻuntamiḻ pōlak kuḷirmāṇ kuṇattōy!
	uḻunturuḷ aḷavaiyiṟ ceḻuntamiḻp pākkaḷ
	nūṟunū ṟiyaṟṟuṅ kūṟupaṭu matiyōy!
	oḷiccela vataṉiṉum vaḷiccela vataṉiṉum
20	neṟippaṭak kiḷakkum miṟaikkavi vallōy!
	aḷavainūṉ marapiṟ piṟaḻā tiyāṇṭuṅ
	koḷavurai nikaḻttum vaḷamuṟum uṇarvōy!
	poruḷiyal aṟintu maruḷaṟap puṉaintu
	kēṭpōrp piṇikkuṅ kāṭcicāl uravōy!
25	paṇṭaip piṟaviyiṟ ṟaṇṭā tāṟṟum
	peruntava muṭaiyai maṉṉē! poruntiyiṉ
	ṟīṅkeṉait talaippaṭ ṭaṉaiyē; ōṅki
	ūḻi celliṉum nīṭu vāḻmati!
	kīḻtticai eḻīi mēṟṟicaip paṭarun
30	tītteṟu ñāyiṟu vaṭaticaip puṇarntu
	teṉṟicai nōkkit ticaitaṭu māṟiṉum,
	vāṉak kaṭaliṉ mīṉeṉa vayaṅkum
	uṭunirai utirntu koṭumai yāyiṉum,
	ulavā vāḻkkaiyiṉ nilaimati ciṟantē!
35	vaṟumai kaḷaintu ciṟumai nīṅki
	naṉṉilai palavum iṉṉē peṟuti!
	varampaṟum iṉpam nirampak kōṭi!
	niṉṉuyirk kuṟuti nāṭit tuṉṉiniṉṟu
	nirainirai kūṟumeṉ uraipiḻai yalaiyē!
40	iṉṉuṅ kēṭṭal vēṇṭiṉ muṉṉumeṉ
	collaḷa vamaintu nalampala eytiṉar
	pallōr pallōr paṇṭum uṇṭāl;
	iṉiyoṉṟu kūṟuveṉ kēṇmati! paṉikeḻu
	mullaiyaṅ koṭikku mallañ ceḻuntēr
45	aruḷvara ītta periyaṉō ilaṉē!
	viḻiyāk kaṇṇa ceḻumayirt tōkaikku
	naṟumpaṭām vīciya uṟuvaṉum ilaṉē!
	pavaṉamā ulakiṉ navaiyaṟap peṟṟa
	amiḻtupoti nelli auvaik kītta
50	citaiyāp pēṟṟiṉ atikaṉum ilaṉē!
	kuṟunaṭaip puṟaviṉ ṟaputikaṇ ṭañcik
	kurutikeḻu paintaṭi muṟaimuṟai taṭintu
	kaṉṉampuk kēṟiya maṉṉaṉum ilaṉē!
	aruntamiḻp pulavōr peruntiṟam uṇarntu
55	poṉṉum maṇiyun taṇṇaṭai nilaṉum
	varaiyātu vaḻaṅkiya aracarum ilarē!
	meyyoṭu miṭaiyāp poyporuḷ īṭṭit
	tāmaṟi aḷavaiyiṟ piṟarkkoṉ ṟīyātu
	tāmē nukarun tīyarō uḷarē!
60	tamiḻvaḻak kaṟiyā tamaivila kūṟuṅ
	kaṟṟaṟi villār maṟṟiṉip palarē! ataṉāṉ
	maṟṟavar kaṭaitoṟum nittalum paṭarntu
	vāyvā ḷātu vallāṅkup pāṭik
	koṉṉē tirital iṉṉātu maṉṉō!
65	koḻunaṉai arumpiya kāñciyaṅ kōṭṭiṟ
	puṉṟalaic ciṟuvar ēṟit tuṉṟukaṭaṟ
	curitiraip pāyntu viḷaiyāṭ ṭayaravum,
	veṇṭōṭu virinta kaitaiyan tātu
	meypaṭat timirnta noyciṟai vaṇṭiṉam
70	vilaiyaṟu muttamoṭu talaimayak kuṟṟuk
	kaṇmayak kēṟi eṇṇila kiṭappa
	nuḷaicciṟu makaḷir vaḷaikkaiyiṉ vārik
	kuṭampurai paṇilam nirampap peytu
	āmai yaṭuppil āmpal neruppiṟ
75	ṟāmaṟi tiṟattāl aṭuvuḻi mayakkaṟṟut
	tuṇṇeṉap paṟantu viṇṇiṭaic cella
	viyappiṭai eḻunta mayakkoṭu niṟkavum,
	vēṟucila ciṟumiyar cāṟukeḻu karumpiṉ
	mīṉciṉai aṉṉa veṇmaṇal kuvaiit
80	tēmpoti maḻalaiyoṭu ciṟupāṭṭicaittu
	vayiṉvayiṉ niṉṟu makiḻvoṭu kuṟuvavum
	aḻakucāl neytal nalamuṟa maruviya
	oṟṟimā nakariṟ poṟṟoḻiṟ polintu
	viṇṭoṭa nivanta iñci vaḷaiiya
85	urukeḻu tirunakar kāṇṭoṟum paraci
	malaikuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa nilaiyuyar talaikkaṭai
	añcuvaru nōkkamoṭu niṉṟaṉeṉ ākac,
	curuḷiruṅ kuñci poṉñāṇ piṇittu
	nilaṉṟoṭa vīḻnta polantukil acaiic
90	cāntupular mārpiṟ pūntoṭai puraḷa
	ēṟucel celaviṉ vīṟupaṭap pōntu
	yāraiyō pulavōy! aṟumukat taiyaṉ
	pukuttuka niṉṉaiyeṉṟu aruḷiṉaṉ, pōti! yeṉṟu
	iṉṉurai oruvaṉ pakara, ataṉetir
95	colluvatu aṟiyēṉ melleṉap pukkup
	poṉpuṉai viḷakkan taṅkaiyil ēnti
	miṉpuṉai makaḷir niralpaṭa niṟpak,
	kaival pāṭiṉi kaṟaiyaṟap peṟṟa
	payaṉuṭai eḻāal nalaṉuṟa marīi
100	viralnuti teṟitta narappicai nōkki
	miṭaṟṟoli porutti neṟippaṭa miḻaṟṟa,
	urumatirn taṉṉa maṇkaṉai muḻavamum
	varaivēy turuviya uraikeḻu kuḻalum
	pēṇuṟu marapiṟ pāṇiyum vallōr
105	pālkeḻu marapiṟ cīritiṉ iyakkac,
	cennerup pārnta poṉṉurait taṭavil
	akiṉey peytu palarniṉṟu pukaippa,
	vaṭamalaip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattut
	teṉmalaip piṟanta naṟuṅkuṟa ṭuraittu
110	viḻumiya tiraṭṭiya ceḻunaṟuṅ kuḻampil
	aivakai viraiyum virāay oyyeṉat
	turuttip peytu ceṟippap palarniṉṟu
	talaippeyaṉ māriyiṉ malaiccān tuṟaippa,
	vāḻaiyuṅ kamukum vayiṉvayiṉ nāṭṭi
115	ōviya nuṭpamum oṇpaṇi nuṭpamum
	mēviya paṭāam mēluṟak kaṭṭik
	kaḻaṅkupurai nittilam ilaṅkat tūkkik
	kēḻkiḷar paṉmaṇi kāḻpaṭa nāṟṟip
	`peruntaṇ kaṇavīra naṟuntaṇ mālai
120	pālvēṟu marapiṟ palapaṭa iyaṟṟi
	elimayirk kampalam nilaṉuṟa virittu
	viriyiṭai eḻuppiya ariyaṇai mītu,
	veṅkatir ñāyiṟu maṇineṭuṅ kōṭṭil
	taṅki irunta takaimai pōlavum
125	vāṉkatir matiyam mīṉkuḻāñ cūḻat
	tāṉpolin teḻunta talaimai pōlavum
	aṟumukat toruvaṉ kuṟunakai ilaṅka
	nacaikeḻu viḻikaḷ ticaimukam parappik
	kuṉṟāt tiruvoṭum viḷaṅkak, kaṇṭunaṉi
130	meynnilait toṇṭar tannilai yaṟiyār
	viḻinīr cinta moḻiyiṭai kuḻaṟa
	uccik kūppiya kaiyiṉar paḻicci
	aruṭkuṟip puṇarntāṅ kamaivoṭu niṟpa,
	veruvaru nōkkamoṭu marampō lāki
135	aruṇilai uṇarēṉ ṟeruḷiṉṟi yōṭik
	kuṭantaṅ koṇṭetir niṉṟaṉe ṉāka,
	maṭattapu ciṉmoḻi makiḻak kūṟi
	aṉpuṟu pulavōy! añcal ōmpumati!
	kālaṅ kaṭanta kōla vaippil
140	nikarā iṉpam mikaipaṭai nukarntu
	kaṭaivaḻi illā nilaiyoṭu polikuvai!
	eṉṟupala nallurai piṉṟaiyuṅ kūṟit,
	taṉmaruṅ kiruntōṟ kuṟippa, avaṉumeṉ
	aṅkai paṟṟic ceṉṟu koṅkukamaḻ
145	nīrāṭu tuṟaiyil niṟuviya aḷavai,
	kārmiṉ aṉaiya ērkeḻu makaḷir
	kuṟunakai miḷiraeṉ arukiṟ pōntu,
	mācu potintu pāci āki
	īrum pēṉun tīrā tuṟaiyumeṉ
150	puṉṟalaik kuñci puṇarppaṟa uḷarip
	puḻukuney uraittup paṉinī rāṭṭi
	īram pulara iṉpukai yūṭṭit
	tāmeṉai viṭuttup pōka, ēṉōṉ
	piṟitōr nalliṭam pukutta āṇṭaik
155	kaviṉkeḻu cilatar viraiyiṉar pukuntu
	viṇṇōr vēntaṉ kalṇeṉap polintaeṉ
	ṟuṉṉaṟ citāar nīkki muṉṉiya
	aravuri aṉṉa āṭai uṭīik,
	kalavaic cāntam pulara aṭṭi,
160	mukainekiḻntu virinta takaicāl naṉmalar
	piṇaiya lākki maṇamuṟa milaiccit,
	tuḷaṅkoḷik kalaṉkaḷ viḷaṅka aṇintu,
	maṇittavi ciṭṭataṉ micaiyeṉai irīik,
	kumari vāḻaiyiṉ kuruttakam virīi
165	aṟucuvaik kaṟiyum aṭicilun tuvaiyum
	veṟikamaḻ kaṉiyum naṟuneyum pālun
	tīñcumait tayirun tēṅkaṉi paḻaṉuṅ
	kaṉṉalum piṟavun tuṉṉa iyaṟṟik,
	kaṭumpaci tīrayāṉ micaikoṟum micaitoṟum
170	iṭumpai iṉṟi iṭaiyiṭai iṭupu
	pāḻvayi ṟeṉṟum nirampap peytaliṟ
	perumporuḷ eytiya vaṟiñaṉiṉ allān
	tiruntapiṉ eṉṉaip poruntak kūuy
	ēṉaiyōr iṭattiṟ ṟāṉeṉaic celuva, āṅkup
175	poḻinī ṟiṭṭu viḻimaṇi tāṅkik
	kāntaḷ pōlac cēntoḷir aṅkaiyiṟ
	civañāṉa pōtam nalaiyaṟac civaṇip
	paḻutaṟu māṇavar kuḻuvoṭu cūḻa,
	vāṉkatir maṇṭilam īṅku vantatupōl
180	tiruvoṭu viḷaṅkum urukupoṟ ṟaviciṟ
	kaṉmaṉam urukkuṅ kāṇṭaku kōlamoṭum
	eṉmaṉam urukki eḻuntiṉi taruḷiya
	cōma cuntara kuruvaṉaik kāṇūuc,
	ceykuva taṟiyēṉ meytaṭu māṟi
185	nilampaṭa vīḻntu kalaṅki niṟpa,
	yāraiyō? eṅkaḷ aṟumukat taiyaṉ
	tantatōr aruḷiṉ vantaṉai pōlum!
	nallai! nallai! ellaiyil kālam
	aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ nilaiyiṉai! viḻumiyai!
190	cevviyai! periyai! auviyam ilaiyeṉ
	ṟiṉiyaṉa palavum paṉivarak kiḷantutaṉ
	malarkkaiyeṉ puṉṟalaic cērttip pulappaṭa
	nīyē, uṭampuṅ poṟiyuṅ kaṭampaṭu karuviyum
	aṟivum vaḷiyum allai; aṟivāyc
195	ceṟiyum peṟṟiyai; maṟṟavai aṟiyāp
	paṇpiṉa; aṉaiyai āyiṉum, muṉpē
	paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇṇiṉ miṭaipaṭu
	mācu potinta nīrmaiyai; vīciya
	oḷippiḻam pāki veḷippaṭum murukaṉ
200	āṉātu curanta aruḷiṉaṉ ākaliṉ,
	niṉṉai maṟaitta tuṉṉiru ṭurantu
	taṉṉaruṭ polivāṉ niṉṉaṟivu koḷīi
	arukā iṉpan tarutaṟ  poruṭṭu
	meynnilai irutti mācu pōkkit
205	taṉṉilai niṉakkut tantaṉaṉ kōṭiyeṉ
	ṟuṟuti kūṟi viṭuppat, tēṟip
	paṉṉiru kaiyaṉ muṉṉar eyti
	imayam pūtta paṉikeḻu cuṉaiyil
	amaiyā tāṭiya umaitaru putalvōy!
210	eriviḻit timaikkun tirunutaṟ ṟantaik
	korumoḻi viritta teruḷuṟum uṇarvōy!
	vāṉōr uṟukaṇ tīrppa mēlōr
	aivaṭi vēlaik kaiyiṉil eṭuttōy!
	vēntaṉ tanta ēnteḻiṟ celvikku
215	varaipurai mārpam varaiyātu aḷittōy!
	kuṉṟavar vaḷartta poṉṟikaḻ vaḷḷikkuk
	koḻukom pākiya viḻumiya tōḷōy!
	aruntamiḻk kīraṉaip poruntik kāttōy!
	emmaṉōrk keḷivanta cemmai yāḷa!
220	oruva! ciṟuva! karumayiṉ muruka!
	maṟaimuṭi taṉṉil niṟaitiru vuruviṉai!
	kaṇṇum uṇarvuṅ katuvāp peṟṟiyai!
	aimperum pūtamum āki niṉṟaṉai!
	ulakuniṉ ṉuruvē, oḷikaṇiṉ vaḻiyē,
225	alakilāc camayat tavvavar tamakkup
	palavē ṟuruviṉ nilavunai nīyē!
	āṟuniṉ mukamē, aintuniṉ vaṭivē,
	nāṉkuniṉ moḻiyē, mūṉṟuniṉ kaṇṇē,
	iraṇṭuniṉ ṟuṇaiyē,oṉṟu niṉ vēlē,
230	maruṇṭa uṇarvoṭu māṭcimai eytā
	muruṭa ṉēṟkun tiruvaruḷ tarunaiyeṉ
	ṟiyāṉaṟi yaḷavaiyiṉ ēttat tāṉuvantu
	kuṟipparu miṉpattup piṟakkiṉaṉ eṉaiyē, atumutal
	iṉpam ārntaṅ kiruttal allatu
235	tuṉpa meṉpa taṟiyēṉ, aṉpoṭu
	nīyum avaṉvayiṟ celiṉē pāyaruḷ
	oruvantan tarukuvaṉ maṉṟa; maruvantu
	talaippeyaṉ māriyiṉ malaippaṭu nīttam
	painniṟap pīliyuñ cenniṟat tukirum
240	vēḻa maruppuṅ kāḻakiṟ ṟuṇiyum
	oruṅku varaṉṟi maruṅku viraiik
	kuṇṭukaṭ paṭukariṉ maṇṭi niṟaintāṅku,
	avalak kavalaiyum uvalaic cuvaiyum
	muraṇuṟu aṟivun tirukiya cerukkum
245	mutalaṟap peyarttuc citaiyā tōṭi
	aṉpiṉ niṟainta iṉpa veḷḷattup
	paḻiyāk kaṭumpoṭum viḻumitiṟ ṟuvaṉṟi
	ēḻula kaḻiyiṉum aḻiyātu
	ūḻi yūḻiyum vāḻmati ciṟantē!	(28)
29. kāppuk kaimmikkuk kāmam perukiyuraittal
ciṟanta poruḷoṟṟic cēvvēḷ ivarai
maṟantiṅ kuyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - paṟantōṭit
tiṇṭōḷ taḻuvuvam iṉṟēṟ ceḻuvaraiyaik
kaṇṭēṟik kīḻviḻuvaṅ kāṇ.	(29)
30. aṭiyuṟaiyiṭutal
kāṇap peṟātator kāṭciyai yēṉuṅ kacinta aṉpāṟ
pēṇap peṟumuk karuviyiṟ kāṇpar piṟaṅku moṟṟi
vāṇap peruntakai yeṉpatu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkumaṉpāṟ
pūṇap peṟuminta mummaṇik kōvaiyuṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē.	(30)
tiruvoṟṟimurukar
mummaṇikkōvai
muṟṟum

ōm civam
tiruvoṟṟi murukar 
mummaṇikkōvai urai


kāppuc ceyyuḷ
nīr vaḷam naṉṟu ōṅkum tiruvoṟṟi nīḷ nakaril - nīrvaḷam peritum ōṅkukiṉṟa tiruvoṟṟiyū  reṉṉum pukaḻālnīṇṭa paṭṭiṉattil tirukkōyil koṇṭa, cūrvaḷam tiṉ vēlōṟku - cūrmāliṉatu ceḻumaiyait tiṉṟa vēṟpaṭaiyai yuṭaiya murukakkaṭavuḷukku, tūyanūl - `muṉṉamaṇik kōvai yeṉṉum ikkuṟṟamaṟṟanūlai, cīr vaḷara - ciṟappōṅkumpaṭi, emmāl iyaṟṟuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - emmaikkoṇṭu ceyvatu oṉṟu uḷḷatu, atu kaimmā mukattaṉ kaḻal ākum - aṅṅaṉañ ceyyuṅ karuvi yāṉaimukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaṭi yākumeṉka.
`naṉṟu perumaipporuḷatu; naṉṟu peritākum eṉṉun tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇka.
`cūrvaḷantiṉ vēlōṉ eṉpatu, cūraṉuṭaiya valimai celvam mutaliyavaikaḷai aḻitta vēlō ṉeṉṉum poruṭṭu; uṭaiyōṉ ṟoḻil uṭaimaimē lēṟṟappaṭṭatu.
`tūyanūl tūyaṉākiya murukakkaṭavuḷaip pāṭutalpaṟṟi innūl tūya eṉa aṭaikoṭukkappaṭṭatu.
innūlaippāṭum ākkiyōṟkum itaṉai ōtuvārkkuñ ciṟappuṇṭāka eṉṉum poruṭṭuc `cīr vaḷara veṉṟār.
`emmāl āl karuvipporuḷil vantatu.
āciriyar innūlait tam muṉaippiṉṟi iṟaivaṉ vaḻiyāy niṉṟu avaṉiyaṟṟa iyaṟṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, iyaṟṟuvatu eṉattaṉviṉaiyāṟ kūṟiṉār; āl, acai.
`kaimmā kaiyaiyuṭaiya vilaṅku; aḵtu yāṉai.
`atu kaḻal ākum eṉṟu viṉai muṭikka.
eṭuttukkoṇṭa nūl iṉitu muṭiyumāṟu mutaliṟ kāppuc ceyyuḷ kūṟutal nūlāciriyarkku marapātaliṉ, aṭikaḷum icceyyuḷāṟ kāppukkūṟiṉar. piṟkāla vaḻakku yāṉaimukak kaṭavuḷ mēl niṟṟaliṉ icceyyuḷum avaraiyē paraviṟṟu.
ippāṭṭu nēricai veṇpā. itu `nīr eṉpatu mutaṟ `kaḻal eṉpatu iṟuvāyāka muppatu coṟkaḷāṟ kūṟappaṭu talāl, innūlum muppatu pāṭṭukkaḷā lākkap paṭṭateṉpatu kuṟippu. itaṉkaṇ il ku āl eṉṉum vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷum ēṉaic coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭṭaṉa. eṉṉai? avvurupukaḷ tāmuñ coṟkaḷō ṭoppap poruḷuṇarttuta luṭaimaiyi ṉeṉpatu. aṟṟāyiṉ, aṉ a mutaliya peyar viṉai vikutikaḷum poruḷuṇarttuta luṭaimaiyiṉ, avai tāmum avvurupukaḷaip pōlac coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭal vēṇṭumā leṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu, oru coṟ ṟoṭariṉiṭaiyē il ku āl mutaliya vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ coṟkaḷiṉuḷ niṉṟum poruḷuṇarttutaṟ kaṭ pirinticaittal pōl, aṉ a mutaliya peyar viṉai vikutikaḷ aṅṅaṉam pirinticaiyāmaiyiṉ, av vikutikaḷ coṟkaḷoṭu cērt teṇṇappaṭutaṟku uriyaṉavallaveṉ ṟuṇarntukoḷka.
iṉi, ikkāppucceyyuḷ `nīr eṉṉuñ collai mutalākavum, `kaḻal eṉṉuñ collai iṟutiyākavuṅ koṇṭu viḷaṅkalāl, nūlum avvāṟē `nīr eṉpatai mutaliluṅ `kaḻal eṉpatai iṟutiyiluṅ koṇṭu viḷaṅku meṉpatūuṅ kuṟippā ṉuṇarappaṭum.
iṉṉum, ippāṭṭiṉ mutalaṭiyil tiruvoṟṟinakarc ciṟappum, iraṇṭāmaṭiyiṟ cūraṉaḻivum, mūṉṟāmaṭiyil tāmum piṟarum payaṉ peṟumāṟum, nāṉkāmaṭiyil avaraip payaṉ peṟuvikkum iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭinilaiyuṅ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, avaitām muṟaiyē poruḷaṟiviṉ virivaiyum, aṟiyāmai yiṉ tēyvaiyum, avviraṇṭaiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkutaṟkuriya uyiriṉ takutiyaiyum, attakutik kēṟpa atu peṟum pēriṉpap pēṟṟaiyuṅ kuṟittu, avvāṟṟāl ittiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇikkōvaiyeṉṉum nūlun tiruvoṟṟi nakaraic ciṟappikkumukattāl uyiriṉaṟivaip puṟapporuḷkaḷiṟ celutti akaṉṟuviriyumāṟu ceytu, piṉṉar ammukattāl aṟiyāmaitēyttu ataṉ uṇarviṉai nuṭpamākki, iṟaivaṉatu pēriṉpattiṉai nukarntu kiṭakkac ceyyumeṉṉum muṟai teḷivittavāṟu.
mēlum, icceyyuḷ `ākum eṉṉum viṉaiyaiyē muṭiviṟ peṟutaṟkurittā yiruppavum, āciriyar ataṉaic `cīrvaḷar eṉpataṟkuṅ `kaḻal eṉpataṟkum iṭaiyē yiṭṭu muṭittiruttalāl, innūlun taṉṉaip payilvārkku immaippayaṉkaḷai muṟṟun tantu iṟutiyil iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭippēṟṟaip payakkumeṉap payaṉ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟellāṅ kāppuc ceyyuḷeṉpatu nūliṉ kaṭkāṇappaṭum nūlamaitikaḷaiyum poruṭkūṟupāṭukaḷaiyum payaṉ mutaliya piṟavaṟṟaiyu mellāṅ kuṟippāṟ ṟaṉkaṭkoṇṭu viḷaṅkutal atutaṉakku ilakkaṇamāmeṉka. āciriyar civañāṉamuṉivarun tamatu civañāṉapōtamāpāṭiyattu maṅkalavāḻttuc ceyyuḷiṉ uraiyiṟutiyilum iṅṅaṉam nūṉutal poruḷellāṅ kuṟippāṟ ṟaṉṉakat taṭakkiniṟṟal maṅkalavāḻttukku ilakkaṇameṉṟuṇarka eṉṟu urai kūṟiṉar. kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkameṉpatum maṅkalavāḻt teṉpatum orupuṭaiyoppumaiyuṭaiya.
1. ceñcoṭu kiḷattal
neñcoṭu kiḷattal eṉpatu taṉ talaivaṉ perumaiyum avaṉṟaṉ aruḷuruvai niṉaitalāl taṉakku viḷaiyum naṉmaiyun talaivi taṉ neñciṟku eṭuttukkūṟal; immuṟaiyiṉai `neñcaṟivuṟūu veṉavum vaḻaṅkupa.
itu mutal nāṟpattōraṭikāṟum maruta nilattiṟkum neytaṉilattiṟkun tiṇaimayakkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār.
(1 mutal 4 aṭi) nīr vaḷam kaviṉṟa cīr vaḷar paḻaṉattu - nīr vaḷattāl aḻakupeṟṟa ciṟappōṅkiya kaḻaṉiyil, nel kural kavviya nal kural paim kiḷi - neṟpayiriṉ katiraik kauviya aḻakiya kuraloliyiṉai yuṭaiya paccaikkiḷi, kāṉal kāvil tāṉ viraintu eyti - kaṭaṟkaraiccōlaiyil viraivāyp paṟantuceṉṟu, poṉvīñāḻal polintu iṉitu iruppavum - poṉniṟamāṉa malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya nāka marattiṉmēl maṉaeḻucciyōṭu aḻakāy uṭkārntirukkavum.
`vaḷaṅ kaviṉṟa mūṉṟām vēṟṟumait tokai, cīr - viḷaiviṉperumai. kural - neṟkatir; itu neṟkatirum... kuraleṉamoḻipa eṉṉum piṅkalantaiyā laṟiyappaṭum.
itu, maruta nilattukkiḷi neytal nākamarattiṟ ceṉṟiruttal kūṟiṟṟu.
(5-8) karum kaḻi vāriya peru mīṉ kuvaii - kariyaniṟat tiṉaiyuṭaiya kaḻiyiliruntu vāriyeṭutta periya mīṉkaḷaik kuvittu, val tiṟal paratavar muṉṟiltoṟum uṇakkum - valiya uṭaluram vāynta cempaṭavar tammuṭaiya vīṭṭu muṟṟaṅka ṭōṟum ularttukiṉṟa, tuṭikkaṇ tuṇiyal koṭikkulam kavarntu - uṭukkaiyiṉ kaṇkaḷaippōl vaṭṭattuṇṭu kaḷāyirukkuṅ karuvāṭukaḷaik kākkaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ tiruṭiyeṭuttukkoṇṭu, marutaṅ kāvil peritu vaikavum - maruta nilattu iḷamaraccōlaiyil ēkip perumitattuṭaṉ amarntirukkavum,
tiṟal - uṭampiṉ uram.
ularttutaṟkāka vakuntu veyilil virittuvaikkap paṭṭukkāyum mīṉ kāṇpārkku uṭukkaiyiṉ kaṇkaḷaippōl iraṇṭu vaṭṭattuṇṭukaḷāyt tōṉṟutalāl, atu `tuṭikkaṭṭuṇiya leṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtivvāṟu tōṉṟutal koḻumīṉ kuṟaiiya tuṭikkaṭṭuṇiyal eṉṉum maturaik kāñciyālum (320) āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tuṭiyiṉkaṇ pōluruṇṭa tuṇikaḷ eṉṟu ataṟkuk kūṟiya uraiyālun teḷiyappaṭum.
itu, neytalnilattuk kākkaiyum mīṉum marutanilattuc ceṉṟamai kūṟiṟṟu.
(9-11) kuḷaṉ uṟai vāḻkkai vaḷa nīrkkōḻi - maruta nilattuk kuḷattiṉkaṇ vāḻum vāḻkkaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya uṭaṟceḻumai vāynta nīrkkōḻi, maṉaiyuṟai nīṅkit tuṇai kūp peyarntu - avviṭattu uṟaitalait tavirntu taṉatu peṭaippaṟavaiyaik kūviyaḻaittuk koṇṭu, kālvaḻi ōṭik kaḻiyiṟ cēravum - vāykkāl vaḻiyāka nīnticceṉṟuneytaṉilattuk kaḻinīriṟ cēravum.
`vaḷam eṉpatai nīrkkuk kūṟutalumām. `uṟai iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu viṉaittokai, piṉṉatu mutaṉilaittoḻiṟpeyar. `tuṇai iccol irupāṟkum urittēyāyiṉum, perum pālum peṇpāl mēṟṟākavē vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
`kūppeyartal orucol; aḻaittaleṉṉum poruṭṭām.
itu, marutanilattu nīrkkōḻi neytaṉilattuk kaḻiyiṟ cērntamai kūṟiṟṟu.
(12-17) pulavu maṇam muṉaiiya nilavuuṟaḻ kuruku - neytaṉilattuk kaḻiyil mikairuntamaiyāṟ pulāl nāṟṟattai veṟuttuviṭṭa nilavaiyotta veḷva kaṇ akal vicumpil naṇṇiya mukilmēl ivarntuḷiya nāraikaḷ eḻutiṅkaḷ - iṭamakaṉṟa vāṉiṟ poruntiya kariya mukiliṉ mēl ēṟit tōṉṟun tiṅkaḷaip pōla, parantu keḻu tuṇinīrt taṭattiṉ maṇam malar ārum - iṭamakaṉṟu uḷḷa teḷinta nīriṉai yuṭaiya marutanilattuk kuḷattiluḷḷa maṇaṅkamaḻum pūkkaḷai mēyntukoṇṭirukkum, irumputirittu aṉṉa aṟal maruppu erumai karumpuṟam maruṅkil - irumpai muṟukkiṉāṟpōl aṟuppu amainta maruppai yuṭaiya erumaikaḷiṉ kariya mutukākiya iṭattil, oruṅki niṟpavum - kūmpi niṟkavum,
`muṉaiiya veṟutta; iḵtipporuṭṭātal cēṟṟu nilai muṉaiiya ceṅkaṭ kārāṉ eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟiṟ (4) kāṇka. `nilavu niṟattiṟ kuvamai. `naṇṇiya poruntiya. `ivarnteḻu orucol nīra.
`tuṇinīr eṉpataṟkut `teḷintanīreṉṟē āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum tuṇinīr mellaval (maturaikkāñci, 283) eṉpuḻi urai kūṟiṉār.
`irumputirittaṉṉa veṉpatu, maruppiṉ valimai yuṇarttiṟṟu. ivvuvamaiyai irumputirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppu eṉṟu kuṟuṅkuṭimarutaṉārum akanāṉūṟṟil (46) eṭuttāṇṭār.
aṟal, toḻilaṭiyākap piṟantapeyar.
`puṟamaruṅku puṟamākiya iṭattil; iṅku maruṅku eṉpatu iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟatu; `maruṅkiṉkaṇ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu urupu keṭaccāriyaiyuṭaṉ niṉṟatu; mel leḻuttu mikuvaḻi eṉṉuñcūttirattil meypeṟa eṉṟamaiyāl iṉṉōraṉṉa muṭikkappaṭumeṉpar uraikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (tolkāppiyam, eḻuttu, 157).
vicumpu nīrkkuḷattiṟkum, mukil erumaikkum, tiṅkaḷ kurukukkum uvamai; erumaikkuk karumpuṟaṅ kūṟiṉamaiyāl, ataṉ uvamaiyākiya mukilukkuṅ karumaiyuraikkap paṭṭatu.
itu neytal nilattu nārai marutanilattu vayalerumai mēṟceṉṟu niṉṟamai kūṟiṟṟu.
(18-19) poṟikeḻuvāḷai - marutanilattu nīr nilai kaḷiluḷḷa viḷakkam amainta vāḷaimīṉ, veṟipaṭap pāyntu muṭaikkaḻiccuṟavoṭu kaṟuvoṭu maṟiyavum - matarppiṉāṟ carēleṉap pāyntu muṭai nāṟṟattaiyuṭaiya kaḻikkaṇuḷḷa cuṟāmīṉoṭu ciṉattuṭaṉ puraḷavum,
`poṟi keḻu vāḷai `oḷi poruntiya vāḷai yeṉap         poruṭarutal poṟi vari varāl eṉpuḻi (cīvakacintāmaṇi, 44) nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruraitta uraiyālaṟiyappaṭum.
veṟi - matarppu.                                                                                                                               
`kāletirn tōṭuṅ kaḻikkaya leṉṟu piṉvarutaliṉ, ivvāḷai mīṉuṅ kālvaḻicceṉṟu pāyumeṉak koḷka. `kālvaḻiyōṭik kaḻiyiṟcēravu meṉṟu mēlē vantamaiyum niṉaivu kūraṟpāṟṟu.
`oṭu urupukaḷ iraṇṭaṉuḷḷum muṉṉatu kalappuṟu poruḷilum, piṉṉatu aṭaimoḻip poruḷilum vantaṉa.
`muṭaikkaḻi pulālnāṟṟa muṭaiya kaḻi; ēṉaiyellā nīr nilaikaḷiṉum uppaḷattukkaḻiyē muṭaimikutiyum iyalpiluṭaimaiyāl, ilakkiyaṅkaḷellām am muṭaināṟṟattaik kaḻikkē ēṟṟikkūṟutal marapu. pulavunāṟiruṅkaḻi yeṉṟu akappāṭṭilum (180) vantatu. pulavukkaḻi yeṉṟu aṭikaḷē mēlum (22 - 109) uraippar.
itu marutanilattu vāḷai neytalnilattuc cuṟavoṭu pōr purital kūṟiṟṟu.
