itukāṟum murukap perumāṉaic ciṟappittu viḷitta paṭiyām; iṉi avaṉpāl aṉparāyiṉār perumaikaḷai virikkap pukuntu, mutaliṟ cūraṉaik kūṟu mukattāl avaṉ āṟṟiya tavavoḻukka viyalpu kūṟukiṉṟār.
(18-34) tīyiṭai niṉṟum nīriṭai mūḻkiyum maram mēl ivarntu talaikīḻt toṅkiyum aṟuvakai irukkaiyiṉ neṟipaṭa amarntum - tīyiṉ naṭuvē niṉṟum nīriṉuḷ mūḻkiyum marattiṉmēl ēṟi ammarattiṉ kiḷaiyiliruntu talaikīḻākat toṅkiyum āṟu vakaiyāṉa tava virukkaikaḷil oḻuṅku tavaṟāmal vīṟṟiruntum, puṟampaṭuttu iḻuttāṅku akampaṭa niṟaittut teḷinilai mukiḻkkum vaḷinilai uḻaṉṟum - uḷḷē ulavuṅ kāṟṟai mutalil veḷiviṭṭup piṉpu veḷik kāṟṟai uḷḷiḻuttuk koppūḻiṉkīḻ uḷḷēporunta  niṟaintu avvāṟṟāl maṉam oruvaḻi niṟṟaliṉ aṟivu teḷiveytum nilaitōṉṟappeṟukiṉṟa mūccai niṟuttuma nilaiyil varuntip paḻakiyum, taṉ toḻil taputtut taṉi mutaṟporuḷiṉ aruḷ eṉum veḷḷam varampu iṟa mikuntu nilaiyum iṭaṉ eṉa ulakelām nōkkiyum - taṉ ceyal keṭuttut taṉakku oppum uyarvum illāmaiyiṟ ṟaṉitta muḻumutaṟ poruḷākiya āṇṭavaṉatu aruḷ eṉṉum veḷḷam aḷavu kaṭappa mikuntu nilaiyuṟum iṭaṅkaḷeṉṟu ulakam muḻumaiyum aruḷākavē kaṇṭum, aimperum pūtamum aimpuṟam niṟīi aivakaittēvum āṅku āṅku amaittup poy muṟai iṉṟi meymuṟai paraviyum - nilam nīr mutalāṉa aintu mutaṟporuḷkaḷaiyum aṭi mutal muṭikāṟumāka aintiṭaṅkaḷil amaittu avvavaṟṟiṟkuriya aivakaiyiṉarāṉa tēvaraiyum aṅkaṅkum eḻuntaruḷuvittup poyvaḻikaḷillāmal meyyāṉa vaḻikaḷil akavaḻipā ṭāṟṟiyum, karuviyum pulaṉum puriviṉṟi nikaḻa oru mutaṟporuḷoṭum aṟiviṉaik kaṭaviyum -  mey mutaliya puṟakkaruvikaḷum maṉam mutaliya akakkaruvikaḷun tattam uṇarvukaḷiṉiṉṟum avāviṉṟi orumukamāynaṭaipeṟa orumutaṟkaṭavuḷākiya āṇṭavaṉatu tiruvuruvattōṭu orumittu niṟkumāṟu taṉatu aṟiviṉaic celuttiyum, iruvakai nilaiyum oru muṟai iṟantu mēl nilai niṉṟu vāṉporuḷ uṇarntum - tāṉ avaṉ eṉap pirittukkāṇum vēṟṟumai nilaiyiṉai avaṉē tāṉeṉak kāṇum oṟṟumai nilaiyāṟ kaṭantu piṟaimaṇṭilattil uṇarvu nilaipeṟṟut tūyacivattōṭu oṉṟiyuṇarntum, ūḻi ūḻiyum o iṉṟinōṟpa - ūḻi ūḻi kālamāka oḻital illāmal tavam muyaṉṟum, pōḻ matic caṭaiyōṉ pōtāṉāka - piḷavupaṭṭa mūṉṟām piṟaiyai yaṇinta caṭaimuṭiyaiyuṭaiya civapirāṉ tōṉṟilaṉāka:
tavañ ceyvārkkut tīyiṭai niṟṟal nīriṭai muḻkal mutaliyaṉa uriyavātal, tavañ ceyvārkku uriyaṉa ūṇnacai yiṉmai nīrnacaiyiṉmai veppampoṟuttal taṭpampoṟuttal iṭam varaiyaṟuttal ācaṉam varaiyaṟuttal iṭaiyiṭṭu moḻital vāyvāḷāmai eṉa eṭṭum; ivaṟṟiṟku uṇaviṉum nīriṉuñ ceṉṟa maṉattait taṭuttalum, aintīnāppaṇum nīr nilaiyiṉum niṟṟaluṅ, kaṭaluṅ kāṭum malaiyum mutaliyavaṟṟil niṟṟalun, tāmaraiyum āmpalum yāmaiyum mutaliya ācaṉat tiruttalum, uṇṭaṟkālai uraiyāṭāmaiyun, tuṟanta kāṟṟoṭṭu vāy vāḷāmaiyum poruḷeṉṟuṇarka. eṉṟu urai yāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar viḷakkiyavāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum (tolkāppiyam, puṟattiṇaiyiyal, 20).
iṉi, yōkañ ceyvārkkuriyaṉa: iyamam niyamam ācaṉam vaḷinilai tokainilai poṟainilai niṉaital camāti eṉa eṭṭum. eṉa avvāciriyar āṇṭuk kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ ācaṉam mutalāṉa āṟuṟuppum muṟaiyē kūṟiṉār. ācaṉam eṉpataṟkut tamiḻ irukkai; ivai āṟu eṉpar piṅkalantai āciriyar; avai: `pattiraṅ kōmukam paṅkayaṅ kēcari, cuvattikañ cukātaṉam eṉpavātaṉam eṉa avar kūṟumāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum; avaṟṟuṭ, pattirācaṉamāvatu: vitaiyiṉkīḻēyuḷḷa narampiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷiluṅ kāṟparaṭukaḷaic cērttal; kōmukamāvatu: iṭatu pommaiyiṉ kīḻ valatukāṟ paraṭṭaiyumvalatu pommaiyiṉ kīḻ iṭatu kāṟ paraṭṭaiyuñ cevvaṉē cērttal; paṅkayamāvatu cevvaṉē kālkaḷai maṭittiruntu, piṉpuṟamāka iraṇṭu kaikaḷaiyuñ celutti, iraṇṭukāṟ peruviralkaḷaiyum piṭittukkoṇṭu, neñciṉukku arukē nālviral iṭaiveḷi yirukkumāṟu mōvāyai vaittu mūkkiṉ nuṉiyaip pārttiruttal; kēcari yāvatu: vitaiyiṉkīḻ narampiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum valattē iṭatu paraṭṭaiyum iṭattē valatu paraṭṭaiyuñ cērtti, muḻantāḷiraṇṭiṉmēl iraṇṭu muḻaṅkaikaḷaiyum vaittuk, kaiviralkaḷai virittu, nāvai nīṭṭi, orumukappaṭṭa niṉaivuṭaṉ mūkkiṉ nuṉiyaip pārttal; cuvattikamāvatu: toṭaikkum muḻantāḷukkum naṭuvē iraṇṭu aṭikaḷaiyum ēṟṟi uṭal nimirntu cevvaṉē yiruppatu; cukātaṉamāvatu; eṅṅaṉamiruntāl maṉavorumaiyum iṉpamum uṇṭākumō aṅṅaṉamiruttal.
iṉi, āciriyar tirumūlar eḻuvakaiyirukkaikaḷ kūṟi, avaṟṟiṉ mēlum eṭṭum pattum aṟupattunālum nūṟumāka irukkaikaḷ paṟpala uḷa eṉpar; atu,
 pattiraṅ kōmukam paṅkayaṅkēcari
  cottiram vīrañ cukātaṉam ōr ēḻum
  uttamamām mutuvācaṉam eṭṭeṭṭup
  pattoṭu nūṟu palavācaṉamāmē	(tirumantiram, 593) 
eṉa avar aruḷic ceytiruttalāl aṟiyappaṭum. mēlē piṅkala muṉivar kūṟiya irukkaimuṟai perumpālum āciriyar tirumūlar muṟaiyait taḻuviniṟṟalum nōkkaṟpālatu.
ataṉpiṉ, vaḷinilaiyākiya `pirāṇāyāmam pukaṉṟār. uṭampiṉ uḷniṉṟa kāṟṟaik kaḻittal `irēcakam eṉṟum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kaḻittapiṉ veḷiniṉṟa kāṟṟai uḷḷiḻuttal `pūrakam eṉṟum, uḷniṟainta kāṟṟaik koppūḻiṉ kīḻuṟuppil niṟuttal `kumpakam eṉṟum yōkanūlār kūṟuvar. aḵtu āciriyar tirumūlar,
 vāmattil īreṭṭu māttirai pūrittē
  ēmuṟṟa muppattiraṇṭum irēcittuk
  kāmuṟṟa piṅkalaik kaṇṇāka ivviraṇ
  ṭōmattāl eṭṭeṭṭuk kumpikka uṇmaiyē	(tirumantiram, 573)
eṉa aruḷic ceytirukkumāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. itaṉai nacciṉārkkiṉiyar,
untiyoṭu puṇarnta iruvakaivaḷiyun
  tantam iyakkan taṭuppatu vaḷinilai
eṉṉun tamatu uraic cūttirattāl viḷakkuvar.
ataṉpiṉ, tokainilaiyākiya, `pirattiyākāraṅ kūṟiṉār. itaṉiyalpu,
orukkāl upātiyai yoṇcōtitaṉṉaip
  pirittuṇarvanta upātippirivaik
  karaittuṇar vuṇṇal karaittal uṇṇōkkal
  pirattiyākārap perumaiyatāmē
eṉṉun tirumantirattāṉum (585),
poṟiyuṇar vellām pulattiṉ vaḻāmal
  oruvaḻippaṭuppatu tokainilaippuṟaṉē
eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiya ruraiccūttirattāṉum viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
ataṉpiṟ, poṟainilai eṉṉun `tāraṇai kūṟiṉār. aimpūtaṅ kaḷāl ākkappaṭṭa ivvuṭampil aṭimutal muḻaṅkālvaraiyiluḷḷa pakuti  nilappakutiyiṉpāṟ paṭum; muḻantāḷ mutal eruvāyil varaiyiluḷḷa iṭam nīriṉ pakutiyākum; eruvāyilmutal neñcam varaiyiluḷḷa iṭan tīyiṉ pakutiyākum; neñcam mutaṟ puruvanaṭuvu varaiyiluḷḷa pakuti kāṟṟiṉpāṟ paṭum; puruvanaṭu mutal talaiyiṉ ucci varaiyilāṉa iṭam vāṉpakutiyiṟ cērntatākum. ivvāṟu aimpūtap pakuppil aṭakkappaṭṭa ivvuṭampiṉuḷ nilattiṉ pakutiyil nāṉmukak kaṭavuḷaiyum, nīriṉ pakutiyil tirumālaiyum, neruppiṉpakutiyil uruttirap perumāṉaiyuṅ, kāṟṟiṉ pakutiyil makēcuvaraṉaiyum, vāṉiṉpakutiyil catācivak kaṭavuḷaiyum vaittu niṉaivaḷavil vaḻipāṭu āṟṟutalē `tāraṇai eṉṉum poṟainilai yākumeṉṟu yōkatattu vōpaniṭataṅ kūṟāniṟkum. āciriyar tirumūlar,
 arittavuṭalai yaimpūtattil vaittup
  porutta vaimpūtañ cittātiyiṟ pōntu
  teritta maṉāti cattātiyiṟ cellat
  tarittatu tāraṇai taṟparattōṭō
eṉat tāraṇaiyiṉ iyalpait tirumantirattil (597) eṭuttōtiṉār. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, tāraṇai yeṉṉum poṟainilaikku maṉattiṉai yoru vaḻiniṟuppatu poṟaiyē eṉavum, ataṉpiṟ pōnta tiyāṉam eṉṉum niṉaivunilaikku,
niṟuttiya ammaṉam nilaitiriyāmaṟ
  kuṟitta poruḷoṭu koḷuttal niṉaivē
eṉavum, ataṉ piṟpōnta camāti eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟṟanilaikku,
āṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟitta ammutaṟ poruḷoṭu
  tāṉ piṟaṉ ākāt takaiyatu camāti
eṉavum uraiccūttiraṅkaḷ vakuttamai kāṇka. ivaṟṟiṉ virivukaḷ `yōkatattu vōpaniṭatattun, `tirumantirattuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka. vintu veṉṉum miṉṉoḷi maṇṭilam eruvāykkuṅ kuṟikkum naṭuvēyuḷḷa mūlattukkumēl nilavuvateṉavum, nātam eṉṉum piṟai maṇṭilan talaiyuccikkaṇ niṟpateṉavum, ivaiyiraṇ ṭaṟkum iṭaiyē toṭarntu niṟkuñ cuḻumuṉai yeṉṉum aṉal nāṭiyiṉ ūṭē vintuviṉoḷi mēleḻuntuceṉṟu uccik kaṇṇatāṉa piṟai maṇṭilattai aḷāva avvaḷāvu talil uṇarvu tōyntu oṉṟi yurukit taṉṉai viḻuṅkik kiḷarum civavoḷiyāy uyir niṟkap peṟutalē camātiyāmeṉavum āciriyar tirumūlar,
 vintuvum nātamum mēruvil ōṅkiṭiṟ
  cantiyilāṉa camātiyiṟ kūṭiṭum
  antamilāta aṟiviṉ arumporuḷ
  cuntarac cōtiyun tōṉṟiṭuntāṉē
eṉṟu tirumantirattil (619) aruḷicceytiruttal niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
taputtal - keṭuttal, uḷḷam aḻiya ūkkunar miṭaltaputtu eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattil (13, 18) iccol ipporuḷil varutalkāṇka.
`poymmuṟai iṉṟi meymmuṟai paravu talāvatu; nāṉ mukaṉai avaṟkuriya nilaiyilaṉṟi avaṟkumēṟpaṭṭa tirumāl nilayil vaittu vaṇaṅkāmaiyun, tirumālai avaṟkuriya nilai yilaṉṟi avaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa uruttiraṉ nilaiyil uyarttu vaittu vaṇaṅkāmaiyum, uruttiraṉai avaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa makēcuraṉ nilaiyilum makēcuraṉai avaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa catācivaṉ nilaiyiluñ catācivaṉai avaṟkumēṟpaṭṭa muḻumutaṟ civattiṉ nilaiyilum vaittu vaṇaṅkāmaiyum, avvavarai avvark kuriya nilaiyiṉmaṭṭumē vaittu vaṇaṅkutalum ākum.
`karuviyum pulaṉum puriviṉṟi nikaḻa eṉpatiṟ karuvi yeṉṟatu meyvāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi eṉṉum puṟakkaruvikaḷ aintaṉaiyum, pulaṉeṉṟatu maṉam, niṉaivu (cittam), aṟivu (putti), muṉaippu (akaṅkāram) eṉṉum akavuṇarvukaḷ nāṉkaṉaiyum; purivu - avā, viruppam; purimāṇ puraviyar eṉpuḻi (paripāṭal, 19, 13) ipporuḷ kāṇka. karuviyum pulaṉum puriviṉṟi nikaḻtalāvatu: kaṇ mutaliya poṟikaḷāl uṇarnta poruḷ kaḷai oruvar tamakkum piṟarkkun tīṅku payavāmuṟaiyil nukartal; piṟarkkuriya toru poruḷ kāṭcikku iṉitātalpaṟṟi ataṉaip peritum avāviataṉait tām paṟṟikkoḷḷa muṉaital oruvarkku ētamām; maṟṟu, aṅṅaṉam piṟarkku urittākāta toṉṟaṉaik koḷḷumuṟaiyāṟ koṇṭu nukartal ēta muṭaittaṉṟām. iṉi, māyāvāta nūlārum avarōṭottār piṟaruṅ, koḷḷu muṟaiyiṟ koṇṭu poruḷkaḷai nukartaluṅ kuṟṟam; ātalāṟ, poṟivaḻi uṇarvu cellāmal muḻutum aṭakkutalē vāymaiyāmeṉak karaiyā niṟpar. poṟivaḻiccellum uṇarvu avvap poṟikaḷāl nukartaṟkuriya paṇṭaṅkaḷai nukarumuṟaiyil vaittu nukarākkāl, uyirkaḷ aṟivum iṉpamum eytāvāy uṇarvillāk kaṟpōl āyviṭumākaliṉ avar kūṟṟup poruntātu; allatūum, aṅṅaṉaṅ karaiyum avartāmun tāṅ karaiyumāṟē naṭavātu, tām vēṇṭuvaṉa vellān tām vēṇṭumaṭṭum nukarntu cellak kāṇṭalāl, avaratu kōḷ avarkkē payaṉṟarātātalun terintu koḷka. ivvuṇmai tēṟṟutaṟ kaṉṟē āciriyar tirumūlar,
 añcum aṭakkaṭak keṉpar aṟivilār
  añcum aṭakkum amararum aṅkillai;
  añcum aṭakkil acētaṉamā meṉṟiṭ
  ṭañcum aṭakkā aṟivaṟintēṉē	(tirumantiram, 2033)
eṉṟaruḷic ceytatūum eṉka.
muḻumatiyai irukūṟākap piḷantu vaittāṟpōṟ kāṇappaṭutal paṟṟip piṟaimati `pōḻmati eṉappaṭṭatu.
(35-40) koḻumkuṟai taṉmeyil palamuṟaitaṭintum - koḻuviya tacaiyiṉait taṉuṭampilirunatu aṟuttu iṭṭum, uṟuppiṉai īrntu neṟippaṭa vīciyum - uṭampiṉ uṟuppukaḷai aṟuttu muṟaiyāy eṟintum, pacciḷam kurutiyai nacci ukuttum - taṉatu uṭampiṉ putiya cevviya cennīrai virumpiccorintum, uyirppali vēḷviyil kaṭaṉpala kaḻippi - uyirkkiṭamāṉa uṭampaiyē paliyāka iṭum vēḷviyiṟ kiriyaikaḷ palavum muṭittu, aritiṉ muyaṉṟa piṉṟaiyum kaṭumaiyākat tavam muyaṉṟa piṉṉum, puricaṭaip periyōṉ pōntilaṉāka-  purinta caṭaimuṭiyai yuṭaiya periyōṉāṉa civaperumāṉ muṉ tōṉṟilaṉāka,
`koḻuṅkuṟai, tacai, kāḻiṟ cuṭṭa kōḻūṉ koḻuṅ kuṟai eṉpatu porururāṟṟuppaṭai (105). taṭintu, aṟuttu, ip poruṭṭātal paripāṭaliṟ (5,4) kāṇka. ariyuntōṟum iṟaivaṉaruḷāl vaḷartaliṟ `koḻuṅkuṟai eṉṟār.
cevviya nalaṉ vāyntamainta kurutiyeṉṟaṟkup `pacciḷaṅ kuruti yeṉappaṭṭatu.
uyir niṟṟaṟkiṭaṉākiya uṭal īṇṭu uyir eṉappaṭṭatu; itu tattam poruḷvayiṟ ṟammoṭu civaṇum ākupeyar (tolkāppiyam, col, 116).
uṭal mutaliya etaṉaiyum oru poruḷākak karutātu veṟukkun taṉatu tuṟavu nilaiyaip pulappaṭuttum poruṭṭum, mikap perun tuṉpaṅkaḷai yellān tāṉ ēṟṟu varuntutalaik kāṭṭu mukattāl iṟaivaṉukku oru pērirakkam varuvittaṟ poruṭṭuñ cūraṉ ittakaiya kaṭuntavaṅkaḷ purintāṉeṉka. tuṉpam poṟuttalē tavattiṟ kaṭaiyāḷam eṉṉum uṇmai,
uṟṟanōy nōṉṟal uyirkkuṟukaṇ ceyyāmai
  aṟṟē tavattiṟ kuru
eṉṉun teyvat tirukkuṟaḷāl (261) naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
ulakiṉar poruṭṭāka ellāmvalla kaṭavuḷ taṉ aṭiyavar taṉpāṟ kāṭṭum aṉpiṉ ṟiṟaṅkaḷellām muṟṟavum pulaṉām varaiyiṟ kāttiruntu, piṉṉarē avarmuṉ eḻuntaruḷi avarkku vēṇṭum pēṟukaḷ aruḷuvaṉākaliṉ iṅṅaṉam viraiviṟ pōntilaṉeṉṟār. puricaṭai - muṟukkuṇṭa caṭai (puṟam. 135).
(40-51) erikiḷar - tī mikukiṉṟa, vēḷvi āṟṟum āḻkuḻiyiṉ - vēḷvi ceyyum āḻnta vēḷvik kuḻiyil, vainuṉaiya eḻuniṟīi - kūriya nuṉiyiṉaiyuṭaiya irupputtūṇai niṟutti, muḻuuṭampum paḻutupaṭap pōḻntu paṭuttup poṉṟa - cūraṉ taṉatu muḻuuṭampum paḻutākumpaṭi ataṉkaṭ pāytalāṟ piḷantaḻittu iṟakka, tampiyar pāyvataṟku ūkkiya kālai - tampimārum aṅṅaṉamē pāyntaḻivataṟku muyaṉṟeḻunta poḻutu, vēy purai paṇaittōḷ pāvaiyum tāṉum āṅku eḻuntu- mūṅkiṟ ṟaṇṭiṉai okkum parutta tōḷkaḷaiyuṭaiya umaippirāṭṭiyun tāṉumāka āṅku viraintu  tōṉṟi vāṉavar talaivaṉ cīritiṉ aruḷiya - tēvarkaṭkut talaivaṉāṉa civaperumāṉ ciṟappāka īntaruḷiya, varampeṟu perumaiyiṉ uram peritu eyti - ariya varaṅkaḷaip peṟṟa perumaiyiṉāl valimai mika aṭaintu, niṉ ciṟu aṭittāmarai vēṟu iṉṟi vaikum aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ vaḻivaḻicciṟanta cūr mutalpōlap pēraṉpu illēṉ - tēvarīruṭaiya ciṉṉañ ciṟiya tiruvaṭit tāmarai malarkaḷiṟ pirivillāmaṟ poruntivāḻum aḻiyāta tiruvaṭippēṟṟiṉkaṇ muṟaimuṟaiyē perumaiyaṭaintu varum cūrar talaivaṉāṉa cūrapatumaṉaippōl ēḻaiyēṉ pēraṉpuṭaiyēṉallēṉ eṉpatu,
vai, nuṇmai; nuṉaiya, kuṟippup peyareccam; eḻu - tūṇ; cūrapatumaṉukkut tampiyar ciṅkamukaṉ mutaliyōr. civapirāṉpāṟcūraṉ iṅṅaṉamellān tavaṅkiṭantu pala peṟalarum varaṅkaḷ peṟṟa varalāṟukaḷellāṅ kantapurāṇattu acurar yākap paṭalattilum varampeṟupaṭalattiluṅ kāṇka.
`vāṉavar talaivaṉ tāṉum pāvaiyum āṅkeḻuntu eṉak kūṭṭuka.
uram, iṅkup perumitattiṉ vaṉmaiyeṉka.
`ciṟumaiaṭi yeṉpatu `cīṟaṭi yeṉap puṇarntatākaliṉ, atu `ciṟiya tiruvaṭi yeṉap poruḷpaṭum. iṟaivaṉ iḷaiña ṉākaliṉ ataṟkēṟpa avaṉṟiruvaṭi cīṟaṭi yeṉappaṭṭatu. `cūr accam, ataṉai viḷaikkum acuraṉukku āṉatu ākupeyar; `cūr accapporuṭṭātal tivākarattiṟ kāṇka.
(52-66) ālavāy amarnta aḻalniṟak kaṭavuḷ - tiruvāla vāyiṉ kaṇ tirukkōyilkoṇ ṭeḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa tīmēṉiyuṭaiya civapirāṉē, centamiḻvaḻakku muntuniṉṟu icaippa - toṉṟu toṭṭuvaruñ cevviya tamiḻvaḻakki ṉiyalpiṉait taṉ etirē vantu niṉṟu collavum, kaṭāviṭai nikaḻttum taṭāpperum pēṟum - iṟaivaṉ viṉaviyavaṟṟiṟku viṭaiyāka ataṉai maṟuttu urai nikaḻttun taṭuttaṟkariya periya tiṟamaippēṟum, iṟaivaṉ kaṇṭa poruḷ varampu aṟintu - kaṭavuḷ aruḷicceyta kaḷaviyaṟ poruḷaḷavu terintu, colneṟi māṭciyum - col loḻuṅkiṉ perumaiyum, poruḷneṟi māṭciyum - poru ḷoḻuṅkiṉ ciṟappum, aḷavaiyiṉ viḷaivum avaṟṟai ārāyum aḷavai muṟaikaḷiṉ muṭipum, teḷivuuṟa virittu - teḷivu uṇṭākumpaṭi virittu viḷakki, cuvaipeṟa uraitta navai il pulamaiyum - coṟcuvai poruṭcuvai keḻuma urai kūṟiya paḻutillāta pulamaippāṭum, maṟaipporuḷ kuṟippu neṟippaṭa āyntu - nāṉmaṟaikaḷiṉ uṭporuṭ kuṟippukaḷai muṟaiyāka ārāyntu , civaṉaiyē mutal eṉac civaṇiya kāṭciyum - civapirāṉaiyē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷeṉat teḷinta aḷāviya aṟivum. cīritiṉ iyainta kīraṉpōla - cemmaiyākap poruntiya nakkīratēvarai oppa, coltoṟum curakkum aṟṟam il iṉpamum - col  ovvoṉṟilum ūṟṟeṭukkuñ cōrtalillāta iṉpamum, paṭṭāṅku kiḷakkum muṭṭuaṟu māṭciyum uḷḷatai uḷḷavāṟē kiḷantukūṟuṅ kuṟaivu nīṅkiya ciṟappum, kalneñcum nekiḻttum aṉpuuṟu moḻiyum - kallokkum uḷḷaṅkaḷaiyum urukkukiṉṟa aṉpu niṟainta coṟkaḷum, oru vaḻic ciṟappa varumuṟai koḷīi - oruṅkē mēmpaṭa varukiṉṟa vakaiyiṉaip porutti, āṟṟuppaṭai colum āṟṟalum illēṉ - tēvarīrukkut `tirumurukāṟṟup paṭai pōlvatōr āṟṟuppaṭai navilun tiṟamaiyum illēṉ.
tarumikkup poṟkiḻi yaḷittaṟporuṭṭu ālavāy amarnta aḻalniṟak kaṭavuḷ amaittukkoṭutta aruntamiḻp pāṭṭiṟkut tāṅkūṟiya kuṟṟaṅ kaḷaitaṟku akkaṭavuḷ etirtōṉṟiya kālattum, nakkīrar viṉāviṭaikaḷāl añcātu kuṟṟaṅ kūṟiyatāṉa varalāṟu iṅkuk `kaṭāviṭai nikaḻttum eṉṉun toṭariṉāṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ivvuṇmai tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇattuḷ tarumikkup poṟkiḻi yaḷitta paṭalattāṉuṅ, kuṟuntokaiyiṉ mutaṟceyyuḷāṉun teḷiyappaṭum.
centamiḻ vaḻakkāvatu palavāṟṟāṉum uyarnta iyalpukaḷ vāynta naṅkaiyar kūntal malariṉ cērkkaiyiṉṟiyē naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻta luṇṭeṉṉum nallicaippulavar vaḻakku.
iṟaivaṉ kaṇṭa poruḷ `iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ. tamiḻ moḻiyiṉkaṇ ippōtu kāṇappaṭum uraikaḷellā vaṟṟuḷḷum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷukku āciriyar nakkīraṉār viritturaitta teḷḷutamiḻuraiyē ellānalaṅkaḷum oruṅku keḻumic cālacciṟantu mutaṉmaipeṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatātal  tamiḻccuvaiyaṟintā rellārkkum oppa muṭintatoṉṟām. ivvuṇmaiyiṉai aṭikaḷē tām iyaṟṟiya uvarā amiḻtaṉṉa ariya urainaṭainūlkaḷil āciriyar nakkīraṉāraiyē palapaṭap pārāṭṭik kūṟiyiruttalē cāṉṟākum. avaṟṟuṭ cila pakutikaḷ varumāṟu.
	cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ coṟporuḷai viṭāmal avaṟṟaik kauvikkoṇṭē ceṉṟu pērurai virippār āciriyar nakkīraṉārait tavira vēṟoruvaraik kāṇṭal iyalātu.
	nakkīraṉāratu urai nekiḻavēṇṭum patattu nekiḻntum, iṟukavēṇṭum patattu iṟukiyum nallicaip pulamai malinta ceyyuṭpōl..... eṇvakai meyppāṭu tōṟṟuvikkum eṇvakaiccuvai vāyntatu.
	avaikkaḷattē kēṭṭārp piṇikkun takaittāṉa pērurai nikaḻttum pērāṟṟal vāyntōr tāṅ kūṟum poruḷai viṉāvum viṭaiyumāy vaittut tarukka nūṉmuṟai vaḻātu toṭarpupaṭuttu viḷakkiccellu māṟu pōlavum...... āciriyar nakkīraṉāratu urai yiṉamaippum polintu tikaḻkiṉṟatu. ittakaiyatoru viḻumiya amaippu ēṉaiyuraiyāciriyar evariṭattum muṟṟak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟilatu.
	āciriyar nakkīraṉār viritta ivvuraip pakuti tarukka iyaipuñ coṟporuḷaṭukku muṭaittāyp pāccuvai virāya urai naṭaittāyp paṇṭaikkālac centamiḻ vaḷantuṟumi akaṉṟu āḻntu teḷintuniṟṟal kāṇka.
	paḷapaḷappāṉa palaniṟac calavaikkaṟkaḷ aḻuttip, poṉ miṉukkup pūcip, palapala aṭukkumāṭaṅkaḷ uṭaittāy vāṉ mukaṭu aḷāyk, kāṇpār kaṇṇuṅ karuttuṅ kavarum nīrmait tāy uyarntōṅki niṟkum eḻunilai māṭampōl āciriyar nakkīraṉāraturai nivantu niṟṟalum, am māṭattiṉ arukē pulvēynta kuṭilum ōṭu mūṭiyatoru ciṟṟilum ēḻamait tōṟṟa muṭaiyavāyt tāḻntu niṟṟalpōl ēṉaiyurai yāciriya ruraikaḷ pīṭukuṟaintu niṟṟalum pirintiṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
	nakkīraṉār coṟṟoṭarkaḷai amaikkum muṟaiyō..... ēṉai uraikārar avaṟṟai amaikkum muṟaikku muṟṟum vēṟāṉa toru taṉmaittāy irukkiṉṟatu. oruporuṇmēl varum pala coṟṟoṭarkaḷai avar oru varicaippaṭat toṭukkun tiṟamum aḻakum peritu pārāṭṭaṟ pālavāṉa taṉicciṟappu  vāyntu miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.
	cāṉṟōrtam mutumoḻikaḷai iṭaiyiṭaiyē maṭuttu uraiyeḻututalil āciriyar nakkīraṉār vēṭkai mikutiyum uṭaiyarātalpōl ēṉai uraikārar iruppak kāṇāmaiyiṉ, avviru pālārkkumuḷḷa iyaṟkai vēṟupāṭukaḷaip pakuttuṇarntu kaṭaip piṭittukkoḷka. (māṇikkavācakar kālam, 452 - 463) eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuniṉ ṟicaitta varalāṟu: pāṇṭiya ṉoruvaṉ maṉaivi taṉakku oppārum mikkārum illāp pēraḻaku vāyntavaḷ. avaḷuṭaṉ orunāḷ avvaracaṉ taṉatu iḷamarap pūṅkāvil ulavukaiyil  avaḷatu kūntal aviḻntu kāṟṟil alaiya ataṉkaṇiruntu oruvakaiyāṉa naṟumaṇaṅ kamakamaveṉṟu kamaḻntatu. ataṉai yuṇarnta aracaṉ ak kūntaliṉ maṇam pūviṉ cērkkaiyālaṉṟi, ataṉkaṇiṉṟum iyaṟkaiyāy eḻutal kaṇṭu `makaḷir kūntaṟkup pūviṉ cērkkaiyiṉṟi iyaṟkaiyilēyum maṇam uṇṭukol! eṉṟu aiyuṟṟavaṉāyt, taṉ avaikkaḷattuḷḷa pulavarai viḷittu, `makaḷir kūntaṟkuḷḷa ciṟappu eṉṉai? eṉṟu viṉāva, evarum ataṟku viṭai kūṟamāṭṭārāyiṉar. ataṉmēl avvaracaṉ poṉpotinta kiḻiyoṉṟaṉait taṉatu avaiyilē tūkki, `makaḷir kūntaṟkuḷḷa iyaṟkaic ciṟappiṉai nuvaṉṟu evar oru tamiḻcceyyuḷ pāṭittaruvarō avar ippoṟkiḻiyiṉaip peṟutaṟkuriyar eṉa aṟivittataṉaṉ. vaṟiyaṉāṉa tarumi eṉṉum ōr iḷaiñaṉ ap poṟkiḻiyiṉaivēṇṭi iṟaivaṉaik kuṟaiyirappa, avaṉ kaiyil oru tamiḻcceyyuḷ varainta naṟukkoṉṟu vantu vīḻntatu. mjid mt‹ gh©oa‹gh‰ br‹Wfh£l, mj‹f© cŸs brŒí£ bghUŸ jh‹ Édhaj‰F ÉilahŒ mikªâU¤jš f©L gh©oa‹ m¡»ÊÆid mt‹ mW¤bjL¤J¡ bfhŸSkhW Vt, m¥nghJ e¡Ñu® v‹D« òyt® bgUªjif m¢ brŒíË‰ bghU£F‰w« csbj‹W T¿kW¥g¤, jUÄ »Ê bgwhdhŒ¥ bgÇJ« cs« ieªJ ïiwtid ÃidªJ mH, clnd mªneu¤âš m›tit¡ fs¤âš xU òyt‹ tªJ njh‹¿, `vkJ gh£o‰F¡ F‰wŠ brh‹dh® aht®? eṉa, uṭaṉē nakkīrar `makaḷir kūntaṟkup pūvum pūviṉ neyyuñ cērntālaṉṟi iyaṟkaiyē atu maṇam uṭaittākātu. aṅṅaṉam irukkak, koṅkutēr vāḻkkai eṉṉum numatu ceyyuḷiṟ.
ceṟi eyiṟ ṟarivai kūntaliṉ
  naṟiyavum uḷavō nī aṟiyum pūvē
eṉa makaḷir kūntal malariṉum mikka iyaṟkai maṇam uṭaitteṉa nīr kūṟiyatu kuṟṟamām eṉṟēm yām eṉṟaṉar. mJnf£l m¥òyt® `kfËÇ‰ áwªj e§ifkh® Tªj‰F ïa‰if kz« csj‹nwh? eṉaviṉava, nakkīrar `naṅkaiyar kūntalum pū mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ cērkkaiyiṉālēyē maṇam uṭaittām eṉa viṭai kūṟiṉar. mj‹ã‹ m¥òyt® `mu«ig khjuh® Tªjš ïa‰if kz« cil¤j‹nwh?’ vd Édt, e¡Ñu® `mtuJ TªjY« ïa‰ifkz« cil¤j‹W’ vd Éil ÆW¥g¥, ã‹D« m¥òyt® `Ú® tÊgL« cik¥ãuh£oah® TªjYŠ bra‰if kzªjh‹ cilanjh? eṉa, nakkīrar `atuvum an  nīrmaiyatē eṉṟu viṭai nuvaṉṟaṉar. vanta appulavar iṟaivaṉē yātalāl nakkīrariṉ cerukkai aṭakkuvāṉ karutit tamatu neṟṟik kaṇṇait tiṟakka, appōtum nakkīrar tamatu piḻaiyuṇarārāy `neṟṟikkaṇṇaik kāṭṭiṉuṅ kuṟṟaṅ kuṟṟamē eṉak karaintaṉar. iṟaivaṉ uṭaṉē maṟaiya, nakkīrar neṟṟikkaṇ veppan tāṅkāmaiyāl orukuḷattil iṟaṅkit, tāñcey piḻaiyiṉaip  poṟukkumpaṭi vēṇṭi iṟaivaṉmēṟ centamiḻppāṭal pāṭi yurukap, piṉṉar iṟaivaṉ avarkku av veppattiṉai nīkkit, taṉ paṭaippiṉ viyattaku amaippiṉ ṟiṟaṅkaḷaiyum, makkaḷiṟ ciṟanta naṅkaimār kūntalum, arampaiyar kūntalum, ulakaṉṉaiyiṉ kūntalum iyaṟkaimaṇaṅ kamaḻum  vakaikaḷaiyum nakkīrar naṉkuṇarumāṟu teruṭṭiṉaṉeṉa nampiyār tiruviḷaiyāṭal kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṟaivaṉ kaṇṭaporuḷ eṉpatu iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ eṉṉum akapporu ḷilakkaṇanūl; ataṟkup pērurai vakuttavar nakkīrar.
civaṇal - aḷavaḷāval, poruntal (piṅkalantai).
`kīraṉ eṉpatu peyar; atu centamiḻ vaḻakkiṟ ciṟappu nōkki varum nakaramākiya iṭaiccolloṭu cērntu `nappūtaṉ eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉapōl `nakkīraṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu; kīr - col (tivākaram); nakkīraṉ - nallacollaṉ allatu colvaṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉ.
`aṟṟam melivu, cōrvu (tirukkuṟaḷ parimēlaḻakarurai, 1186).
`paṭṭāṅku, paṭṭapaṭi, nikaḻntapaṭi, atāvatu uṇmai.
`āṟṟuppaṭai īṇṭut tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai.
(67-74) āṅku ataṉ karuvāy uḷ uṟai nuṇporuḷ - at tirumurukāṟṟup paṭaiyiṉkaṇ ataṉ karupporuḷāy akattē kiṭakkum nuṭpak karuttukaḷai, viḻumitiṉ eṭuttu vaḻuvu aṟa amaittu - ciṟappākat turuvi yeṭuttuk kuṟṟam iṉṟāka amaittu, toṇṭar ārat taṇṭātu koṭuttu - centamiḻt teyvat toṇṭarkaḷ nukarntu niṟaiyat taṇiyāmaṟ koṭuttu, niṉ tiruvaṭik kiṭanta perukiya aṉpiṉum -niṉatu tiruvaṭiyiṉkaṭ kiṭanta mikunta aṉpiṉālum, tamiḻ varampu uṇarnta kamaḻuṟum aṟiviṉum - centamiḻ ellai teḷinta maṇam viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉālum, uraittiṟam nilai yiṭum varaippaṭā viṟaliṉum - uraivaṉmai nilaikāṇum aḷavupaṭāta āṟṟaliṉālum, tāṉē taṉakku nikar eṉa viḷaṅkiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṉum allēṉ - tāṉē taṉakku nikar eṉṉumpaṭi pukaḻ viḷaṅkiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṉ eṉṉum uraiyāciriyaṉum allēṉ;
`karu, karukkoṇṭa poruḷ; eṉṉuḷak karuvai yāṉ kaṇṭilēṉ (tiruvācakam, tiruccatakam, 41) eṉṉum aruṇmoḻiyaik kāṇka.
taṇṭātu - taṇiyātu; taṇṭā īkait takaimāṇ kuṭumi puṟam, 6.
`ārtal nukartal, uṇṇutal; iccol ipporuṭ ṭākavē maṇamalar ārum irumpu tirittaṉṉa aṟal maruppu erumai eṉa muṉṉum (1, 15-6) vantamai niṉaivukūrka.
uraittiṟam nilaiyiṭalāvatu, tollāciriyar pāṭṭu kaṭku  ituvō atuvōveṉa ētum nilaippaṭāmal mayaṅkak kiṭakkum palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa uraip poruḷkaḷil vaṉmaiyuḷḷatu ituvē yeṉat tuṇintu nūlāciriyar karuttai nilaiyiṭutal eṉka. āciriyar nakkīraṉār iyaṟṟiya tirumuru kāṟṟuppaṭaikku uraiyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uraiyē iññāṉṟuñ ciṟantu viḷaṅkutalkoṇṭum itu teḷiyappaṭum.
(74-82) niccalum -  nāṭōṟum, varukkaiccuḷaiyum porukkarai māvum koḻuṅkaṉi vāḻaiyuñ ceḻuñcuvaik kaṉṉalum oruṅku talaimayaṅkiya arumperum kalavaiyiṉ - palāccuḷaiyum porukkarai māmpaḻamuṅ koḻumaiyāṉa vāḻaikkaṉiyum vaḷamāṉa iṉiya cuvaiyuḷḷa carukkaraiyum oṉṟucērntu  kalanta arumai perumaiyuṭaiya iṉcuvaik kūṭṭai oppa, aruñcol vaḻakkamum - ariya ceñcoṟ kōppum, tiruntiya naṭaiyum - tiruttamāṉa ceyyuḷnaṭaiyum, vaṇṇavēṟṟumaiyum - palappala vaṇṇappāṭṭukaḷiṉ vakaikaḷum, taṇ eṉum oḻukkamum - avaṟṟiṉ kuḷirnta ōcainaṭaiyum, oṉṟu nirampiya kuṉṟāt tiruppukaḻ vēṟuvēṟu iyampiya vīṟu uṟutavattiṉ aruṇakiri eṉum periyaṉum allēṉ - oruṅku niṟainta nalaṅ kuṟaiyāta tiruppukaḻ eṉṉun tēvarīratu pukaḻmālaiyaip palavēṟu vakaippaṭat tiruvāy malarntaruḷiya mēṉmaimikka tavapperumaiyiṉai yuṭaiya aruṇakirinātar eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōṉum allēṉ;
`niccalum iyampiya veṉka; `nicca eṉuñ col nāṭōṟum eṉṉum poruṭṭātal niccamum peṇpāṟkuriya veṉpa eṉṉun tolkāppiyakkaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattiṟ kāṇka (8); `nicca niccal eṉat tirintatu;  `niccal ēttu nelvāyilār eṉat tēvārattum pōntatu; iccol vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu `nitya eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
porukku - takaṭṭuvaṭivāy veṭikkum vaṇṭal, māmarattiṉ aṭi atupōl veṭittiruttaliṟ porukkaraimā eṉṟār; itaṉaip poriyarai yeṉṟuṅ kūṟupa.
palāccuḷaipōl ovvoru collum aṭaivē patintu niṟṟalum, māṅkaṉipōl ellāccollun tiraṇṭu oru  poruṇ mēlavāy iṉimai payantu niṟṟalum, vāḻaiyaṅkaṉi cīppuccīppāyp pirintu kāṇappaṭutalpōṟ ceyyuṭōṟum vaṇṇaṅkaḷ vēṟu vēṟāyppirintu iṉitu tikaḻtaluṅ, karampiṉ pākupōl avvaṇṇaṅkaḷ kuḷirnta ōcaiyiṉavāy oḻukutalum nālvēṟuvamaikaḷāṟ kuṟittār.
vīṟu ciṟappu (tirukkuṟaḷ, 665): vīṟu uṟutavam - mēṉmēṟ ciṟakkun tavam.
(83-88) nūl iṭai vaitta vāliya poruḷiṉum - nūlkaḷiṉiṭaiyil nūlāciriyarāṟ peytu vaikkappaṭṭa tūya poruḷkaḷaiyum, uraiyiṭai virinta purai amai teḷiviṉum - annūlkaṭku uraiyāciriyarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa uraikaḷiṉuḷ virintuḷḷa uyarvu poruntiya teḷivuṇar viṉaiyum, kēṭpōr uṇarap pāṟpaṭa vakuttu - pāṭaṅkēṭkum māṇavarkaḷ teḷivāy uṇarumpaṭi avaṟṟaip pala pakutikaḷāka vakuttukkoṇṭu, niṉpukaḻ aṉaittumtaṉperu matiyiṉ tēvarīruṭaiya pukaḻtakka iyalpukaḷaiyellāntamatu akaṉṟa aṟiviṉāl uḷamkoḷa viḷakkum vaḷam keḻu pulamai nārāyaṇaṉ eṉum kuruvaṉum  allēṉ - ammāṇavariṉ uḷḷaṅ koḷḷum muṟaiyil viḷakkavalla vaḷamaicāṉṟa pulamaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷai yeṉṉun tamiḻāciriyaṉum   allēṉ;
`vāliya,iṅkut tūyaveṉṉum poruṭṭu; (piṅkalantai, 4058)
poruḷiṉum teḷiviṉum eṉpaṉa iraṇṭaṉ ṟokai;
`purai uyarviṉ mēlatu. purai yuyarvākum (tolkāp piyam, col, 302).
pulamaikku vaḷamaiyāvatu, aiyamiṉṟit teḷitalun teḷivuṇṭāka viḷakkutalumākum.
`nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷai yeṉpār aṭikaṭkuc centamiḻ aṟivuṟuttiya tamiḻāciriyarāvar. kuruvaṉ. āciriyaṉ; vāṉōr kuruvaṉē pōṟṟi  eṉat tiruvācakattil (5, 68) iccol ipporuṭṭāy vantatu.
`kurucil eṉṉuñ colluṅ kuruveṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṟ ṉōṉṟiyatākum. kuru = oḷi, aṟivoḷiyai yuṭaiyavaṉ, ataṉait tarupavaṉ kuruveṉap paṭṭāṉ. vaṭamoḻikkaṭ ceṉṟa pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ ituvum oṉṟu.
(89-97) kaḷaviyal taṉakkup palapaṭa iyampi-  `kaḷaviyal eṉa vaḻaṅkum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷukkup pala vakaiyākap poruḷkūṟi, paḷakuaṟu naṉporuḷ teḷiyātu uḻaṉṟa pulavōr kaḷippa nalamuṟat tōṉṟi - avaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟam nīṅkiya nallurai īteṉat teḷiyamāṭṭāmal uḷḷam alaivuṟṟa kaṭaiccaṅkap pulavarkaḷ tamatu varuttam nīṅkik kaḷikkumāṟu avarmuṉ naṉmaiyuṇṭāka eḻuntaruḷivantu, meypporuḷ kāṭṭit tamiḻ vaḻippaṭuttum - uṇmaiyurai ituvēyeṉa āciriyar nakkīratēvar eḻutiya  uraiyaik kāṭṭuvittu akattamiḻp poruḷai oru vaḻippaṭuttiyum, pul amaṇ mikuntu naluṇarvu aḻintu- muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiruppai uṇarāmaiyiṉ iḻipuṟṟa camaṇ camayam paravi iṟaimutaṟ poruḷaṟivu vāynta caivacamaya nalluṇarvu keṭṭu, perum peyar vaḻutiyum aruntamiḻk kūṭalum uyvaḻi kāṇātu uḻitarukālai - periya pukaḻvāynta pāṇṭimaṉṉaṉum ariya centamiḻppayiṟci mikka maturaiyampatiyum naṉṉeṟippaṭṭu uyyum vakaikaḷ kāṇāmal alaivuṟṟuk kiṭantapoḻutu, oy eṉap pōntu mey neṟi kāṭṭiyum - tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉāka viraintu vantu tōṉṟic caivameṉappaṭum meyvaḻi tēṟṟi viḷakkiyaruḷiyum, taṇ aruḷ purinta niṉṉē - pōla iṉiya tiruvaruḷ ceyta tēvarīrē pōla;
iṟaiva ṉaruḷāṟ peṟṟa iṟaiyaṉārākap poruḷ eṉṉum akattami ḻilakkaṇattiṟku aññāṉṟaip pulavar palaruṅ kaṇṭa uraikaḷuḷ nakkīraṉāruraiyē ciṟantateṉap pulappaṭuttiya uruttiracaṉmar murukappirāṉ piṟaviyē yeṉṉum akkaḷaviyal uraippāyiram.
taṉ, cāriyai; paḷaku, kuṟṟam; paḷakaṟuttuṭaiyāṉ kaḻal paṇintilai (tiruvācakam, tiruccatakam, 35) eṉpatiṟpōla.
`oy, viraivupporuḷatu; iḵtipporuṭṭātal nuṅkaiyatu kēḷā vaḷavai yoy yeṉa eṉṉum porunarāṟṟup paṭai (152) yaṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum.
`niṉṉē, ēkāram tēṟṟam; iṅkuk kūṟappaṭum pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ neṭumāṟaṉ eṉṉuṅ kūṉpāṇṭiya ṉāvaṉ.
murukapperumāṉē tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉāka eḻuntaruḷivantu pāṇṭi nāṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu amaṇ aḻittuc centamiḻt tēvārattāṟ caivam viḷakkiyaruḷiṉāreṉṟu nampiyāṇṭār nampikaḷ, mayilēntiya vaḷḷal taṉṉai yaḷippa matipuṇarnta eyilēntiya caṇpai nātaṉ eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattuc ceyyuṭkaḷil nuvalutal kāṇka (tiruñāṉa campantar tiruvantāti, 64.)
(98-108) centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuṟa virittu - centamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya vaḻakkumuṟaikaḷai mutalil virittu viḷaṅkacceytu, viṭi ñāyiṟu eṉa - viṭiyaṟkālattil eḻukiṉṟa pakalavaṉaippōla,  muṭinilaic caivam ēṉai ellāccamayaṅkaṭkum muṭinta nilaiyil vīṟṟiruppatāṉa uyarnta caivacamayattiṉ, paim katir virittu - putiyavāṉa aṟivoḷi kaḷai eṅkum virittu, kaṅkulil kūmpiya virai aviḻtāmarai purai uravōr uḷam puriñekiḻntu alarat tarum iyal ciṟappa - iraviṉkaṭ kuvinta maṇam parappun takaiyavāṉa tāmarai moṭṭukaḷai okkum aṟiñar uḷḷaṅkaḷ kaṭṭaviḻntu malarumpaṭi ceyyum āṟṟal ciṟantutōṉṟa, uraiyiṉum karuttiṉum varai amai nōkkiṉum poruntak kāṭṭi varuntiṟaṉ mikuttu - collālum accolliṉ karuttālum aḷavamainta nōkkālum poruttamāka viḷakki varum valimai yiṉai mikuttukkoṇṭu malaivupaṭum uḷḷattu aṟivoṭu kūṭāc ciṟupuṉ māntar kuṟumoḻi kaḷaintu - muṉpiṉ aṟivu māṟupaṭum uḷḷaṅkaḷai yuṭaimaiyiṉ meyyaṟivoṭu poruntāta ciṟiyarāṉa iḻinta makkaḷiṉ ciṟu coṟkaḷai nīkki, niṉ pukaḻ virikkum aṉpil ciṟanta cōmacuntara kuruvaṉum allēṉ - tēvarīruṭaiya aruṭpukaḻkaḷaiyē viritturaikkum pēraṉ piṟ ciṟanta cōmacuntara āciriyaṉum allēṉ;
centamiḻ nūl aṟiviṉiṉṟi annūliṉkaṭ ceṟit tuṇarttappaṭṭirukkum muṭinilaic caivavuṇmaikaḷai viḷakkutal ēlātākaliṉ, `centamiḻ vaḻakku muntuṟa virippā rāyiṉareṉpatu.
takuti vāynta makkaṭkaṉṟi ēṉaiyōrkkuc camaya vaṟivunūṟ poruḷ aṟivuṟukkap paṭāvākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉan takuti vāyntu kēṭpārai ēṉait takutiyillā makkaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟu pirittuc ciṟappittal vēṇṭumākaliṉ avar `uravōr eṉṟum, avaratu nalluḷḷam `viraiyaviḻ tāmarai yeṉṟuñ ciṟappikkappaṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum avaraccamayavaṟivunūṟ poruḷ kaḷai ārāycci muṟaiyiṟ kēṭṭu aruḷmaṇam viriyap peṟāmaiyiṉ, avaruṭaiya takuti vāynta viḻumiya uḷḷaṅkaḷ malarun takutiyi luḷḷa mukaikaḷai oppavē uḷḷaṉaveṉṟaṟkuk kaṅkuliṟ kūmpiya viraiyaviḻ tāmarai purai uravōr uḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. ivvariya uvamaikaḷiṉ nuṭpa aḻaku iṅkup peritum uḷḷuṇarntu makiḻaṟ pālatākumeṉka.
uraiyiṉum karuttiṉum nōkkiṉu meṉpavaṟṟiṟku mūṉṟaṉurupu virittukkoḷka; urai-col; karuttu - coṟporuḷ; nōkku - poruḷai nuṉittuk kāṇṭal.
`ciṟupuṉ māntar ēṉaic camayattavar; kuṟumoḻi, avaratu poruḷ nirampāc ciṟumaiyuṭaiya col, muṭinta meyccamaya mākiya caivattiṉ mēṉmaiyai yuṇarum pēraṟiviṉṟi vēṟu camayap poyp poruḷkaḷai yellām vīṇ paṟṟuḷḷattāl vaṟitē virittu vaḻakkiṭṭut tiritaliṉ avarellāñ `ciṟupuṉ mānta reṉavum, avaratu poruḷil moḻi `kuṟumoḻi yeṉavum iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
ip pakutiyiṟ cōmacuntara kuravar tiruñāṉa campantarōṭu uvamikkappaṭutal kaṇṭukoḷḷappaṭuma, cōmacuntara kuravar tiruñāṉa campantarēpōl, itteṉṉāṭeṅkum māyāvāta vētāntam, pāñcarāttiram mutalāṉa ayal mataṅkaḷ talaiyeṭukka voṭṭāmal avaṟṟiṉ ciṟumaiyai eṭuttuka kāṭṭic ciṟanta cittānta caivattiṉ perumaiyai naṉku viḷakki aḵtu iṉitu taḻaittōṅkumāṟu ceyta periyārāvar. ivaratu camayat toṇṭiṉ viḻuppam aṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiya cōmacuntarak kāñcic ceyyuṭkaḷāl naṉkaṟiyappaṭum.
(109-114) poruḷ peritu īṭṭap perukuṟum avāviṉum - celvattai nirampavuñ cērppataṟku mikukiṉṟa avāviṉ kaṇṇum, īṭṭiya oḷ poruḷ ivaṟik kūṭṭi makkaḷum maṉaiviyum tuykka nalki niṉkaḻal vaṇaṅkā iṉṉā maṭiyiṉum - aṅṅaṉan tokutta mēṉmaiyāṉa poruḷai mēlum avā mikuntuperukkit tam putalvarkaḷum maṉaiviyum nukarak
koṭuttut tēvarīruṭaiya tiruvaṭikaḷai vaṇaṅkāta tīya cōmpaliṉ kaṇṇum, ariya nāṭkaḷai vaṟitē pōkki - ēḻaiyēṉuṭaiya ariya vāḻ nāṭkaḷai vīṇāyk kaḻittu, aṟam tiṟampuḷiyil ciṟantaṉeṉ maṉ - aṟanilai piṟaḻum iṭattu mikutiyuñ ciṟanta viḷaṅkiṉēṉ;
immai maṟumaip payaṉkaḷaip payattaliṟ, poruḷ `oṇporuḷ eṉappaṭṭatu; oṇporuḷ kāḻppa iyaṟṟi yārkku eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷukkup parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟiya uraiyaik kāṇka (760).
`ivaṟal, avā mikal; īṭṭamivaṟi icaivēṇṭā āṭavar (1003) eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ.
`makkaḷum maṉaiviyun tuykka nalki yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippu apporuḷaik kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkam mutaliya nalvaḻikaḷiṟ celavaḻiyāmaiyaik kuṟittatu.
`tiṟampu  uḷi tiṟampum iṭattu; uḷi iṭattu eṉa ēḻaṉurupi ṇaṉ poruḷil vantatu; iyalpuḷik kōlōccum eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟpōla (545) uḷi mūṉṟāvataṉ poruḷ paṭuvatōr iṭaiccol eṉṟa parimēlaḻakiyāruraiyai maṟuttu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mantira vitiyiṉ marapuḷivaḻā eṉpuḻi muṟaimaiyiṉ vaḻuvāta antaṇareṉa aintāvatu viritalāṉum, `iyalpuḷik kōlōccum maṉṉavaṉ eṉpuḻi muṟaimaiyilē ceṅkōl naṭāttum eṉa ēḻaṉurupu viritalāṉum uḷi yeṉpatu mūṉṟaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantataṉṟu. eṉak kūṟumurai niṉaiviṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu.
`ciṟantaṉeṉ eṉpatil eṉ taṉmai yorumai viṉaimuṟṟu vikuti (tolkāppiyam, viṉaiyiyal, 6).
aṟantiṟampā neṟiyiṟ ciṟantu niṟkavēṇṭuvatāyirukka atu ceytilēṉ eṉa oḻinta poruḷai yuṇarttutaliṉ `maṉ oḻiyicaikkaṇ vantatu.
(115-122) iṉaiya tīyeṉ niṉ niṉaivarum tiruvaṭi - ittakaiya tīmaiyuṭaiyēṉ tēvarīruṭaiya niṉaittaṟ keṭṭāt tiruvaṭikaḷai, viḻinīr uṟaippa - kaṇṇīr tuḷippa, muḻumeyum paṉippa - muḻu uṭampum naṭuṅka, nirai nirai vārātu urai naṉi kuḻaṟa - muṟai muṟaiyēvarāmaṟ pēccukaḷ mikavun talaitaṭumāṟi vara, poṟivaḻi aṟivum  ceṟiyum uṭkaruviyum palavaḻik kavarātu oruvaḻinikaḻa - meyvāy mutaliya aimpoṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē aṟivum av vaimpoṟikaḷoṭu nerukkamuṟum maṉam mutaliya akak karuvikaḷum palavaḻiyākap pirintu pōkāmal orēvaḻiyāy nikaḻa, aṉpu uruvākum niṉ toṇṭar pōla - aṉpiṉ  uruvamāy amaiyun tēvariṉ tiruttoṇṭarkaḷai oppa, nīṅkā aṉpil nilainiṉṟu - aṟāta pēraṉpil uṟutiyāy nilaittu, yāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō pāṅkupaṭamoḻimō - eṅṅaṉam aṭaivēṉō urimaiyuṇṭākak kūṟuvīrāka veṉpatu.
icceyyuḷ āṇṭavaṉaiyē viḷittut tamatu tāḻnilai kūṟumukattāl, avaratu aruḷnilai peṟutal ariteṉak kūṟiya vāṟām. `moḻimō veṉpatil `mō muṉṉilai yacaiccol (tolkāppiyam, iṭaiccolliyal, 26); kāmañ ceppātu kaṇṭatu moḻimō eṉṉum kuṟuntokaic ceyyuḷiṟ pōla (2).
niṉ niṉaivarun tiruvaṭi (115) aṉpuruvāku niṉ toṇṭar pōla aṉpil nilainiṉṟu (120-1) yāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō (122) eṉak kūṭṭuka.
`uṟaittal, tuḷittal; iccol ipporuṭṭātal teṇpaṉi yuṟaikkuṅ kāl eṉṉuṅ kalittokaiyāl (15) teḷiyappaṭum.
paṉittal naṭuṅkutal; naraimuka vūkam pārppoṭu paṉippa eṉak kuṟuntokaic ceyyuḷil (249) iccol ipporuṭṭāy varutal kāṇka.
`kavartal, palavēṟākap pirivupaṭal; antaṇ kāviri vantukavarpu ūṭṭa (puṟanāṉūṟu, 35) eṉpuḻip pōla.
viḻinīruṟaittalum meypaṉittalum urai kuḻaṟalum aṟivum ēṉai uṭkaruvikaḷum oru vari nikaḻtalumellām aṉpuruvākun toṇṭarkaṭku aṭaiyāḷamāku meṉpatu.
meytā ṉarumpi vitirvitirt tuṉvirai yārkaḻaṟkeṉ
  kaitāṉ ṟalaivaittuk kaṇṇīr tatumpi vetumpiyuḷḷam
  poytāṉ ṟavirntuṉṉaip pōṟṟi cayacaya pōṟṟiyeṉṉuṅ
  kaitā ṉekiḻa viṭēṉuṭai yāyeṉṉaik kaṇṭukoḷḷē
eṉṉum maṇivācakap perumāṉatu tiruvācakat tēṉuraiyiṉ kaṇ (tiruccatakam, 1) iṉitu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka. pāṅku - urimai (tivākaram); niṉṟiruvaṭikaḷaip peṟutaṟ kurimai yillāta eḷiyaṉēṉ ataṉaip peṟutaṟ kurimaiyuṭaiyaṉāmāṟu kūṟuvīrāka eṉṟavāṟu. (19)
eṉavē, `iḷaiyōy (9) periyōy (17) eṉa ellām valla iṟaivaṉai viḷittuc `cūr mutalpōlap pēraṉpillēṉ (51) kīraṉpōla (61) āṟṟuppaṭai colum āṟṟalu millēṉ (66) tāṉē taṉakku nikareṉa viḷaṅkiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṉu mallēṉ (73-4) aruṇakiriyeṉum periyaṉu mallēṉ (82) nārāyaṇa ṉeṉuṅ kuruvaṉu mallēṉ (88) cōmacuntara kuruvaṉu mallēṉ (108)aṟan tiṟam puḷiyiṟ ciṟantaṉeṉ maṉ (114) iṉaiya tīyeṉ niṉniṉaivarun tiruvaṭi (115) yāṅṅaṉam peṟuveṉō (122) eṉṟu avaṉatu aruḷnilai peṟutal ariteṉak kūṟiyavāṟu.
20. tōḻi kiḷḷaiyait tūtuvēṇṭal
moḻiyuṅ kuḻaṟa muḻuvuṭampum paittu
  viḻiyun tuyilkūṭā veyyōṉ - paḻiyait
  tiruvoṟṟic cēyōṟ kuraiyāy kiḷiyē
  yuruvuṉṉai yoppa tuṇarntu.
(i-ḷ) moḻiyum kuḻaṟa- collum taṭumāṟa, muḻu uṭampum paittu - muḻuuṭampum pacantu, viḻiyum tuyil kūṭā veyyōḷ - taṉṉai virumpiya eṉ arumait talaiviyiṉ kaṇkaḷun tūkkam poruntā; paḻiyai - ūravar alarukku ētuvāṉa aṭakkutaṟkariya ittuyara nilaikaḷai, tiruvoṟṟic cēyōṟku uraiyāy - tiruvoṟṟi murukaṟku eṭuttuc colvāyāka, kiḷiyē uru uṉṉai oppatu uṇarntu - arumaik kiḷiyē! teḷiviṉṟic collāṭalālum muḻu vuṭampum pacantu niṟṟalāluṅ kaṇṇimaikaḷ tuyil kūṭāmaiyālum eṉ talaiviyiṉ uruvum uṉṉaiyē ottu uṉakku iṉamātal uṇarntu eṉpatu;
`uṇarntu uraiyāy eṉak koḷka.
`paittu pacumai eṉpataṉaṭiyākap piṟanta viṉaiyeccam; `pai eṉpatē paccainiṟattai yuṇarttum eṉṉum piṅkalantai.
murukappirāṉai virumpi niṉṟāḷākaliṉ, talaivi veyyōḷeṉap paṭṭāḷ; vemmai, viruppam; aṉṟik kātal veppam uṭaiyāḷ eṉiṉum poruntum; aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ ac collukku `veppameṉap poruḷ kūṟikkoḷka.
iṉi ivviraṇṭu poruḷkaḷumē iṅkup poruntu mākaliṉ ataṉai iraṭṭuṟa moḻintu koḷḷalumām.
kiḷḷai tūtupōtaṟku iyaiyumeṉak kāraṇaṅ kaṟpitta tiṟam itaṉkaṇ mika aḻakitātal kaṇṭu makiḻtalvēṇṭum (20)
21. aruṇilai yuraittal
uṇarntā raṟiviṉun tōṉṟā toḷikku morumuruka
  ṉiṇarntār curikuḻaṉ maṅkaiya rōṭeṉ ṉiruviḻiyiṟ
  puṇarntā reyiloṟṟi mēviya vāṟu puṉiṟucellā
  vaṇantāḻ kuḻaviyait tāyceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmaiyaṉṟē
(i-ḷ) uṇarntār aṟiviṉum tōṉṟātu oḷikkum oru murukaṉ - tāṉ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷān taṉmaiyai uṇarntava raṟiviṉukkum veḷippaṭāmal oḷikkum oppaṟṟa murukappirāṉ, iṇarntuār curikuḻal maṅkaiyarōṭu eṉ iruviḻiyil puṇarntu - neruṅki niṟainta curuṇṭa kūntalkaḷaiyuṭaiya vaḷḷi teyvayāṉaiyeṉṉum ammaiyar pākarāy eḻuntaruḷivantu eṉ iraṇṭu kaṇkaḷiluṅ kalantavarāy, eyil oṟṟi mēviyavāṟu - matilkaḷ uyarnta tiruvoṟṟinakaraic cērntavakai, puṉiṟu cellā vaṇam tāḻ kuḻaviyait tāyceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmai yaṉṟē - īṉṟaṇimai kaḻiyāta pacciḷantaṉmai poruntiya kuḻantaiyait tāyē virumpic ceṉṟu taḻuviya aruṭ ṭaṉmaiyēyāmaṉṟō; eṉpatu.
kaṭavuḷiṉ uṇmaiyuṇarntālum avaṉai meyyaṉpāṟ karainturuki vaḻipaṭātārkku avaṉ nērē tōṉṟā toḷintiruntē aruḷpurivaṉākaliṉ, uṇarntā raṟiviṉun tōṉṟātoḷikku moru murukaṉ eṉṟār; uṇarntārk kuṇarvariyōṉ tillaic ciṟṟampalattoruttaṉ eṉṉun tirukkōvaiyāruraiyum ikkaruttē paṟṟi vantatu.
iṇarntu - neruṅki; iṇariya ñāṭpiṉuḷ eṉṟār kaḷavaḻiyilum (34).
uṇarntāraṟiviṉun tōṉṟātoḷikkum orumurukaṉ ētumaṟiyā ēḻaiyēṉ iruviḻiyiṟ puṇarntāreṉṟu tammait tāḻtturaittal karutip, `puṉiṟucellā vaṇam tāḻ kuḻavi yeṉṟu kuḻaviyai ētumaṟiyāp puṉiṟṟaṇimaip paruvattil vaittu vitantaruḷiṉār. kuḻantaiyait tāy ceṉṟu pulliya vaṇmaiyai iṅku uvamaiyākak koḷka. vaṇmaiyai oppataṉṟē yeṉṟu uraittukkoḷka. (21)
22. eḻil nalaṅkūṟi neñcaṟivuṟuttal
(1-9) vaṇpuṉal nilaiyil taṇaṟal pōlavum - vaḷamāṉa nīr nilaikaḷiṉ aṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭuṅ kuḷirnta karumaṇalai oppavum, tāmarai moytta vaṇṭiṉam pōlavum - tāmarai malarkaḷai mūciya kariya vaṇṭukkūṭṭaṅkaḷai oppavum, paḷiṅkil tōynta nīlniṟam pōlavum - oru paḷikkuk kaṇṇāṭiyiṉkaṇ ūṭuruvit tōṉṟuṅ karunīlaniṟattai oppavum, tirumuka maruṅkiṉ varumuṟai tuṇarntu - tirumukattiṉ pakkattē oḻuki varum vakaiyil aṭara vaḷarntu, āṅku iṭaiiṭai cuḻaṉṟu nīṇṭu kaṭai kuḻaṉṟu - aṅṅaṉamē iṭaiyiṭaiyē cuḻintu nīḷamāy vaḷarntu nuṉicuruṇṭu, kaṇ oḷi katuvum kuñciyai - kaṇṇoḷiyaik kavarun talaimayiriṉai; aṉṉai irupāl kūṟiṭṭu orupāl cūḻiyum maṟṟorupāl paṉiccaiyum tiruvatiṉ amaippa - umaippirāṭṭiyārāṉa tāyār irupirivāka vakuttu orupirivil uccikkoṇṭaiyum maṟṟorupirivil piṉṉal muṭippuñ cevvitiṉ amaippa, naṟumkatuppu oḷirum perum ciṟappāṉum - avvāṟṟāl iṉiya at talaimayir mēṉmēlum viḷaṅkukiṉṟa periya mēṉmaiyiṉālum;
puṉal nilai, nīrnilaikaḷ; avai `vaṇpuṉalnilai yeṉappaṭṭa maiyiṉ, eṉṟum aṟāta vaḷamāṉa nīroḻukkum nīrpperukkum uṭaiya āṟukaḷiṉ maṭukkaḷ vāvikaḷ mutalāṉa nīrnilaikaḷē īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum; avaṟṟiṉkīḻ iṭaiyiṭaiyē karumaṇaṟ parappukaḷ kāṇappaṭutalum iṅku niṉaivukūraṟpāṟṟu.
puṉalum paḷiṅkum mukattiṉ teḷivukkun, tāmarai mukattiṉ cevviya vaṭivukkum, aṟalum vaṇṭiṉamum nīṉiṟamum at tirumukattiṉ pakkaliṟ carintukiṭakkun talaimayirukkum uvamaikaḷāy niṉṟaṉa.
`kuñci, āṇpāṉ mayir; mēṟ katuppeṉa varutalum atu. iṭaiyiṭai curintu nīṇṭu kaṭai kuḻalaluṅ kaṇṇoḷiyaik kavarumāṟu karumaiyiṟ paḷapaḷappuṭaimaiyum aḻakiya talaimayiriṉ iyalpākum.
`tuṇarntāṅku eṉpatil, āṅku, aṅṅaṉamē eṉṉum poruṭṭu; tuṇarntu - kottāka neruṅkivaḷarntu; `tūmaruḷiruḷ tuṇarntaṉaiya kuñciyaṉ eṉṟār cūḷā maṇiyilum (kumārakālac carukkam, 6).
katuval - kavartal; paḻaṉavāḷaika tūum ūraṉ eṉṟār kuṟuntokaiyilum (8).
`cūḻi yeṉpatu uccikkoṇṭai (piṅkalantai); `paṉiccai yeṉpatu piṉṉi vaḷaittu muṭittalām; iḵtu aivakai muṭippukaḷuḷ oṉṟeṉka (tivākaram).
tiruvatu, cevvitu, eṉṟāṟpōl īṟupeṟṟu vantatu; tiruvattavar (nālaṭiyār, 57) eṉap piṟavāṟum varutal kāṇka.
ippakuti murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ aḻakiya talaimayirc ciṟappiṉaik kūṟi makiḻntavāṟām.
(10-12) cemmaṇip palakaiyiṉ - civanta māṇikkak kallālākiya oru palakaiyiṉkaṇ, ammaṇikuyiṟṟi - aḻakiya nīlamaṇikaḷai aḻutti, varampu uṟa viṭutta vaṇṇampōla - appalakaiyiṉ ōrattiṟ porunta amaitta vakaipōla, puruvakkoṭi paṭarnta - karuniṟamāṉa puruvameṉṉuṅ koṭiyāṉatu ōṭiya, virinutalāṉum - akaṉṟa neṟṟiyiṉālum;
cemmaṇi, māṇikkamaṇi, atu civantirutataliṉ. ammaṇi - aḻakiya nīlamaṇi; `maṇi eṉṉuñ col taṉṉiyalpil nīlamaṇiyiṉaiyē yuṇarttal maṇi nīlamaṇiyē eṉṉun tivākarattiṟ kāṇka. am - aḻaku (piṅkalantai).
kuyiṟṟi - aḻutti; iḵtipporuṭṭātal marakata maṇiyoṭu vayiraṅ kuyiṟṟiya eṉpuḻik kāṇka (cilappatikāram, 5, 147, aṭiyārkku nallārurai).
cemmaṇip palakaikkuk karuniṟa varampu amaiyuṅ kāṭci aḻakitātal niṉaivukūrka. vaṇṇam, vakai.
(13-15) annutal kiḷarnta poṉpōl cevviḻi - anneṟṟiyiṉkaṇ eriyum poṉ otta civanta neṟṟik kaṇṇāṉatu, utti eṉṉum tuttip pai aravu muṭimēl maṇi eṉa - piḷḷaiyāratu neṟṟimēl umaip pirāṭṭiyār toṅkaviṭṭa utti yeṉṉum aṇikalamāṉatu poṟikaḷ vāynta paṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya oru ciṟu nākampōṟ ṟōṉṟāniṟka annākattiṉ talaimēṟ kāṇappaṭuñ civanta māṇikka maṇiyiṉai yoppat taṅki yiruttalāṉum;
piḷḷaiyāratu neṟṟikkaṇ, avaratu neṟṟimēṟ ṟoṅkaviṭap paṭṭa uttiyeṉṉum aṇikalattiṉ muṉaiyiṟ patitta māṇikka maṇipōṟ civappāy oḷirntateṉṟum, avvuttiyākiya aṇikalam oru ciṟu nākappāmpiṉaiyum avvaṇikalattiṉ muṉaiyiṟ patitta cemmaṇi annākattiṉ uccikkaṇṇatāṉa māṇikkattiṉaiyum ottiruntaṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟiyapaṭi.
utti - aṇikala (āparaṇa)t toṅkal (tivākaram). tutti - poṟi (tivākaram). pai - paṭam (piṅkalantai).
(16 - 18) veḷ paṉit tiraḷai - veṇṇiṟamāṉa paṉik kaṭṭiyai, kaṇṇuṟappōḻntu - iṭam iṭaippaṭap piḷantu, tuvar niṟam ūṭṭi - ataṉkaṭ civanta niṟattaiyum ēṟṟi, civaṇuṟa amaittāṅku - poruttamuṟa amaittāṟ pōla, irupuṟam katuppu maruvutalāṉum - mukattiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷiluṅ kaṉṉaṅkaḷa aṇaintu tōṉṟutalālum;
`pōḻnteṉṟatu veṭṭuvāyiṟ ṟōṉṟum paḷapaḷap piṉai uvamittaṟkeṉṟu koḷka. tuvar niṟam, cenniṟam; itaṉkaṇ eccavummai tokkatu. `tuvar civappu niṟattai yuṇarttutal tuvaritaḻc cevvāyt tuṭiyiṭaiyōyē (cilappatikāram, 6, 26) eṉpatiṟ kāṇka.
`katuppeṉṟatu kaṉṉaṅkaḷai (tivākaram).
(19-20) kuḻaiyoṭu keḻumum taḻai cevi iraṇṭum - kuṇṭalaṅkaḷoṭu poruntum vaḷarnta kātukaḷ iraṇṭum, ō eṉum vaṭivoṭu mēvutalāṉum - ō vecaṉṉum ōṅkāra vaṭivattuṭaṉ amaintu viḷaṅkutalāṉum;
kuḻai - kuṇṭalam; miṉṉukkuḻaiyum poṟṟōṭum miḷira eṉṟār cīvakacintāmaṇiyilum (16 58). ōṭuvurupu ataṉōṭiyainta oppalerāppurai (tolkāppiyam, col, 75).
(21 - 23) naṭuvil kuḻintu vaṭṭittu amainta - puṟattē naṭuviṟ kuḻiyāki vaṭṭamā yamainta, paṭupoṉ kiṇṇam - cempoṉṉālāṉa kiṇṇamāṉatu, mukam kaviḻttu aṉṉa - muṉpakkam kaviḻttu vaittāṟpōla, mōvāy nivanta muṟaimaiyāṉum - vāyiṉ mōvāy naṭuviṟ ciṟitu kuḻiyākic cuṟṟi uyarntuviḷaṅkum vakaimaiyālum,
vaṭṭittal, vaṭṭamātal maruntil kaṇicci varunta vaṭṭittu (puṟam, 42). `paṭupoṉ kiṇṇa meṉpataip `poṉ paṭukiṇṇa meṉak koṇṭu, `poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kiṇṇam eṉap poruḷuraittuk koḷka; `nivattal uyartal; āvatu iṅku eṭuttuttōṉṟutal.
(24 - 27) kiḷai ari nāṇal kiḻaṅku - kappukaḷ ariyappaṭṭa nāṇaṟpulliṉ kiḻaṅkāṉatu, maṇaṟku īṉṟa muḷaiyum - maṇaliṉ kaṇ viṭṭa veḷḷiya muḷaiyum, muruntum - mayiliṟakiṉaṭiyum, viḷainīr muttamum - kaṭalnīril viḷaiyum veṇmukattukkaḷum, kūrntu niraintu valleraṉa oḷivirintu eḻuntu oḷir eyiṟṟoṭu poruntuta lāṉuma - kūrmaiyāyum varicaiyāyum valimaiyāyum amaintu veḷēleṉṟu oḷiviṭṭu niṉṟu viḷaṅkumpaṟkaḷōṭu oṉṟiyamaitalāṉum,
mēṟ kappukaḷai yarintu naṭṭa nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku viraivil muḷaiviṭutalālum, ammuḷai kūrmaiyāy veṇṇiṟattavā yiruttalāluṅ kiḷaiyari nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku maṇaṟ kīṉṟa muḷai uvamaiyāka eṭukkappaṭṭatu; ivvaṭi akanāṉūṟṟil tāvil  naṉpoṉ (212) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷilirunteṭutta mēṟkōḷ.
`maṇaṟku itiṟ kuvvurupu ēḻaṉ poruḷil vanta vēṟṟumai mayakkam; nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅkiṉ muḷaiyum muruntum muttamum  muṟaiyē veṇmaiyiṟ kūrmaikkum, veṇmaiyil varicaikkum, veṇmaiyil vaṉmaikkum uvamaikaḷeṉka. īṇṭu varum `oṭu vurupu oppumaipporuḷatākum; itu cēṉāvaraiya ruraiṟiyaṟ kāṇka (tolkāppiyam, col 13).
(28 - 30) kovvaik kaṉiyum koḻuviya pavaḻamum - civanta kovvaip paḻamum ceḻumaiyāṉa cenniṟam amainta pavaḻamum, cevviya alleṉa - tamakku nikarāṉa ciṟanta cenniṟa muṭaiya valla veṉṟu, ilavu itaḻ puraiyum cennīr itaḻoṭu maṉṉutalāṉum - ilavamara malariṉitaḻkaḷai ōkkuñ civanta taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiya utaṭukaḷoṭu poruntutalāṉum,
kovvaip paḻattaik kiḷḷai kōtutalāṉum, pavaḻakkallaik kaṭaintutuḷaiyiṭṭu makaḷiraṇitalāṉum avaipiḷḷaiyā ritaḻkaḷukku oppākat takā; maṟṟu ilavamalariṉ itaḻō aṅṅaṉam ūṟupaṭutaliṉmaiyiṉ atuvē avaritaḻukku oppākat takkateṉṟavāṟu; ilavitaḻc cevvāy iḷamut tarumpa eṉṟār cilappatikārattilum (14, 136). nīrmai iyalpākiya kuṇam, niṉṉuṭai nīrmaiyum eṉṉun tirukkōvaiyāril iccol ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka.
(31 - 36) muttak kōvai muṟaimuṟai vaitta nattuvarc ceppu naṉkaṉam tiṟanteṉa - muttu mālaikaḷ varicai varicaiyāy vaikkappaṭṭa niṟañ ciṟanta pavaḻac cimiḻiṉ poruttu vāyāṉatu aḻakitākat tiṟantāṟpōlavum, arakku uru ūṭṭiya āmpal naṟumukai taḷai nekiḻntu alarnta taṉmaittu eṉṉa - cenniṟattait tōyvitta alliyiṉ nalla pūmoṭṭāṉatu kaṭṭu aviḻntu malarnta viyalpiṉatu pōlavum, muḷ eyiṟu ilaṅka - kūriya paṟkaḷ oḷikāṭṭi viḷaṅka, nakutoṟum nakutoṟum cevvāy tōṉṟum avvāṟṟāṉum - nakaikkumpōtu nakaikkum pōtellām apperumāṉatu civanta vāyāṉatu viḷaṅkit teriyum avvakaiyiṉālum;
nakutoṟum nakutoṟum muḷḷeyiṟu ilaṅkac cevvāy tōṉṟumeṉat toṭaricaittuk koḷka. uvamaikaḷil muṉṉaiyatu paṟkaḷukkum piṉṉaiya vāykkumeṉat teḷiyaṟpālaṉa. `nattuvar eṉpatil na - ciṟappupporu ḷuṇarttuvatōriṭaic col; nacceḷḷai, nappālattaṉ, nakkīraṉ eṉṟāṟpōla eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (cīvaka cintāmaṇi, 482).
naṉkaṉam -naṉṟāka, aḻakitāka; nāṭaka makaḷirkku naṉkaṉam vakutta eṉpuḻi (maṇimēkalai, 2, 30) ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka.
eṉa, uvamap poruḷil vantatu.
`arakku, civappu; uru, niṟam; arakkuṇ pañcikaḷ tiraṭṭi (kaṉakamālai, 8) eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇiyil iccol ip poruṭṭātal kāṇka.
(37-39) eḻum iṭam kuḻintu -mutaṟkaṇ eḻukiṉṟa iṭattiṟ kuḻiyāki, viḻumitiṉ oḻuki - cīritāka nīṇṭu, taḷavu arumpu eṉṉa vaḷam kaṉintu ilaṅki - mullai arumpai oppa aḻaku mutirntu viḷaṅki, muṉ eḻu mūkkiṉ taṉmaiyāṉum - mukam eḻumpiya mūkkiṉ ṟaṉmaiyiṉālum;
vaḷam, aḻaku; kaṉital, iṅku `mutirtal eṉṉum poruṭṭu; kaṉṉimai kaṉiyāk kaikkiḷaik kāmam eṉṉum paripāṭalil (11, 136) iccol ipporuḷil vantatu.
(40-42) ōr aruḷ kaṭal iṅku īr uru vākik karai puṇarntu kiṭanta muṟaimaipōla - ōr aruṭṭaṉmaiyuṭaiya kaṭalāṉatu iṅku iraṇṭu uruvaṅkaḷāyp pirintu karai poruntik kiṭanta vakaimaipōla puruvakkoṭi kaṭavāp peruviḻiyāṉum - puruvamākiya oḻuṅkiṉaik kaṭantu cellātu amaiyum periya viḻikaḷālum,
`aruṭkaṭal eṉappaṭṭatu, kaṇkaḷiṉ aruṭ ṭaṉmaiyai eṇṇi; uvamaiyaik karuttūṉṟuka.
koṭi - paṭarkoṭi, oḻuṅku eṉa iruporuḷum uraittuk koḷka; puruvattai nōkkiyavaḻi avai paṭar koṭiyai yottiruntaṉa; kaṇṇākiya kaṭalai nōkkiyavaḻi avai ak kaṭalukku vakutta karaiyoḻuṅkai yottiruntaṉa veṉka.
(43-45) viraiaviḻ tāmarai naṟai ukutteṉṉa - maṇam virikkuñ centāmarai malar tēṉ corintāṟpōla, kēṭṭoṟum iṉikkum pāṭṭoṭu taḻīic ceḻuvāy malarnta moḻiyiṉāṉum - kēṭkum pōtellān teviṭṭātu cuvaikkiṉṟa icaikaḷuṭaṉ cērntu aḻakiya malarvāyiṉ kaṇiruntu tōṉṟuñ coṟkaḷālum;
pāṭṭu, icai; piḷḷaiyāratu malarvāyp piṟakkuñ coṟkaḷ icaiyiṉimai vāyntaṉa eṉṟapaṭi.
itu kāṟum murukap pirāṉṟaṉ piḷḷaimaik kōlattai niṉaintu aṇinturaittatākum; mēl varuvaṉa avaṉṟaṉ kaṭṭiḷamaik kōlattaiyun tirumaṇak kōlattaiyum niṉaintu puṉainturaittatākum eṉka.
(46-48) valampuri aṉṉa vāl naṟum kaḻuttoṭum - valam puric caṅkai otta veṇmaiyāṉa nalla kaḻuttōṭum, ēṟṟiṉattu eruttiṉ murippu eṭuttataṉṉa kuvavip puṭaitta piṭariṉālum - ciṟanta ēṟukaḷiṉ iṉattaiccērnta eruttu māṭṭiṉ imil uyarntāṟ pōṟ ṟiraṇṭu parutta piṭariṉālum;
tirumēṉiyeṅkum nīṟu pūcappaṭṭiruttaliṉ veṇmai yāṉa kaḻutteṉṟār; iṉi, vāṉ naṟuṅ kaḻuttu eṉappirittu aḻakiya nalla kaḻutteṉalumām; aḻakāvatu caṅkupōl vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa vaṭivuṭaiyatāyiruttal.
eṭutteṉṟatu, iṅku uyarnteṉṉumporuṭṭu; kāḷaiyān taṉmaimikunta imilēṟu eṉṟaṟku `ēṟṟiṉat teruttiṉ eṉappaṭṭatu.
`piṭareṉpatu, puṟaṅkaḻuttu, murippu - imil (cilappatikāra urai, 17).
(49-62) muḻavu eṉac carintu -  mattaḷam pōṟ carivuṭaiya tāy, val valam eṉat tiraṇṭu - valiyatiraḷ kallai oppat tiraṭciyuṭaiya tāy, varai eṉa nimirntu - malaiyaippōl uyarntu, piṉ eḻu eṉat tiṇintu - piṉṉum irupput tūṇpōl tiṭpam vāyntu, malaiyoṭu poruta mālkaḷiṟu eṉṟu pulavōrkūṟum poruṇmoḻi kāṭṭa - malaiyiṉoṭu pōrceyta periya āṇyāṉai yeṉṟu pulavarkaḷ uraikkum meyyuraikku ilakkiyamāka, yāṉai eṉṉum pālnaku tirumoḻi koṅkai maruppiṉ nuṅkak kutti orupāl perumpōr viḷaippa - teyvayāṉaiyeṉṟu collappaṭum amiḻtattaiyum ikaḻum iṉcolliṉai yuṭaiya ammaiyār tamatu koṅkaiyākiya kūrṅ kompiṉāl murukavēḷ mārpan taṉṉai viḻuṅka aḻutti orupakkattil mikunta kātaṟpōr ceyya, orupāl - maṟṟoru pakkattil, vaḷḷi yeṉṉum kaḷ aviḻ kuḻalāḷ - vaḷḷiyammaiyār eṉappaṭun tēṉcintuṅ kūntalaiyuṭaiya pirāṭṭiyār, taṭamulai vētiṉ - tamatu akaṉṟa koṅkaiyākiya oṟṟaṭattāl, paṭupuṇ oṟṟa uḷam koṇṭu eḻīi - teyvayāṉai yammaiyār muṉpu kātaṟpōr ceytamaiyāl uṇṭāṉa puṇṇai oṟṟutaṟku uḷam niṉaintu eḻuntu, uṟum iṭaṉ paṭāatu orupuṟam oṟṟa varum muṟai nōkki - ap puṇṇuṟṟa iṭattiṟ paṭātapaṭi maṟṟōriṭattil oṉṟutaṟku varun taṉmaiyaik kaṇṭu, koḻunaṉai piṇait tamarā naṟum toṭalai - am murukavēḷ mārpiṟ poruntik kiṭakkiṉṟa koḻumaiyāṉa moṭṭukaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa maṇaṅkamaḻuṅ kaṭappa mālaiyāṉatu, muṟuvalittu eṉṉa muṟukku uṭaintu acaiya - puṉṉakai purintāṟpōlak kaṭṭuviṭṭu malarntu puraḷa, vāḷvali nilaiyum tōḷiṉālum - vāṭpaṭaiyiṉ vallamai nilaitta tōḷkaḷālum;
ulam - tiraḷkal (tivākaram). eḻu - irupputtūṇ (tivākaram). 
malaiyoṭuporuta mālyāṉai iḷampūraṇar urai (tolkāppiyam, col, 73)
pulavōr kūṟum poruṇmoḻi kāṭṭat teyvayāṉai koṅkaikaḷ murukavēḷ mārpoṭu porutaṉaveṉka; vaḷḷi mēluḷḷa cīṟṟattāl teyvayāṉai murukappirāṉ mārpoṭu porutaṉaḷeṉavun, taṉ kātalaṟku avaḷ ceyta ūṟu pāṭṭiṉait tīrppāḷpōl vaḷḷi taṉ taṭamulai vētu koṇṭu oṟṟiṉāḷeṉavum orunayan tōṉṟa uraittār.
nuṅka - viḻuṅka; mārpiṉkaṇ aḻuntiya kuttiṉai ammārpu ēṟṟu viḻuṅkiyatai yottateṉa murukappirāṉukku ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa vēṭkaiyiṉaik kuṟippiṭṭār; nuṅkal viḻuṅkutaṟ poruṭṭātal cīvakacintāmaṇi (4, 244) nacciṉārk kiṉiya ruraiyiṟ kāṇka.
iṭappuṟam amarntirukkum teyvayāṉaiyammaiyārāl nērnta iṭatupakkattup puṇṇai oṟṟi āṟṟātu, valappuṟam amarntirukkum vaḷḷiyammaiyiṉ koṅkai perumāṉukku valatu puṟattilēyē poruntumākaliṉ, uṟum iṭaṉ paṭāatu orupuṟam oṟṟa varumuṟai nōkki eṉṟaruḷic ceytār.
vētu - vemmai oṟṟaṭam; mulaivētiṉ oṟṟi eṉṟār kalittokaiyilum (106).
`kaḷḷaviḻ kuḻalāḷ eḻīi orupuṟam oṟṟa varumuṟai nōkki eṉat toṭar kūṭṭuka. `eḻutal, iṅku uḷḷaveḻuc ciyaik kuṟittatu.
kaṭappamālaiyiṉ malar malarntirukkun tōṟṟattai nakuvatākak kūṟiyatu taṟkuṟippēṟṟam.
marā- veṇkaṭampu (tivākaram); piḷḷaiyār uvantatu kaṭappa mālaiyātal uruḷiṇarkkaṭampiṉ olitārōyē eṉṉum paripāṭalālum (5, 81), marāat turuḷpūntaṇṭār puraḷummārpiṉaṉ eṉṉun tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyālum (10-11) aṟiyappaṭum.
ippakutikaḷiṉ uruvaka uvamaik kaṟpaṉaiyaḻakukaḷ peritum makiḻaṟpālaṉa vākum.
(63-69) kaival vēkaṭi - kaivaṉmai yuṭaiya maṇiyiḻaip pōṉ, aitu uṟak kaṭaintu - aḻaku keḻumat tiraṭṭi, vaḻuvaḻuppu ākkiya - maḻamaḻappākac ceyta, koḻum tukirp palakai - vaḷamāḷa pavaḷappalakaikaḷ, puṇarntu oruṅku iraṇṭu kiṭantatupōla - iraṇṭu cērntu oṉṟāypporuntik kiṭantatupōla, kaṇakaṉṟu ōṅkiya kaviṉ keḻu mārpattu - iṭam akaṉṟu vaḷarnta aḻaku poruntiya mārpiṉkaṇ, yāṉai maruppu uḻuta cuvaṭupōlavum - yāṉaiyiṉ kompu uḻutamai yāl uṇṭāṉa aṭaiyāḷam pōlavum, varaiyoṭu paṭarnta vallipōlavum - malaiyiṉmēṟ paṭarnta vaḷḷik koṭi pōlavum, vari mūṉṟu oḻukiya tiruviṉāṉum - mūṉṟu kōṭukaḷ nīṇṭu kiṭanta mēṉmaiyiṉālum;
vēkaṭi - maṇimācu akaṟṟuvōṉ; tirumaṇikuyiṟṟunarai vēkaṭikaḷ eṉpar cilappatikāra uraikārar (5, 46). `vēkaṭi tamiḻccol, itu vaṭamoḻikkaṇṇuñ ceṉṟatu. aitu - aḻaku, (cilappatikāraurai, 3, 152).
piḷḷaiyārtan tirumēṉi niṟañ civappākaliṉ avaratu tirumārpattiṟkup pavaḷappalakaiyiṉai uvamai kūṟiṉār. yāṉaiyeṉṉuṅ kuṟippiṉālum `valli yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippi ṉālum avaiyiraṇṭum muṟaiyē `teyvayāṉai `vaḷḷi yeṉṉuṅ kātaṟ kiḻattiyariṉ koṅkaimukaṭu uriñciya kātaṟceyalkaḷaik kuṟippavāyiṉa.
mārpiṉkaṇ varimūṉ ṟoḻukutal ciṟanta āṭavariṉ nallilakkaṇamākum; perumaiyaiyuṭaiya mārpiṟ kiṭakkiṉṟa uttama ilakkaṇamākiya civanta mūṉṟu variyiṉaiyum (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyurai, 106) eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ivvuṇmaiyai naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟuvar. varaiyakaṉ mārpiṭai variyu mūṉṟuḷa (kēmacariyā rilampakam, 51) eṉpatu cintāmaṇi.
(70-73) maḻaiyum maraṉum kuḻaintu oruṅkuōṭa - nīr curanta puyaluṅ kaṟpakamaramuñ cōrntu uṭaṉkulaiya, viḻumiya poruḷpala vēṇṭinarkku utavum - ciṟanta poruḷkaḷpalavum vēṇṭiya aṭiyārkku aḷikkum, tāḷ toṭu taṭakkaiyil muḻantāḷait toṭum aḷavu nīṇṭa periyakaiyil, tāy uvantu ītta - aṉṉaiyākiya umaippirāṭṭi makiḻntu koṭutta, vālitiṉ viḷaṅkum vēliṉāṉum veḷḷitāka miḷirum vēṟpaṭaiyiṉālum;
`maraṉ īṇṭuk kaṟpakamaram; aḵtu intira ṉulakil uḷḷatu; maḻaiyuṅ kaṟpakamum intira ṉulakiṟkuk kīḻ maṇṇulakiṟ payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷai maṭṭumē taravallaṉa vaṉṟi, vāṉulakiṟku mēluḷḷa uyarnta tēvar nilaikaḷaiyum avaiyiṟṟiṟkum mēlāṉa pēriṉpa vīṭṭulakiṉaiyun taravallaṉa alla. maṟṟup, piḷḷaiyār tantirukkaikaḷō maṇṇulaka viṉpaṅkaḷoṭu, viṇṇulaka viṉpaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṟkum mēlāka vīṭṭulaka viṉpamum aḷikka vallaṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
āṇmakkaḷiṟ ciṟantārtaṅ kaikaḷ muḻantāḷaḷavum nīṇṭiruttal, tāṭṭāḻ taṭakkait taṉimati veṇkuṭaiyāṉ eṉṉum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālaic ceyyuḷāṉ uṇarka. `ītta koṭutta eṉap poruḷ taral cempoṉ vayiriyarkku ītta (puṟam, 9) eṉpuḻiyuṅ kāṇka.
(74-77) pālnurai mukanta paricoṭu porūum - pāliṉ nuraiyai aḷḷi eṭutta taṉmaiyai okkum, tūveḷ aṟuvai acaii - tūya veḷḷiya āṭaiyai uṭuttu, mūvakai maṭiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅki - mūṉṟu vakaiyāṉa maṭippukaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅki, vaṭivuṭaṉ carintu - aḻakiya vaṭivuṭaṉ carivuṭaiyatāy, naṭattoṟum kuluṅkum vayiṟṟiṉāṉum - naṭakkuntōṟuṅ kuluṅki yacaikiṉṟa vayiṟṟiṉālum;
`pālnurai, tūymaikkum veṇmaikkum, meṉmaikkum uvamaiyākaliṉ, `tūveḷḷaṟuvai yeṉa aṟuvai vitanturaikkappaṭṭatu. `aṟuvai, taṟiyil nīḷaneytatu piṉ tuṇṭutuṇṭāy aṟukkappaṭutaliṉ vanta peyar.
`acaii, uṭuttu, iḵtipporuṭṭātal, pulittōlai araikku acaittu (maḻapāṭi, 1) eṉavaruñ cuntarar tēvārattāl teḷiyappaṭum.
`maṭi, eṉṟatu, iṅku vayiṟṟiṉ cataimaṭippukku.
(78-82) eḻum iṭam tacaintu - eḻukiṉṟa iṭam tacai koṇṭu, piṉ - ataṟkumēl, oḻukutoṟum melintu - nīḷumiṭamellām varavara melivuṭaiyatāy, maḻaik kaṭak kaḷiṟṟiṉ puḻaikkai ottum - maḻaikku nikarāy oḻukum matattiṉaiyuṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉ tutikkaikku oppākiyum, koḻum kaṉi vāḻaiyiṉ kōḻ arai ēyppa ūṟum oḷiyum vīṟuuṟat tikaḻum - koḻuviya paḻaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya vāḻai maraṅkaḷiṉ vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa aṭimaraṅkaḷai oppa maḻamaḻa veṉṉum ūṟum paḷapaḷaveṉṉum oḷiyum ciṟappuṟa viḷaṅkum, niṟam keḻu marapiṉ kuṟaṅkiṉāṉum - cenniṟam poruntiya taṉmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya toṭaikaḷālum;
eḻumiṭam, tōṉṟum mutaliṭam; maḻaikkaṭam, uvamat tokai; puḻaikkai, toḷaiyuṭaiya kai. ūṟu, toṭṭuṇarvu. piḷḷaiyār tirumēṉi cenniṟattatākaliṉ `niṟaṅkeḻu kuṟaṅku eṉṟār; niṟam īṇṭuc cenniṟam.
(83-89)eppāl poruḷum tuppuṟa vaikum nilaikkaḷam eṉpatu peyariṉ kāṭṭi - evvakaiyāṉa poruḷun taṉpāl aṇukiṉ avai aḻuk kakaṉṟu tūytāki nilaipeṟutaṟkuriya nilaikkaḷamām uṇmaiyait taṉ peyariṉālēyē viḷakki, muḷai iḷa  ñāyiṟṟiṉ  - kīḻpāl eḻukiṉṟa iḷaiya katirava ṉetirē, taḷai ñekiḻntu virinta - muṟukkaviḻntu malarnta, eri akai tāmarai pōla-  nerupput taḻaittateṉa viḷaṅkun tāmaraimalarai oppa, viripukaḻt toṇṭarkaḷ etiril kaṇṭiṭa - paraviya aruṭpukaḻai yuṭaiya aṭiyārkaḷ etirē kaṇṭiṭalum, malarnta nikar il iṉpa naṟai muṟai oḻukki - uṭaṉē malarntu oppillāp pēriṉpameṉṉun tēṉait toṭarpāka oḻukac ceytu, perumaiyoṭu vaikum tiruvaṭiyāṉum - aruṭ perumaiyoṭu vīṟṟirukkun tiruvaṭiyiṉālum;
tuppu - tūymai (piṅkalantai), `ñāyiṟṟiṉ eṉpataṉ piṉ etirē eṉa oru col varuvitturaikka.
`muḷai, iṅku viṉai. `eriyakai tāmaraiyeṉa iṅku vantāṟpōlavē eriyakaintaṉṉa vēṭil tāmarai yeṉap porunarāṟṟuppaṭai (159) yiṉ kaṇṇum varutal kāṇka. eriyakaintateṉa veṉṟu cila varuvittuk koḷka. ñāyiṟṟi ṉetirē tāmarai malarntāṟpōla aṉpoḷi tatumpun toṇṭarkaḷetirē piḷḷaiyār tan tiruvaṭittāmarai malarāniṟku meṉka. `aṭi ellāvaṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭaiyāvatu. tāṭṭāmaraikāṭṭit taṉkaruṇait tēṉkāṭṭi eṉṟa tiruvācakat tirumoḻiyum iṅṅaṉamē nuvaṉṟamai yaṟika (tikuvammāṉai, 6).
(90-102) peru maṇal ekkarc ciṟukuṭi vāḻkkaik kuṟumoḻi mākkaḷ - uyaramāṉa maṇaṟkuṉṟiṉ kaṇṇē ciṟiya kuṭivāḻkkaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya puṉcoṟpēcum makkaḷākiya neytalnilattu āṭavar, uṟumīṉ paṭuttum - kaṭaliluḷḷa periya mīṉkaḷai valaiyilē akappaṭuttum, āḻtirai mūḻki vīḻmaṇi eṭuttum - āḻamāṉa kaṭaliṉ kaṇ mūḻki virumpattakka muttuc cippikaḷai eṭuttum, karum kaṭal ūrnta veḷvaḷai vāriyum - kariya kaṭalnīriṉmēṉ mitantu vanta veḷḷiya caṅkukaḷai aḷḷiyum, nirainirai karaiyil kuvaiyiṉar cella - varicai varicaiyākak karaiyiṉkaṇ avaṟṟaik kuvittaṉarāy ēka, (aññāṉ) maṟikayal ukaḷum tirumuka nuḷaicciyar - ōṭi mīḷuṅ kayaṉmīṉkaḷākiya kaṇkaḷ taṉ irupuṟamum puraḷukiṉṟa aḻakiya mukattaiyuṭaiya avartam neytal nilattup peṇṭir, koṭicci tanta kaṭuppuṭai naṟavum - kuṟiñci nilattup peṇkaḷ koṇarntu koṭutta kaṭuttalaiyuṭaiya kaḷḷum, kuṭatti koṇarnta palappaṭu mutiraiyum - mullainilattup peṇṭir koṇṭuvantu tanta avarai kārāmaṇi mutaliya palavākiya payaṟuvakaikaḷum, uḻatti eṭutta viḻuttaku paḻaṉum - marutanila makaḷir cumantuvanta ciṟappumikka vāḻai mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷum, peṟuvilaiyāka - tām peṟukiṉṟa vilaip poruḷāka, ciṟumīṉ noṭuttum - avaṟṟiṟku īṭākat tam nilattu vaḷaṅ kaḷākiya ciṟiya mīṉkaḷai viṟṟum, iṭaiiṭai muttam puṭaipuṭai aḷantum - ataṉōṭu ataṉiṭaiyiṭaiyē muttuk kaḷai pakkaṅkaṭōṟum aḷantu koṭuttum, kōṭukoṇṭu pīṭuuṟa māṟiyum - caṅkukaḷai yeṭuttup perumaipeṟa māṟṟikkoṇṭum, orucār pēroli nikaḻtta- iṅṅaṉamāka orupakkattil ip paṇṭamāṟṟup pērōcaiyai viḷaikka;
ekkar - maṇaṟkuṉṟu (puṟanāṉūṟu, 177).
kuṟumoḻi; piṟarai yikaḻntupēcuñcol eṉpar aṭiyārkkunallār (cilappatikāram, 6, 63).
`makkaḷeṉṉātu `mākka ḷeṉappaṭṭār avar ciṟucoṟpēcutaliṉ.
`tirai ākupeyar vīḻ - virumpappaṭum; tām vīḻvār meṉṟōḷ eṉpuḻippōla (tirukkuṟaḷ). `maṇi īṇṭu muttu maṇikaḷaiyuṭaiya cippiyiṉai yuṇarttiyatu ākupeyar.
`kuvaiyiṉar muṟṟeccam; cella, kaṭalpaṭu poruḷ kaḷaik karaiyiṟ koṇarntu kuvittappaṭiyāy nīrukkuṅ karaikkuñ ceṉṟu koṇṭirukka veṉka.
tēṭkaṭup paṉṉa nāṭpaṭu tēṟal (puṟanāṉūṟu, 392) eṉpataṉāl īṇṭuk kūṟiya naṟaviṉ kaṭupput tēḷiṉ kaṭuppai yuṭaiya tātal aṟika. naṟavu- kaḷ, naṉaimutir cāṭi naṟaviṉ eṉṟār puṟattilum, (297). noṭuttal - viṟṟal, mīṉnoṭuttu neṟkuvaii (paṟam, 343). puṭai -pakkam, orupuṭai pāmpu koḷiṉum (nālaṭiyār, 148).
(102-108) oru cār - maṟṟoru pakkattil, paṉimalai nivanta paṇpupōla - paṉiyiṉāl mūṭappaṭṭa malaikaḷ uyarntuḷḷa taṉmaiyai oppa, cēṇtoṭa ōṅkiya uppuk kuṉṟam - vāṉam toṭum paṭi uyarnta uppu malaikaḷai, talaiyaḻittu umaṇar potiyuṟac cērttiya - ucciyiliruntu avaṟṟai yaḻittu uppu vāṇikar uppai mūṭṭaikaḷākkic cērtta, oḻukai - vaṇṭiyiṉ, uruḷai - cakkaraṅkaḷ, muḻumaṇal putaitaliṉ - cumaiyiṉāl avviṭattu niṟainta maṇaliṉuḷ aḻuntutaliṉ, piṭarkoṭuttu eḻuppik kaṭumpakaṭu urappum - avaṟṟait tam puṟaṅkaḻuttuk koṭuttu mēltūkkiyapaṭiyāy avvaṇṭiyiṟ pūṭṭiya valiya erutukaḷai vāyāl ataṭṭukiṉṟa, tāḷāṇ mākkaḷ talaip paṭum uñaṟṟum - muyaṟciyiṟ ciṟanta makkaḷiṉ mēmpaṭum uḻappum.
nivanta - uyarnta, mākkaṭal nivantu eḻutaruñ, ceñ ñāyiṟṟuk kaviṉai eṉṟār puṟattilum (4).
`cēṇ ākupeyarāy vāṉattai uṇarttum; `cērttiya, `ceyta veṉṉum peyareccat tiripu. umaṇar - uppu viṟpār; `oḻukai, vaṇṭi; iccoṟkaḷukku ipporuḷuṇmai nōṉ pakaṭṭumaṇa roḻukaiyoṭuvanta eṉṉuñ ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭai (55) yiṟ kāṇka.
`kaṭumpakaṭu eṉpuḻik kaṭumai valimaiyai yuṇarttā niṉṟatu. `neytal nilamākaliṉ, kaḻiyiṭattu maṇalil uruḷai kaḷ putaital kūṟappaṭṭatu.
`tāḷ muyaṟci; `talaippaṭum eṉpatai iṅkuc `ceyyu meṉṉum eccamākak koḷka. `tāḷāṇmākkaḷ eṉṟatu īṇṭuvaṇṭi yōṭṭuvārai.
`uñaṟṟu muyaṟci; māṇṭa uñaṟṟilavarkku (64) eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ.
(109-112) pulavuk kaḻi muḷaitta tuvar itaḻt tāmarai - pulālnāṟṟam nāṟukiṉṟa kaḻiyiṉkaṇ vaḷarnta civanta itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya centāmarai malaril, puṉṉai nuṇtātu poṟippa - puṉṉai malariṉ poṉṉiṟamāṉa nuṇṇiya makarantappoṭikaḷ mēliruntu cinta, tuṉṉiya uruvoṭu viḷaṅkum tōṟṟam - aḵtu appoṭikaḷāl, niṟainta poṉṉuruvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa kāṭciyāṉatu, eriyiṭai - eriyum neruppiṉkaṇ, urukiya pacupoṉ kaviḻppa - nīrāy urukiya pacumpoṟ kuḻampaik kīḻmukamākak kaviḻttiṭutalum, ōṭiya tiruvoṭum poliyum cevviyum - aḵtu oḻukiya aḻakōṭu oppa viḷaṅkum aḻakum;
`poṟippa, iṅkuc cintiya veṉṉum poruṭṭu; itaṟkut `teṟippa veṉac cintāmaṇi (46) uraiyil uraikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutuvar.
`tuṉṉiya veṉpatu `niṟaintaveṉap poruṭaral, tuṉṉik kuḻaikoṇṭu tāḻnta kuḷirmaram eṉṉum nālaṭiyāriṟ kāṇka. (167).
`eri centāmarai malarukkum, `pacumpoṉ puṉṉainuṇ tātukkum uvamaiyākum.
cevvi - aḻaku (cūṭāmaṇinikaṇṭu, 11).
pacumpoṉṉaip pōlavē puṉṉaittātum mañcaḷniṟa muṭaiyatātal kāṇka.
tiruvoṭum, ōṭu oppumaikkaṇ vantatu.
(113-115) maruviya - poruntiya; neytal am kāṉal aitu uṟakkiṭanta oṟṟimānakaril - kaṭaṟkaraikkaṇ uḷḷa kaḻiyaṭutta cōlai aḻakumikak kiṭantu tiruvoṟṟimā nakariṉkaṇ.
kāṉal - kaḻikkaraiccōlai (piṅkalantai).
`aitu, aḻaku; piṟaviyaḷ āyiṉaḷ peṟṟiyum aiteṉa eṉpatu maṇimēkalai (10,2). orucār pēroli nikaḻtta, orucār (102) tāḷāṇmākkaḷ talaippaṭum   uñaṟṟum (108) tiruvoṭum poliyuñ cevviyum (113) maruviya neytalaṅ kāṉal kiṭanta tiruvoṟṟi mānakareṉak koḷka. `nikaḻtta, uñaṟṟum cevviyum maruviya, kāṉal kiṭanta tiruvoṟṟi mānakar eṉa viṉaimuṭikkappaṭum.
tiruvoṟṟiyūr neytalum marutamum mayaṅkiyatākaliṉ, iṅkut tiṇai mayakkaṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu. itu mutaṟceyyuḷilum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭamai niṉaivukūrka.
(115-116) kaṟṟait tōkai mayiṉmēliruntu payilutalāṉum - kaṟṟaiyā yirukkum pīliyiṉai yuṭaiya mayiliṉmēl eḻuntaruḷiyiruntu palakālum viḷaṅkutalālum;
`payiluta leṉpatu palakāl nikaḻcciyiṉai yuṇarttal karappiluḷḷamoṭu vēṇṭumoḻi payiṟṟi (puṟam, 34) yeṉṉumiṭattup palakāṟ kūṟi yeṉavarum paḻaiya uraikoṇṭu iccoṟku ipporuḷuṇmai teḷiyappaṭum.
(117-118) kāṇṭoṟum iṉikkum kāṭciyum uṭaiyaṉ - pārkkumpōtellām makiḻvait tarukiṉṟa tōṟṟamum uḷḷavaṉ; uraippat tīrāc ciṟappum uṭaiyaṉ - colli muṭiyāp perumaiyum uḷḷavaṉ;
uraippat tīrāc ciṟappeṉpatu, evvaḷavu coṉṉālum muṭitalillāta perumaiyeṉka. `ummai, eccam;
(119-123) tam uyirkku iṉiya cemmoḻip putalvarai nōkka nōkka - tamatu uyirkkiṉiya iṉcoṟ putalvarkaḷaip pārkkap pārkka, mīk kiḷar kātal tantaiyarkku eḻutalpōl - mēṉmēṉ mikukiṉṟa aṉpu tantaimārkku viḷaitalpōla, kaṇṭiṭum viḻi aḷavu amaiyāk kaḻiperum kātal - taṉṉaik kāṇuṅ kaṇkaḷiṉ aḷavil amaiyāta niṟainta pēraṉpiṉai, eytiṉārkku uṟukkum ceytiyum uṭaiyaṉ - taṉṉai aṇukikkāṇum pēṟupeṟṟa aṭiyārkaṭku viḷaikkuñ ceykaiyumuḷḷavaṉ;
`cemmoḻi yeṉpuḻic `cemmai yeṉpatu iṅkut tantaiyarkku iṉitākun taṉmaiyaik kuṟiyāniṉṟatu. `uṟukkum uṇṭākkum.
(124 - 125) urukeḻu tōṟṟam maruvuvaṉ eṉṟu - accam poruntiya tōṟṟattai mēṟkoḷvaṉō eṉa niṉaintu, veruvaral oḻiti iṉṟē - niṉ añcutalai oḻippāyāka ippoḻutē;
uru - accam, uru uṭkākum (tolkāppiyam, uriyiyal, 4); iṟaivaṉ taṉ aṭiyār añcutaṟkētuvāṉa taṉ ṟeyva vaṭivaik kāṭṭātu avar kaṇṭu makiḻtaṟkuttakka aḻakiya iḷavaṭiviṉaiyē kāṭṭi aruḷ ceyvaṉeṉpatu,
aṇaṅkucāl uyarnilai taḻīip paṇṭaittaṉ
  maṇaṅkamaḻ teyvat tiḷanalaṅkāṭṭi
eṉṉun tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai aṭikaḷāl (289-290) aṟiyap paṭum. veruvaral - añcutal, (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai, 241). `āl acai.
(125-128) murukaṉ kuḻavik kōlattum viḻumitiṉ amarvaṉ - murukapperumāṉ kuḻantai yuruvattiluñ ciṟantu tikaḻvaṉ; maṭavāṉ makaḷir taṭamulai kuḻaikkum iḷamaik kōlattum vaḷamaiyoṭu polivaṉ - maṭappattiṉai yuṭaiya peṇkaḷiṉ periya koṅkaikaḷaik kuḻaiyumpaṭi ceyyuṅ kāḷaip paruvattiluñ celvap polivuṭaṉ viḷaṅkuvaṉ; ataṉāṉ - ataṉāl;
`maṭavaral makaḷir - `maṭappattiṉaiyuṭaiya makaḷir eṉap poruḷuraippar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (neṭunalvāṭaiyurai, 39).
(129-135) miṉ eṉat tōṉṟik koṉṉuṟa maṟaintum - miṉṉalpōṟkāṇappaṭṭup payaṉillāmaṟ kaḻintum, vātuvaṉ naṭavāk kalimā pōlat tītuuṟu neṟiyil tiṟampaṭac ceṉṟum-kutiraip pākaṉāl naṭattappaṭāta cerukkumikka kutiraiyaip pōl tīmai mikukkum vaḻiyil tiṟamaiyāka ōṭiyum, pāku aṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉ paṇpupōla uṟutikoḷḷāc ciṟumaiyiṉ mikuntum - yāṉaip pākaṉaik koṉṟu neṟippaṭātu mīṟum yāṉaiyiṉ iyalpaip pōl oru naṉmaiyai mēṟkoṇṭoḻuka māṭṭāk kīḻmaiyil mikutippaṭṭum, oruvaḻi nilaiyātu ōṭip palavaḻik kavarkkum eṉ nilaimai il maṉaṉē - oru muṟaiyilum nilaippatillāmal ōṭip palavakaiyilum piḷavupaṭṭoḻuku kiṉṟa eṉatu nilaittalillāta neñcamē yeṉpatu.
maṉaṉē eṉpataṉ ēkāram viḷi; `miṉ tiṭumeṉat tōṉṟatalukku uvamai; `koṉ payaṉiṉmaip poruḷatu; koṉṉē vaḻaṅkāṉ (nālaṭiyār, 1, 9,) eṉpuḻippōla.
vātuvaṉ, kutirai ōṭṭuvōṉ (tivārakam). `kali, iṅkuc cerukkiṉai uṇarttum (puṟam. 15).
pāku, pākaṉ, (cilappatikāram, 15, 46). ciṟumaiyiṉ, ciṟumai yiṉkaṇ; kavarttal, piḷavu paṭutal (puṟam, 35). āvatu, orē niṉai vāyillāmaṟ pala niṉaivukaḷuṭaiyatātalaik kūṟiyavāṟām.
maṉaṉē (135) ataṉāṉ (128) veruvara loḻitiyāl (125) eṉa muṭikka.
eṉavē, maṉaṉē (135) eṉat talaivi taṉatu neñcattai viḷittu, naṟuṅ katuppoḷirum peruñ ciṟappāṉum (9) virinuta lāṉum (12) muṭimēṉ maṇi eṉak kuṭikoḷalāṉum (15) katuppu maruvutalāṉum (18) ōveṉum vaṭivoṭu mēvutalāṉum (20) mōvāy nivanta muṟaimaiyāṉum (23) eyiṟṟoṭu poruntu talāṉum (27) itaḻoṭu maṉṉutalāṉum (30) cevvāy tōṉṟu mavvāṟṟāṉum (36) mūkkiṉ ṟaṉmaiyāṉum (39) peruviḻiyāṉum (42) moḻiyiṉāṉum (45) piṭariṉāṉum (48) tōḷiṉāṉum (62) varimūṉṟoḻukiya tiruviṉāṉum (69) vēliṉāṉum (73) vayiṟṟiṉāṉum (77) kuṟaṅkiṉāṉum (82) tiruvaṭiyāṉum (89) mayiṉmēliruntu payilutalāṉum (116) kāṭciyumuṭaiyaṉ (117) ciṟappu muṭaiyaṉ (118) ceytiyum uṭaiyaṉ (123) tōṟṟam maruvuvaṉ eṉṟu (124) taṉ talaivaṉākiya tiruvoṟṟic cevvēḷiṉ eḻil nalaṅkaḷ kūṟik, kuḻavik kōlattum, amarvaṉ (126) iḷamaik kōlattum polivaṉa taṉāṉ (128) eṉṟum avaṉ kuḻavikkōlattilēyē iruppa vaṉallaṉ; avaṉ kaṭṭiḷamaik kōlattiṉ kaṇṇum polintu nampāl aṇuki naṅ kātaṟṟuyarattaiyuṅ kaṭṭāyan tīrppāṉ kāṇ eṉat tuṇivu viḷakki, veruvaral oḻitiyāl iṉṟē (125) eṉa aṟivuṟuttiyavāṟu teḷiyaṟpālatu. eṉavē icceyyuḷ eḻil nalaṅ kūṟi neñcaṟivuṟuttaṟkaṇ vantamai kāṇka veṉpatu. (22)
23. aruṭara vēṇṭal
maṉṉu moruyāṉai yūṭa maṟuyāṉai
  taṉṉutavi vaḷḷi taṭamulaika - ṭuṉṉuvippa
  voṟṟi nakarvaikum vōlō yoruperitō
  paṟṟi yeṉaiyāḷ paricu
(i-ḷ). maṉṉum oru yāṉai ūṭa - taṉakku oru kūṟāyp poruntun teyvayāṉai eṉṉum orutti taṉṉuṭaṉ ūṭutal ceyyumpaṭi, maṟuyāṉai taṉ utavi - taṉakku muṉṉōṉāṉa yāṉai mukak kaṭavuḷiṉatu aruḷutaviyāṉatu, vaḷḷi taṭa mulaikaḷ tuṉṉuvippa - vaḷḷiyammaiyāriṉ perutta koṅkaikaḷaip poruntuvippa, oṟṟi nakar vaikum vēlōy - ittakaiya kātal viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ tiruvoṟṟi nakaril eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum vēṟpaṭaiyai yuṭaiya murukapperumāṉē, oruperitō paṟṟi eṉai āḷ paricu - aṇuki ēḻaiyēṉai āṇṭaruḷun taṉmai tēvarīrukku oru peritākumō veṉpatu.
yāṉai mukak kaṭavuḷ iṅku `yāṉai yeṉappaṭṭār; avar, tam iḷavalākiya āṟumukak kaṭavuḷukku vaḷḷiyammaiyai maṇaṅ kūṭṭivaitta varalāṟu kanta purāṇattu `vaḷḷiyammai tirumaṇap paṭalattil 110-vatu ceyyuḷiliruntu 115-vatu ceyyuḷ varaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
paricu - paṇpu, taṉmai (tivākaram); pōrum paricu pukaṉ ṟaṉarō eṉṟār tirukkōvaiyārilum (182).
24. tōḻi talaivi kuṟippaṟital
paricu piṟitoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parumuttap paimpaṇaicūḻ
  liripuka ḻoṟṟi varuvēlar pōlum viṭaikkaḷiṟoṉ
  ṟaritiṟ peyarntiṅku vantatuṇ ṭōveṉpa raṅkucelvar
  teriviṟ periyar ceyalō ciṟiyareñ cēyiḻaiyē.
(i-ḷ) paricu piṟitu oṉṟu aṟiyēṉ - uṇmai vakai vēṟoṉṟun terintilēṉ, parumuttup paim paṇaicūḻ viripukaḻ oṟṟi varuvēlar pōlum - parumaiyāṉa muttaṅkaḷ uḷḷa paciyavayalkaḷ cūḻnta paranta pukaḻiṉaiyuṭaiya tiruvoṟṟi nakariṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷi viḷaṅkivarum vēlavar pōlum iṅkuvantu eṉṉai viṉāviyavar, viṭaikkaḷiṟu oṉṟu - āṇ yāṉaiyoṉṟu, aritiṉ peyarntu iṅku vantatu uṇṭō eṉpar - eyta eṉ ampiṉukku arumaiyākat tappi yōṭi ippakkam vantatuṇṭōveṉa eṉṉai viṉavuvar, aṅku celvar - yāṉ illai yeṉavē appakkam pōvar, terivil periyar ceyalō ciṟiyar - avar aṟiviṟ periyarā yiruntum aṭuttaṭuttup pōntu aṅṅaṉam viṉavuñ ceyaliṟ ciṟiyarāyu mirukkiṉṟār; em cēy iḻaiyē cevviya nakaikaḷai yaṇinta em talaiviyē eṉpatu.
iṅkuk kūṟappaṭum muttam vayalkaḷil viḷaiyum muttameṉka. 
iṅṅaṉamellāṅ kūṟuṅkāl talaivikku neñcaṅ kaṭuttatu kāṭṭum veḷikkuṟippukaḷ koṇṭu tōḻi avaḷatu kātaṟkaruttait terintukoḷvaḷ. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟik kuṟippaṟiyu miyalpu iruṅkaḷiyāyiṉṟiyā ṉiṟumāppa eṉṉun tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaiyāriṉālum (52) uṇarappaṭum.
25. kaḻaṟṟetirmaṟai
terivu, aṟivu; ipporuṭṭātal terivuṟak kēṭṭa tiruttaku nallīr eṉṉuñ cilappatikārattiṟ kāṇka. (30, 185).
(1-15) iḻai nekiḻ paruvaral eytiya makaḷiriṉ - tām aṇinta aṇikalaṅkaḷaik kaḻalac ceytaṟku ētuvāṉa taṅ kātalarp pirinta tuṉpamuṟum makaḷiraippōl, maḻaittaṭam tōḷkaḷ iḷaittut tōṉṟa - kuḷircci poruntiya periya tōḷkaḷ melintu tōṉṟavum, pāl kaṭalpōlat tūttakap paranta nūlpaṭu kēḷviyum cīrppaṭātu oḻiya - pāṟkaṭalaip pōl tūymaippaṭavirinta nūlārāycciyiṉ kaṇ aṭippaṭu kiṉṟa niṉatu kēḷviyaṟivum cemmaip paṭāmal tavaṟi yoḻiyavum, ciṟuneṟiccellum aṟiyā māntarum nalvaḻic cellat teḷḷitiṉ kāṭṭum iḻukkā naṭaiyum vaḻukkip pōka - ciṟumaiyāṉa tīyavaḻikaḷilē oḻukum naṉmai yaṟiyā makkaḷum nalla vaḻiyilē tirunti yoḻukumpaṭiyāka attuṇai viḷaṅkak kāṭṭum niṉatu paḻutupaṭāta oḻukkamum vaḻuvip pōkavum, innilai nīyum tiriyiṉ - ittakaiya iḻinilaiyil nīyum nilaitirivataṉāl, piṉ ataṟkumēl, oru pauvanīr vetumpiṉ vaḷāvunīr uṇṭō - oru kaṭalnīr veppaṅ koḷvataṉāl ataṉai vaḷāvukiṉṟa ataṉiṉuṅ kuḷirnta perunīrum vēṟu uṇṭō? illaiyē; kuṉṟu nilaitavaṟip pantupōl uruḷil ceṉṟu vaḻi aṭaikkum kuṉṟiyum uṇṭō - oru malaiyē taṉ iruppiṉiṉṟum piṟaḻntu pantu pōl uruṇṭu ōṭumāṉāl ataṉai ataṉ etiriṟceṉṟu vaḻiyaṭaittu nilaippikkuñ ciṟiya kuṉṟimaṇitāṉum uṇṭō? illaiyē; kaṭum kaḷiṟṟu oruttal toṭu maṭuttu uṇṇiṉ muruṅkātu ōmpum peruṅkalam uṇṭō - valiya āṇyāṉai oṉṟu  vayaliṉ uṭpukuntu uṇavu koḷḷumāṉāl aḵta ḻiyātapaṭi pātukākkum periya mūṭukalam oṉṟu  uṇṭō? illaiyē; eṉṟu pala kūṟi niṉṟu - eṉṟu pala aṟivurai kaḷellām eṉakkuk kūṟiniṉṟu, eṉai neruṅki naṉmai nāṭiya eṉ uyir naṇpā - eṉṉaik kaḻaṟi eṉ naṉmaiyai virumpiya eṉ uyir naṇpaṉē eṉpatu.
pirivāṟṟāt tuṉpam makaḷirai melivittaliṉ itaṟku muṉ avaruṟuppukaḷil iṟukkamāyirunta aṇikalaṉkaḷ nekiḻtal paṟṟi `iḻai nekiḻ paruvaral eytiya makaḷiriṉ eṉṟār.
paruvaral, tuṉpam; tū, tūymai; tūttaka, tūymaippaṭa; nīyum eṉṉum ummai uyarvu.
vaḷāvu nīruṇṭō, kuṉṟiyumuṇṭō, peruṅkala muṇṭō veṉṟatu, pāṅkaṉ talaimakaṉaip pārttu, `ellā muṇarntoḻukum periyaiyākiya nīyē nilaitavaṟi niṉṟāl niṉṉiṟ ciṟiyarākiya ēṉaiyōruḷ niṉṉait tiruttuvārum uḷarkol eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttuṅ kuṟipput tōṉṟutaṟ poruṭṭu. ivai iṅṅaṉamē,
kuṉṟam uruṇṭāṟ kuṉṟi vaḻiyaṭai yākātavāṟu pōlavum, yāṉaitoṭu uṇṇiṉ mūṭuṅ kalam illatu pōlavum, kaṭal vetumpiṉ vaḷāvunīr illatu pōlavum emperumāṉ niṉ uḷḷam aṟivu niṟai ōrppuk kaṭaippiṭiyiṉ varaittaṉṟik kaimik kōṭumē yeṉiṉ niṉṉait teruṭṭaṟ pāla nīrmaiyar uḷarō nī piṟaraitteruṭṭiṉallatu
eṉa iṟaiyaṉārakapporuḷ (3 -m nūṟpā) uraiyilum āciriyar nakkīrar mikavum aḻakāka uraittiruttal niṉaivu kūraṟpālatu.
`oruttal, yāṉai mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ āṇukkup peyar. ipporuḷ.
pulvāy puliyuḻai maraiyē kavari
  colliya karāmō ṭorutta loṉṟum
  vārkōṭ ṭiyāṉaiyum paṉṟiyu maṉṉa
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷāl (marapiyal, 35, 6) uṇarappaṭum. kaḷiṟukkuk kaṭumaiyāvatu valimai; `kaṭumai, valimai;
`toṭu, vayal; maruta nilamum .............. toṭuveṉalākum (10:656) eṉpatu piṅkalantai. yāṉai yeṉṉuṅ kuṟippāl ivvayalaik karumpu viḷai vayal eṉal okkum.
`muruṅkal, aḻital; kuṉṟiyum, itaṉ ummai etirmaṟai.
`neruṅki yeṉṟatu, iṅku arukil niṉṟeṉpataṉṟu, iṭitturaittu allatu kaḻaṟi eṉṉum poruṭṭu; tol kāppiyaṅ, kaṟpiyalil (150) piḻaittu vantirunta kiḻavaṉai neruṅki eṉpuḻi neruṅki eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar `kaḻaṟi eṉap poruḷuraittatūuṅ kāṇka.
(16-17) maṟi iḷam kaṉṟu - māṉ kaṉṟukaḷ, muṟi uṇum tāyoṭu - taḷirilaikaḷaik kaṟittut tiṉṉun tattan tāyuṭaṉ, kuḻaip putaltōṟum - iḷantaḷirkaḷ niṟaintuḷḷa ciṟu tūṟukaḷilellām, kuḻīi ukaḷa - kūṭit tuḷḷittuḷḷip pāytalālum;
maṟi eṉpatu māṉukkum peyarātal yāṭuṅ kutiraiyum navviyum uḻaiyum, ōṭumpulvāy uḷappaṭa maṟiyē eṉṉun tolkāppiya marapiyaṟ cūttirattiṟ (12) kāṇka.
putal, ciṟu tūṟukaḷ; paimputal erukki (25) eṉpatu mullaippāṭṭu, `kuḻīi aḷapeṭai, iṅkuccollicai; ukaḷa, ukaḷalālumeṉak kāraṇapporu ḷuraikka; mēl varuvaṉavellāmum iṅṅaṉamē uraikkaṟpālaṉa. ukaḷal - tuḷḷal, tirimaruppiralai pullarun tukaḷa eṉṟār aka nāṉūṟṟilum (14).
(18-19) varaiyāṭu varuṭai iṭaivāy tāṇṭi-malaikaḷil viḷaiyāṭukiṉṟa `varuṭai yeṉṉum oruvakai māṉ iṉam aṅkuḷḷa malaippiḷavukaḷait tāṇṭi, uḷḷam cerukkit tuḷḷit tiritara - uḷḷam eḻuccikoṇṭu kutittu ulāvu talālum;
`varuṭai itu `varuṭaimāṉ eṉa oruvakai māṉ. itu varaiyāṭu varuṭait tōṟṟam pōla (139) eṉa iṅṅaṉamē varum paṭṭiṉappālaiyaṭiyiṉ uraiyāl iṉitaṟiyappaṭum.
`cerukkutal, iṅku `eḻuccikoḷḷutal eṉakkoḷka. malaikaḷil tiritarum vēṟoruvakai māṉiṉaṅkaḷ makiḻcciyāl malaiveṭippukaḷait tāṇṭi viḷaiyāṭutal iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
(20-21) nilamtoṭṭu vīḻnta kuluṅku mayirk kavari - nilattiṟ paṭṭuk kīḻttoṅkiya kuluṅkukiṉṟa mayirkkaṟṟaiyai yuṭaiya `kavarimā veṉṉum oruvakai vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ, mel mela acaii mētakac cella - mella mella acaintu naṭantu perumaippaṭa ēkutalālum;
(22-23) muḷavumā tolaicciya vaṉkaṇ kāṉavar - muḷḷam paṉṟikaḷaik koṉṟa vaṉkaṇmai mikka vēṭṭuvar, ñelikōl pottiya neruppil kāycca - avaṟṟiṉ iṟaicci kaḷait tīkkaṭai kōlāl uṇṭākkiya neruppil iṭṭu vatakkutalālum;
`muḷavu eṉpatu, muḷḷam paṉṟiyiṉ peyar; mūṇṭeḻu ciṉattucceṅkaṇ muḷavu muḷḷarintu kōtta (kaṇṇappa, 20) eṉṉun periyapurāṇat tirumoḻiyil ipporuḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka. muḷavumā tolaicciya eṉa malaipaṭu kaṭāttiṉ kaṇṇum (176) ittoṭar iṅṅaṉamē varutal niṉaivu kūraṟpālatu. ñelikōl - tīkkaṭaikōl eṉavum, `pottutal - koḷuttutal eṉavum poruṭaral, olikaḻai picainta ñelicori yoṇpoṟi, paṭuñemal putaiyap potti eṉṉum akanāṉuṟṟiṟ (39) kāṇka.
(24-26) ōri paranta - nīlaniṟam paraviya, tēṉ iṟāl eṭumār - tēṉkūṭṭiṉai aḻitteṭuttal vēṇṭi, kaḻaikkaṇ kuṟaittu - mūṅkiṟ kaṇukkaḷait taṟittu, nalattaka iyaṟṟiya -naṉṟākac ceyta, mālpu vaittu iṭantoṟum kuṟavar ēṟa - kaṇṇēṇi porutti am malaikkāṭṭiṭaṅkaḷellāṅ kuṟavar ēṟutalālum;
`ōri, nīlaniṟam; ipporuḷuṇmai nīṉiṟavōri pāynteṉa (524) eṉṉum malaipaṭukaṭāttaṭiyālum, ōriyeṉpatu tēṉ mutirntāṟ parakkum nīlaniṟam eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟ (109) ṟuraiyālum aṟiyappaṭum.
`iṟāl, tēṉkūṭu; `eṭumār, viṉaiyeccam; `tītumaṟuṅ kaṟumār eṉpuḻippōla (puṟanāṉūṟu, 93). kuṟaittu, taṟittu; `mālpu mūṅkilēṇi; kaṇṇēṇiyeṉpatum atu; mālpu kaṇ ṇēṇiyiṉ pēr (7, 54) eṉpatu cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu.
`iṭantoṟum, tēṉkūṭukaḷ toṅkuñ ceṅkuttāṉa malaippāṟaikaḷiṉ iṭamelāmeṉpatu. kuṟavar, kuṟiñcinila makkaḷ; avar vēṭar.
(27-28) tiṉaik kuṟu makaḷir - tiṉainellai ulakkaiyāṟ kuttum peṇkaḷ, cuvaippaṭa miḻaṟṟum kiḷḷai uṟakkum vaḷḷai olippa - cevikku iṉimai yuṇṭākumpaṭi icaikkum  kiḷikaḷaiyum uṟaṅkavaikkum paṭiyāṉa ulakkaip pāṭṭiṉai olittalālum;
kuṟutal - kuttutal kolyāṉaik kōṭṭāl vetir neṟkuṟuvām nām eṉṟār kalittokaiyilum (42).
miḻaṟṟal - collal (tivākaram), iṅkē pāṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil vantatu.
ūḻmuṟai yulakkai yōcci vāḻiya, takumaṉaik kiḻavar tam vaḷaṅ kūṟi, makaḷir pāṭuvatu vaḷḷaippāṭṭē eṉṉun tivākarattāl vaḷḷai yiṉṉateṉṟuṇarka.
`uṟakkum, meṉmai valittup piṟaviṉaipporuḷ payantatu.
tiṉaikuṟu makaḷir icaipaṭu vaḷḷaiyum eṉṉum malaipaṭukaṭāttaṭiyōṭu (342) itaṉai yoppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(29-33) maruppu iṭaic cuṟṟiya poruppu uyar vēḻattup puḻaikkai ēyppa - tan tantaṅkaḷiṭattuc cuṟṟiya malaipōl uyarnta yāṉaiyiṉ tutikkaiyai oppa, viḻuttaka mutirnta tāḷoṭu vaḷainta kural - tiṉaippuṉattu naṉṟāka muṟṟiya tāḷkaḷuṭaṉ nuṉivaḷainta katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya tiṉaippayirkaḷ uḷḷa puṉattiṉkaṇ, paṭukiḷi ōppum viṭukavaṇ cuḻaṟṟi - appuṉattilē viḷainta tiṉaikkatirkaḷaik koṇṭupōka vantu viḻukiṉṟa kiḷikaḷai ōṭṭukiṉṟa kalviṭu kavaṇaik kaiyiṟ cuṟṟiyapaṭiyāy, kōlvaḷaik koṭicci - tiraṭci yamainta vaḷaiyalkaḷai yaṇinta kuṟattiyar, mēl itaṇ amara - uyarntu mēlē uḷḷa paraṇiṟ polivuṭaṉiruttalālum;
`cuṟṟiya puḻaikkai yeṉak koḷka. maruppiṭaic cuṟṟiya puḻaik kai, tāḷoṭu vaḷainta kural tiṉaippayirkku uvamai; kural, katir; `neṟkural kauviya (2) veṉa mutaṟceyyuḷiṉ kaṇṇum iḵtipporuḷil vantamai kāṇka.
`opputal, ōṭṭutal; viṭukavaṇ, eṟikavaṇ; āvatu kalleṟi kavaṇ. `kōḷvaḷai tiraṭci poruntiya vaḷaiyal; āykō lavir toṭi eṉṉum maturaikkāñciyil (563) ic col ipporuḷil varutal kāṇka. `itaṇ, paraṇ; neṭṭitaṇ ēṟum ip puṉattiṉaḷē (53) eṉpatu kallāṭam. ip pakutiyaik kuṟiñcippāṭṭiṟ pōnta
neṟkoḷ neṭuvetirk kaṇanta yāṉai
  muttār maruppiṉ iṟaṅkukai kaṭuppat
  tuyttalai vāṅkiya puṉiṟutīr peruṅkural
  naṟkōṭ ciṟutiṉaip paṭupuḷ ōppi
eṉṉum pakutiyōṭu (35-38) oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.
(34-35) irumkal viyal aṟai varippa - periya akaṉṟa kaṟpāṟaimēl taṅkiḷaikaḷ paṭṭuk kīṟumpaṭiyāka, tāaya nalamuṟu vēṅkai polam vī ukuppa - paranta aḻakumikka vēṅkaimaraṅkaḷ poṉṉiṟamāṉa tam pūkkaḷaik kīḻē coritalālum;
varittal - kīṟal, kōlañceytal, eḻutal eṉṉum poruḷatu; tāya-  paranta; iccoṟkaḷ kāyāñcemmal tāayp palavuṭaṉ, īyaṉmūtāy varippa eṉa akanāṉūṟṟil (14) ipporuḷil varutal kāṇka. `iruṅkalviyalaṟai eṉṉum ic coṟṟoṭar kuṟuntokaiyil niraivaḷai muṉkai (335) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil varutal kāṇka.
nalam, iṅku aḻaku, iḷanalam kāṭṭi (tirumurukāṟṟup paṭai, 290) eṉpuḻippōla. polam, poṉṉiṟam.
(36-42) tēkkuilai puytta puṉtalaic ciṟāar - tēkkilaikaḷaip paṟitta pulliya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kuṟac ciṟuvarkaḷ, mīkkiḷar kōṭṭil paritiyiṉ toṭutta tēttaṭai piṟaikkōṭu uḻuteṉak kīṇṭu - mēlē vaḷarntu viḷaṅkiya malaiyiṉ koṭumuṭiyil ñāyiṟu pōṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa tēṉaṭaiyiṉai nilāviṉ vaḷainta nuṉiyāṉatu uḻutuviṭavē ataṉāṟ kīṟuṇṭu, māyōṉ mārpil ceñcēṟu kaṭuppap pāyiḻiyum paṭunaṟavu mukantu - tirumāliṉ mārpiṉmēṟ pūciya civanta naṟumaṇakkuḻampai oppap pāyntoḻukukiṉṟa iṉiyatēṉai ōr ēṉattil mukantuvantu, pacum tiṉaip piṇṭiyōṭu acumpuuṟa aḷāy - putiya tiṉaimāvuṭaṉ at tēṉ tuḷikkumpaṭi kalantu, nirainirai vaittu viraivoṭum āra - varicaivaricaiyāka vaittu viraivāka uṇṇutalālum;
`puyttal, paṟittal; kōṭupuykkallātuḻakkum nāṭakēḷ (38) eṉak kalittokaiyil iḵtuipporuḷil varutalkaṇṭu  koḷka; iccol  ikkālattiṟ `piyttal eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
vēṭṭuvacciṟār eṇṇey muḻukkuk kāṇāmaiyiṉ avar tantalaimayir paḻuppuniṟamā yiruttal paṟṟip puṉṟalaic ciṟār eṉṟār; puṉmai - paḻuppu niṟam (piṅkalantai).
`paritiyum piṟaikkōṭuṅ kūṟiyatu malaiyiṉ uyarn tōṅkiya ucciyaik kuṟittaṟ poruṭṭeṉka.
tēṉ, aṭai eṉpaṉa oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittē eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl (puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal, 49) nilaimoḻiīṟu keṭṭu iṭaiyē takaravoṟṟu iraṇṭupeṟṟut `tēttaṭai yeṉap puṇarntaṉa.
`kīṇṭu, kīṟuṇṭu (tivākaram); kīṇṭa pāṟoṟum (acurartō. 15) eṉak kantapurāṇattum pōntatu. tirumakaḷ ñemuṅkap pullutaliṉ, avaḷ mārpattiṉ ceñcēṟu tirumāl mārpiṉkaṇṇum poruntuvatāyiṟṟu.
paṭunaṟavu, cuvaipaṭunaṟavu; piṇṭi-mā; `acumpu nīrttuḷi; acumpiṉiṟ ṟuṉṉi eṉṉun tirucciṟṟampalakkōvai yāril (1`49) ipporuṭṭāy varutal kāṇka.
ippakutiyiṉ kaṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa uvamaikaḷ mikka pulamait tiṟam vāyntavai.
(43) kaṇkaḷi koḷḷum paṉmalaiyaṭukkattu - kaṇkaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻcciyaṭaikiṉṟa palamalaikaḷai yaṭutta malaiccāralil,
aṭukkam - malaiccāral, (puṟanāṉūṟu, 168). `aṭukkattu ēḻaṉ urupu virittukkoḷka.
ukaḷa (17) tiritara (19) cella (21) kāycca (23) ēṟa (26) olippa (28) amara (33) ukuppa (35) āra (42) kaṇkaḷikoḷḷum paṉmalaiyaṭukkattu (43) eṉat toṭarntukoḷka veṉpatu.
(44-53) maravamum - kuṅkuma maramum; kuravamum - kurāmaramum, porikeḻu puṉkum- neṟporipōṉṟa pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya puṉka maramum, erikiḷar ceyalaiyum - tī eritalai oppa viḷaṅkum acōka maramum, viri iṇar māvum - virinta pūṅ kottu kaḷuṭaiya māmaramum, parāraic cāntum - parutta araikaḷai yuṭaiya cantaṉa maramum, pāvai ñāḻalum - cittirap pāvaipōl viḷaṅkum malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya kōṅka maramum, viritalai vāḻaiyum - talaiviritaluḷḷa vāḻai maramum, viyaṉ ciṉaip palavum - periya periya kiḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya palāmaramum, koṉṟaiyum - koṉṟai maramum, puṉṉaiyum - puṉṉai maramum, `tuṉṟiya naṟavamum - aṭarntuḷḷa aṉicca maram, eṉa ivaikaḷ, kuḻaimukam tōṟṟi - taḷir nuṉikaḷait tōṟṟi, koḻunaṉai arumpi - koḻumaiyāṉa mokkukaḷ arumpeṭuttu, puriaviḻ malaril ciṟuvaṭuk kiḷarntu - avvarumpukaḷ kaṭṭaviḻntu malarnta malarkaḷiṟ ciṟiya vaṭukkaḷ viḷaṅki, tīviḷiyāki - pacuṅkāy āki, tīm kaṉi tūṅki - iṉiya paḻaṅkaḷ toṅkaviṭṭu, taṇṇiḻal payantu - kuḷirnta niḻalait tantu, kaṇ kavar vaṉappiṉ - kaṇkaḷaik kavarkiṉṟa aḻakuṭaiyatāy, mukil kaṇṇuṟaṅkat talaimaiyoṭu polimē orupāl - mēkaṅkaḷ taṉ ucciyil amarntu uṟakkaṅ koḷḷumāṟu ammalaiccāraṟ cōlai uyarvuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum oru pakkalil eṉpatu.
maravam - kuṅkumamaram (tivākaram). kuravam - pāvaipōṉṟa pūkkaḷuṭaiya oruvakaimaram, naṟamalark kuravampāvai, niṟaiyap pūttaṇintu eṉṟār cintāmaṇiyilum (1270). puṉkamarattiṉ pūkkaḷ neṟpori pōṉṟiruttaliṉ `porikeḻupuṉku eṉṟār; poriyeṉap puṉku aviḻ akaṉṟuṟai eṉpatu akanāṉūṟu (116).
tīkkoḻuntu eritalpōl attuṇaic cevvoḷiyuṭaṉ acōkiṉ taḷirkaḷ viḷaṅkutalāl `erikiḷar ceyalai eṉappaṭṭatu. attac ceyalait tuppuṟaḻ oṇtaḷir (273) eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟ ṟaṭiyiṉkaṇṇum ivviyalpu tuppu uṟaḻ oṇtaḷir eṉṟu kiḷantōtappaṭutal kāṇka. ñāḻal - kōṅkamaram (tivākaram).
naṟavam - aṉiccamaram (tivākaram).
parumai arai, parāraiyeṉat tirintatu; parārai marāattu (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai, ) eṉpuḻippōla.
`vaṭu veṉpatu, iṅkup piñcukaḷ tōṉṟum vaṭu. tīviḷi - pacuṅ kāy (piṅkalantai). `tūṅkal toṅkaleṉṉum poruṭṭu. mukilkaḷ tavaḻumpaṭi attuṇai uyaramāyc cōlaikaḷ viḷaṅkiṉaveṉka.
`polimē yeṉṉuñ ceyyumeṉṉu muṟṟil īṟṟayal ukarattoṭuṅ keṭṭatu.
(54-64) cuḷainirai amainta koḻum paḻam tūkki - cuḷaikaḷ varicaiyāy amainta koḻuviya kaṉikaḷait toṅkaviṭṭu, kuṟumalar aviḻnta naṟuvirai teḷittu - taṉ ciṟiya pūkkaḷ malarntamaiyāl iṉiya maṇam parappi, vīcukāl acaiyum pācilai narantamum - vīcukiṉṟa kāṟṟiṉāl acaiyum pacumaiyāṉa ilaikaḷ nirampiya nāratta maraṅkaḷum, nalaṉuṟu makaḷir ilavu itaḻ katuviya muṟuval aṉṉa - aḻaku vāynta makaḷiriṉ ilavampūvi ṉitaḻai okkum itaḻkaḷiṉ cenniṟattaip paṟṟiya muḷaippaṟkaḷ oppa, ciṟuvitai peyta polaṉ kuṭam eṭutta ulavai mātuḷaiyum - ciṟiya vittukkaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa poṟkuṭam okkum paḻaṅkaḷ tūkkiya kōṭukaḷaiyuṭaiya mātuḷa maraṅkaḷum, karumaṇi puraiyum oru kaṉi nāvalum - karumaiyāṉa maṇiyai okkum oppaṟṟa paḻaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya nāvaṉ maraṅkaḷum, uvarkkaḻi maṇakkum tuvarkkāl vakaḷamum - uvartta nīruḷḷa kaḻiyiṭaṅkaḷum maṇakkumāṟu naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻuñ civanta kāmpukaḷuḷḷa pūkkaḷ niṟainta makiḻa maramum, poṉtiraḷ kaṭuppat teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum eḻilkaṉi paḻutta elumiccam putalum - poṉṉuruṇṭai oppat teḷivāy viḷaṅkum aḻakiya kaṉikaḷ paḻutta elumiccam putarkaḷum, kāvatam kamaḻa mēvuvaaṉṟē orupāl - kāvatattolaivu maṇaṅkamaḻumpaṭi ammalaiccōlaiyi ṉoru pakkaliṟ porunti niṟpaṉavākum; aṉṟu ē acainilai.
nirai, varicai; pacumaiilai, pācilai; `narantam nāranta maram, (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, 94).
nalaṉ, `aḻaku; pūnalam eṉpataṟkup `pūviṉākiya aḻaku eṉṟu parimēlaḻakiyār uraikūṟiṉār (paripāṭal, 16, 18).
ilavitaḻ mātuḷavitaikaḷiṉ orupāṟ cemmaikkum muṟuval orupāl veṇmaikkum uvamaikaḷ.
`polaṉkuṭ meṉpatu īṇṭuc ciṟu poṟkuṭam pōṟṟōṉṟum mātuḷampaḻattiṟku āyatu uvamaiyākupeyar. ulavai marakkompu (tivākaram).
vakuḷam - makiḻamaram (tivākaram); tuvarkkāl - pavaḷaniṟam uḷḷa kāmpu; ataṉaiyuṭaiya malarum ammalaraiyuṭaiya maramum īṇṭu `vakuḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa; ippavaḷakkāl mallikai yiṉaiyun tamiḻ nāṭṭavar `makiḻ eṉkiṉṟaṉar; pavaḷaniṟakkāmpu illāta vēṟoru vakaiyiṉaiyum `makiḻ eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
`kāvata meṉpatu iraṇṭēkāṟkal; itu muṉṉuṅ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
