(65-78) kulikam ūṭṭiya talaimaiyiṉ tikaḻntu - cenniṟam ēṟṟiya mēṉmaiyil viḷaṅki, navvi nōkkiyar cevvāypōla - māṉ pārvaiyiṉarāṉa makaḷiratu civanta vāypōṉṟa, koḻum kaṉi uṭaiya kovvaik koṭiyum - koḻuviya paḻaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya kovvaik koṭikaḷum, kaṟpu uṭai makaḷir muṟpaṭac cūṭum varicaiyiṉ mikunta paricu uṭai mullaiyum - kaṟpoḻukkamuḷḷa mātar mutaṉmaiyākac cūṭikkoḷḷun takutiyiṉ mēmpaṭṭa perumaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya mullaik     koṭikaḷum, tiṉpatu kallāp puṉtalai mantikaṟittut tuḷḷum kaṟivaḷar koṭiyum - vakaiterintu tīṉi tiṉṉutalai iṉṉun teriyāta ciṟiya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya kuraṅkukkuṭṭikaḷ tampaṟkaḷāṟ kaṭittu uṟaippiṉait tāṅkāmal tuḷḷukiṉṟa miḷakukāykaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟa miḷakukoṭikaḷum, aṇil puṟam kaṭukkum variyuṭaik koṭumkāy koḷḷaiyil kāytta veḷḷarik koṭiyum - aṇiṟpiḷḷaiyiṉ mutukiṉai okkum mūṉṟuvarikaḷuḷḷa vaḷainta veḷḷarikkāykaḷ mikutiyākak kāytta veḷḷarikkoṭikaḷum, coluyar marapiṉ mel ilaik koṭiyum - pukaḻmikka taṉmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya veṟṟilaik koṭikaḷum, ciṟupaḷikku uruṇṭai ceṟivuṟuttaṉaiya tīm kaṉik kulaikaḷ tūṅka vayiṉvayiṉ nantātu vaḷarum muntirik koṭiyum - ciṟiya kaṇṇāṭi uruṇṭaikaḷ oru kottāka nerukki vaittāṟpōla iṉiya paḻakkulaikaḷ toṅkā niṟka iṭantoṟuṅ keṭātu vaḷarkiṉṟa koṭimuntirik koṭikaḷum, iṭaiiṭai tāay miṭaiyumāl orupāl - iṭaiyiṭaiyē tāvip piṉṉit tōṉṟum maṟṟorupakkattē eṉpatu.
kulikam, cenniṟam (tivākaram).
`nōkki yeṉpataṉ ikaram peṇpāl vikuti.
kaṇavaṉaip pirintu avaṉ pirivuṉṉit taṅ kaṟpiṉaip pēṇum makaḷiratu oḻukkamē mullaiyoḻukkamākaliṉ, `kaṟpuṭai makaḷir cūṭum mullai yeṉa aḵtiṅkuk kiḷantu kūṟappaṭṭa. kaṟpoṭu poruntik kaṇavaṉcoṟ piḻaiyātu illiruntu nallaṟañ ceytal makaḷiratu iyaṟkai mullai yātaliṉ atu muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (tolkāppiyam, akattiṇaiyiyal, 5). varicai - takuti (ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, 217).
miḷakukāykaḷ kāramuṭaiyavākaliṉ avaṟṟait tiṉṟāl nā eriyum eṉpatait teriyā iḷaṅkuṭṭikaḷ eṉpār tiṉpatu kallāp puṉṟalai manti eṉṟār. `tuḷḷuṅkaṟi tuḷḷutaṟkētuvākiya kaṟi eṉak kāriya kāraṇap poruṭṭu.
veḷḷarikkāy aṇil mutukupōl varikaḷ uṭaimai, aṇilvarik koṭuṅkāy eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉālum (246) aṟiyappaṭum.
mellilai - veṟṟilai, (cīvakacintāmaṇi, 62); vāyiṉuṭ peytu mellappaṭutaliṉ veṟṟilai `mellilai eṉap peyar peṟalāyiṟṟu; viṉaittokai; ellāc ciṟappu nikaḻccikaḷilum veṟṟilai vaḻaṅkappaṭum pukaḻuṭaiyatākaliṟ `colluyar marapiṉ eṉṟu aṭaikoṭuttār.
ceṟivuṟuttal - neruṅkaccērttal, ceṟaviṉaip polinta cempūṅ kaṇṇiyaṉ (paripāṭal, 22, 21) eṉpuḻippōla.
`nantātu, keṭāmal; nantāviḷakku (cintāmaṇi, 3144); nāṭaṟkariya nalattai nantāt tēṉai (tiruvācakam, tiruppoṟ cuṇṇam, 15) eṉṟaruḷicceyvar maṇivācakapperumāṉ.
tāay - tāvi, kalaitāya uyarcimaiyattu eṉṉum maturaik kāñciyiṟ (332) pōla.
(79-81) naṭuvil - ittakaiya cōlaiyiṉnaṭuvil, pāyoḷi matiyam - paraviya oḷiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tiṅkaḷāṉatu, paritivemmai yiṉ - katiravaṉ vemmaiyiṉāl, iḷakip puṉalāy iḻintatu kaṭuppa - uruki nīrāyk kīḻē oḻukip paraviyatupōla, teḷinīr vāvi oṉṟuuḷatu - teḷinta nīriṉaiyuṭaiya kuḷam oṉṟu uḷḷatu;
matiyamē iḷaki nīrāy iḻintateṉṟatu, nīriṉ kuḷircci tūymai cuvai naṉmai miṉumiṉuppu teḷivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ keṉka.
`kaṭuppa uvamaurupu; tēṅkamaḻ marutiṇar kaṭuppa (tirumuruku, 34) eṉpuḻippōla. ivvuvamai mika aḻakitu.
(81-84) atu ciṟukāltōṟum uṟunīr oḻukki - antak kuḷam ciṟuciṟu vāykkāl kaṭōṟum mikunta nīriṉai oḻukacceytu, vālukam paranta kōliya pāttiyil - veṇmaṇal nirampiya vaḷaittamaikkappaṭṭa pāttiyiṉkaṇ, pacciḷampullai niccalum vaḷarkkum - pacumaiyāṉa iḷampullai eṉṟum vaḷarac ceyyum; atāṉṟu, atuvumallāmal;
maṟṟu, acai; uṟunīr, mikkanīr; vālukam - veṇmaṇal; vēlaivālukattu eṉṉuñ cilappatikārattiluṅ kāṇka. (6, 131).
pātti, ciṟuvayal; karumpiṉ pātti eṉpatu patiṟṟup pattu (13, 3).
`niccal eṉṉuñ collum poruḷum muṉṉē viḷakkap paṭṭaṉa.
(85-98) aṉṉac cēval aṇimayirp peṭaiyoṭum poṉṉam tāmarai polintu vīṟṟiruppavum - aṉṉap paṟavaiyiṉ āṇ aḻakiya tūviyiṉaiyuṭaiya taṉatu peṇ aṉṉattuṭaṉ poṉpōṉṟa aḻakiya centāmarai malariṉ mēṟ kātaliṉāl viḷaṅki oṉṟāy makiḻntirukkavum, nittilam payanta nattu-muttukaḷīṉṟa caṅkaṅkaḷ, vāṉ maṭuvil - vāṉamākiya nīr nilaiyil, mīṉiṉam vaḷaiiya nāḷmatipōla - viṇmīṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ cūḻnta muḻunilāppōla, pāl keḻu marapiṉ parantu cellavum - taṉpakkattē poruntiya taṉmaiyuṭaṉ paravi ēkavum, koḻum kayal mātar viḻi eṉa miḷiravum - koḻumai yāṉa keṇṭai mīṉkaḷ makaḷiruṭaiya kaṇkaḷ pōṟ puraḷavum, karuverinuṭaiya paruvarāl ukaḷavum - kariya mutukiṉaiyuṭaiya parutta varāl mīṉkaḷ aṅkumiṅkum puraḷavum, muṟṟāmañcaḷ ciṟupuṟam kaṭukkum iṟavu karai maruṅkil curintu tuḷḷavum - muṟṟāta pacumañcaṭ kiḻaṅkiṉ ciṟiya mutukiṉai okkum iṟāmīṉ karaiyarukiṟ curuṇṭu tuḷḷavum, ciṟuvaḷi eṭuppac ciṟutirai eḻīi muṟaimuṟai urāayk karaiyiṉaic cāravum - ciṟukāṟṟu eḻuppa ataṉāl melliya ciṟṟalaikaḷ eḻuntu oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyp parantuceṉṟu karaiyiṉaip poruntavum, ceḻunīrk kuvaḷaiyum kaḻunīrppōtum muḻuneṟi āmpalum kaḻiyavum kuḻumik kāṇpōy piṇikkum māṇpiṉatu - ceḻumaiyāṉa nīrkkuvaḷaiyeṉṉum nīlappūvum ceṅkaḻunīrmalarum alli pāṭṭum mikutiyum niṟaintu kāṇpavar uḷḷattait taṉvayap paṭuttum māṭciyiṉai yuṭaiyatākum akkuḷam eṉpatu.
aṉṉattiṉ tūvi maruvutaṟku meṉmaitaru mākaliṉ, `aṇimayirppeṭai yeṉa iṅkuc ciṟappikkappaṭṭatu.

(98-103) āṅku - ammalaiccāraliṭattē, potiyac cāntam tataiya acaii - potiyamalaiyiṉ cantaṉamaṇam niṟaiya ulāvi, arumpu aṟa virinta naṟumpu aḷaii - mukaiyāntaṉmai nīṅka malarnta maṇamuḷḷa malarkaḷait tuḻāvi, viraiyum tātum niṟaiyamukantu - muṟaiyē naṟumaṇamum, makarantamum nirampa aḷḷikkoṇṭu, talaittalai vīci nalattaka ulāam teṉṟalum eṉṟum ōvātu - iṭantoṟum vīci iṉimaiyāka acaiyun teṉṟaṟkāṟṟum ennēramum nīṅkātu; atāaṉṟu - atuvumaṉṟi;
cāntattiṉiṉṟu viraiyum, naṟumpūviṉiṉṟu tātum mukanteṉa niral niraiyāk koḷka.
`tataiya, niṟaiya, tatai ilai vāḻai (47, 10) eṉpatu aiṅkuṟunūṟu.
(104-125) kuṉṟik kaṇṇa kuyilka ḷeṅkum tuṉṟiya makiḻviṉ naṉṟu kūvummē - kuṉṟimaṇiyiṉ cenniṟamuṅ karum puḷḷiyumpōṟ cuṟṟac cenniṟamum naṭuviṟ karuniṟamum vāynta viḻikaḷai yuṭaiyavāṉa kuyiṟ paṟavaikaḷ accōlai yeṅkum mikunta kaḷippiṉāl iṉitākak kūvāniṟkum; āṭu vāl ciralkaḷ nāṭoṟum kūṭi ōṭumīṉ arunti vīṭuṟā - āṭukiṉṟa vālaiyuṭaiya cicciliyeṉṉum oruvakaik kuruvikaḷ nāṭōṟum oṉṟucērntu annīr nilaiyil ōṭukiṉṟa ciṟu mīṉkaḷaik kauvi viḻuṅki eṉṟum avviṭattaiviṭṭu nīṅkā; tukir kōtta aṉṉa ukiruṭaic ciṟukāl puṟa marap potumpiṉ iṟaikūrummē - pavaḷaṅkaḷai oṉṟu kōttāṟpōṉṟa nakaṅ kaḷaiyuṭaiya ciṟukāliṉaiyuṭaiya puṟākkaḷ marappontiṉ kaṇ taṅkāniṟkum, tūṅkaṇam kurīip pāṅkupaṭat teṟṟiya aruntoḻil kuṭampaiyiṉ poruntum - tūkkaṇaṅ kuruvikaḷ tām aḻakupaṭap piṉṉiya ariya vēlaippāṭuṭaiya kūṭukaḷil aṭaiyum, paim ciṟaikkiḷḷai orukāl tūkkip puṉciṟu kiḷaiyil kaṇtuyil koḷumē - pacumaiyāṉa iṟakkaikaḷai yuṭaiya kiḷikaḷ tamatu orukālait tūkkik koṇṭu melliya ciṟu kiḷaikaḷil niṉṟu kaṇkaḷ tūkkaṅkoḷḷum, aṟuvai maṭittaṉṉa paṟaikeḻu nārai ciṟukāltōṟum varum mīṉ nōkkit tokutiyāka mikutiyoṭu vatimē - melliya veḷḷait tuṇiyai maṭittāṟpōṉṟa iṟakkaikaḷ poruntiya nāraikaḷ ciṟiya vāykkālkaṭōṟum ōṭivarukiṉṟa mīṉkaḷai etirpārttuk koṇṭu kūṭṭamāka maṉakkiḷarcciyuṭaṉ taṅkiyirukkum, vaṇṭum tēṉumvarikkaṭaip piracamum immeṉa muraṉṟu naṉmalartōṟum aḷinaṟa māntik kalikūrummē - vaṇṭum tēṉvaṇṭum varivaṇṭum tēṉīyum eṉpaṉa immeṉṉum oliyuṇṭāka olittu nalla malarkaḷ tōṟuñ ceṉṟu mutirnta tēṉ paruki makiḻccimikum, pūvākkaṇṇa tōkai virittu muḷai iḷa ñāyiṟṟu iḷaveyil eṟippap pīṭuṟu maññai āṭuṟum - iyaṟkaiyē tiṟantiruppatallatu iṭaiyē tiṟavāta kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiyavāṉa tam tōkaikaḷai vaṭṭamāy virittuk kālaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum iḷaṅkatirōṉatu meṉmaiyāṉa veyiloḷi vīca appōḻtu perumai mikka mayiliṉaṅkaḷ viḷaiyāṭā niṟkum, iṉṉum kūṟalvēṇṭiṉ - iṉṉum eṭuttuc colla vēṇṭumāṉāl, piṉṉum - mēlum, eṉ col aḷavu amaiyā - eṉatu colvarampilamaiyāta, mallal ampotumparp palperum ciṟappum kaṇṭilai - attuṇaic ceḻumaiyāṉa aḻakiya am maraccōlaiyiṉ pala peruñciṟappukkaḷum nī kaṇṭaṉaiyallai yeṉavē kūṟal takum eṉpatu.
ip pakutiyiṟ cōlaivaḷam mika aḻakākak kūṟap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
kaṇṇa, kuṟippu muṟṟeccam; kaṇṇavāṉa kuyilkaḷeṉak koḷka. `tuṉṟutal, neruṅkutal; aḵtiṅku `mikunta veṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṭa vantatu.

`ciral eṉpatu ciccilikkuruvi; itu maṟintu nīṅku maṇic ciral kāṇeṉa (paḷikka, 24) eṉa maṇimēlaiyiṉ kaṇṇum varutal kāṇka.  vīṭu viṭutal; nīṅkal; uyirkkolai puritalāl iṟaivaṉatu vīṭṭulakiṉai eytā eṉa oru poruḷ tōṉṟu taluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka. aṉṟum ēvum acai mēlvaruvaṉavum aṉṉa.

(125-147) aṉṟi āṅku - allatu appotumpariṉkaṇ, aṉiccam mēl paṭiṉum taṉittuyar uḻakkum taḻaimalarc cīṟaṭi kuḻaipaṭa naṭantu - aṉiccamalariṉmēṟ paṭṭālum mikka tuṉpa maṭaiyun taḻainta centāmarai malaraṉaiya ciṟiya tiruvaṭikaḷ tacaimella  naṭantu, poḻiliṭam pukuntu - cōlaiyuḷ nuḻaintu, pūm taṭamaruṅkil - pūkkaḷ niṟainta akkuḷattarukil, viḻaitaka niṉṟa paḻutil pāvaiyai - viruppamuṇṭākumpaṭi niṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta pāvai pōlvāḷai, eṉuḷam eṉṉum taṉ amar kiḻiyil - eṉatu uḷḷameṉṉun taṉakkupporuntiya iraṭṭut tuṇiyil, vaḻuviṉṟu eḻutiya eḻil ōviyattai - piḻaiyillāmal eḻutiya aḻaku cittiram pōlvāḷai, amiḻtu poti tuvarvāyk kiḷavi cila miḻaṟṟum - amiḻtam niṟainta taṉatu civanta vāyiliruntu coṟkaḷ cila pēcum, māniṟam koṇṭa tū niṟak kiḷiyai - pacumai niṟam koṇṭa tūya uruviṉaiyuṭaiya kiḷipōlvāḷai, kāṭu uṟai vāḻkkai taṉ iṉam pirintu - kāṭṭil vāḻkiṉṟa vāḻkkaiyai yuṭaiya taṉ kūṭṭattai viṭṭu nīṅki, nāṭu uṟai vāḻkkai naṉkaṉam maruviya - nāṭṭiṉkaṇ vāḻum vāḻkkaiyai nallapaṭiyākap poruntiya, kuṭam māṇ koṅkai maṭamāṉ piṇaiyai - kuṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa periya koṅkaikaḷai yuṭaiya iḷamāṉpēṭu pōlvāḷai, kiḷicciṟai eṉṉum paḻippuaṟu pacumpoṉ karaittu - kiḷicciṟai eṉṉum mācaṟṟa oruvakaip pacumpoṉṉai nīrāy urukki, īṇṭu eḻutiya taraik kiḷar valliyai - ataṉāl innilavulakil eḻuti yamaitta nilattiṉiṉṟum eḻunta poṉṉiṟamāṉa vallik koṭi pōlvāḷai, kāmak kaṭum paci nām uṟak kaḷaiyum - kāmam eṉṉum koṭiya paciyāṉatu accam aṭaiyumpaṭi ataṉai nīkkum, peṟutaṟku ariya uṟutuṇai maruntai - aṭaivataṟku arumaiyāṉa ciṟanta tuṇaiyāyuḷḷa maruntupōlvāḷai, kāṇṭal cellā aruvapporuḷeṉa - kāṇṭal iyalāta uruvamil poruḷeṉṟu kūṟum, pūṇṭōr pukaḻurai puraipaṭṭu oḻiya - pēr pūṇṭa periyōrkaḷatu pukaḻ takka uraiyāṉatu paḻutupaṭṭoḻiyu māṟu, miṉ eṉa miḷirntu eṉ kaṇ etir polinta - miṉṉalpōl piṟaḻntu oḷi vīci eṉ kaṇkaḷmuṉ viḷaṅkiya, karumpiṉum iṉikkum eṉ arumpeṟal uyirai - karumpaip pārkkiṉum iṉikkiṉṟa eṉ peṟaṟkariya uyir pōlvāḷai, eṉ uḷam maṉṉiya tuṉ iruḷ nīṅka - eṉatu uḷḷattil nilaipeṟṟa ceṟinta malaviruḷāṉatu oḻiyumāṟu, muṟpaṭak koḷuviya poṉcuṭark koḻuntai - eṉṉetiriṟ poruttiya poṉṉiṟamāṉa oḷikkoḻuntupōl vāḷai, pulam koḷak kāṇum poṟiyum ilaiyāl - kāṭciyāka kāṇum nalviṉai yum niṉakkillai yeṉpatu.
taḻaimalar, viṉaittokai; ciṟumai aṭi, cīṟaṭi.
kiḻi, eḻutupaṭam; īcaṉa cāntu merukku maṇintōr kiḻipiṭittu ( tirukkōvaiyār, 74) eṉpar maṇivācakaṉār; aḵtiṅku appaṭameḻutun tuṇiyai uṇarttāniṉṟatu. amartal - poruntutal, (puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai, 4, 5).
uṇmaiyuṭaiyaḷākaliṉ, cilacoṟkaḷē avaḷ vāyi ṉiṉṟum eḻuvavāyiṉa. aṉṟi muḷḷeyiṟupōḻtal añcic cilacolvē pēcuvaḷeṉa uraittalumām. miḻaṟṟal, nirampā meṉcoṟ kūṟal; talaivi puttiḷamaiyuṭaiya maṭavaralākaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam `miḻaṟṟu meṉappaṭṭatu. uruvakamāy vanta kiḷiyiṉiyaṟkaikkum iccoṟporuḷ okkum. `amiḻtu poti vāy eṉṟamaiyiṉ, avvāyiṉiṉṟeḻuṅ kiḷavikaḷellām amiḻtamākumeṉpatu kuṟippāṟ peṟappaṭum. niṟam iraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉatu vaṇṇamum piṉṉatu uruvamum uṇarttum.
`naṉkaṉam eṉpataṟku `naṉṟāka eṉṟu poruḷuraippar paripāṭaluraiyil (15:25).

eṉṟu teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉārum (tirukkuṟaḷ, kuṟippaṟital, 1) kūṟutal iṅku niṉaivu kūraṟpālatu.
uyireṉa uruvakap paṭuttiṉamaiyiṉ, `kāṇṭal cellā aruvapporuḷeṉa ataṉ iyalpuraittup piṉ viyantu moḻiyappaṭṭa teṉka.
uṟṟunōkkuṅkāṟ kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum oruvaruk koruvar avaravaruyiraic ceṟinta pollāp paḻamalaviruḷai nīkkikkoḷḷum iyaṟkaiyuṟaviṉarāvar; itaṉāṉaṉṟē tirukkuṟaḷ vētattiṉum maṉaiyāḷ vāḻkkaittuṇai (ati. 6) eṉappaṭṭatūu meṉpatu. itaṉai viḷakkutaṟ poruṭṭē ilakkiyaṅkaḷilellām `maṉaikku viḷakku maṭavār eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkatturaikaḷāl vāḻkkait tuṇaiyāvār malaviruḷ nīkkum oḷiviḷakkāka vaittup pārāṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. itu muṉṉuṅ kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu (innūl, 4: 18 urai).
pāvai, ōviyam mutaliyaṉa uruvakaṅkaḷ.
(148-162) āvaṇa oliyum arum kaṭal oliyum oṉṟu talaimayaṅki eṉṟum ōvā oṟṟimānakaril - kaṭaitteruviṉ viṟpaṉai oliyum aḷattaṟkariya kaṭaloliyum oṉṟu kalantu eṉṟum nīṅkāmalirā niṉṟa tiruvoṟṟimā nakariṉkaṇ, taṉ takappolinta paciyatōr maññaimicai amarcevvēḷ - taṉatu aḻakiya iḷamaic cevvikkut takumpaṭi viḷaṅkiya pacumainiṟa muḷḷatoru mayiliṉ mēl eḻuntaruḷāniṉṟa cemmai niṟam poruntiya murukaṉ, tēvarum mayaṅkum mūvā māyam palamuṟai iyaṟṟiya talaimai tīrcūriṉ pēr uram vacinta kūrvaṭivēlaṉ - tēvarkaḷum mayaṅkumpaṭi yāṉa aḻiyāta māyac ceyalkaḷaip palataṭavai ceyta talaimai nīṅkun takaiyaṉāṉa cūraṉatu akaṉṟa mārpaip piḷanta kūrmaiyuḷḷa cevviya vēṟpaṭaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉ, nōṉā uḷḷattu nāṉmukaṉ teḷiyat talaiyil kuṭṭiya nalaṉuṟu kumaraṉ - `ōm eṉṉum orumoḻiyiṉ uṇmaipporuḷ nāṇun tavam vāyāta uḷḷattaiyuṭaiya nāṉmukaṉ ataṉ poruḷ teḷiyumpaṭi avaṉ talaiyiṟ kuṭṭiya aḻaku  poruntiya iḷamparuvamuḷḷa murukapperumāṉ, viṇṇavarkku ariyaṉ - tēvarkaṭkuṅ kiṭaittaṟ karumai yāṉavaṉ, emmaṉōrkku eḷiyaṉ - āṉāl aṭiyēṉpōṉṟa eḷiyōrkkuk kiṭaittaṟ keḷimaiyāṉavaṉ, piṟaimuṭi putalvaṉ - piṟai taṅkiya caṭaimuṭiyai yuṭaiya civaperumāṉukkup putalvaṉ, maṟai muṭi mutalvaṉ - nāṉ maṟaikaḷiṉ muṭiviṭattukkut talaivaṉ, umaitaru ciṟuvaṉ - umaip pirāṭṭiyār īṉṟeṭutta putalvaṉ, emai aḷi uṟuvaṉ - eḷiyēmai aruḷkūrntu kākkum mikkōṉ, paṉṉiru kaiyaṉ - paṉṉiraṇṭu tirukkaikaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉ, eṉ uyirkku aiyaṉ - eṉatu āruyirkku uyirāyuḷḷa talavaiṉ eṉṉum murukap perumāṉuṭaiya, tiruvaṭi niṉaiyā aṟivilarpōla - tiruvaṭi kaḷai eṇṇum pēṟupeṟāta aṟivillā makkaḷaippōl, eṉ etirtōṉṟip paṉmuṟai kaḻaṟiṉai - eṉmuṉ vantu palataram iṭitturaittaṉai;
talaimaitīr, talaimaitīrnta; murukaṉaip pakaiyāmuṉ ṉirunta talaimai avaṉaippakaittapiṉ tīrntamaiyiṉ talaimaitīr cūr eṉṟār. vacinta, piḷanta; vacittataik kaṇṭamāka (5: 38) eṉpatu paripāṭal.
`nōṉā uḷḷa meṉappaṭṭatu, ōṅkārattiṉ meyp poruḷai uṇarun tavamiyaṟṟap peṟāta uḷḷattai yuṭaiya nāṉmukaṉeṉka; nōṉāmai - tavampuriyāmai; nōṉṟal eṉṉuñ colliṉ etirmaṟai. nāṉmukaṉ ōṅkārattiṉ karuttuṇarāmaikkāka murukapperumāṉ avaṉ talaiyiṟ kuṭṭiya varalāṟu,
eṭṭoṇāta akkuṭilaiyiṟ payaṉilait teṉṟē
  kaṭṭuraittilaṉ mayaṅkalum itaṉporuḷ karutāy
  ciṭṭi ceyvatu ittaṉmaiyatō eṉāc cevvēḷ
  kuṭṭiṉāṉ ayaṉnāṉkumā muṭikaḷuṅkuluṅka
eṉṉuṅ kantapurāṇac ceyyuḷāl (ayaṉaic ciṟaiparipaṭalam, 11) aṟiyappaṭum.
piṟai muṭi, ākupeyar; maṟaimuṭi, maṟaikaḷiṉ muṭinta karuttu.
uṟuvaṉ - mikkōṉ (cilappatikāram, 9, 53).
iṟaivaṉ ṟiruvaṭi niṉaiyā aṟivilār uṇmaikāṇum nīrarallarākaliṉ, avarpōl nīyum eṅkaḷ iyaṟkaik kāta luṇmaiyaiyum ak kātaṟpēṟṟai nalkiya eṉ kātaṟ celviyiṉ arumai perumaiyaiyum uṇarātu kaḻaṟukiṉṟaṉai yeṉat talaivaṉ taṉpāṅkaṉai nōkkik kūṟuṅkaruttu viḷaṅka iṅkut `tiruvaṭi niṉaiyā vaṟivilarpōla veṉṟaruḷic ceytār.
kaḻaṟutal, iṭittu uṟutiyuraittal; aṉpila kaṭiya kaḻaṟi (14: 8) eṉpatu aiṅkuṟunūṟu.
(163 - 167) īṅku itu viṭuttu - iṅku nī iṅṅaṉaṅ kaḻaṟalai oḻittu, āṅkucceṉṟu - ammarap potumpariṉ kuḷattarukiṟ ceṉṟu, tuṭiiṭai orukai ūṉṟi vaṭiviḻi ticaimukam parappi nacaipaṭap pārkkum irumkaṇ māṉpiṇai kāṇil - uṭukkai pōṉṟa taṉ iṭuppiṉkaṇ taṉatu orukaiyai vaittuk koṇṭu vaṭuvakirpōlun taṉ kaṇkaḷai nāṟpuṟaṅkaḷilum pōkkik kaṇṭārkku viruppamuṇṭākumpaṭi pārkkum periya pārvaiyai yuṭaiya māṉpēṭupōlum eṉ talaiviyaip pārttāl, neruṅki niṉṟu iṅku uraiyalai iṉi - ittuṇai aṇukiniṉṟu iṅṅaṉam iṉimēl uraip pāyallaiyeṉpatu.
vaṭi māvaṭuviṉpiḷavu, vaṭikkaivai eṉṉun tirukkōvaiyāruraiyai nōkkuka.
nacai, viruppam, nacaitara vantōr nacaipiṟakkoḻiya (puṟanāṉūṟu, 15) eṉpuḻippōla.
`iruṅkaṇ eṉpuḻi `irumai perumaiyeṉavum, `kaṇ pārvaiyeṉavum poruḷpaṭum. nerukkam, maṉanerukkam.
naṇpā (15) paṉmuṟai kaḻaṟiṉai (162) māṉpiṇaikāṇil (166) neruṅki niṉṟiṅku uraiyalai iṉi (167) eṉa muṭikka, (25)
26. pirivāṟṟāta talaivi tōḻiyoṭu kūṟal
 iṉiyā reṉakkiṅ kiṉiyā riyaintār
  muṉiyār muṉintu moḻiyār - paṉimoḻiyāy
  puḷḷoṉ ṟivarntu pukaḻoṟṟit tannakarkku
  naḷḷiruṭkaṭ ceṉṟār namar.
(i-ḷ) iṉi yār eṉakku iṅku iṉiyār iyaintār - iṉi mēl yār eṉakku iṅku iṉiyarāy iṇaṅkiyuḷḷavar, muṉiyār muṉintu moḻiyār - nammavar ciṉavār, ciṉan toṉṟu pēcār; paṉimoḻiyāy - kuḷirnta iṉcol uṭaiya uyirttōḻī, puḷ oṉṟu ivarntu pukaḻ oṟṟit tam nakarkku - mayileṉṉun paṟavai oṉṟaṉmītu mēṟkoṇṭu pukaḻmikka tiruvoṟṟiyūreṉum tam tirunakarattukku, naḷ iruḷkaṇ ceṉṟār namar - ceṟinta iruḷil naṅ kātalar ceṉṟār eṉpatu.
orukālattilum muṉitalum muṉintu pēcutalu millā attuṇai aṉpiṉarāṉa namar, puḷḷoṉ ṟivarntu tam nakarkkuc ceṉṟār; iṉi yāreṉakku iṅku iṉiyarāy iyaintār irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu karuttu.
iyaintār viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyar; kātalarāy iyaintavar eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ivartal - mēṟkoḷḷal, ivartan teṉmēṉimē lūrumpacappu eṉpataṟkup parimēlaḻakiyā ruraitta vuraikāṇka (tirukkuṟaḷ, 1182). naḷ iruḷ, naṭu irā. (26)
27. neñcaṟi vuṟuttal
  namarā yiruntupiṉ ētila rāvar nayamilarun
  tamarāva reṉṉiṟ ṟamarpiṟa reṉṟu takavucolli
  amarāṭi niṟṟal vaṟitukaṇ ṭāyalai yāḻiyoṟṟik
  kumarāveṉ ṟōtik kuṭantaṅkoṇ ṭēttak kuṟikkoṇeñcē
(i-ḷ) namarāy iruntu piṉ ētilarāvar makkaḷiṟ cilar cila kālam namakkuc cuṟṟattārāyiruntu piṉ ayalavarāy viṭuvar, nayamilarum tamarāvar - aṉpillāta ayalavarum orukāl namakku vēṇṭiyōrāvar, eṉṉil - eṉṟāl, tamar piṟar eṉṟu takavu colli amarāṭi niṟṟal vaṟitu kaṇṭāy - ulakattil nammavareṉṟum ayalavareṉṟum perumai pēcip pōrāṭi niṟṟal vīṇeṉṟaṟivāy, alai āḻi oṟṟik kumarā eṉṟu ōti kuṭantam koṇṭu ēttak kuṟikkoḷ neñcē - ātalāl alaimikka kaṭalai aṇittākavuṭaiya tiruvoṟṟimurukā eṉṟu collik kaikūppi meyvaḷaittu vaḻuttat tuṇivukoḷvāy neñcamē eṉpatu.
`nayam, īram, naṇpāṟṟārāki nayamilaceyvārkkum (tirukkuṟaḷ, 998). takavu - perumai, takavēyuṭaiyāṉ ṟaṉaic cāra eṉṉun tiruvācakamāmaṟaiyil (yāttiraippattu, 2) ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka.
`kuṭantameṉpatu oruvakai añcali; atāvatu `kaikūppi meyvaḷaittu vaṇaṅkal; kuṭantampaṭṭuk koḻumalar citaṟi eṉat tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyilum (229) ituvarum; kuṭantaṅ kaikūppi meykōṭṭiniṟṟal eṉṟār tivākarattilum.
kuṟikkoḷal, maṉattuḷ vaittuc ceyal muṭikkun tuṇivu koḷḷutal; kuṟikkōṭṭakaiyatu koḷkeṉat tantēṉ eṉpatu cilappatikāram, 30, 63. (27)
28. pulavarāṟṟup paṭai
aḵtāvatu, paricupeṟṟut tirumpum pulavaṉ oruvaṉ atu peṟuṅ karuttuṭaṉ etirvarum pulavaṉoruvaṉai vaḻiyiṟ kaṇṭu taṉakkup paricaḷitta aracaṉatu perumaiyum avaṉpāl atu peṟavēṇṭum vakaimaiyum piṟavun teḷiyauṇartti, uṇarttiya anneṟiyē avaṉai uyppatākum. eṉavē `āṟṟuppaṭai yeṉpatu etirppaṭṭārait tām paricupeṟṟa vāṟṟiṟ paṭuppippatu eṉap poruḷtarumeṉka. ivviyalpu.
 āṟṟiṭaik kāṭci yuṟaḻat tōṉṟip
  peṟṟa peruvaḷam peṟāark kaṟivuṟīic
  ceṉṟu payaṉetirac coṉṉa pakkamum
eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattāṟ (puṟat. 36) peṟappaṭum.
(1-11) neñcu nekku uṭaintu - neñcam nekiḻntutaḷarntu, pañcu kaṇ aṭaiyā -kaṇ pañcaṭaintu, uṭumpu urittaṉṉa kaṭumpaci maruṅkul paḻuppuṭaitōṉṟic ceḻippiṉṟi vaika - uṭumpait tōlurittāl oppak koṭiya paciyiṉaiyuṭaiya vayiṟṟiṉ pakkattuḷḷa vilā elumpukaḷ tacaiyillāmaiyāṟ pakkaṅkaḷiṟṟōṉṟic ceḻumaiyillāmalirukka, vaṟumaiuḻanta uṟumaṉaik kiḻattiyoṭu - avvāṟellām vaṟumaiyil varuntikkiṭakkum aṉpiṉ mikka maṉaikkiḻattiyuṭaṉ, nāḷum nāḷum vāḷātu kalāyttu - nāṭōṟuṅ kāraṇamiṉṟiyē caṇṭaiyiṭṭu, nōṉāp paciyiṉ āṉātu aḻūum puṉ talaik kuḻavitaṉ tiṟam nōkki - poṟuttaṟkariya paciyiṉāl iṭai viṭātu aḻukiṉṟa aḻukkuṟṟa talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tam kuḻantai kaḷiṉ nilaiyaikkaṇṭu, kaṉavu kāṇpoḻutum naṉavu eṉat tōṉṟum aruntuyar eṉṟum perum tuyar uṟuppa - avaṟṟai yellām iṭaiyaṟātu eṇṇutalāṟ kaṉavukāṇuṅ kālaṅkaḷilum naṉavupōlavē tōṉṟum uṉṉutaṟkum ariya tuyaraṅkaḷ ennāḷum mikka varuttattai mikuttiṭāniṟpa, miṭikeḻuvāḻkkaiyil kuṭiyāyiruntu - aṅṅaṉam vaṟumai poruntiya ēḻaimai vāḻkkaiyilēyē kuṭiyiruppāyiruntu, maḻuṅkiya uḷḷattu oṭuṅkiya pulavōy - kiḷarcciyavinta uḷḷattiṉaiyuṭaimaiyāṟ ceyaloṭuṅkiya pulavōy! eṉpatu.
`aṭaiyā ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉaiyeccam; nilaṅkiḷaiyā nāṇiniṉṟōḷ (akanāṉūṟu, 16) eṉpuḻip pōla.
`uṭumpurit taṉṉa maruṅkul eṉappaṭṭatu, paciyāl vayiṟu mutukoṭṭi vilā elumpeḻuntu niṟṟaliṉ. paḻu - vilā elumpu, poruvilyāṉaiyiṉ paḻuppōṟ poṅkukāyk kulaiyavarai eṉṟār cintāmaṇiyilum (1561). uṭumpurittaṉṉa eṉpeḻumaruṅkiṟ, kaṭumpiṉ kaṭumpaci eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉum (68) pōntamai nōkkuka.
uṟumaṉaikkiḻatti, aṉpiṉmikka naṉmaṉaikkiḻatti; vaṟumaiyiṉ koṭumaiyāl illattil aṭikkaṭi kāraṇa miṉṟiyē caṇṭaikaḷ uṇṭātal iyalpātalāl, `nāḷum nāḷum vāḷātu kalāyttu eṉappaṭṭatu; kalāyttal - caṇṭaiyiṭutal, cintāmaṇi 1950.
vaṟumaiyil uḻaluñ ceḻumaiyillāk kuḻantaikaṭkut talaicaṭaittu aḻukkēṟik, kāṇa aruvaruppāyirukku mātaliṉ avai `puṉṟalaik kuḻavikaḷ eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
tiṟam - nilaimai; miṭimai - tīrāvaṟumai.
(12-13) iyaṟṟamiḻ varampu tiṟappaṭa āyntu - iyaṟṟamiḻ nūṉ muṭivait tiṟampaṭa ārāyntu, pulamkoḷa nirampiya nalam kiḷar aṟivōy - uḷḷattiṉ iṭam muḻutuṅ koḷḷumāṟu niṟainta naṉmaimikka aṟivuṭaiyōy;
`iyaṟṟamiḻ eṉpatu, muttamiḻkaḷuḷ oṉṟu; ēṉaiya icaittamiḻ `nāṭakattamiḻ eṉka. iyaṟṟamiḻāvatu tiruntiya vaḻakkāy nām pēcuvatum eḻutuvatumāṉa centamiḻākum. varam peṉṟatu, iṅku nūl varampu.
(14-15) vaḻuoṉṟu illā viḻumiya oḻukkam - piḻaipāṭu ciṟitu millāta ciṟanta oḻukkattai, uyiriṉum ōmpum ceyir aṟuperiyōy - taṉ āruyiriṉum peritāka matittuk kākkuṅ kuṟṟamaṟṟa perumai yuṭaiyōy;
oḻukkam uyiriṉum ōmpappaṭum eṉṟu poyyā moḻiyuṅ kaṭṭuraiyāniṟkum. `oṉṟu iṅkuc ciṟiteṉṉum poruṭṭu; māyaikku uṇarvoṉṟu millaiyeṉṟē vaittiṭum (civañāṉa cittiyār, 1: 17) eṉpuḻippōla. ceyir - kuṟṟam.
(16) koḻuntamiḻ pōlak kuḷirmāṇ kuṇattōy - ceḻuntamiḻ moḻiyaippōṟ kuḷircciyum māṭciyum mikka iyalpiṉōy;
cuvainirampiya meṉcoṟkaḷālum āḻntakaṉṟa ciṟanta karuttukkaḷālum namatariya centamiḻmoḻi taṉṉai mēvuvārkkuk kuḷircciyum māṭciyumā yiruttalpōṟ, pulavarun tam iṉcoṟkaḷālum nalloḻukkaṅkaḷālun tammai naccuvārkkut taṇṇeṉṉu māṇpuṭaiyarāyk kāṇappaṭuvareṉka. tamiḻukku ittaṇmaiyum māṭciyu miruttal, uyarmatiṟkūṭaliṉ āynta oṇtīntamiḻiṉ tuṟaivāy nuḻaintaṉaiyō eṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ aruḷic ceyta tirukkōvaiyār tirumoḻi yāṉun teḷiyappaṭum.
(17-18) uḻunturuḷ aḷavaiyiṟ ceḻuntamiḻp pākkaḷ nūṟunūṟu iyaṟṟuṅ kūṟupaṭu matiyōy - uḻuntu uruḷukiṉṟa avvaḷavu virainta nērattiṟ ceḻuntamiḻp pāṭṭukaḷ nūṟunūṟākac ceyyum pakuttaṟiyum matinuṭpa muṭaiyōy;
aḷavai, iṅkuk kālavellai. kūṟupaṭumatiyāvatu nallataṉ nalaṉun tīyataṉ tītum pakuttuṇarum aṟivu.
(19-20) oḷiccelavu ataṉiṉum vaḷiccelavu ataṉiṉum - oḷiyōṭṭattiṉ viraivaip pārkkiṉum kāṟṟōṭṭattiṉ viraivaip pārkkiṉum mikuviraivāka, neṟippaṭak kiḷakkum miṟaikkavi vallōy - oḻuṅkupaṭa icaikkuñ cittirakkavi vallapulavōy;
oḷiccelaveṉpatu ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī mutaliyavaṟ ṟiṉiṉṟu puṟampōtarum oḷiyōṭṭaṅkaḷ. `celavu, celkai; iṅku viraivunōkki ōṭṭameṉakkoḷka. veruvaruñ celaviṉ vekuḷivēḻam (porunarāṟṟuppaṭai, 172).
(21-22) aḷavainūl marapil piṟaḻātu - tarukkanūl muṟaiyil tavaṟātu, yāṇṭum koḷa urai nikaḻttum vaḷam uṟum uṇarvōy - eṅkuṅ kēṭpōr uḷaṅkoḷa urai virikkum vaḷamuṟṟa uṇarvuṭaiyōy;
aḷavainūl, tarukkanūl; vaḷam, ārāyntukaṇṭa nūṟporuḷvaḷam.
(23 - 24) poruḷiyal aṟintu - eṭuttukkoṇṭa virivuraipporuḷiṉ vakaimai terintu, maruḷ aṟap puṉaintu - mayakkamaṟa aḻakuppaṭuttuk kūṟi, kēṭpōr piṇikkum kāṭcicāl uravōy - kēṭpavarkaḷ uḷḷattait taṉ vayappaṭuttum meyyuṇarvu nirampiya aṟivuvaliyuṭaiyōy;
`puṉaital coṟkaḷaiyuṅ karuttukkaḷaiyum iṭam nōkkic curukkiyum perukkiyum uvamittum uruvakittu mellām aḻakupaṭuttik kēṭpārkku iṉpam viḻaivu vēṭkai mutalāṉa meyppāṭu uṇṭākac collutal; iṅṅaṉam aḻakupaṭuttuc collappaṭṭaṉavē paṇṭaik kālattil `ilakkiyam eṉavum, `nūl nayam eṉavuṅ kūṟappaṭṭu vantaṉa. `naviṟoṟum nūlnayam pōlum eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvarum itaṉaic ciṟanteṭuttup pārāṭṭuvar. āṉāl, iṭaik kālattō avvaḻakupaṭuttum urai `kaṟpaṉai yeṉak kūṟappaṭṭup, piṟkālattē atuvum nilaimāṟi `iyaṟkaikku māṟākap poyyurai puṉaital eṉṉum iḻivuporuḷil vantu vaḻaṅkip perumai kuṉṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
(25-28) paṇṭaip piṟaviyil taṇṭātu āṟṟum perum tavam uṭaiyai maṉṉē - paḻam piṟavikaḷil tavaṟāma liyaṟṟum perumaiyāṉa tavattiṉai nirampavum uṭaiyai; porunti iṉṟu īṅku eṉait talaippaṭṭaṉaiyē - poruntutalvara iṉṟu iṅku vantu eṉṉai etirppaṭṭaṉaiyē, ōṅki ūḻicelliṉum nīṭu vāḻmati - mēmpaṭac ciṟantu ūḻikaḻiyiṉu neṭuṅkālam vāḻvāyāka;
taṇṭal - tavaṟutal (piṅkalantai); ataṉ etirmaṟai `taṇṭāmai; ivai `taḷ eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṟ piṟantaṉa; kaḷvārkkut taḷḷum uyirnilai eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum.
maṉ, mikutikkaṇ vantatu. talaippaṭal - etirppaṭal. poruntutal varat talaippaṭṭamaiyāvatu vaṟumaiyuḻantu atu tīrnta oru pulavaṉai, vaṟumaiyuḻakkum maṟṟoru pulavaṉ atutīrtaṟ pāṉmaiyil vantu etirppaṭṭamai eṉka.
(29-34) kīḻtticai eḻīi mēṟṟicaip paṭarum tītteṟu ñāyiṟu vaṭaticaip puṇarntu teṉticai nōkkit ticaitaṭu māṟiṉum - kiḻakkup pakkattil tōṉṟi mēṟkup pakkattiṟ cellum neruppeṉac cuṭum ñāyiṟu vaṭakkup pakkañ cērntu teṟkup pakkam nōkkic ceṉṟapaṭiyāy iṅṅaṉan ticai taṭumāṟiṉālum, vāṉak kaṭaliṉ mīṉeṉa vayaṅkum uṭunirai utirntu koṭumaiyāyiṉum - vāṉameṉṉuṅ kaṭaliṉkaṇ mīṉkaḷeṉa viḷaṅkum uṭukkulaṅkaḷ utirnta viḻutalāl ulakattiṟ koṭiya nikaḻccika kaḷuṇṭāyiṉum, ulavā vāḻkkaiyiṉ nilaimati ciṟantē - keṭāta nalvāḻkkaiyiṟ ciṟantu nilaippāyāka;
paṭaral - cellal; uṭu - viṇmīṉ; nirai, kūṭṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu; ulakiṉkaṭ kēṭukaḷ viḷaiyiṉum niṉ nalvāḻvu keṭātu iṉitūḻi vāḻntu iṉpuṟukaveṉpatu ippakutiyiṉ karuttu.
(35-36) vaṟumai kaḷaintu ciṟumai nīṅki - vaṟumaiyainīkki eḷimai oḻintu, naṉṉilai palavum iṉṉē peṟuti - nalla nilaimaikaḷellām ippoḻutē peṟuvāyāka;
nilai, iṉpaṅkaḷ palavun tuyttaṟkēṟṟa nilaimai. `iṉṉē ippoḻutē eṉap poruḷ paṭutal, uṟṟatiṉṉē iṭaiyūṟu eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇiyuḷḷuṅ (226) kāṇka.
(37) varampaṟum iṉpam nirampak kōṭi - ellaiyaṟṟa iṉpam nirampak koḷvāyāka;
ellaiyaṟṟa iṉpam, aḷavaṟṟa iṉpam; `kōṭi eṉpataṟkuk `koḷ pakuti; atu mutal nīṇṭu takaram virittalum viyaṅkōḷ īṟum peṟṟuk `kōṭi yeṉṟāyiṟṟu. kōṭi, koḷḷuti; `vāḻmati, nilaimati, peṟuti, kōṭi yeṉpaṉa vāḻttutaṟ poruṇmaikkaṇvanta viyaṅkōḷ viṉai muṟṟukaḷ.
(38-39) niṉ uyirkku uṟutināṭi - niṉatu uyirkku naṉmai payattaṟkuriyavaikaḷai ārāyntaṟintu, tuṉṉi niṉṟu - niṉṉai aṇuki niṉṟu, nirainirai kūṟum eṉ urai piḻaiyalai - muṟai muṟaiyākak kaṭṭuraittuc collum eṉ coṟkaḷait tavaṟaviṭātiruppāyāka!
nāṭi - ārāyntu; nāṭātu naṭṭaliṟ kēṭillai tirukkuṟaḷ.
(40-42) iṉṉum kēṭṭal vēṇṭiṉ - iṉṉum nāṉ eṭutturaittalai nī kēṭṭal virumpiṉāyāṉāl, muṉṉum eṉ col aḷavu amaintu nalam pala eytiṉar pallōr pallōr paṇṭum uṇṭu - nāṉ niṉaittuk kūṟum eṉ moḻikaḷiṉ ellaiyil niṉṟu naṉmaikaḷ palavum aṭaintavar palar palar muṉṉum uṇṭu;
(43) iṉiyoṉṟu kūṟuveṉ kēṇmati - ātalāl iṉi oṉṟu colluvēṉ ataṉaik kēṭpāyāka! veṉpatu
`kūṟuveṉ eṉpatil `eṉ īṟu taṉmaiyorumai viṉai muṟṟu (tolkāppiyam, viṉaiyiyal, 6); koṉṉoṉṟu kiḷakkuveṉ kēṇmatiperuma eṉa muṉṉum vantamai niṉaivu kūrka.
(43-45) paṉikeḻu mullaiam koṭikku - kuḷircci poruntiya mullaiyiṉkoṭikku, mallal am ceḻumtēr - celvattiṟ ciṟanta aḻakiya tērai, aruḷvara - taṉakku irakka muṇṭāka, ītta- ataṟkuk koḻukompākumpaṭi niṟutti utaviya, periyaṉō ilaṉē - īkaiyiṟ periyaṉākiya pāriyō iññāṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
vaḷḷaliṟ ciṟanta pāriyiṉ ivvariya periya īkaicceyal,
		          curumpuṇ
naṟuvī yuṟaikku nāka neṭuvaḻic
ciṟuvī mullaikkup peruntēr nalkiya
piṟaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi vīḻuñ cāraṟ
paṟampiṟ kōmāṉ pāriyum
eṉac ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyiṉkaṭ (87 - 91) peritu pārāṭṭap paṭutalum iṅku niṉaivukūraṟpālatu.
`am īriṭaṅkaḷiṉuñ cāriyai; īṟṟēkāram irakkap poruḷatu; mēlvaruvaṉavum aṉṉa.
(46 - 47) viḻiyāk kaṇṇa ceḻumayirt tōkaikku - imaiyāk kaṇkaḷuḷḷa niṟattāṟ ciṟanta mayirkaḷ vāynta mayilukku, naṟumpaṭām vīciya - kuḷiraṉukāmaip poruṭṭut taṉatu iṉiya pōrvaiyai ataṉmēl eṟinta, uṟuvaṉum ilaṉē - takkōṉākiya pēkaṉum iññāṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
ivvaḷḷaliṉ aruñceyalum,
vāṉam vāytta vaḷamalaik kavāaṟ
  kāṉa maññaikkuk kaliṅka nalkiya
  varuntiṟa laṇaṅki ṉāviyar perumakaṉ
  peruṅka ṉāṭaṉ pēkaṉum
eṉac ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyiṉkaṇ (84-87) varum. tōkai, ākupeyar.
(48-50) pavaṉamā ulakil navai aṟap peṟṟa amiḻtu poti nelli - pātāḷa ulakattiṉiṉṟu paḻutaṟappeṟṟa amiḻtam niṟainta karunellik kaṉiyai, auvaikku ītta - auvaiyeṉṉum nam centamiḻp pirāṭṭikku avar nīṭu vāḻumāṟu utaviya, citaiyāp pēṟṟiṉ atikaṉum ilaṉē - aḻiyāp pukaḻākiya pēṟṟiṉai eytiya atikamāṉum iṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
atiyar kōmāṉiṉ ivvaruñceyalum,
		         mālvaraik
kamaḻpūñ cāraṟ kaviṉiya nelli
yamiḻtuviḷai tīṅkaṉi yeḷavaik kītta
vuravucciṉaṅ kaṉalu moḷitikaḻ neṭuvē
laravakkaṭaṟ ṟāṉai yatikaṉum
eṉṟu (ciṟupāṇ, 99-103) kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
`pavaṉamāvulaku nākarulaku; cākkāṭu keṭuttamaiyiṉ, aḵtu `amiḻtupoti nelli yeṉak kiḷantōtappaṭṭatu. ik karunellimaram oru periya malaimuḻaiñciṉ aṭiyilē niṉṟamai, toṉṉilaip perumalai viṭarakattu arumicaik koṇṭa, ciṟiyilai nellit tīṅkaṉi eṉṉum auvaiyār pāṭalāl (puṟanāṉūṟu, 91) aṟiyappaṭutaliṉ, ataṉkaṉi pātāḷavulakiṉiṉṟu peṟappaṭṭateṉṟār. pavaṉam - pātāḷam.
(51-53) kuṟunaṭaip puṟaviṉ taputi kaṇṭu añci - taṉṉai aṭaikkalamāyppukka ciṟiya naṭaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya oru puṟāviṉ aḻiviṉaikkaṇṭu ataṟku añci, kuruti keḻu paimtaṭi muṟaimuṟai taṭintu - ceṅkuruti tōynta taṉatu uṭampiṉ paccait tacaiyai ataṟku īṭākumpaṭi ciṟitu ciṟitāka aṟuttuvaittu, kaṉṉam pukku ēṟiya maṉṉaṉum ilaṉē - aṅṅaṉam vaittum aḵtu īṭākāmaiyiṉ muṭivil tāṉē niṟaittaṭṭil tāviyēṟi ammukattāl īṭuceytu ataṉaip piḻaippitta oru cōḻavēntaṉum iṉṟu vāḻntilaṉē!
iṅṅaṉam uyirkoṭuttup puṟavukātta peruṅkoṭait tiṟam,
taṉṉakam pukka kuṟunaṭaip puṟaviṉ
  ṟaputi yañcik cīrai pukka
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉkaṇṇum (43) ciṟappikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. taputi - aḻivu. taṭi- tacai; kaṉṉam - niṟaittaṭṭu.
(54-56) arum tamiḻp pulavōr perum tiṟam uṇarntu - arumaivāynta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ periyamēṉmaiyai uṇarntu pārttu, poṉṉum maṇiyum taṇṇaṭai nilaṉum varaiyātu vaḻaṅkiya aracarum ilarē - avarkaṭkup paricākap poṉṉum maṇiyum marutanilattūrkaḷum vayalkaḷum ērāḷamāy utaviya puravalarkaḷum iññāṉṟu vāḻntilarē!
tiṟam - mēṉmai, cōti tiṟampāṭi, tiruvācakam, 7, 57. taṇṇaṭai - marutanilattūr (tivākaram). `varaiyātu vaḻaṅkiya veṉṟatu, aḷavupārāmal īntaveṉṉumporuṭṭu. iṅṅaṉam varaiyātu vaḻaṅkiya aracarkaḷ paṇṭiruntaṉa reṉṉum uṇmaikkuc cāṉṟu varumāṟu;
`patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉum nūlil ēḻām pattup pāṭiya kapilar eṉṉum pulavar perumāṉukkuc cēramāṉ celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ eṉṉum vaḷḷal nūṟāyiraṅ kāṇamum oru kuṉṟēṟik kaṇṭa nāṭum paricil koṭuttāṉ.
āciriyar uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum māpperum pulavarkku avar icaitta `paṭṭiṉappālai yeṉṉum nūliṉ ceḻuntamiḻt tiṟam uṇarntu cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ patiṉāṟu nūṟāyirampoṉ paricu aḷittāṉ.
`paraṇar eṉṉum pulavarpirāṉ `patiṟṟuppattil aintām pattup pāṭik, kaṭalpiṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṭam umpaṟkāṭṭu vāriyaiyum avaṉ makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēralaiyum paricil peṟṟāṉ.
iṉṉōraṉṉa paḻaiya paricil varalāṟukaḷ kaṇakkaṟṟaṉa uḷa; avaiyellām iṅku virippiṟ perukum.
munnū ṟūrttē taṇpaṟampu naṉnāṭu
  munnū ṟūrum paricilar peṟṟaṉar
eṉavarum (puṟanāṉūṟu, 110) paḻantamiḻilakkiyat tirumoḻikaḷum iṅkuniṉaivukūraṟpālaṉa.
(57-59) meyyoṭu miṭaiyāp poyporuḷīṭṭi mey vaḻikaḷoṭu kalavāta aḻiyumporuḷkaḷait tokuttu, tām aṟi aḷavaiyil piṟarkku oṉṟu īyātu - tāmaṟintavaraiyil takkārkku oṉṟu koṭāmal, tāmē nukarum tīyarō uḷar- tāmē nukarkiṉṟa tīya iyalpiṉarō mikappalarāy irukkiṉṟaṉar. `tīyarō eṉpatil ōkāram mikutipporuḷ.
miṭaiyā - kalavāta; miṭaital - kalattal, poyyō ṭiṭaimiṭaintacol (nālaṭiyār, 80) eṉpuḻippōla.
`tam maṉaccāṉ ṟaṟiya oṉṟuṅ koṭār eṉṟaṟkut `tāmaṟi yaḷavaiyiṟ piṟarkkoṉ ṟīyātu eṉṟār. aṉṟi ataṟkut `tamakkut tōṉṟiya aḷavilāyiṉum ētēṉuṅ koṭāmal eṉṟu poruḷuraittalumām. `poyporuḷ eṉpuḻip poymai nilaiyāmaip poruḷil vantatu.
(60-61) tamiḻvaḻakku aṟiyātu - centamiḻ vaḻakkukaḷiṉ nuṭpamum āḻamum parappum viḻuppamum aṟiyamāṭṭāmal, amaivu ila kūṟum - poruttamillātavaṟṟaip pēcum, kaṟṟaṟivu illār iṉip palar - kaṟṟaṟivillātavarē ippoḻutu mikap palarāvar; ēkāran tokkatu. `maṟṟu acai.
centamiḻaik kallātavar ataṉ ṟiṟamaṟiyārākaliṉ, poruntātaṉavellāmpēci ikaḻtaluñ ceyvar. centamiḻukku naṉmai ceyyāmaiyoṭu tīmaiyuñ ceyvārē ulakil mika uḷareṉṟu itaṉkaṭ kuṟippittavāṟām.
(61-64) ataṉāl ātaliṉāl, avarkaṭai toṟum nittalum paṭarntu - av viyalpiṉāruṭaiya illiṉ kaṭaivāyil tōṟum nāḷuñceṉṟu, vāyvāḷātu vallāṅkuppāṭi - vāy cummā irāmal vallavāṟellām pāṭṭukaḷ pāṭi, koṉṉētirital iṉṉātu maṉ - vīṇē alaital tīyatām mikavum; maṟṟu,  ō acai.
vallāṅku - vallapaṭi; vaṭiyanāviṉ vallāṅkuppāṭi (47) eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu. payaṉum iṉṟi iḻivum nērtaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam paṭarntu pāṭittirital iṉṉātāyiṟṟu.
eṉṟitukāṟun, tammai etirppaṭṭa pulavaṉainōkki, aṟivum īkaiyaṟaṅkaḷum uḷḷa celvarkaḷātal aṟiñar kaḷātal illaiyāyk, kalviyaṟivillātavarum picuṉarumē palarāy nirampiya iññāṉṟu nilaiyutal illāp poruḷpeṟa vēṇṭi attakaiyōrai naccinacci uyarnta centamiḻppākkaḷ pāṭi avarpāṟ cellutal peritun tītātal kūṟiṉār. ataṉpiṉ ellāmvalla murukapperumāṉ ṟiruvaruḷai viḻaintu cēṟalē naṉṟāmeṉak kūṟuvāṉ ṟoṭaṅki mutaṟkaṇ appemmāṉṟirukkōyil koṇ ṭeḻuntaruḷiyirukkun tiruvoṟṟimānakarc ciṟappiṉai nuvalkiṉṟār.
(65-67) koḻunaṉai arumpiya kāñci am kōṭṭil - koḻumaiyāṉa moṭṭukaḷ arumpiya kāñcimarak kiḷaiyil, puṉtalaic ciṟuvar ēṟit tuṉṟukaṭal curitiraippāyntu viḷaiyāṭṭayaravum - eḷiya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya neytaṟ ciṟuvarkaḷ ēṟi akkiḷaiyiṉ arukiluḷḷa kaṭaliṉkaṭ curuḷum alaiyiṟ kutittu nīr viḷaiyāṭutalaic ceyyavum;
`kāñci itu marutanilattiṟkuriyamaram eṉpatu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyāṟ (179) pulaṉākiṉṟatu; aḵtu iṅku neytal nilattukkaṇ vantatu karupporuḷ mayakkam; itu tiṇaimayakkuṟutaluṅ kaṭinilaiyilavē eṉṉun tol kāppiya akattiṇaiyiyaṟ cūttirattāl amaikkappaṭum.
puṉṟalaikku viḷakkam muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ayartal - ceytal, (tivākaram)
(68-77) veṇtōṭu virinta kaitai am tātu meypaṭat timirnta noyciṟai vaṇṭiṉam - veṇṇiṟamāṉa maṭalkaḷ virinta tāḻampūviṉ makarantappoṭikaḷ uṭampumuḻutum paṭumpaṭi pūcik koṇṭa melliya ciṟakukaḷaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, vilai aṟu muttamoṭu talaimayakkuṟṟuk kaṇmayakkēṟi eṇṇilakiṭappa - annilattil uṇṭāṉa vilaiyillā muttukkaḷuṭaṉ oruṅkukalantu tēṉ uṇṭa mayakkan talaikkēṟi eṇṇaṟṟaṉa orē niṟamāyk kiṭappa, nuḷaic ciṟu makaḷir vaḷaikkaiyiṉ vāri - neytal nilattuc ciṟu peṇkaḷ tammuṭaiya vaḷaiyalaṇinta kaikaḷāl avaṟṟai aḷḷic cērttu, kuṭampurai paṇilam nirampap peytu - kuṭattai okkuñ caṅkukaḷiṉuḷḷē niṟaiyac  corintu, āmai aṭuppil āmpal neruppiṉ tām aṟi tiṟattāl aṭuvuḻi āmai ōṭṭiṉālāṉa aṭuppiṉkaṇ cevvāmpaṟ pūvālāṉa neruppil tāmaṟinta vakaiyāṟ camaiyal ceyyumpoḻutu, mayakku aṟṟut tuṇṇeṉap paṟantu viṇṇiṭaic cella - avvaṇṭukaḷellām kaḷḷuṇṭa mayakkam oḻintu tiṭumeṉap paṟantu vāṉveḷiyiṟ cella viyappiṭai eḻunta mayakkoṭu niṟkavum - ammakaḷirellām viyappūṭeḻunta matimayakkat toṭu niṟkavum;
`timirtal, pūcikkoḷḷutal, (maṇimēkalai, 19, 86). noymai - meṉmai; vaṇṭukaḷ tātutimirntu veṇṇiṟa māyt tēṉum uṇṭu aṟivumayaṅki muttukkaḷōṭu muttuk kaḷāy vēṟṟumai aṟiyappaṭāmaṟ kiṭantaṉa. talaimayaṅkal - oruṅkukalattal, pukaiyavuñ cāntavum pūviṟ puṉainavum, vakaiteri vaṟiyā vaḷantalai mayaṅkiya (cilappatikāram, 14, 177, 178.) iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa neytalnilac ciṟāriṉ viḷai yāṭṭiyalpukaḷ peritum uṟṟuṇarntu makiḻaṟpālaṉa.
(78-81) vēṟucilar ciṟumiyar - vēṟucilarāṉa neytalnilac ciṟupeṇkaḷ, cāṟukeḻu karumpiṉ - cāṟu nirampiya karumpaiyē ulakkaiyākappaṟṟi, mīṉciṉai aṉṉa veṇmaṇal kuvaii- mīṉ muṭṭaikaḷaippōṉṟa veṇṇiṟamāṉa noyya maṇalkaḷai nellākak kuvittu, tēmpoti maḻalaiyoṭu ciṟupāṭṭu icaittu - iṉimai niṟainta maḻalai moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ciṟuciṟu vēṭikkaippāṭṭukaḷ pāṭi, vayiṉvayiṉ niṉṟu - iṭaṅkaṭōṟum niṉṟapaṭiyāy, makiḻvoṭu kuṟuvavum - makiḻcciyoṭu maṇalnel kuṟṟavum
karumpaiyē ulakkaiyākak kaippaṟṟi maṇalaiyē nellākak kuvittu ataṉāṟ kuṟṟic ciṟumiyar viḷaiyāṭṭayarvar eṉṟavāṟu
veṇmaṇalaiyoppa mīṉciṉaikaḷum veṇmaiyum meṉmaiyum nuṇmaiyum uṭaiyavātal iṅku niṉaivukūraṟ pālatu. kuṟutul kuṟṟutal, iṭittal; melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pāṭiṉaḷ kuṟiṉē eṉṟār kuṟuntokaiyilum, 89: kol yāṉaik kōṭṭāl vetirneṟ kuṟuvāmnām eṉṟār kalittokaiyilum, 42.
(82-83) aḻakucāl neytal nalam uṟa maruviya oṟṟi mānakaril - iṅṅaṉamellām aḻaku nirampiya neytalnila iyalpukaḷ cemmaiyākap poruntiya tiruvoṟṟi mānakariṉ kaṇ;
(83-87) poṉtoḻil polintu viṇtoṭa nivanta iñci vaḷaiiya urukeḻu tirunakar kāṇṭoṟum paraci - poṟ pūccukkaḷālāṉa toḻiṟpāṭukaḷ aḻakupeṟa viḷaṅki vāṉantoṭṭu uyarnta matilkaḷ vaḷainta tōṟṟañ cāṉṟa tirukkōyilaip pārkkum pōtellān toḻutu, malai kuyiṉṟu aṉṉa nilai uyar talaik kaṭai - malaikaḷait tuḷaittu amaittāṟpōṉṟa vāyilnilaikaḷ uyarnta tirukkōpuravāyiṟpaṭiyiṉkaṇ, añcuvaru nōkkamoṭu niṉṟeṉaṉāka - añcutaluḷḷa pārvaiyuṭaṉ otuṅki niṉṟēṉāka
 iñci-  matil, tivākaram. kuyiṉṟu eṉpatil mutaṉilai kuyil atu tuḷaittal eṉap poruḷpaṭum, kuṉṟu kuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa ōṅku nilai vāyil eṉpatu neṭunalvāṭai, 88.
`nakar iṅkuk kōyil; mukkaṭcelvar nakarvalañ ceyaṟkē eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉkaṇṇum (6) vantatu.

vīṟu - polivu; cāṟayarkaḷattu vīṟupeṟat tōṉṟi tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai, 283.
yāraiyō yeṉpatil ai ō acai; nampi nī yārai eṉṟāḷ
cīvakacintāmaṇi, 835. pukuttutal - uṭceluttutal.
pōti - varuti muṉṉilaiyēval; pōtutal - cellutal varutal iṉi aṅku murukapperumāṉ vīṟṟirunta tirukkōlac ciṟappu 133-ām aṭikāṟum viritturaikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(96-97) poṉ puṉai viḷakkam tam kaiyiṉ ēnti - poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷait taṅkaḷ kaikaḷil ēnti, miṉ puṉai makaḷir niralpaṭa niṟpa - miṉṉoḷiyiṉāl amaikkappaṭṭā leṉat tikaḻum iḷamakaḷirkaḷ varicaiyāy niṟpa;
`viḷakkam eṉpatu irumaṭiyākupeyarāyk karuviyai yuṇarttum. `miṉpuṉai yeṉpuḻi `puṉai uvamapporuḷil vantatu.
puṉaital- ceytal, varimaṇaṟ puṉaipāvaikku, puṟanāṉūṟu, 11.
(98-101) kaival pāṭiṉi - yāḻ icaittaliṟ kaitērnta pāṇmakaḷ, kaṟai aṟap peṟṟa payaṉuṭai eḻāl nalaṉuṟa marīi - kuṟṟam nīṅkappeṟṟa payaṉuḷḷa yāḻai aḻakuṇṭākat taṉmaruṅkē irutti, viral nutiteṟitta - taṉ viral nuṉikaḷāṟ cuṇṭiya, narappu icainōkki - narampukaḷiṉ icaiterintu, miṭaṟṟoli porutti neṟippaṭa miḻaṟṟa - taṉ kuralōcaiyai iṇaittu ōcaikaḷ muṟaippaṭa icaippavum;
pāṭiṉi - pāṭumpeṇ; icaipāṭutaṟkeṉavē orukulam `pāṇkulam eṉṟu paṇṭaināḷil iruntamaiyiṉ, `pāṭiṉi yeṉpāḷ pāṇkulappeṇṇāvā ḷeṉka. kaṟai - kuṟṟam; eḻāl - yāḻ; `payaṉuṭaiya eḻāl eṉa iṅkuvantāṟpōlavē pāṭalpaṟṟiya payaṉuṭaiya eḻāal eṉap porunarāṟṟup paṭai (56) yiṉuḷḷum varutal niṉaivukūrka. maruvutal - aṇukutal (tivākaram), maḻīi collicaiyaḷapeṭai. `teṟittal, iṅku mīṭṭal.
(102-105) urum atirntaṉṉa maṇkaṉai muḻavamum - iṭi iṭittāṟpōl olikkum mārccaṉaiyuṭaiya tiraṇṭa mattaḷamum, varaivēy turuviya uraikeḻu kuḻalum - malai mūṅkilaik kaṭaintu tuḷaitta pukaḻporuntiya pullāṅ kuḻalum, pēṇuṟumarapiṉ pāṇiyum - iṉṉum pōṟṟaṟkuriya taṉmaikaḷ vāynta tāḷam mutaliya icaikkaruvikaḷum, vallōr - icaikkavalla icait tuṇaivarkaḷ, pālkeḻu marapiṉ cīritiṉ iyakka - avvavaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭukaḷ viḷaṅkun taṉmaiyiṟ cevvaṉē yiyakkavum;
`maṇkaṉai muḻavam eṉpataṟku `mārccaṉaiyaiyuṭaiya tiraṇṭa muḻavu eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār poruḷuraittār; muḻaviṉ oru pakkattē pūcappaṭum pacaiyoṭukūṭiya maṇṇai mārccaṉai yeṉpar (cilappatikāram, 10,139).
maṇṇamai muḻaviṉ paṇṇamai cīṟiyāḻ
  oṇṇutal viṟaliyar pāṇi tūṅka
eṉṉum porunarāṟṟuppaṭai (110) yaṭiyuraiyilum nacciṉārk kiṉiyar maṇ eṉpataṟku mārccaṉai eṉṟum, pāṇi eṉpataṟkut tāḷam eṉṟum poruḷuraippar.
turuval - kaṭaintu tuḷaittal, ivvāṟu ceytalait turuvit tuḷaittal eṉpar aṭiyārkkunallār (cilappati kāram, āycciyar kuravai, kollaiyañcāral urai)
(106-107) cenneruppu ārnta poṉ urait taṭavil - civanta neruppu niṟainta poṉpūccup pūciya tūpakkālil, akil ney peytu palarniṉṟu pukaippa - akilney ūṟṟip palarniṉṟu pukaippavum;
`cenneruppu eṉṟatu, kaṉal mikka neruppai; urai - pūccu; taṭavu - tūpakkāl, iccol ipporuḷ payattal, maṭavaraṉ makaḷir taṭavunerup pamarntu (ūrkāṇ, kātai 99) eṉṉuñ cilappatikārattāl uṇarappaṭum.
(108-113) vaṭamalaip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattu - vaṭakku malai yeṉappaṭum imayamalaiyiṉkaṇ uṇṭāṉa periya oḷiyuḷḷa vaṭṭakkalliṉkaṇ, teṉmalaip piṟanta naṟum kuṟaṭu uraittu - teṟkumalai yeṉappaṭum potiyamalaiyil viḷainta maṇamuḷḷa cantaṉak kaṭṭaiyait tēyttu, viḻumiya tiraṭṭiya ceḻunaṟum kuḻampil - cīritākat tiraṭṭiyeṭuttuk karaitta ceḻumaiyāṉa naṟumaṇak kuḻampil, aivakai viraiyum virāay - aintuvakaiyāṉa maṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷuṅ kalantu, oy eṉat turuttip peytuceṟippa - curukkākat `turutti yeṉṉum oruvakait teḷikkuṅ karuviyilviṭṭu nirappa, palar niṉṟu talaippeyal māriyiṉ malaiccāntu uṟaippa - palapēr varicaiyāy niṉṟu kārkālamutaliṟ poḻiyum maḻaiyaip pōl ap potiyamalaic cantaṉakkuḻampaic coriya;
`vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭam eṉpataṟku `aḻakukeḻumiya vaṭṭakkal eṉap poruḷuraip piṉumām.
 vaṭamalaip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattut
  teṉmalaip piṟanta cantaṉa maṟuka
eṉavaruñ cilappatikārat (antimālai. 37-8) tirumoḻikku ataṉ arumpata uraiyāciriyar iṅṅaṉamē poruḷuraippar.
`aivakaivirai āvaṉa; `kōṭṭam, turukkam, takaram, akil, cantaṉam eṉpavai (piṅkalantai); takkōlam, ēlam, ilavaṅkam, cātikkāy, karppūram eṉṉun tivākaramum, aṭiyārkku nallāruraiyum (cilappatikāram, 5, 26).
`oyyeṉa viraivupporuṭṭu, nuṅkaiyatu kēḷā vaḷavaiyoyyeṉa (152) eṉpatu porunarāṟṟuppaṭai.
`turutti, paṉṉīrppuṭṭil pōlvatākat tōliṟ ceyyap paṭṭu maṇakkuḻampu pīccuṅ karuvi; ittakaiyatoru karuvi paṇṭaik kālattilēyē uṇṭeṉpatu, neymāṇ civiṟiyar (paripāṭal, 6, 34) eṉpataṟku uḷ viṭṭa puḻuku neyyāṉ māṇṭa turuttiyarāy eṉap parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟiya vuraiyāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
uṟaippa - mikaviḻa, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai (379) urai.
(114-122) vāḻaiyum kamukum vayiṉvayiṉ nāṭṭi - vāḻai maraṅkaḷum pākkumaraṅkaḷum iṭaṅkaṭōṟum niṟutti, ōviya nuṭpamum oṇpaṇinuṭpamum mēviya paṭāam mēluṟakkaṭṭi - ōviya nuṭpaṅkaḷum ēṉaip piṉṉal mutaliya aḻakiya toḻil nuṭpaṅkaḷum poruntiya tiraiyai mēṟparappil aṇaiyak kaṭṭi, kaḻaṅku purai nittilam ilaṅkattūkki - kaḻaṅku eṉṉum oruvakai viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuviyaippōṉṟa parumuttaṅkaḷaik kōvaiyāka viḷaṅkat toṅkaviṭṭu, kēḻkiḷar paṉmaṇi kāḻpaṭa nāṟṟi - aṅṅaṉamē niṟam mikunta palamaṇikaḷaik kōvaiyākat toṅkavaittu, perum taṇ kaṇavīra naṟum taṇ mālai pālvēṟu marapil palapaṭa iyaṟṟi - periya kuḷirnta cevvalari mālaiyaiyum oḻinta naṟiya kuḷirnta mālaikaḷaiyum palavakaiyāṉa viyalpiṟ palavēṟu vakaiyāka aṅkaṅkum aḻakupeṟa amaittu, elimayirk kampalam nilaṉuṟa virittu - elimayiriṉālāṉa virippai nilam paṭiya virittu, viriyiṭai eḻuppiya ariyaṇai mītu - avvirippiṉ naṭuvil niṟuttappaṭṭa ciṅkak kaṭṭiliṉmēl;
ōviyam - cittiram. kaḻaṅku - kaḻaṟcikkāy (tivākaram), aṇaṅkaṟikaḻaṅkil naṟṟiṇai, 8. kāḻ - kōvai, vaṭam, orukāḻmuttam iṭaimulaiviḷaṅka akanāṉūṟu, 73.
peruntaṇ kaṇa vīra mālai, naṟuntaṇmālai eṉap pirittuk kūṭṭip poruḷuraikkum nacciṉārkkiṉiya ruraiyē īṇṭaikkum uraiyāka vaikkappaṭṭatu.
kaṇavīram - cevvalari; peruntaṇ kaṇavīra naṟun taṇmālai (236) eṉat tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyiṉkaṇ ittoṭar iṅṅaṉamē varutal niṉaivukūraṟpāṟṟu. `elimayir eṉpatu, veḷḷeliyiṉ aṭivayiṟṟiluḷḷa mika melliya veṇ mayir; elimayir koṇṭu āṭai mutaliyaṉa neytal cilappatikāra vuraiyiṉkaṇṇum (14: 205), cenneruppu uṇuñ cevvelim mayir, anneruppaḷavu āy poṟ kampalam eṉṉuñ cīvaka cintāmaṇiyiṉ kaṇṇuṅ (2686) kāṇappaṭum.
(123-129) vem katir ñāyiṟu maṇineṭum kōṭṭil taṅkiyirunta takaimai pōlavum - veppam mikunta katirkaḷai yuṭaiya pakalavaṉ nīla niṟam poruntiya - uyarnta kīḻmalai yucciyil vaikittōṉṟiya taṉmaipōlavum, vāṉ katir matiyam mīṉ kuḻām cūḻat tāṉpolintu eḻunta talaimaipōlavum - vāṉattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa taṇkatir nilavu viṇmīṉ kūṭṭañ cūḻat tāṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya talaimainilai oppavum, aṟumukat toruvaṉ kuṟunakai ilaṅka - āṟu tirumukaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya oppaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ puṉṉakai viḷaṅka, nacaikeḻu viḻikaḷ ticaimukam parappi - viruppam mēviya taṉ paṉṉiru viḻikaḷait ticaikaṭōṟuñ cūḻappārttu, kuṉṟāt tiruvoṭum viḷaṅka - kuṟaiyāta aḻakōṭum aṅkeḻuntaruḷi viḷaṅkā niṟpaveṉka.
`maṇi nīlamaṇi, aḵtiṅku nīla niṟattiṉ mēlāyatu ākupeyar.
takaimai pōlavun, talaimai pōlavuṅ kuṉṟāt tiruvoṭum viḷaṅkaveṉak kūṭṭuka. `eḻunta iṅkut tōṉṟiya veṉṉum poruṭṭu.
nacai - vēṭkai, akanāṉūṟu, 22; uyirkaḷ mummalap piṇi tīrntu taṉ ṟiruvaṭip pēriṉpattaip peṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum vēṭkaiyoṭu nōkkutaliṉ `nacaikeḻu viḻikaḷ eṉṟār.
(129-136) kaṇṭu naṉi - apperumāṉai naṉṟākak kaṇṭu, meynnilait toṇṭar tam nilai aṟiyār - uṇmaiyāṉa tavanilai vāynta aṭiyārkaḷ tamatu nilai teriyāmal uṇarviṟantavarāy, viḻinīr cinta - tam irukaṇkaḷum nīr coriya, moḻi iṭaikuḻaṟa - pēccukaḷ iṭaiyiṭaiyē teḷiviṉṟit taṭumāṟa, uccik kūppiya kaiyiṉar - talaimēṟ kuvitta kaiyiṉarāy, paḻicci - paraci, aruḷ kuṟippu uṇarntu - tiruvaruṭ kuṟippai uṇarntapaṭiyāka, āṅku amaivoṭu niṟpa - aṅku amaitiyāy niṟka, veruvaru nōkkamoṭu maram pōlāki aruṇilai uṇarēṉ teruḷiṉṟi ōṭik kuṭantam koṇṭu etirniṉṟeṉa ṉāka - aṭiyēṉ añcutaluḷḷa pārvaiyuṭaṉ marampōl uṇarviḻantu tiruvaruṇilai teriyēṉāyt teḷiviṉṟi viraintōṭik `kuṭantam eṉṉum vaṇakkaṅ koṇṭu apperumāṉ ṟirumuṉ niṉṟēṉāka;
kuḻaṟal - teḷivillāp pēccu.
`naṉṟāyk kaṇṭu eṉṟaṟku naṉikaṇṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. `kaiyiṉar mutaliyaṉa, muṟṟeccam; paḻiccutal - ēttutal; `kuṭantam eṉpatu muṉṉum viḷakkappaṭṭatu.
(137-145) maṭam tapu cil moḻi makiḻak kūṟi aṟiyāmai keṭukkuñ cila iṉcoṟkaḷai aṭiyēṉ makiḻumpaṭi tiruvāy malarntaruḷi, aṉpuṟu pulavōy - aṉpumikka pulavōy, añcal ōmpumati - añcutalai nī oḻippāyāka, kālam kaṭanta kōla vaippil nikarā iṉpam mikaipaṭa nukarntu kaṭaivaḻiyillā nilaiyoṭu polikuvai - kāla varampillāta aḻakiya mēl ulakattil iṇaiyillāta pēriṉpattai nirampanukarntu iṟuvāyillā nilaiyuṭaṉ nī nilaiyāy viḷaṅkuvāy, eṉṟu pala nallurai piṉṟaiyum kūṟit taṉ maruṅku iruntōṉ kuṟippa - eṉṟu pala naṉmoḻikaḷ piṉṉum moḻinta taṉ pakkal iruntōṉāṉa ēvaḷāḷaṉoruvaṟkuk kuṟippiṭa, avaṉum eṉ am kai paṟṟicceṉṟu koṅkukamaḻ nīrāṭu tuṟaiyil niṟuviya aḷavai - avvēvalāḷaṉum eṉ uḷḷaṅ kaiyaip piṭittuk koṇṭupōy maṇaṅ kamaḻkiṉṟa nīrāṭumiṭattil niṟuttiya nērattil;
taputal - keṭutal; paṟaitapu mutukuruku (180) eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟiṉ uraiyiṟ kāṇka.
`cilmoḻi, cilamoḻi; añcil ōti (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, 180) eṉpuḻi aḻakiya cilavāṉa mayiriṉaiyuṭaiyāy eṉpar uraikārar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. añcal ōmpumati eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyiṟ kaṇṭatu (291). `kālaṅ kaṭanta kōla vaippu eṉṟatu. murukapperumāṉ vīṟṟirukkum aruḷulakattai yeṉka; kōlam - aḻaku (piṅkalantai). kaṭaivaḻi - muṭivunilai; `akam kai yeṉpatu nilaimoḻiyiṉ mutaloḻiya ēṉaiyavellāṅ keṭṭu melliṉam mikuntu `aṅkai yeṉa niṉṟatu; itu,
 akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kaimuṉ variṉē
  mutaṉilai yoḻiya muṉṉavai keṭutalum
  varainilai yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka;
  melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa
eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāl (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, 20) muṭikkappaṭum. `akaṅkai yāvatu uḷḷaṅkai; aṅkaiyuḷ nelli (puṟap. ve. 2: 13) eṉpuḻippōla; tōḻamaiyāṉa aṉpai viḷakkutaṟku `aṅkaipaṟṟi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
maṇap paṇṭaṅkaḷ nirampap payilkiṉṟamaiyiṉ, nīrāṭu tuṟai maṇaṅ kamaḻvatāyiṟṟeṉka. koṅku- maṇam; koṅkalar pūmpoḻil (cilappatikāram 10, 220) eṉpataṉkaṇ iccol ipporuṭṭātal teḷiyappaṭum.
(146-154) kārmiṉ aṉaiya ērkeḻu makaḷir kuṟunakai miḷira eṉ arukil pōntu - kārkālattu miṉṉalaiotta aḻaku poruntiya iḷamakaḷir puṉṉakai viḷaṅka eṉpakkattil vantu, mācupotintu pāci āki īrumpēṉum tīrātuuṟaiyum eṉ puṉtalaik kuñci puṇarppuaṟa uḷari - aḻukku nirampip pāciyāy īrum pēṉum nīṅkātu uyirvāḻum eṉatu civanta talaiyiluḷḷa mayiraic cikkaṟakkōti, puḻukuney uraittup paṉinīr āṭṭi īram pulara iṉpukai ūṭṭit tām eṉai viṭuttuppōka - puḻukuney tēyttup paṉinīrāl nīrāṭṭip piṉ ataṉ īram ularumpaṭi maṇamiṉiya akiṟpukaiyaip pukaṭṭi avarkaḷ eṉṉai viṭṭuc cella, ēṉōṉ piṟitōr nalliṭam pukutta - maṟṟōr ēvalāḷaṉ maṟṟoru ciṟanta iṭattil eṉṉai uṭcelutta;
kārkālattē miṉṉalkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttut tōṉṟip paḷīr paḷīr eṉa vīci aḻakiya koṭikkoṭiyāyk kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, kārmiṉ aṉaiya eṉappaṭṭatu. muṉṉum miṉpuṉai makaḷir eṉa vantamai kāṇka. im makaḷir ēvaṉ makaḷirāvar; `kuṟunakai yeṉappaṭṭatu, makiḻcciyai viḷakka.
pāciyāy - caṭaiyāki; (cūṭāmaṇi, 4, 63). puṇarppu - cikku; uḷartal - mayirkōtal; tammēṉinōkkār talaiyuḷarār eṉpatu ācārakkōvai (77). eḷney iṭṭu nīvāmaiyiṟ civanta mayir uṭaimaiyiṟ puṉṟalaiyeṉṟār; paṭṭiṉappālai (90) uraiyuṅ kāṇka. puḻuku, `puṉuku eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. uraittal - tēyttal; paṉinīr itu kuḷircciyum maṇamum uḷḷamaiyiṉ, ippeyar peṟṟatu.
(154-163) āṇṭai - aṅku, kaviṉkeḻu cilatar viraiyiṉar pukuntu - aḻakumikka ēvalāḷar viraivuṭaiyarāy nuḻaintu, viṇṇōr vēntaṉ kaṇ eṉap polinta eṉ tuṉṉal citāar nīkki - viṇṇavar kōṉukku uṭampeṅkum uḷḷa kaṇkaḷpōl viḷakkamāyt terinta eṉatu taikkappaṭṭa kiḻinta kantaiyai akaṟṟi, muṉṉiya aravu uriaṉṉa āṭai uṭīi - matikkat takunta pāmpiṉ tōlpōṉṟa melliya āṭaiyai uṭuttu, kalavaic cāntam pulara aṭṭi- naṟumaṇap paṇṭaṅkaḷ kalanta cantaṉak kuḻampai mārpil ularumpaṭi appi, mukainekiḻntu virinta takaicāl nalmalar piṇaiyalākki maṇamuṟa milaicci - mokku muṟukkaviḻntu malarnta aḻaku nirampiya ciṟanta malarkaḷai mālaiyākat toṭuttu maṇaṅ kamaḻumpaṭi cūṭṭi, tuḷaṅku oḷik kalaṉkaḷ viḷaṅka aṇintu - niḻalāṭukiṉṟa oḷiyaiyuṭaiya aṇikalaṉkaḷai aḻaku viḷaṅkumpaṭi pūṭṭi, maṇittavicu iṭṭu ataṉmicai eṉai irīi - aḻakiya irukkaiyai iṭṭu ataṉmēl eṉṉai irutti;
cilatar - ēvalceyvōr; naṉkaṉi cilataṉ uṇṇanaccuvēṉ (mutti, 127) eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇiyil ic coṟporuḷ kāṇappaṭum.
tuṉṉal - taittal; citār - kantai; vēṟṟiḻainuḻainta vēr naṉai citāar (69) eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu; `tuṉṉal citāar eṉa iṅku vantāṟpōlavē,

  tuṉṉaṟ citāar tuvara nīkki

aravuri, meṉmaikkum veṇmaikkum uvamai; `uri, mutaṉilait toḻilāku peyarāyt tōlai uṇarttum; aṭṭi - appi; aḷinīṭaḷakattiṉ aṭṭiyatātum eṉṟār tirukkōvaiyārilum. takai, aḻakeṉṉumporuṭṭu.
`piṇaiyal eṉpatu malarkaḷiṉ kāmpukaḷiraṇṭaiyum etiretirē oṉṟaṉmēl oṉṟāka vaittup piṇaittuk kaṭṭum mālaiyākum. ākavē iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭṭuñ caraṅkaḷ `piṇaiyaṉmālai yeṉavuṅ kūṟappaṭum. tūmalarp piṇaiyaṉmālai (periyapurāṇam, taṭuttāṭkoṇṭa. 18) eṉpataṉ uraiyaik kāṇka.
(164 - 174) kumari vāḻaiyiṉ kuruttakam virīi - iḷamaiyāṉa vāḻaiyiṉ kuruttai uḷvirittu, aṟucuvaikkaṟivum - āṟu vēṟu cuvaiyuḷḷa kaṟivakaikaḷum, aṭicilum - cōṟum, tuvaiyum - tuvaiyalum, veṟikamaḻ kaṉiyum - maṇam vīcukiṉṟa muppaḻamum, naṟuneyum - nallaneyyum, pālum - āviṉpālum, tīm cumait tayirum - iṉiya ēṭṭuttayirum, tēm kaṉi paḻaṉum - tittippuk kaṉinta ēṉaip paḻavakaikaḷum, kaṉṉalum - carukkaraiyum, piṟavum - piṟa tiṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷum, tuṉṉa iyaṟṟi - neruṅkappaṭaittu, kaṭum pacitīra yāṉ micaitoṟum micaitoṟum - mikka pacinīṅka yāṉ uṇṇuntoṟum uṇṇuntoṟum, iṭumpaiiṉṟi iṭaiiṭai iṭupu - tuṉpa meṉpa tillāmal iṭaiyiṭaiyē avarkaḷ piṉṉum iṭutalāl pāḻ vayiṟu eṉṟum nirampap peytaliṉ - muṉ pāḻāykkiṭanta eṉ vayiṟu niṟaiyumpaṭi yāṉ ataṉkaṇ avaṟṟai iṭutalālē, perum poruḷ eytiya vaṟiñaṉiṉ allāntu iruntapiṉ - mikka celvattai aṭainta vaṟiyavaṉaippōl makiḻntiruntapiṉ, eṉṉaip poruntak kūuy ēṉaiyōr iṭattil tāṉ eṉaic celuva - eṉṉai maṉam porunta aḻaittu maṟṟōriṭattil av vēvalāḷaṉ eṉṉaic celutta;
`kumari yeṉpatu iḷamaipporuṭṭu, itaṉai `īṉātavāḻai eṉpar cilappatikāra (16, 42) arumpatavuraiyāciriyar; kumari vāḻaiyiṉ kuruttakam virittu eṉpatu cilappatikārac coṟṟoṭar.
`cumaittayir ēṭṭut tayireṉavum, ēṭu āṭaipōl mēl mūṭiyiruttaliṉ, āṭaittayir eṉavum vaḻaṅkum; ivvuṇmai tīm pāṟ cumait tayirum (kōvintaiyār, 73) eṉṉuñ cintāmaṇiyāṉum ataṉ uraiyāṉun teḷiyappaṭum.
`tēṅkaṉipaḻam eṉpuḻik `kaṉipaḻam viṉaittokai. `veṟikamaḻkaṉi yeṉa muṉ vantiruttaliṉ ataṉai muppaḻam eṉavum, `tēṅkaṉipaḻam eṉṉum itaṉai ēṉaip paḻavakaikaḷeṉavum uraittukkoḷka. `piṟavum eṉṟatu, vaṭai pāyacam mutaliya tiṉpaṇṭa vakaikaḷ.
`iyaṟṟi paṭaitteṉṉum poruṭu; ilaiyil iṭam aritāmpaṭi kaṟivakaikaḷ neruṅka vaikkappaṭutaliṉ, `tuṉṉa viyaṟṟi yeṉappaṭṭatu; avvaḷavu ērāḷamāṉa kaṟivakaikaḷ eṉpatu karuttu. aṭuttaṭutatu iṭutalāluṇṭān tuṉpaṅkaḷaic ciṟitum pārāmal avvēvalāḷar aṉpuṭaṉ iṭutaliṉ, `iṭumpaiyiṉṟi iṭaiyiṭai iṭupu eṉṟār. `iṭupu viṉaiyeccam; ceypuveṉṉum vāypāṭu.
neṭunāṭ paṭṭiṉiyā yiruntamaiyiṉ `oṉṟumillā vayiṟu eṉpatu tōṉṟap `pāḻvayiṟu eṉappaṭṭatu; pāḻ, oṉṟu miṉmai.
allāntu - makiḻnta; ipporuṭṭātal ataṉetir, collēmātaliṉ allāntukalaṅki eṉpuḻikkāṇka (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, 143).

(174-185) āṅku - avviṭattil, poḻinīṟu iṭṭu - aruḷ poḻikiṉṟa tirunīṟṟai iṭṭu, viḻimaṇi tāṅki - civapirāṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷ eṉappaṭuñ civamaṇikaḷ aṇintu, kāntaḷpōlac cēntoḷir aṅkaiyil civañāṉapōtam navaiyaṟac civaṇi - ceṅkāntaḷ malarkaḷpōlac civantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aḻakiya kaiyiṟ `civañāṉapōtam eṉṉum meykaṇṭanūlaik kaṟpāratu kuṟṟam nīṅkumpaṭi ēnti, paḻutuaṟu māṇavar kuḻuvoṭu cūḻa - kuṟṟamaṟṟa māṇākkarkaḷ ēṉait takutiyuḷḷa aṉpar kūṭṭattuṭaṉ cūḻntirukka, vāṉkatir maṇṭilam īṅku vantatupōl tiruvoṭu viḷaṅkum uruku poṉ tavicil - vāṉattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa oḷimaṇṭilamē iṅku iṟaṅkivantāṟ pōla mēṉmaiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa urukāniṉṟa poṉṉiṉ oḷivāynta irukkaiyil, kalmaṉam urukkum kāṇtaku kōlamoṭu - kallotta vaṉṉeñcaiyum urukkukiṉṟa kāṇṭaṟkuttakka tirukkōlattuṭaṉ, eṉmaṉam urukki eḻuntiṉitaruḷiya - ēḻaiyēṉatu uḷḷattaiyum urukki iṉiteḻun taruḷiya, cōmacuntara kuravaṉaik kāṇūu - cōmacuntara tēcikaṉaik kaṇṭu, ceykuvatu aṟiyēṉ meytaṭu māṟi nilampaṭavīḻntu kalaṅkiniṟpa - ceyvatu teriyēṉāy uṭampu vitirttu nilattiṟ porunta viḻuntu vaṇaṅki añci niṟpa;
`poḻinīṟu aruḷpoḻiyun tirunīṟu; viḻimaṇi, viḻiyākiya maṇi, āvatu `akkamaṇi yeṉpatu. `cērntu, civantu; itaṟkuc cemmai, mutaṉilai.
`navaiyaṟa veṉṟatu, `kuṟṟamnīṅka eṉṉum poruṭṭu; kuṟṟamāvatu malam māyai viṉai yeṉṉum mūṉṟaṉ cērkkai yāluyir kaṇ māṭṭu uṇṭāvatu; `navai kuṟṟameṉṉum poruṭ ṭātal, arunavai āṟṟutaliṉṟu eṉṉum nālaṭi yāriṟ (295) kāṇka.

urukāniṉṟa poṉ oḷi mikutiyumuṭaimaiyiṉ poṉ miṉukkup pūciya tavicukku aḵtu aṭaimoḻiyāyiṟṟu.

(186-192) yāraiyō eṅkaḷ aṟumukattu aiyaṉ tanta tōr aruḷiṉ vantaṉaipōlum - nī yār eṅkaḷ āṟumukak kaṭavuḷ aḷittatō raruḷiṉāl iṅku vantaṉaipōlum, nallai nallai - nī naṉṉilaiyuṭaiyai naṉṉilaiyuṭaiyai, ellaiyil kālam aḻiyāp pēṟṟiṉ nilaiyiṉai - aḷavupaṭāta kālam aḻiyāppēṟṟuṭaṉ nilaiyāka vāḻvai; viḻumiyai - ciṟap puṭaiyai; cevviyai periyai auviyam ilai - cemmaiyuṭaiyai perumaiyuṭaiyai aḻukkāṟu mutaliya tīya iyalpukaḷilai, eṉṟu iṉiyaṉa palavum paṉivarak kiḷantu - eṉa iṉiyavāṉa coṟkaḷpalavum uḷḷaṅ kuḷirumpaṭi iṉimai yāyc colli, taṉ malarkkai eṉ puṉtalaic cērtti - tamatu malaraṉaiya tirukkaiyai eṉatu eḷiya talaimēl vaittu;
malarkkaiyait talaiyiṟ cērttal, taṉkaṇ niṟainta aruḷāṟṟalai māṇākkaṉukku uyttu avaṉṟaṉ uṭal uyirkaḷait tūymaiceytaṟ poruṭṭu āciriyaṉceyyum aruḷvayamāṉa aṟituyilmuṟai; itaṉaic camayanūlkaḷuḷ `tīkkai yeṉpa.
(192-207) pulappaṭa - viḷaṅkumpaṭiyāka, nīyē uṭampum poṟiyu kaṭampaṭukaruviyum aṟivum vaḷiyum allai - uyirākiya nī uṭampum aimpoṟiyum av vaimpoṟikaḷākiya kāṭṭu vaḻic cellum akakkaruvikaḷ nāṉkum aṟivum uyirppum allai; aṟivāyc ceṟiyum peṟṟiyai-  aṟiviyaṟkai yuṭaiyaiyāy yātoṉṟu paṟṟiṉ ataṉ vaṇṇamāy niṟkun taṉmaiyai; maṟṟuavai aṟiyāp paṇpiṉa - coṉṉa avaiyellām aṟiyāta taṉmaiyaṉa; aṉaiyaiyiṉum muṉpē paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇṇiṉ miṭaipaṭu mācupotinta nīrmaiyai - nī aṅṅaṉam aṟiviyaṟkai uṭaiyaiyāyiṉun toṉṟutoṭṭē paṭalam mūṭiya kaṇkaḷaippōṟ ceṟikiṉṟa aṟiyāmaiyākiya kaṟai mūṭiya iyalpiṉai, vīciya oḷip piḻampāki veḷippaṭu murukaṉ āṉātu curanta aruḷiṉaṉākaliṉ - āṉāl iyaṟkaiyē viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟivoḷip piḻampāy veḷippaṭu murukapperumāṉ oḻiyātu ūṟṟeṭukkum aruḷuṭaiyaṉātalāl, niṉṉaimaṟaitta tuṉṉiruḷ turantu taṉ aruṭpolivāl niṉ aṟivu koḷīi - niṉṉai iyaṟkaiyē maṟaitta ceṟinta malaviruḷai ōṭṭit taṉ aruḷviḷakkattāl niṉ aṟivai viḷakki, arukā iṉpam tarutaṟporuṭṭu - ammukattāl niṉakkuk kuṟaiyāta pēriṉpattait tarum poruṭṭu, meynilai irutti mācupōkkit taṉ nilai niṉakkut tantaṉaṉ - cemporuḷ kāṇum meyyuṇarviṉ nilaiyil niṉṉai irutti niṉatu malavaḻukkaik kaḻittut taṉatu tiruvaṭip pēriṉpanilaiyai niṉakkuk koṭuttaruḷiṉaṉ; kōṭi  - nī ataṉaip peṟṟukkoḷvāyāka; eṉṟu uṟuti kūṟi viṭuppat tēṟip paṉṉirukaiyaṉ muṉṉar eyti - eṉṟu uyirkkuṟuti payappaṉa aṟivuṟuttu viṭuppa avvāṟṟāl uḷḷanteḷivaṭaintu paṉṉiru tirukkaikaḷaiyuṭaiya murukappemmāṉṟirumuṉ aṭaintu;
pulappaṭak (192) kūṟi (206) eṉa iyaittuk koḷka.
kaṭampaṭal - neṟiccellal, kaṭam - kāṭṭuneṟi; kaṭampala kiṭanta kāṭuṭaṉkaḻintu (kāṭukāṇ, 90) eṉpatu cilappatikāram; aimpoṟikaḷiṉūṭu taṉvayamiṉṟic cellum maṉavaṟivu palavāṟṟālum alaikkappaṭutaliṉ aimpoṟikaḷ kāṭākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. vaḷi - uyirppu, mūccu.
`maṟṟavai yeṉṟaṉa; uṭampu aimpoṟi akakkaruvi vaḷi mutaliyaṉa. `mācu āṇavamalameṉṉum aḻukku; `vīciya oḷi vīciya; aḵtāvatu oḷiviḷaṅkiya veṉṟaṟku. `viṉaiyiṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya veṉpatu karuttu.
`tuṉiruḷ ceṟinta āṇava valliruḷ; āṇavamalam aṟiyāmai payattaliṉ, iruḷeṉappaṭuvata marapu; tuṉṉiruḷē eṉṉun tiruvācakattil (civapurāṇam) pōla. `turantu ōṭṭi; eṉṉuṭaiyiruḷai ēṟat turantum (kīrttit tiruvakaval) eṉṟu maṇivācakapperumāṉ ipporuḷilēyē aruḷic ceytal kāṇka; punāṉūṟṟilum (14) muṉpu turantu caman tāṅkavum eṉappōntatu.
`emnilai yeṉṟatu, ematu cērkkaiyil eṉṟaṟku. kōṭi, koḷ pakuti; tēṟi, kalaṅkiya uḷḷam teḷinteṉka.
ippakutiyiṟ caivacittānta nuṭpaṅkaḷ mika aḻakākat teḷittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(208-231) imaiyampūtta paṉikeḻu cuṉaiyil amaiyātu āṭiya umaitaru putalvōy - imaiya malaiyiṉkaṇ viḷaṅkiya kuḷircciporuntiya nīrūṟṟil tōṉṟi avāaṭaṅkāmal viḷaiyāṭiya umaippirāṭṭiyār tanta kuḻantāy, eriviḻittu imaikkum tirunutal tantaikku orumoḻiviritta teḷuṟum uṇarvōy - tīkkaṇ tiṟantu oḷiviṭum aḻakiya nutalaiyuṭaiya tantaiyākiya civaperumāṉukku ōmeṉum orumoḻip poruḷai virittaṟivuṟutta teḷivu mikka pēraṟivuṭaiyōy, vāṉōr uṟukaṇ tīrppa mēl ōr aivaṭivēlaik kaiyiṉil eṭuttōy - tēvarkaḷuṭaiya tuṉpaṅkaḷai nīkkumporuṭṭu muṉṉorukāl oppaṟṟa takaṭṭuvaṭiviṉai yuṭaiya kūriya vēlaik kaiyiṉil ēntiyōy, vēntaṉ tanta ēntu - eḻil celvikku varaipurai mārpam varaiyātu aḷittōy - tēvarkōṉ īnta mikka aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya putalviyākiya teyvayāṉaikku niṉatu malaipōṉṟa mārpāṟ ṟaḻuvum iṉpattai aḷavu paṭāmal utaviyōy, kuṉṟavar vaḷartta poṉtikaḻ vaḷḷikkuk koḻukompu ākiya viḻumiya tōḷōy - kuṉṟattuṟaivārāṉa kuṟavarkaḷ eṭuttu vaḷartta aḻaku viḷaṅkiya vaḷḷiyammaiyākiya pūṅkoṭi pōl vāḷukkuk koḻukompāyniṉṟa ciṟanta tōḷkaḷai yuṭaiyōy, arumtamiḻk kīraṉaip poruntik kāttōy - ariya tamiḻnalamvāynta nakkīratēvarai aṇukik kātta pemmāṉē, emmaṉōrkku eḷivanta cemmaiyāḷa - ēḻaiyēṉ pōṉṟārkku eḷitil eḻuntaruḷivanta cemmaiyuṭaiyōṉē, oruva - oru muḻu mutalōṉē, ciṟuva - iḷaiyōṉē, karumayil muruka - nīlamayilai ūrtiyāka uṭaiya murukapperumāṉē, maṟaimuṭitaṉṉil niṟai tiru uruviṉai- tirunāṉ maṟaikaḷiṉ muṭipil niṟaikiṉṟa ciṟanta uruvuṭaiyai, kaṇṇum uṇarvum katuvāp peṟṟiyai - kaṇṇuṅ karuttum aṇukip paṟṟaviyalāta peruntaṉmaiyuṭaiyai, aimperum pūtamum ākiniṉṟaṉai - maṇ mutalāṉa aimperum poruḷkaḷum puṇarntu niṉṟaṉai, ulakuniṉ uruvē - avvaim mutaṟporuḷkaḷiṉ kalavaiyāṉa ivvulakam niṉakku uruvāyuḷḷatu, oḷikaḷ niṉ viḻiyē - ñāyiṟun tiṅkaḷun tīyumāṉa oḷikaṉ niṉakkuk kaṇkaḷāyuḷḷaṉa; alakilāc camayattu avvavar tamakku palavēṟu uruvil nilavukai nīyē - eṇṇilāc camayaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya avvaccamayat tārkkum palavēṟu vakaiyāṉa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷil viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉai nīyē, āṟu niṉ mukam - āṟu niṉ tirumukamām, aintu niṉ vaṭivu - maṇmutalāka aimmutaṟ poruḷkaḷum niṉ uruvākum, nāṉku niṉmoḻi - tirunāṉmaṟaikaḷākiya nāṉkum niṉ tirumoḻikaḷākum; mūṉṟu niṉ kaṇ - mūṉṟu niṉ kaṇkaḷākum, iraṇṭu niṉ tuṇai - vaḷḷi teyvayāṉaiyeṉa iruvar niṉ vāḻkkaittuṇaivarāvar, oṉṟu niṉ vēl - oṉṟu niṉ vēlākum, maruṇṭa uṇarvoṭu māṭcimai eytā muruṭaṉēṟkum tiruvaruḷ tarukai - mayaṅkiya aṟivoṭu aruṭṭaṉmai aṭaiyamāṭṭāta muraṭṭuttaṉa muṭaiyēṟkun tiruvaruḷ tarukiṉṟaṉai;
`pūtta, iṅku `viḷaṅkiya veṉṉumporuṭṭu; mīṉāram pūtta viyaṉkaṅkai nantiya eṉṉum paripāṭaliṟ (16, 36) pōla. ciṟumakār nīril avāvaṭaṅkāmal viḷaiyāṭutal iyaṟkai yākaliṉ `amaiyātu āṭiya veṉṟār. `eri ākupeyarāl neṟṟikkaṇṇai uṇarttum; imaittal - oḷiviṭal, tivākaram. `orumoḻi, `ōm eṉpatu, aḵtu ākupeyarāy ataṉ poruḷai uṇarttiṟṟu; iḵtu eṇṇi yurukum kurunātaṉ eṉpāl uraitta ōrmoḻiyē eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷum aruḷicceytaṉar. uṟukaṇ - tuṉpam; āvatu tēvarkaṭkuc cūraṉāl viḷainta tuṉpam eṉka.
aimmai - takaṭṭuvaṭivu, tivākaram.
vaṭivēl - vaṭittuk kūrmaiyākkiyavēl; murukappirāṉ kaiyatu vaṭikkappaṭṭa taṉṟāyiṉum, vēl eṉṉum peyarop pumai paṟṟi avvāṟaṭai koṭuttār; piḷḷaiyāratu kaivēl aruḷ vaṭiviṉatu; upāṅkamellān tāṉaruḷ eṉṟār civañāṉa cittiyārilum (1, 47). tēvarkaṭku vēntaṉ intiraṉ.
`vaḷḷi yeṉpatu `orukoṭi yeṉavum poruḷpaṭu mākaliṉ, atukaṇṭu aṭikaḷ `koḻukompu eṉat tanturaitta aḻaku iṅku niṉaivukūrntu makiḻaṟpālatākum. eṉavē `vaḷḷi yeṉṉuñ collukku `vaḷḷiyammai yeṉṉum pūṅkoṭi pōl vāḷuk keṉa uraikūṟi makiḻkaveṉpatu. teyvayāṉai ammaikku `mārpam aḷittōy eṉavum, vaḷḷiyammaikkuk koḻukompākiya tōḷōy eṉavum aruḷicceyta kuṟippu, teyvayāṉaiyaip pōlallāmal vaḷḷiyammaiyaik kaḷaviṉāl vēṭṭa vēṟupāṭṭuṇmai viḷaṅkavaittaṟ poruṭṭeṉka.
karumai, iṅku nīlaniṟam uṇarttum. maṟaimuṭi, vētattiṉ muṭinta karuttu. alaku - aḷavu. `uruviṉai eṉpatil `ai muṉṉilai vikuti; `nilavunai, nakaram peyariṭainilai; `tarukai eṉpuḻiyum atu. `mukamē mutalāṉavaṟṟil varum ēkāraṅkaḷ acai. maruṇṭa uṇarvuṭaiyār eytā māṭcimaiyāvatu iṟaivaṉatu aruṭ ṭaṉmaiyēyāku mākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam urai kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(231 - 235) eṉṟu yāṉ aṟi aḷavaiyiṉ ētta - eṉṟiṅṅaṉamellām yāṉ terintavaraiyil vaḻutta, tāṉ uvantu kuṟippu arum iṉpattup piṟakkiṉaṉ eṉai - apperumāṉ maṉamakiḻntu cuṭṭiyuṇartaṟkariya pēriṉpattil uyarttu vaittaṉaṉ eṉṉai, atumutal iṉpam ārntu aṅku iruttal allatu tuṉpam eṉpatu aṟiyēṉ - atumutalāka iṉpam nukarntu avaṉ ṟiruvaṭikkaṇ vāḻntirutta lallāmal tuṉpameṉpatu ciṟitum aṟiyēṉ;
tāṉ, perumāṉ; iṟaivaṉṟarum pēriṉpanilai iṉṉa teṉṟu kuṟittukkūṟal iyalātatākaliṉ, `kuṟipparum iṉpam eṉappaṭṭatu. eṉṟatu, avaṉ cuṭṭiyuṇarappaṭā ṉeṉpatu karutteṉka.
`piṟakkiṉaṉ, piṟaviṉai; `uyarttu vaittaṉaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ; `piṟaṅkal, uyartal; perumai piṟaṅkiṟ ṟulaku eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ (23) pōla; ārtal - nukartal.
(235-269) aṉpoṭu nīyum avaṉvayiṉ celiṉē pāy aruḷ oruvantam tarukuvaṉmaṉṟa - uḷḷaṉpoṭu nīyum ap perumāṉpāṟ ceṉṟāl avaṉṟaṉ perukiya tiruvaruḷai uṟutiyāy niṉakkut tantaruḷuvāṉ mikavum niṉṉai aṇuki, talaippeyal māriyiṉ maruvantu malaippaṭu nīttam - kārkālat tuvakkattiṟ peyyum peru maḻaiyiṉāṟ porunti malai mukaṭṭi luṇṭākum aruviveḷḷam, paim niṟap pīliyum cemniṟat tukirum vēḻa maruppum kāḻ akil tuṇiyum oruṅku varaṉṟi maruṅku viraiik kuṇṭu kaṇ paṭukaril maṇṭi niṟaintāṅku - pacumaiyāṉa niṟam vāynta mayiṟṟōkaiyum civanta niṟam vāynta pavaḷamum yāṉaikaḷiṉ veṇṇiṟamāṉa kompum akakkāḻ uḷḷa atiṟkaṭṭaikaḷiṉ tuṇṭum oṉṟāka vārikkoṇṭu ammalaiyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil viraintōṭi vīḻntu āḻamuṉa iṭattaiyuṭaiya perum paḷḷaṅkaḷil mikkuc ceṉṟu niṟaintāṟpōl, avalak kavalaiyum uvalaic cuvaiyum muraṇuṟu aṟivum tirukiya cerukkum mutalaṟap peyarttuc citaiyātu ōṭi aṉpiṉ niṟainta iṉpa veḷḷattu - vaṟumaiyāluṇṭāṉa kavalaiyum ulakap poy nukarcciyum māṟupaṭukiṉṟa tīya aṟivum muṟukiyacerukkum aṭiyōṭu illaiyāmpaṭi nīkki iṭaiyil aḻiyāmaṟ ceṉṟu aṉpiṉāl niṟainta iṉpameṉṉum veḷḷattiṉkaṇ, paḻiyāk kaṭumpoṭum viḻumitiṉ tuvaṉṟi ēḻulaku aḻiyiṉum aḻiyātu ūḻi ūḻiyum vāḻmaticiṟantē - paḻikkappaṭāta cuṟṟattāruṭaṉ ciṟappāy niṟaintiruntu ēḻulakam aḻintālum aḻiyāmal ūḻiūḻikkālamum ellā nalaṅkaḷum mikuntu vāḻvāyāka veṉpatu.
`nīyum eṉṟatu, etirppaṭṭa pulavarai; pāyaruḷ - perukiya tiruvaruḷ; oruvantam - uṟuti; iccol ipporuṭṭātal,
veruvanta ceytoḻukum veṅkōla ṉāyiṉ
  oruvantam ollaik keṭum
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷāl (563) teḷivāyp peṟappaṭum. `maṉṟa teḷivupporuḷatu; maṉṟaveṉkiḷavi tēṟṟañ ceyyum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (col. 267). maruvaral - maruval; āvatu aṇukaleṉka. aṇaittal, kiṭṭal, poruntutal mutaliya vaṟṟiṟkellām itaṉaip potuc collāka uraippa; talaippeyal māriyiṉ maruvantu malaippaṭunīttam, eṉak kūṭṭuka.
pīli - mayiṟṟōkai; `pīlipey cākāṭum acciṟum eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ, 475. kuṇṭu - āḻam; paṭukar - paḷḷam, tivākaram.
tuṇi - tuṇṭu. varaṉṟal - vārutal. maṇṭi - mikkucceṉṟu. kaṭaṟpaṭai kuḷippamaṇṭi (puṟanāṉūṟu, 6) eṉpataṉuraiyaik kāṇka.
avalam - miṭi, vaṟumai; keṭuniṉṉavalam perum pāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, 38.
`uvalaiccuvai yeṉṟatu, iṅku ulakap poy nukarcciyai; uvalai - poy; uvalaiccamayaṅkaḷ ovvāta cāttiramām (tiruvācakam, tirutteḷḷēṇam, 17) eṉpuḻip pōla. poynukarcci yāvatu `poruḷallavaṟṟaip poru ḷeṉṟuṇarum (tirukkuṟaḷ, meyyuṇartal, 1). uṇarcci yāṉākum nukarcciyeṉka. iṅṅaṉam oṉṟu kiṭakka oṉṟāṉa nilaiyaṟṟa meyyallāc ciṟu nukarcci iṅku uvalaic cuvaiyeṉṉun toṭarāṟ cuṭṭiyuraikkappaṭṭamai peritum iṉpam payappatākum.
tiruvaruḷ nōkkattiṟku māṟupaṭṭa aṟivu  eṉṟaṟku `muraṇuṟu aṟivu eṉṟu aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭatu. tirukiya cerukku - mikutiyāṉa allatu muṟukiya cerukku, kaṭuṅkatir tirukaliṉ cilappatikāram, 12, 1.
kaṭumpu - cuṟṟattār, (puṟanāṉūṟu, 153) tuvaṉṟi - niṟain tiruntu. iḷaiyarum mutiyaruṅ kiḷaiyuṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi (perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, 268) eṉpataṉ uraiyaik kāṇka. (28)
29. kāppuk kaimmikkuk kāmam peruki yuraittal
 ciṟanta poruḷoṟṟic cevvē ḷivarai
  maṟaṅtiṅ kuyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - paṟantōṭit
  tiṇṭō ṭaḻuvuva miṉṟēṟ ceḻuvaraiyaik
  kaṇṭēṟik kīḻviḻuvaṅ kāṇ.
(i-ḷ) ciṟantaporuḷ - ēḻaiyēṉ uyirkkuc ciṟanta poruḷ, oṟṟic cevvēḷ - tiruvoṟṟinakaril eḻuntaruḷiyirā niṉṟa murukap perumāṉē yāvar, ivarai - ipperumāṉai, maṟantu iṅku uyirvāḻa māṭṭōm - maṟantu ivvulakil uyir vāḻamāṭṭōm, paṟantu ōṭittiṇtōḷ taḻuvuvam - viraintu ceṉṟu avaratu valiya tōḷait taḻuvuvōm, iṉṟēl - aṅṅaṉam atu kūṭātāyiṉ, ceḻuvaraiyaik kaṇṭu ēṟik kīḻviḻuvam - oru periya malaiyaik kaṇṭu ataṉ ucciyil ēṟik kīḻē uruṇṭu viḻuntu uyir tuṟappōm;
`ciṟantaporuḷ oṟṟic cevvēḷ ivarai maṟantu iṅku uyirvāḻa māṭṭōm eṉpataṉ karuttu,
  eṉṉi lāru meṉakkiṉi yārillai
  eṉṉi lummiṉi yāṉoru vaṉṉuḷaṉ
  eṉṉu ḷēyuyirp pāyppuṟam pōntupuk
  keṉṉu ḷēniṟku miṉṉampa rīcaṉē
eṉṉum apparaṭikaḷ tēvāra (tiruviṉṉampar, tirukkuṟun tokai, 1) aruṇmoḻik karuttōṭu ottuniṟṟal niṉaivu kūraṟ pāṟṟu.
`cevvēḷ civanta niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya vēḷ eṉa murukap pirāṉ mēl niṉṟatu; vēḷ - kāmaṉ; māṭṭōm, taḻuvuvam, viḻuvam eṉap paṉmaiyāka vantatu, talaivi taṉṉaiyotta ēṉaik kaṟpuṭai makaḷiraiyañ cērttuk kūṟiyavāṟām; allatu avaḷ taṉṉait taṉmaip paṉmaiyil vaittuk kūṟiṉa ḷeṉṟaluḷ oṉṟu. `paṟantu eṉṟatu, viraivuk kuṟippu; ceḻuvarai, vaḷamāṉa malai āvatu uyarntamalai yeṉṟaṟku; kāṇ, muṉṉilaiyacai.
itu, cevilittāy mutaliyōratu kāvaliṟ ciṟaip paṭṭuḷḷa talaivi kāmamperukit taṉatu kaṟpuṭaimai uraitta vāṟām. taṉṉilaimaiyaip piṟaraṟiyavoṭṭātapaṭi evvaḷavu aṭakkiṉālum taṉṉuḷḷa nikaḻcciyāl aḵtu aṭaṅkātu veḷippaṭutalē iṉaiya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ `kaimmikal eṉappaṭum. kaimmikal nalital cūḻcci vāḻttal (poruḷ, 260) eṉṉun tolkāppiyattāṉum ataṉ uraiyāṉum itu teḷiyappaṭum. (29)
30. aṭiyuṟaiyiṭutal
`aṭiyuṟai yiṭutalāvatu, tiruvaṭikkāṇikkai celuttu taleṉka.
kāṇap peṟātator kāṭciyai yēṉuṅ kacittavaṉpāṟ
  pēṇap peṟumuk karuviyiṟ kāṇpar piṟaṅkumoṟṟi
  vāṇap peruntakai yeṉpatu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkumaṉpāṟ
  pūṇap peṟuminta mummaṇik kōvaiyuṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē.
(i-ḷ) kāṇap peṟātatōr kāṭciyaiyēṉum - ēṉai evvāṟṟāṉuṅ kāṇṭaṟkiyalātatōr ariya tōṟṟa muṭaiyaiyē yāyiṉum, kacinta aṉpāl - nekiḻnta uḷḷaṉpiṉāl, pēṇappeṟum mukkaruviyiṉ kāṇpar - pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa niṉaivu coṟ ceyal eṉṉum mūṉṟu karuvikaḷālum niṉṉaik kāṇum peruntavamuṭaiyōr niṉṉaikkāṇpar, piṟaṅkum oṟṟi vāṇap peruntakai eṉpatu koṇṭu - viḷaṅkum tiruvoṟṟimānakaril tirukkōyil koṇṭuvāḻum pēraruṭṭaṉmaiyuṭaiya perumāṉē! eṉṉum ak karuttai uṭkoṇṭu, vaḻaṅkum aṉpāl - ēḻaiyēṉ tēvarīrukku aṭiyuṟaiyāka vaḻaṅkum uḷḷaṉpiṉāl, pūṇappeṟum inta mummaṇikkōvai uṉ pūṅkaḻaṟkē - tēvarīr aṇiyappeṟum inta mummaṇikkōvai yeṉṉum nūl num aḻakiya tiruvaṭikaṭku eṉpatu.
pēṇal iṅkuppātukāttup pōṟṟutal eṉka; eṉṟatu, aṉpu neṟiyiṉiṉṟu piṟaḻātapaṭi kāttup pōṟṟutaleṉpatu, vāṇar, vāḻnar eṉpataṉ tirivu. `peruntakai yeṉṟatu, vaḻaṅkuvār evarāyiṉum avaratu aṉpiṉaiyuṉṉi ataṉai ēṟkum peruntaṉmaiyuṭaiyā reṉṟaṟku. `mummaṇikkōvai innūliṉ peyar.
cālavum ariyaiyāyiṉum aṉpiṉāl eḷiyaiyātal pōl im `mummaṇikkōvai yeṉṉum nūlai nī pūṇṭaruḷutal icaiyātēṉum, aṉpiṉāl vaḻaṅkutaliṉ nī ataṉai ēṟṟu aṇintaruḷutal kūṭum eṉṟu uraittaruḷiya vāṟām. aṉpiṟkoṉṟumē ellāmvalla iṟaivaṉ eḷiyaṉām uṇmai, yāvarāyiṉum aṉpa raṉṟi aṟiyoṇā malarccōtiyāṉ (tiruvācakam, ceṉṉip pattu, 1) eṉṉuñ centamiḻ aruḷuraiyāl iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum. (30)
